### 1. [Sketch, Don't Paint: Allocating Test-Time Compute in Generative Visual Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21812v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21812

**作者：** Sheng Lu、Guangpin Tao、Tong Lu、Ying Tai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21812v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21812v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究生成式视觉推理的测试时计算分配：通过控制实验比较「直接回答／纯文本思考／渲染视觉思考」三种模式，以及每条视觉思考所需的去噪步数，发现因果性视觉思维在约8步时已可读出与原计划一致的信息，装饰性思维可省略、有害思维反而降分；并提出 sketch-and-vote，用少步草图多次独立采样并投票选答。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 THINK／LOOK／STOP 的收益与成本核算和停止决策，其「何时渲染视觉证据」「少步渲染是否已保留任务必要信息」及草图投票的答案聚合方式，可供核查 latent 视觉状态的保真度、冗余与动态计算价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sketch, Don't Paint: Allocating Test-Time Compute in Generative Visual Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal models increasingly reason by synthesizing pixels, sketching maze paths within interleaved chains of thought or rolling out video solutions frame by frame. Each visual thought runs the full diffusion schedule inherited from photorealistic synthesis, so iterative denoising dominates inference cost. We study how to allocate test-time compute along two axes: when to render visual thoughts rather than thinking in text or answering directly, and how many denoising steps each thought requires. Controlled interventions that vary only how the thought is rendered uncover three regimes. Causal thoughts carry the plan and become legible early: an 8-step sketch nearly matches a 50-step render in accuracy, commits to the same plan as often as two independent full renders agree, and in video models mostly fails by stalling short of the goal, so the rollout abstains. Decorative thoughts can be omitted without loss, whereas harmful thoughts lower accuracy. Because the regime depends on both model and task, always drawing is suboptimal. Across 14 model-benchmark pairs of three interleaved reasoners, answering directly, without any thinking, wins 9 pairs, and choosing among direct, text-only, and visual tiers from ten labeled examples per pair raises mean accuracy from 60.7% (always drawing) to 65.1%. Where drawing pays, we propose sketch-and-vote, which samples 8-step sketches (a step count chosen without labels) from independent noises until one answer reaches three votes. With this one setting, sketch-and-vote beats full rendering with fewer denoising steps on an image reasoner and two video reasoners, by 8.6 points on VSP spatial planning and by 9.6 and 22.1 points on mazes.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Does the Model Use What It Cites? Counterfactual Tests of Grounded Reasoning Chains in Small Video-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21940v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21940

**作者：** Jugal Gajjar、Kamalasankari Subramaniakuppusamy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21940v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21940v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出无需标注的「cite gap」干预检验：遮蔽模型自有推理链引用的帧内 box，并与同帧同尺寸对照 box 的遮蔽作配对比较，统计正确答案翻转比例，用以判断答案是否真的依赖所引用区域；在 2–3B 视频语言模型上，CLEVRER 的 cite gap 为 0.089、NExT-QA 仅 0.003，说明引用可验证且相关却不一定被使用。

**相关性简评：** 直接针对「答案是否真的使用视觉证据」，与 LVR 中的视觉旁路、证据筛选和贡献分配问题高度对应；其配对反事实遮蔽可作为核查 latent/显式引用的视觉证据因果作用的现成线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does the Model Use What It Cites? Counterfactual Tests of Grounded Reasoning Chains in Small Video-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** A grounded reasoning chain cites a frame span and a box for every step, so a reader can check where the model looked. Whether the answer depends on the cited region is a different question. We test it by intervention: the regions a model cited in its own chain are blacked out, and the share of correct answers that flip is compared with blacking out boxes of the same size elsewhere on the same frames. The paired difference, the cite gap, needs no annotation. We apply it to 2-3B video-language models trained on exact CLEVRER chains and on pseudo-labelled NExT-QA chains. With the same weights, the cite gap is 0.089 on CLEVRER and 0.003 on NExT-QA. Pooled over models, masking the cited boxes flips 2.2 times as many correct answers as the control on CLEVRER and 1.1 times as many on NExT-QA, also among the answers that depend on the video at all, while the boxes pass a grounding check on both. Masking the cited entity on every frame, instead of the cited box, flips 1.9 times as many answers as its control. The NExT-QA chains were supervised to cite two frames of entities that stay visible on most frames, so this outcome is what the supervision predicts; the point is that the usual check on grounded chains cannot see it. A citation can be verifiable and relevant without the answer depending on it, and a test of grounded reasoning should remove what a chain names, not only where it points.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 4 figures, 27 tables. Code: https://github.com/JugalGajjar/CausalSight

</details>

### 3. [Deciding Without Pointing: A Case Study of Whether a Decision Model Localizes the Anomalies It Detects](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21995v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21995

**作者：** Hsiang-Hung Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21995v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21995v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 以 Clef 决策模型（Qwen 骨干加决策头，逐选项输出概率而不生成文本）在工业异常检测基准上做案例研究，检验其检测决策是否定位到所报异常，并通过隐藏图像 token、分析决策头路由注意力与骨干注意力、探测骨干状态来判断信息实际来源。

**相关性简评：** 直接给出视觉旁路与视觉证据未被决策使用的诊断证据：隐藏图像 token 检测几乎不变、决策头 attention 图仅 0.391 像素 AUROC，且骨干注意力定位随问题文本变化，对「答案是否使用 latent 视觉内容」及状态探测、贡献分配方法可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deciding Without Pointing: A Case Study of Whether a Decision Model Localizes the Anomalies It Detects

**abstract/A1：** Decision models can answer a typed question about an image with a probability per option, without generating text. In our case study of Clef-flash (post-trained Qwen3.5-9B backbone) on industrial inspection benchmarks, we ask whether it localizes the anomalies it detects. We obtained uneven results with zero-shot detection: per-category image AUROC 0.924 on MVTec AD and 0.668 on MVTec AD 2. On the former, the decision head's routing attention produces 0.391 mean per-image pixel AUROC, and hiding the image tokens from it leaves detection unchanged. The multimodal LLM's backbone attention does point at the defect, however it falls below a center prior when we change question. It falls below chance if we remove textual context in the question, partly due to backbone attention leaving the object, while the head's map hardly changes. The backbone's states carry the location when probed with normal images, on the test-split images, however, the decision depends on the defect region. A larger model, Clef, that comes with a post-trained Qwen3.8-27B backbone, gives no useful map in the decision head either. We found that it's possible to recover anomaly location by asking question specifically on an image region, retraining the head or reading the backbone, but none matches dedicated few-shot localizers.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures, 14 tables. Preprint of the technical part of an MSc thesis (Politecnico di Milano). Code and pre-registrations are held privately until venue submission

</details>

### 4. [Thinking Harder Doesn't Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22033v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22033

**作者：** Adi Krishnamoorthy、X. Y. Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CY、cs.HC、econ.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22033v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22033v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在基于 WebShop、SQID 与 WANDS 构建的三个多模态商品选择基准上评测十个前沿模型，检验提高推理努力是否提升购买决策质量、其自报置信度是否可信校准；结果显示更多思考不显著提升正确率，却显著抬高置信度，且多数模型校准普遍不佳、错误在不同模型间高度重合。

**相关性简评：** 这是一篇直接针对「THINK 的收益与成本」的负结果诊断，可为推理努力是否换取正确率、以及能否用自报置信度做停止或筛选证据的信号提供核查线索；但其衡量的是商品选择正确率，是否真正利用了图文证据中的视觉细节需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Thinking Harder Doesn't Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous shopping agents that process multimodal product listings (consisting of visual and text data) and recommend purchases on behalf of consumers. Naturally, one might assume that models with more thinking power would make higher-quality purchasing decisions. To test this belief, we ask two questions about such agents: does asking a model to think harder make it choose better products, and does the confidence it reports tell us the likelihood it may be wrong? We benchmark ten frontier models on three product-choice benchmarks built from WebShop, Amazon's SQID and Wayfair's WANDS platforms to see if LLMs with more thinking power actually make better-quality product purchase decisions. Across all three benchmarks, we found that increasing the thinking effort does not increase decision quality in a statistically meaningful way. The same change in thinking effort raises stated confidence at a statistically significant level, so extra thinking buys confidence rather than correctness. We also measure the calibration of these models (how well their reported confidence percentage matches their true correctness percentage) in these tasks and show that most models are not well-calibrated at every effort level. We also find that errors are strongly shared: when one model errs in a task, several others tend to err on that same task. Furthermore, on tasks where several models all err, a large number of them pick the same wrong product.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [Not All Evidence Is Admissible: Evidence Governance for Tool-Augmented Medical Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22169

**作者：** Runxun Zhang、Onofrio Antonio Catalano、Jinqiao Wang、Jingwei Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22169v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出工具增强医疗 agent 把工具输出无条件当作证据会导致「证据劫持」，即本可答对的病例因错误工具输出被翻转，并用 V-information 解释；据此提出 CIEG 框架，把每个工具输出写成可对照原始输入核验的卡片，由只看输入与卡片的独立 checker 裁定其能否作为证据，reasoner 仅凭准入卡片作答，无需训练，在医疗问答、眼底青光眼筛查与胸片肺炎分类上取得较高准确率。

**相关性简评：** 与关注视觉证据筛选、证据是否被真正使用以及「看得更少反而更好」的收益归因直接相关，其区分内容筛选增益与单纯缩减上下文的思路可供核查，并可能用于 LVR 中视觉旁路与工具输出准入的分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Not All Evidence Is Admissible: Evidence Governance for Tool-Augmented Medical Agents

**abstract/A1：** Tool-augmented medical agents treat whatever their tools return as evidence. We show that this default is costly: medical tools fail silently, and a frozen reasoner cannot ignore what enters its context, so many cases it would have answered correctly are flipped to wrong once tool outputs are added. We call this evidence hijacking. $\\mathcal{V}$-information explains the phenomenon: the reasoner has lost the option to ignore, so a tool output can carry negative usable information, and moving the decision of what to keep outside the reasoner restores that option. The theory further shows that part of any filtering gain comes merely from letting the reasoner see less, and only selection by content lets it see better. Building on this, we recast tool-augmented reasoning as evidence governance, which separates deciding what counts as evidence from the reasoner that answers, and propose context-independent evidence governance (CIEG), the first medical agent framework that rules, after acquisition, on whether each individual tool output may enter as evidence. Every tool output is written as a card that can be checked against the raw input; an independent checker that sees only the input and the cards decides which cards become evidence; and the reasoner answers from the admitted cards alone, with no training anywhere. On medical question answering, fundus glaucoma screening, and chest X-ray pneumonia classification, it achieves the highest accuracy among models of comparable scale, and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art tool-using agents and multi-agent systems built on the same backbone at lower cost.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [CueClash: Frontier Vision-Language Models Judge 3-D Relations Close to the Picture Plane](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22180v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22180

**作者：** Soham Dan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22180v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22180v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建 CueClash 渲染场景，将 2-D 图像线索与 3-D 地面布局分离，测量前沿 VLM 判断五种空间关系时对画面捷径的依赖，并通过俯视布局图探查其内部深度表示。

**相关性简评：** 直接诊断 VLM 是否把画面线索而非场景几何用于空间推理，并考察内部布局图对深度顺序与尺度的保留，与「视觉旁路」「几何细节是否留存于内部状态」及证据筛选高度相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CueClash: Frontier Vision-Language Models Judge 3-D Relations Close to the Picture Plane

**abstract/A1：** A vision-language model (VLM) can judge 3-D relations from the picture or from the ground layout. In photographs the two usually agree. We separate them with CueClash, rendered scenes in which the 2-D cue for each of five relations is right on exactly half the statements. The shortcut gap is accuracy when the cue is right minus accuracy when it is wrong. For Claude Opus 5.5, GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.6 Flash at low reasoning effort it is +18 to +37 percentage points. It is +7 to +29 without two relations whose cue-wrong items are confounded with borderline cases or occlusion. Given ground coordinates and each relation's definition, they answer 99 to 100% correctly. Pixel measurements with camera depths remove most of the gap for objects fitting through openings. The failure lies in the step from the picture to the layout. The maps that the models draw of the scene from above mostly keep the depth order but, like the picture, shorten depth. GPT-5.5's maps stay close to a copy of the picture. Opus 5.5's and Gemini's maps undo about 0.6 of this shortening. Gemini's then shrink all depths by a further uniform factor. Opus 5.5 follows the picture mainly on fits, and its maps correct depth about as much as human observers of real scenes do. The three models also lose accuracy on CV-Bench photographs where a reliable 2-D cue misleads. In two open models the layout information reaches the language model, but their maps lose much of it.

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [Similar Is Not Useful: Selecting In-Context Demonstrations by Closed-Loop Utility](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22295v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22295

**作者：** Jiachen Li、Zhigen Zhao、Shihao Li、Dongmei Chen、Long Chen、Zhuo Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22295v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22295v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验「视觉相似」是否等于「有用」：在冻结的检索增强 VLA 上让示范池可在运行时替换，用 60 个固定池的闭环成功率回归出各示范的效用，并比较高效用池、最相似池与随机池的表现。

**相关性简评：** 直接给出「相似不等于有用」的量化证据，与视觉证据筛选、以及嵌入相似度能否承载任务必要信息的问题相通；其闭环效用打分可供核查是否能迁移到推理期的证据或示范选择。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Similar Is Not Useful: Selecting In-Context Demonstrations by Closed-Loop Utility

**abstract/A1：** Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) policies fill their context with the demonstrations whose frames look most like the current observation under frozen DINOv2 embeddings. We ask whether visual similarity selects the demonstrations that make the policy succeed. For a frozen retrieval-augmented VLA (RICL on $π\_0$-FAST, LIBERO), we make the demonstration pool swappable at run time and score each demonstration's utility by regressing the closed-loop success of 60 fixed-size pools on their membership. On initial states disjoint from those used for scoring, pools of the five highest-utility demonstrations succeed on 66.4% of 760 paired episodes across 19 tasks, pools of the five most similar on 55.7%, and random pools on 55.5%. Utility beats similarity by 10.8 points ($p=3.2\\times10^{-6}$; positive on 15 of 18 untied tasks), including on 12 tasks the policy was never primed on, and similarity-selected pools do no better than random ($+0.1$ points). Within a task, a pool's visual similarity to the test scenes tracks its success at Spearman 0.10; across tasks, similarity separates tasks whose scenes differ and falls to chance when two tasks share a scene. The gain comes from on-task closed-loop evidence: best-of-ten direct search over pools recovers two-thirds of it with 100 rollouts. Against the shipped 20-demonstration pool, utility-curated pools of five lead by 7.0 points over 560 paired episodes from two evaluation runs ($p=0.006$), and utility-ordered retrieval inside the full pool leads by 4.5 points ($p=0.055$).

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [Cross-Modal StateKV Injection for Communication in Multi-LLM Systems across Temporal Segments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22304v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22304

**作者：** Xiaoqian Zhang、Zhihao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22304v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22304v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Cross-Modal StateKV Injection：观察互补视频帧的 provider 模型将视觉 token 位置的 KV 状态对齐投影到 carrier 表示空间并门控残差注入，使多 LLM 系统以 latent KV 而非文本传递视觉观察；在 MLVU 和 Video-MME 上比较其与纯 carrier、文本通信的准确率和延迟。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉证据能否以内部 KV 状态跨模型保留和融合，可供核查 latent 视觉通信的信息保真度、任务依赖收益及额外计算成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Modal StateKV Injection for Communication in Multi-LLM Systems across Temporal Segments

**abstract/A1：** Video understanding with multiple multimodal language models requires a communication interface that can transfer complementary visual observations. Text-based communication is interpretable, but introduces an intermediate generation stage and can omit information relevant to subsequent reasoning. We study Cross-Modal StateKV Injection, a provider--carrier framework that communicates through key--value (KV) states at visual-token positions. Provider models observe complementary video frames; their visual KV states are aligned and projected into the carrier representation space and integrated through gated residual updates. Injection preserves the carrier KV-cache sequence length, while retaining the additional computation required by the providers and the communication module. The reported experiments on MLVU and Video-MME compare carrier-only inference, text-based communication, and StateKV with two provider architectures. Results show modest, task-dependent accuracy gains and lower reported latency than the evaluated text-communication baseline, with some categories remaining unchanged or decreasing. Increasing provider-side frame coverage offers additional gains on selected tasks without extending the carrier cache. These findings motivate further study of visual KV communication under explicitly controlled observation and computation budgets.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Equal contribution: Xiaoqian Zhang and Zhihao Zhang

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [Can Vision-Language Models Truly Understand Images? A Benchmark Beyond Commonsense Bias](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22360v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22360

**作者：** Ling Lin、Heng Su、Xiangzeng Liu、Yang Bai、Congcong Zhu、Jingrun Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22360v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22360v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 ContraSense 基准，用违背常识的图像-文本对削弱统计先验，评测多种开源与闭源 MLLM 是否真正依赖视觉证据作答，并报告最强闭源模型约 60% 准确率、远低于人类的 96%。

**相关性简评：** 该诊断直接检验 VLM 是否因语言先验而绕过视觉内容，可作为核查 LVR 中视觉旁路与信息坍缩代价、以及 latent 状态是否保留任务必要视觉细节的评测参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Vision-Language Models Truly Understand Images? A Benchmark Beyond Commonsense Bias

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. However, it remains unclear whether their predictions are grounded in the visual content itself or are largely driven by statistical co-occurrence patterns and commonsense correlations learned from large-scale image-text corpora. Existing vision-language benchmarks predominantly consist of samples that conform to human commonsense, allowing models to achieve strong performance by exploiting dataset priors rather than genuinely understanding image content. To address this limitation, we present ContraSense, a systematically constructed benchmark for evaluating faithful vision-language understanding. ContraSense consists of carefully curated counter-intuitive image-text pairs that deliberately violate common expectations, substantially reducing the effectiveness of statistical biases and requiring models to rely on visual evidence for reasoning. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on a diverse set of representative open- and closed-source MLLMs. The results reveal a substantial performance gap between current MLLMs and humans: even the strongest closed-source model achieves only about 60\\% accuracy, compared with 96.04\\% for human participants. These findings demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle with faithful visual understanding and highlight the need for more robust multimodal reasoning grounded in image content. The ContraSense benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingLin00/ContraSense.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [SpatialMind: Adaptive Multi-Level Geometric Fusion for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22415v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22415

**作者：** Shibin Zhao、Hao Tan、Fan Yang、Haixin Wang、Feilong Chen、Yidong Ma、Chaoyang Zhao、Ming Tang、Jinqiao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22415v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22415v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 MLLM 的 3D 空间推理，通过实验说明不同认知层次的空间任务需要不同粒度的几何表征，并提出 SpatialMind：用受文本指令、视觉语义与几何表征共同条件的门控网络，在 LLM 解码器各层自适应调节浅、中、深几何特征的贡献，并加负熵正则防止门控塌缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定多级融合不足」的诊断与逐层按任务调节几何信息注入，直接关系到 LVR 关注的「任务必要视觉与几何信息是否保留在内部状态」，且门控对几何证据的筛选是否造成信息坍缩、与层深和任务难度的关系可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpatialMind: Adaptive Multi-Level Geometric Fusion for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate promising capabilities in 2D visual understanding, yet remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning. Recent studies explore integrating 3D reconstruction models into MLLMs. However, these approaches either use a single geometric granularity across all tasks or adopt a fixed multi-level fusion scheme, regardless of task-specific reasoning requirements. Through systematic experiments on multi-level geometric fusion, we find that spatial tasks at different cognitive levels (e.g., distance estimation vs. route planning) require geometric representations of different granularities, revealing that fixed fusion cannot accommodate diverse spatial reasoning requirements. To bridge this gap, we propose SpatialMind, a task-adaptive framework for dynamically integrating multi-level geometric representations. SpatialMind employs GeoFus, a multi-layer geometric fusion module built around a gating network jointly conditioned on textual instructions, visual semantics, and geometric representations. This network adaptively modulates the contributions of shallow, intermediate, and deep geometric features across the layers of the LLM decoder. Furthermore, we introduce a negative-entropy regularization loss to mitigate gate collapse. Gate visualizations indicate that GeoFus adapts geometric granularity to task requirements. Experiments demonstrate that SpatialMind achieves competitive performance across spatial benchmarks, attaining a state-of-the-art score of 72.3 on VSI-Bench. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhaosb-star/SpatialMind.

**comment/C1：** Includes supplementary material. Code available at https://github.com/zhaosb-star/SpatialMind

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [PACD: Provenance-Aware Consensus Decoding for Mitigating Cross-Modal Hallucinations in Omnimodal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22456v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22456

**作者：** Chang Sun、Yijun Tian、Zhenwei Tang、Yuchen Deng、Yifan Simon Liu、Chenxu Wang、Wei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22456v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22456v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练解码框架 PACD，通过相关性引导的候选裁决与基于来源（provenance）的共识恢复，利用逐层模态证据来抑制全模态大模型的跨模态幻觉。

**相关性简评：** 其逐层模态来源证据的一致性评估与候选仲裁，直接涉及 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、模态贡献分配与答案是否真正使用对应模态内容，可供核查噪声模态与错误共识如何扭曲推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PACD: Provenance-Aware Consensus Decoding for Mitigating Cross-Modal Hallucinations in Omnimodal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Omnimodal large language models (Omni-LLMs) integrate audio, video, and text for multimodal reasoning, yet remain vulnerable to cross-modal hallucinations, where one modality improperly influences predictions about another. Existing training-free decoding methods mitigate these errors through modality perturbation or relevance-based weighting. However, due to interference from irrelevant modality cues during fusion and difficulty detecting shared errors, these methods may suppress correct unimodal predictions and allow erroneous consensus to persist. To address these limitations, we propose Provenance-Aware Consensus Decoding (PACD), a training-free framework for mitigating cross-modal hallucinations. PACD comprises two complementary paths based on whether the branches reach consensus. For non-consensus cases, Relevance-Guided Candidate Arbitration identifies task-relevant unimodal evidence and selectively arbitrates between predictions using alternative candidates. For consensus cases, Provenance-Based Consensus Recovery leverages the outranking principle to evaluate the consistency and strength of layer-wise modality evidence for alternative answers, selectively resolving erroneous consensus under confidence constraints. Extensive experiments on four Omni-LLMs and five benchmarks across two tasks demonstrate the superiority of PACD. Compared to existing methods, PACD achieves up to 27.6% relative improvement over standard decoding across CMM, AVHBench, and SVHalluc for cross-modal hallucination mitigation, and an average relative improvement of 7.7% over best baseline across OmniVideoBench and FutureOmni for general audio-visual reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [Disagree to Prune: Reallocating Visual Tokens Where a Small VLM Dissents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22472v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22472

**作者：** Yanchao Li、Jiaqing Xie、Ben Gao、Yusu Zhang、Beilun Wang、Yanbo Wang、Xiaohua Xu、Zhongyu Huang、Tianfan Fu、Nanqing Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22472v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22472v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究视觉 token 剪枝后答案何时可信，提出 PRUDENT：先用约一半预算作答，再以小 VLM 在完整图像上的答案分歧识别可能丢失证据的问题，将节省的 token 用于双倍预算重答。

**相关性简评：** 工作直接涉及按问题动态分配视觉证据与计算；其「小模型分歧」信号可供核查能否用于 LVR 的 LOOK/STOP 决策及视觉证据不足诊断，但并非 latent 状态生成方法。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Disagree to Prune: Reallocating Visual Tokens Where a Small VLM Dissents

**abstract/A1：** Visual token pruning speeds up large vision-language models (VLMs) by keeping only a part of their visual tokens. Most existing methods improve the ranking of the tokens and then answer every question once with the same budget. With strong rankings, pruning hurts only a few questions, and signals computed on the kept tokens see little of what it removed. We find that, on most of these questions, a small VLM given the full image disagrees with the pruned answer, since it still sees the missing evidence. We propose PRUDENT, which first answers with about half the budget and spends the saved tokens on re-answering the disagreeing questions at twice the budget. We show when such re-answering pays and why the small VLM should read the full image. On 18 benchmarks, PRUDENT outperforms eight existing pruning methods at the same average budget of visual tokens. Its lead widens as the budget tightens, and holds across five InternVL sizes. PRUDENT also has the highest average in two more model families. The disagreement of the small VLM flags most pruning failures of all eight methods, and re-answering on it also improves the two strongest with their own rankings. Other checkers give similar gains, and the extra tokens go where the first pass hurts the answer. These results suggest that identifying which pruned answers are trustworthy provides an additional lever for visual token pruning beyond the ranking of tokens. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PRUDENT.

**comment/C1：** 45 pages, 8 in the main text. Code and data: https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PRUDENT

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22541v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22541

**作者：** Jiachun Li、Zhuoran Jin、Kang Liu、Jun Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22541v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22541v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MentalGrid 基准，考察 MLLM 在无中间视觉反馈条件下模拟空间状态演化的能力，并指出简单场景受感知限制、复杂场景受多步推理不足限制，进而用 1.6 万条空间样本的三阶段课程学习训练出 SpatialWalker。

**相关性简评：** 「无中间视觉反馈的动态空间状态模拟」直接涉及 VLM 内部状态是否保留任务必要几何信息及多步状态更新，其感知／推理瓶颈分解与课程训练可供核查内部状态使用与视觉旁路的区分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Spatial reasoning is a fundamental component of world understanding for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing research has advanced the perception of spatial relations in static scenes, the domain of dynamic spatial simulation remains relatively unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce MentalGrid, a benchmark focused on spatial reasoning tasks that require models to simulate evolving spatial states without intermediate visual feedback. Extensive evaluation on our benchmark reveals a significant deficiency in the spatial simulation capabilities of existing models. Specifically, we find that performance is constrained by perceptual limitations in simple simulation scenarios and hindered by inadequate multi-step reasoning as complexity increases. To address these challenges, we propose a three-stage curriculum learning framework utilizing 16k specialized spatial reasoning samples. This approach yields SpatialWalker, which achieves a 33.8% average improvement over its base model, surpassing proprietary models like Gemini-3-Flash in spatial simulation. Furthermore, SpatialWalker demonstrates robust generalization, maintaining a 4.9% average gain on out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrating the pivotal role of spatial simulation as a fundamental building block for broader spatial intelligence.

**comment/C1：** Findings of EMNLP 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Lost in Read-out: Locating the Image Quality Gap in Vision-Language Models with a Closed-Form Edit](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22564v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22564

**作者：** Zhuangzi Li、Shilv Cai、Jian Jin、Zhiyi Pan、Haihong Xiao、Weisi Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22564v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22564v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在五个 VLM 上用逐层线性探针区分图像质量判断失败是「看不见」还是「说不出」，发现质量信息在视觉编码器与最后语言层都高度可解码，损失主要发生在最终 read-out，并用闭式编辑最后一层单个神经元做干预验证。

**相关性简评：** 提供了「内部状态保留信息但答案未使用」的逐层诊断加因果干预范式，与 latent 内容是否被答案使用、read-out 瓶颈和信息坍缩直接相关；但任务是图像质量评分而非多步视觉推理，向 LVR 场景的可迁移性需阅读后自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lost in Read-out: Locating the Image Quality Gap in Vision-Language Models with a Closed-Form Edit

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) are widely used as zero-shot image quality assessors, yet their good-vs-poor answers agree only moderately with human opinion. We ask whether these models fail to see quality or fail to say it. Layer-wise probes on five VLMs point to the second: quality is linearly decodable from the vision encoder and still from the last language layer, far better than the answer reveals, and about a hundred labels recover most of it. The loss lies mainly in the final read-out, which partly reads something else: relative to human opinion, it over-rewards colorfulness and under-rewards sharpness on all five models. We test this by intervention: if the read-out is at fault, a closed-form edit of a single last-layer neuron, fitted to many prompts at once, should close the gap and no more. Both hold. Under prompts never used for fitting, the edit recovers 86--100\\% of what a ridge head on the last-layer state, fitted under the same prompts, finds beyond the answer on KonIQ. Where that gap is small, it gains little, too little for our transfer criterion on the two largest models; where the head itself is below the answer, it lowers the score; and it works only when written from the middle of the language model on. Unlike unconstrained fine-tuning, the edit leaves perception question answering intact. It does not carry over to yes-or-no or rating questions, and it reverses the good--poor contrast after sentiment-laden sentences. Code will be made available.

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Semantic-Guided Geometric Decoding for Token-Efficient Visual Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22577v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22577

**作者：** Zhaojie Chen、Junyan Yang、Le Yang、Qi Dang、Yucheng Huang、Qianyi Che、Zhaoqiang Chen、Xinqi Zhu、Qiang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22577v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22577v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SemGeo，用单个 grid token 做实例粗定位，再由几何解码器复用视觉特征与隐藏状态并行回归离散框坐标，并结合 Gaussian 软标签和基于框集合奖励的强化后训练来对齐训练与推理。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉几何信息在内部状态中的保留与并行坐标生成，其“隐状态复用解码”和软标签监督可供核查 LVR 中空间表示、token 效率及训练-推理分布错配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Semantic-Guided Geometric Decoding for Token-Efficient Visual Grounding

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a unified interface for language-conditioned visual perception. In pixel-to-sequence grounding, autoregressive coordinate generation requires multiple sequential decoding steps per box, increasing inference cost with the number of instances. Pixel-to-embedding methods reduce this overhead but lack explicit spatial encoding when using a shared special token. We introduce SemGeo, a token-efficient visual grounding framework that represents each instance with a single grid token for coarse localization. A geometric decoder reuses visual features and hidden states to recover precise discrete box coordinates in parallel. To train proposal generation and box localization together, we introduce Gaussian soft labels that encode coordinate distances during supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Reinforcement post-training further combines box-set rewards with coordinate supervision on model-generated proposals. This stage trains the language model and geometric decoder on their own predictions to mitigate the mismatch between teacher-forced training and inference. Across eight benchmarks spanning object detection, referring object detection, and document layout grounding, SemGeo achieves the highest F1@mIou among compared MLLMs on six benchmarks. It also delivers $2.07\\times$ and $1.25\\times$ the mean box-generation throughput of representative grounding baselines under vLLM and Transformers, respectively, while achieving higher accuracy in both comparisons.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [PACT: Plan-Action Consistency Training by Preserving Correct Plans and Repairing Actions in GUI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22591v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22591

**作者：** Zhuohan Cai、Zhangxuan Gu、Runze Li、Beitong Zhou、Ming Wen、Hongbo Yu、Shuheng Shen、Changhua Meng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22591v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22591v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 GUI agent 中「计划正确但动作执行错误」的执行鸿沟，提出 PACT 训练框架：用参考动作定位执行失败，遮蔽推理计划片段上的负向策略梯度以保护已验证计划，再用偏好替代目标修复动作，在 AndroidWorld、AndroidControl 与 OSWorld 上取得提升。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 关注的贡献分配与监督直接相关：把结果级 advantage 拆分到推理计划与动作 token 上，可供核查其思路是否适用于 latent 推理状态的信用分配，以及「答案是否真正使用推理内容」的诊断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PACT: Plan-Action Consistency Training by Preserving Correct Plans and Repairing Actions in GUI Agents

**abstract/A1：** GUI agents can formulate a correct immediate plan in their reasoning yet emit an action that fails to execute it, creating an execution gap. This recurring mismatch undermines reliable task completion, as a single execution error can derail a multi-step workflow. In outcome-supervised reinforcement learning, broadcasting a negative response-level advantage across all generated tokens risks penalizing valid planning together with erroneous execution. To address this credit-assignment conflict, we introduce PACT (Plan-Action Consistency Training), a training framework that combines reference-aligned plan protection and plan-aligned action repair without altering the original scalar action reward. Using annotated reference actions to identify execution gaps, PACT protects the verified plan by masking negative policy-gradient contributions across its token span. Keeping the generated reasoning unchanged as context, it then favors the reference action over the emitted erroneous action through a preference surrogate with stop-gradient pair weights. Compared with the outcome-only baseline, PACT raises AndroidWorld task success from 60.34% to 68.97% (+8.63 pp), while improving rule-based plan-action consistency on AndroidControl High from 83.71% to 89.74% (+6.03 pp). Adapting PACT to desktop agents also improves task success from 40.92% to 44.17% (+3.25 pp) on the full 369-task OSWorld benchmark. Together, these results establish PACT as an effective credit-assignment framework for bridging execution gaps and improving task completion across mobile and desktop GUI environments.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 11 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [SpatialCast: Reading the 3D World via Cast Shadows](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22657v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22657

**作者：** Enhan Li、Jinghao Zhou、Jiaze Zhang、Hongyang Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22657v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22657v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SpatialCast 基准，利用投影阴影考察 MLLM 能否把 3D 场景的几何、位姿、光照与视角同图像观察联系起来，并设置三类任务评估投影一致性、干预后的变化预测及由视觉变化反推物理变化。

**相关性简评：** 它直接诊断多模态模型能否筛选并利用视觉证据推断场景结构与物理变化，与 LVR 中的视觉信息保留、证据筛选和因果作用评估有明确联系，值得关注其任务设计与失败模式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpatialCast: Reading the 3D World via Cast Shadows

**abstract/A1：** As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance toward understanding and interacting with the physical world, spatial reasoning becomes essential for understanding scene structure and anticipating the effects of physical changes. Existing spatial benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating spatial relations, memory, and viewpoint reasoning, but less systematically assess how models connect physical scene conditions with visual observations. We introduce SpatialCast, a benchmark that uses cast shadows to evaluate this ability. Cast shadows provide observable evidence of a 3D scene. Their image appearance depends on object geometry and pose, illumination, and viewpoint. We examine this relationship through three complementary tasks that assess scene-projection consistency, predict intervention-induced changes, and infer the physical changes behind visual transitions. The benchmark contains 21,000 synthetic questions with separate scene and geometry test partitions, complemented by 200 SpatialCast-Real questions from 20 photographed scenes. Evaluations show that closed-source MLLMs outperform general-purpose open-weight MLLMs and MLLMs designed for spatial reasoning, which remain near chance on the synthetic benchmark, while even strong closed-source models struggle with shadow-length and light-height judgments. We also use SpatialCast training data for GRPO post-training, improving open-weight MLLM performance on the benchmark. Further evaluations show improvements on several external spatial benchmarks, suggesting transfer of the learned capabilities. These experiments support SpatialCast as a controlled resource for diagnosing spatial reasoning capabilities and studying their learning and generalization.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages (main paper), 7 pages (supplementary material), 5 figures and 6 tables in the main paper

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Know When to Zoom: How Small Is Too Small for Vision-Language Models?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22709v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22709

**作者：** Siddharth Vohra、Min Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22709v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22709v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用「视力测试」式小字号编码题与 601 个真实照片小目标问题，量化七款 VLM 在图像缩放和拒收下可读细节的损失，并比较切块与两步「先指出区域再放大」两种放大策略，以及基于不确定回复触发的按需放大。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联 LOOK 与 STOP 的收益—成本权衡：报告了放大带来的准确率增益、约四成的图像 token 开销，以及细节尺寸预检失灵而「模型自称不确定」可作为可靠触发信号，可供核查视觉证据筛选与动态计算停止策略。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Know When to Zoom: How Small Is Too Small for Vision-Language Models?

**abstract/A1：** Online vision-language models often miss small details in large images, because the service shrinks the image, or rejects it, before the model ever sees it. A car 20 pixels wide in an 8000 by 6000 drone photo reaches Claude about 6 pixels wide, and OpenAI's default rejects the photo outright. We measure how much detail survives for seven models from four vendors, using an eye exam of printed codes and 601 questions about small objects in real photos. The smallest text a model reads half the time grows from 6 pixels or less on a small image to a predicted 10 to 13 pixels on a 7680 by 4320 photo at default settings. Zooming wins much of the detail back. Splitting each photo into up to nine tiles raises accuracy by 11.1 to 14.5 percentage points for Claude and Gemini, and by avoiding rejections it lifts GPT-6.1 Sol from 50.7% to 83.7%. A two-step zoom, in which the model first names the region it needs, matches or beats tiles on Gemini and comes within 2.5 points of them on Claude, with about 40% of their image tokens. When a detail is lost, Claude and Gemini both say they cannot tell about half the time, but they fail differently in the rest: Gemini gives a confident wrong answer in 43% to 48% of these cases, Claude in only 5% to 9%. A check based on detail size, run before the call, misses most of the gain from tiles on Claude and Gemini, but Claude's own reply is a reliable trigger: tiling only after an unsure reply comes within 1.4 points of always tiling with 62% to 70% of the image tokens. We turn these findings into five rules for when an agent should zoom, and release the eye exam, every model answer and code at https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the SENSE workshop at WACV 2027. 10 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Code, data and a plain-language walkthrough: https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom/

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Practice in Text, Act in Pixels: Correcting Where Perception Fails](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22722v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22722

**作者：** Zhongling Xu、Hairong Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22722v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22722v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 GUI agent 从文本界面练习迁移到截图执行时的感知失败：发现屏幕阅读器的误读集中出现在相同界面元素上，于是用少量真实截图微调阅读器，使其输出对应文本视图，而策略保持不变。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把文本—截图差距定位为视觉感知环节的系统性错误，并比较截图预算用于阅读器对齐还是策略训练；可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、感知瓶颈和训练监督分配，但不涉及显式 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Practice in Text, Act in Pixels: Correcting Where Perception Fails

**abstract/A1：** GUI agents can learn cheaply from a text view of an interface and act on screenshots through a vision-language screen reader. Yet on a simulated mobile platform with a low-cost reader, policies that complete most tasks in text fail on many of the same tasks on screenshots. We ask where a small budget of real screenshots is best spent to close this gap. We find that the reader's misreads are systematic and recur on the same interface elements. Noise matched to the reader's error rates, a natural sim-to-real remedy, misses this structure, and in a pre-specified comparison it did not beat training the policy directly on screenshots. We therefore correct the misreads at their source with reader alignment, which fine-tunes the reader on screenshots paired with their text views. The reader learns to output each screen's text view, as in text practice, and the policy stays unchanged. Spent on the reader, the screenshot budget gives 80.9% full-task success on new task instances, against 55.6% when spent on training the policy. Given the same labeled screenshots, adapting the reader also beats adapting the policy, and a single aligned reader improves every text-trained policy we tried without retraining any of them.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 8 figures, 19 tables. Code: https://github.com/Carlxuzhl/practice-in-text ; aligned reader: https://huggingface.co/xuzhl/practice-in-text-aligned-reader

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [LoopZip: Visual Token Compression for Efficient Looped Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22728

**作者：** Weizhong Huang、Zhicheng Liu、Yan Zhang、Fei Chao、Xiawu Zheng、Liujuan Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22728v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** LoopZip 提出面向 looped VLM 的免训练视觉 token 压缩：在 backbone 前聚合出紧凑候选池，跨调用平均同一共享注意力层前两次调用的指令注意力来选 token，并用权重共享的 value 修正对齐两次调用的输出，在两个开源 looped VLM、11 个基准上降低显存与端到端耗时。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及循环式 VLM 迭代计算中视觉 token 重要性随调用漂移、早期选择可能丢弃后续所需信息的问题，与迭代深度的成本收益及视觉细节能否留在内部状态相关；其跨调用注意力聚合与输出偏差度量可供核查迭代推理中的信息坍缩与证据保留。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoopZip: Visual Token Compression for Efficient Looped Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen computation by reusing shared layers, approaching larger models' capabilities with fewer parameters and easing the storage and deployment burden of stacking independent layers. However, repeatedly processing long visual sequences still incurs substantial compute and GPU memory costs. Visual token compression reduces these costs by shortening sequences. Existing methods mainly select original tokens directly or combine selection with aggregation. In looped models, the former may overlook changing token importance across calls and discard information needed later; the latter lack joint consideration of output changes across calls, limiting information preservation. We propose LoopZip, a training-free visual token compression algorithm addressing both issues. Visual aggregation builds a compact candidate pool before the looped backbone, and cross-invocation selection averages instruction attention from the first two existing calls of the same shared attention layer to retain tokens needed by both states. Weight-tied value correction then jointly fits shared visual-value adjustments to both pre-selection layer outputs, reducing output deviations across states. To validate LoopZip's effectiveness, we conduct extensive experiments covering two open-source looped VLMs, eleven benchmarks, and three pruning ratios. On LoopVL-1B, LoopZip retains 97.16% of the original model's performance at 50% pruning ratio and exceeds the strongest baseline by 5.36% at 87.5% pruning ratio. Across Hugging Face Transformers, vLLM, and SGLang, LoopVL-1B with LoopZip reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 58.36%-58.98% and achieves 2.01-2.47x end-to-end speedups over the original model.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Teaching What to Look For: Privileged Perception Questions for Agentic On-Policy Distillation in Video Captioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22755v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22755

**作者：** Yifan Xu、Ruopeng Gao、Zhixiao Wang、Jiafu Tang、Yichun Yang、Zihao Wang、Yuanxing Zhang、Limin Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22755v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22755v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PQ-OPD：训练时用不泄露答案的「感知问题」指明应检查哪些视觉证据，引导教师模型在视频中更全面地探索，再通过学生轨迹上的 on-policy distillation 将这种探索策略迁移到学生的推理、工具调用和描述生成中。

**相关性简评：** 其「看哪里、取什么证据」的训练监督与主动感知策略，直接关联 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、LOOK 决策及特权信息监督问题；可核查感知问题是否真正提升证据覆盖与推理贡献，而非仅改善描述质量。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Teaching What to Look For: Privileged Perception Questions for Agentic On-Policy Distillation in Video Captioning

**abstract/A1：** Detailed video captioning requires comprehensive and faithful descriptions of objects, actions, and temporal events. Fixed-frame sampling, however, can miss brief motions, small objects, and other fine-grained evidence, motivating captioning agents that actively seek relevant moments and inspect local regions. Training such behavior is challenging because, unlike video question answering, generic captioning instructions do not specify what visual evidence should be acquired. We propose Perception-Question On-Policy Distillation (PQ-OPD), which uses training-only Perception Questions (PQs) to guide active video perception. PQs specify what should be inspected without revealing the corresponding answers, encouraging the teacher to explore the video more comprehensively and focus on relevant visual evidence. Along trajectories generated by the student, on-policy distillation transfers this guidance to the student's reasoning, tool calls, and captions; no PQs are required at inference. Across three detailed-captioning and two caption-to-QA benchmarks, PQ-OPD consistently improves the corresponding untrained agents across teacher-student scales and input-frame budgets. Under our evaluation protocol, PQ-OPD achieves the strongest primary-metric results among the compared open-source models on DREAM-1K, CaReBench, ShortVidBench, MotionBench, and VidCapBench-AE. Our ablations, together with analysis of temporal coverage and answer leakage, further indicates that the student internalizes the training-time guidance as a more effective video exploration policy.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [NexSight: Verifiable Tasks for Fine-Grained Agentic Long-Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22790v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22790

**作者：** Tianci Xie、Li Chen、Pinxue Guo、Rui Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22790v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22790v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 NexSight：用仅凭证据即可重建答案的验证流程，从 108 个长视频中筛选出 310 个证据可追溯的细粒度任务（SightLine），并配套统一的多尺度交互测试框架与任务轨迹级监督，用于评测和训练 agentic 长视频理解。

**相关性简评：** 其证据可追溯的验证设计、跨片段重新取证与防漏计检查，以及把求解轨迹转成观测决策监督，可供核查 LOOK 时机、证据筛选与 STOP 判定的收益和成本，但论文聚焦外部视频检索，未涉及内部 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NexSight: Verifiable Tasks for Fine-Grained Agentic Long-Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained questions about long videos ask how many times an action occurs, in what order events unfold, or exactly when a state changes. Answering them can require finding brief moments scattered across hours of footage. Agentic models that decide when and where to look promise to find these moments, yet this ability is hard both to measure and to learn. Sparse supporting evidence may establish that an action occurred without establishing its total count; verifying timeline-wide claims requires checking for missed occurrences and double counting. Answer-only supervision, in turn, does not explicitly supervise the search for evidence. We present NexSight, which builds verifiable tasks to both evaluate and train agentic long-video understanding. Its agentic pipeline, SightForge, accepts a task only if a verifier blind to the draft answer can reconstruct it from evidence densely reacquired from the raw video and counterexample checks fail to refute it. This yields SightLine, 310 evidence-traceable tasks from 108 long videos, evaluated through SightLoop, a shared multiscale harness supporting temporal exploration, local inspection, evidence integration, and revisiting while recording returned observations. On disjoint videos, solver trajectories on verified tasks become step-level supervision for observation decisions. The strongest model reaches only 54.52% on SightLine, yet 79.35% when given the annotated evidence. With under 6K examples, Sight-35B nearly doubles its base model's interactive accuracy (21.29% to 42.26%), outperforms the 397B model of its family, begins using explicit spatial inspection after post-training, which its base model never uses, and gains over 8 points on two external long-video benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, including references and supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [Visual Harness Scales General VLMs to Medical Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22815v2>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22815

**作者：** Shaowen Wan、Hanqi Jiang、Zihao Wu、Junhao Chen、Weihang You、Ruiyu Yan、Bowen Chen、Tianming Liu、Lin Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22815v2>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22815v2>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VERA 医疗视觉 harness，为通用 VLM 配备交互式视觉工具和无损诊断证据记忆，并通过遗漏与可靠性评估驱动迭代检查、测量和答案修订。

**相关性简评：** 工具化反复取证与证据记忆可对应「LOOK」与视觉证据筛选，遗漏评估触发的修订循环则可供核查「STOP」的停止依据与计算成本；但其证据走显式文本记忆而非 latent 状态，与 LVR 内部状态工作的联系需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Visual Harness Scales General VLMs to Medical Images

**abstract/A1：** We show that an appropriate visual harness can substantially improve the performance of general vision-language models (VLMs) across diverse medical imaging tasks. We introduce VERA, a medical visual harness that equips general VLMs with interactive visual tools and systematic diagnostic evidence management. With VERA, general VLMs interactively explore medical images and progressively acquire diagnostic evidence, which is preserved in a lossless memory for subsequent retrieval and use. After submitting an initial answer, the VLM receives an assessment of potential omissions and the reliability of identified findings. This assessment guides further image inspection and measurement, followed by answer revision in an iterative agentic loop until sufficient evidence is obtained. Across 11 tasks and four imaging modalities, VERA improves performance on all tasks with Qwen3.8-27B, with notable gains in spleen measurement (63.3% to 98.3%), renal search (57.5% to 71.2%), and pulmonary nodule detection (0.0% to 28.2%). High-capability API-based VLMs achieve even higher performance with VERA. For example, GPT-6.1 Sol improves pulmonary nodule detection from 10.8% to 69.2% with VERA, demonstrating the complementary benefits of stronger model capabilities and an effective medical visual harness. Our results highlight the potential of visual harnesses as a new paradigm for scaling general VLMs to medical imaging tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Pixel is All You Need](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22820v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22820

**作者：** Jie Chu、Haoting Shi、Fucheng Zhang、Jiawei Liu、Junwei Liao、Ying Wen、Muning Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22820v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22820v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统检验图像、视频及统一多模态生成器能否仅凭像素中的文本进行符号推理，并从零训练纯像素空间生成模型，让其在画布上读题、写出中间步骤并作答。

**相关性简评：** 该工作直接考察视觉生成模型是否真正使用画布内容推理，而非依赖文本旁路；逐帧中间步骤与 RL 后训练也关联 LVR 的推理状态生成、监督和动态计算，可供对照像素空间与 latent 空间的推理路径。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pixel is All You Need

**abstract/A1：** Text, images, audio, touch, and even robot actions can, in principle, all be rendered onto a single canvas, suggesting an ambitious goal of unifying all modalities within vision. Language provides the natural first test. To subsume it, a visual model must reason directly over text in pixels. Across a wide range of image, video, and unified multimodal generators, however, we find that their visual generation modules cannot perform symbolic reasoning over text on the canvas. They succeed only when an underlying text module or prompt rewriter already supplies the answer, effectively reducing the generator to a mere renderer, a limitation that post-training does little to resolve. To test whether this failure stems from pixels themselves or from current training paradigms, we train a family of purely pixel-space generative models from scratch without any text pathway. With images as their sole input and output, these models read questions, write intermediate steps, and produce answers directly on the canvas. Remarkably, they exhibit hallmark behaviors of large language models. Performance improves predictably with scale following a power law. Writing intermediate steps frame by frame substantially outperforms single-frame answering, echoing chain-of-thought, while reinforcement-learning post-training further strengthens reasoning. Moreover, sharing one canvas allows the same model to reason across modalities, a capability that text-only language models lack. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of these phenomena in purely pixel-space generative models free of text tokens.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables, including references and appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [Synergizing Evidence Retrieval and Utilization for Self-Improving Multimodal Scientific Document Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22837v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22837

**作者：** Frank Nie、Ethan B. Liu、Yuan Zhu、Jindong Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22837v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22837v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Retriever–Perceiver–Reasoner 智能体框架，做多模态科学文档问答：先按上下文预算逐步检索文本、图表证据，再由 Perceiver 在连续空间构造问题条件化的 latent ontology 引导 Reasoner 生成有据答案，并用检索潜力与 latent 效力两类反馈定位错误来源、协同训练各组件。

**相关性简评：** 其 latent ontology 可视为对多模态证据的隐式组织与筛选，LEF 试图区分「检索不足」与「latent 引导无效」，与视觉证据筛选、信息坍缩及证据获取/使用的贡献分配直接相关，可供核查 latent 内容是否真被答案使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synergizing Evidence Retrieval and Utilization for Self-Improving Multimodal Scientific Document Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Scientific document reasoning requires an agent to acquire and integrate evidence distributed across text, figures, and tables. Retrieval can identify relevant sources while missing complementary support, and answer-level feedback alone does not reveal whether an error stems from evidence acquisition or evidence use. We present an agentic Retriever--Perceiver--Reasoner framework for multimodal scientific document reasoning. The Retriever sequentially selects source-aligned evidence under a context budget. Given the selected evidence and question, the Perceiver constructs a question-conditioned latent ontology, an implicit organization of multimodal evidence in a shared continuous space. The Reasoner combines this latent guidance with the original sources to produce grounded answers. We train the three components through diagnostic-guided co-evolution: alternating updates optimize them with a shared answering reward, while Retrieval Potential Feedback (RPF) and Latent Efficacy Feedback (LEF) identify questions that need better retrieval or latent guidance and direct targeted resampling. Across four benchmarks and seven evaluation settings, our framework obtains average scores of 69.9 and 75.0 with 7B and 8B Reasoner backbones, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.0 and 9.1 points, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [SlideJev: Order-Stable and Tissue-Grounded Jev Decisions for Whole-Slide Pathology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22879

**作者：** Lei Wu、Jiashuai Liu、Di Zhang、Chunze Yang、Jiusong Ge、Junbo Lu、Yongqi Liang、Wenjie Zhao、Ni Zhang、Chen Li、Zeyu Gao、Qidong Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22879v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 SlideJev，用 Jev 分别计算各候选答案的支持与拒绝对数似然差并并行评估，同时以候选条件化的视觉 token 门控更新，将判断绑定到全切片组织证据上。

**相关性简评：** 其针对选项顺序敏感和绕过组织证据的捷径，正对应 VLM 推理中答案是否真正使用视觉内容与视觉旁路问题，可供核查其证据门控与并行候选验证机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SlideJev: Order-Stable and Tissue-Grounded Jev Decisions for Whole-Slide Pathology

**abstract/A1：** Whole-slide visual question answering (VQA) offers a natural-language interface to pathology, allowing diagnostic and phenotypic questions to be answered directly from tissue slides. However, current vision-language models (VLMs) rely heavily on linguistic priors: their answers are sensitive to the order of the candidate answers, and can be reached through shortcuts that bypass the tissue evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce SlideJev, a framework that separately verifies candidate answers against the slide and learns to ground these judgments in tissue evidence. To connect parallel candidate evaluation with visual evidence learning, we adopt Jev and score each candidate by the difference between support and rejection log-likelihoods from the original language-model head. We compute these scores in parallel using separate prompts with the same format and no option-position labels, then normalize them for answer selection. To ground these scores in tissue evidence, candidate-conditioned reading and comparison guide gated updates to visual tokens across decoder layers. With the backbone frozen, we train these visual modules through the same scores using question-answer and paired-image supervision. Across three whole-slide pathology VQA benchmarks, SlideJev achieves the highest mean overall accuracy and 100% answer consistency across four option orders. Image-control experiments further show that SlideJev achieves the largest average accuracy gains from correct images over mismatched and image-free inputs across the three datasets. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/leowu-ai/SlideJev.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22880

**作者：** Guoqing Wang、Pin Tang、Xiangxuan Ren、Chao Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22880v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AgentWorld，用动态绑定到各交通参与者的离散 agent token 构成紧凑、结构化的 agent-centric 世界状态，通过显式几何 grounding、推理导向 SFT 与 agent 导向强化学习训练，联合支撑推理与轨迹规划。

**相关性简评：** 针对像素级未来帧保留大量规划无关外观细节的问题，该文正是 LVR 中「哪些视觉证据应进入 latent 状态」的表示侧研究，其 agent token 的筛选与 grounding 机制可供核查信息压缩与视觉旁路的影响；但限于自动驾驶闭环规划，泛化到通用多模态推理仍需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** Recent progress in integrating vision-language-action (VLA) models with world modeling has significantly advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by allowing systems to reason about imagined futures instead of merely reacting to current observations. However, most world VLA models generate future frames at the pixel level with plenty of dense visual tokens. Although such generated futures can be visually plausible, they preserve many driving-irrelevant appearance details, i.e., only a small portion of the tokens directly encode critical planning-relevant factors, such as agent geometry, motion dynamics, and interactions. This introduces a significant performance bottleneck. An important question thus arises: what world state should a world VLA model reason over to support planning beyond pixel-level prediction? We argue that planning-oriented world modeling should focus on a compact and structured agent-centric latent state that captures the interactions between objects in the driving scene over time. As such, we introduce AgentWorld, an agent-centric VLA world model that uses discrete agent tokens jointly for reasoning and trajectory planning. Each token is dynamically grounded to a surrounding agent associated with its object's state, such as semantic identity, 3D geometry, motion, and interaction context. AgentWorld learns these tokens through three key processes: explicit geometric grounding, reasoning-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and agent-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive open-loop and closed-loop experiments under diverse driving scenarios show that AgentWorld enhances planning capabilities, fully exploits visual evidence and agent dynamics, and yields interpretable agent-centric world states.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [PhysScope: Agentic Multimodal Physics Reasoning with Grounded Visual Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23010v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23010

**作者：** Haoyang Liu、Kecheng Liang、Xinyu Li、Weixing Chen、Yang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23010v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23010v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PhysScope，一个无需更新权重的推理期视觉外壳：将物理适用范围与视觉观测关联、显式构造派生视图，并维护作用域感知工作记忆，以纠正物理图解推理中的前提越界错误。

**相关性简评：** 其作用域登记与依赖失效机制直接涉及视觉证据筛选、内部状态更新和错误修订，可供核查 LVR 中证据约束、视觉旁路与工作记忆管理的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhysScope: Agentic Multimodal Physics Reasoning with Grounded Visual Memory

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) can correctly interpret physics diagrams yet apply constraints outside the states or conditions in which they hold, causing premise errors to propagate through subsequent calculations. We introduce PhysScope, an inference-time visual harness that supports self-correction of these scope mismatches without updating model weights. PhysScope enables the model to link physical scopes directly to its visual observations and explicitly constructs derived views to support reasoning. The harness imposes explicit constraints on state distinctions, conditions, and physical scopes during model reasoning, and checks agent-proposed revisions against sources and scopes. Accepted revisions invalidate registered dependents, thereby maintaining scope-aware working memory. On PhysAlign, PhysScope achieves higher solving scores than direct solving across all six evaluated VLMs. With Gemini-3.8-flash, it outperforms Canvas-CoT by 9.34 percentage points and substantially improves solving accuracy on the out-of-distribution (OOD) PhysUniBench benchmark. Ablation studies support explicit scope management and dependency maintenance as design principles for multimodal physics reasoning agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [FrameRouter: Frame Budget Routing for Long-Video Understanding on Video-MME-v2 and Beyond](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23055v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23055

**作者：** Bingjun Luo、Jialin Guo、Siqi Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23055v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23055v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Video-MME-v2 上分析统一帧预算与问题证据需求不匹配的现象，发现不同题型偏好不同冻结采样策略且单纯增加帧数并不稳定提升指标，据此提出免训练的 FrameRouter：用证据需求路由器在固定平均预算下为每个问题分配不同帧数。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及「LOOK」环节的视觉证据获取与预算的收益／成本权衡，其按需分配、边际收益递减的观察可供核查 LVR 中证据筛选与动态计算分配；但其作用停留在输入帧采样层，未涉及 latent 状态或推理轨迹，迁移线索需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrameRouter: Frame Budget Routing for Long-Video Understanding on Video-MME-v2 and Beyond

**abstract/A1：** Long-video question answering benchmarks like Video-MME-v2 typically evaluate systems under a uniform frame budget for every question, despite stark variation in the evidence each query actually demands. We analyze this mismatch on Video-MME-v2 and find that different question subsets prefer different frozen frame-budget policies, while uniformly increasing the frame count does not reliably improve all metrics. These findings motivate fixed-mean frame-budget routing, a controlled inference setting in which each question may receive a different frame budget while the average budget matches a uniform reference. We introduce FrameRouter, a training-free test-time framework that combines an evidence-demand router, a budget-constrained allocation rule, and a frozen bank of frame-sampling policies. By controlling how much visual evidence each question receives while leaving the underlying samplers unchanged, FrameRouter is orthogonal to query-aware frame selection. Across Video-MME-v2 and several long-video understanding benchmarks, FrameRouter improves long-video question answering by using the same average visual budget more selectively across questions. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/FrameRouter.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23127v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23127

**作者：** Basavaraj R M、Harshal Chandel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23127v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23127v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 GIB-200K 基准，用检索噪声、图像退化与跨模态冲突条件，并借理想贝叶斯线索组合推导 Knowledge Inertia、Sensory Capture 与 Grounding Illusion Score，量化多个开源 VLM 在文本与图像证据间的实际权重。

**相关性简评：** 直接诊断 VLM 的回答是否真正依赖视觉证据还是被检索文本主导，与视觉旁路、信息坍缩及证据筛选评价高度相关；可供核查其权重估计能否用于检验推理过程中的模态贡献分配，但该测量停留在输出层面而非内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?

**abstract/A1：** Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model's own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w\_T/w\_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w\_T/w\_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/Basavarajearlybird/GIB-200K (archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24320174)

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [VIVID: Beyond Static Multi-Image Reasoning with Localized Visual Differences and Textual Entity Dynamics in Video MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23153v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23153

**作者：** Chengqian Ma、Yuanze Zeng、Hongyu Wang、Mingfeng Lin、YiCheng Xiao、Yu Chen、Pengyu Li、WenHao Wang、Yuexiao Ma、Xiawu Zheng、Fei Chao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23153v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23153v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对视频 MLLM 在紧凑帧预算下退化为静态多图推理的问题，提出 VIVID：用帧间 patch 差分保留局部运动，配合自适应关键帧规划、双路变化记忆与实体状态链构建 Dynamic Tokens，并在冻结 27B 主干上只训练极少量参数。

**相关性简评：** 其「局部差异保留 + 自适应选帧 + 实体状态链」直接关系到内部状态是否保留视觉细节、实体关系及视觉证据筛选与动态获取，值得核查这些增量 token 是否真被答案使用、而非旁路信息坍缩。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VIVID: Beyond Static Multi-Image Reasoning with Localized Visual Differences and Textual Entity Dynamics in Video MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Video question answering under tight frame budgets requires preserving localized motion and event order, yet current video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) reduce comprehension to static multi-image reasoning by discarding unselected frames and encoding keyframes independently: (1) Spatially, whole-frame pooling dilutes small moving targets into static backgrounds; (2) Temporally, dropping intermediate frames or averaging tokens erases inter-frame motion, confusing direction, speed, and order. We propose VIVID (Visual Inter-frame Variations and Individual Dynamics), which moves beyond static snapshots like human vision: keyframes capture core states while inter-frame Patch Diffs preserve neighboring changes within $K\_f$ keyframes. Autonomous Delta-Driven Keyframe Planning (ADKP) selects anchors from a $3\\text{ fps}$ pool and feeds surrounding patch differences into Dual-Road Change Memory (DRCM), forming Dynamic Tokens at zero extra anchor tokens alongside a camera-stabilized Active Motion Region of Interest (ROI) and chronological Entity State Chains. Adding $0.22\\%$ trainable parameters to a frozen $27\\text{B}$ backbone, VIVID-Small ($50$ examples, $\\sim 1.5$ GPU hours) beats all eleven baselines in overall accuracy across $8,083$ questions and trails only VIVID-Large ($3,600$ examples), which leads on MotionBench, Video-MME, and TempCompass.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables. Code: https://github.com/step-out/VIVID

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Say Less, Hallucinate Less? Diagnosing and Restoring Object Coverage in Preference-Optimized Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23179v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23179

**作者：** Hansang Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23179v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23179v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断 LLaVA-1.5-7B 四个偏好优化检查点在降低物体幻觉的同时压低了物体类别覆盖率，用 EOS logit 偏置模拟提前停止来量化早停可复现多少 PO 增益，并提出免训练、以 base caption 补回高置信类别的恢复方法。

**相关性简评：** 与「视觉细节是否被保留并进入输出」以及 STOP 停止决策和覆盖之间的权衡直接相关；其召回受控评价与 base-only 类别筛选可供核查，但讨论对象是 caption 输出而非 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Say Less, Hallucinate Less? Diagnosing and Restoring Object Coverage in Preference-Optimized Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Preference optimization (PO) is the standard way to reduce object hallucination in large vision-language models. We find that four public PO checkpoints of LLaVA-1.5-7B also lower object coverage. They name fewer object categories, and their lower hallucination rates bring no significant gain in set-level object F1 on CHAIR. We therefore ask how much of the PO gain the base model can reproduce by stopping earlier. A positive end-of-sequence (EOS) logit bias lets the base model reach the recall of each PO model, and this early stopping alone reproduces 58 to 86 percent of the CHAIRi gain of three checkpoints on two disjoint COCO sets. Writing longer does not bring the lost categories back. Under greedy decoding, a negative EOS bias recovers only 2 to 9 percent of the lost CHAIR recall of these checkpoints, and 94 to 96 percent of the added object mentions repeat categories already named. On CHAIR, the base caption of the same image still names about half of the missed object categories. We therefore propose base-guided object-category restoration, which appends high-confidence base-only categories to the PO caption without training. With thresholds fixed on a development set, it brings three checkpoints within one point of the base recall on a held-out COCO set with a lower distinct-object hallucination rate than the base model. Against the recall-matched base stopping curve, its gain holds for RLAIF-V near the base recall and for RLAIF-V and CHAIR-DPO at an intermediate recall. We will release the code of the recall-controlled evaluation.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 6 figures, 19 tables. Main text and appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [Learning Grounded Reasoning from Human Gaze](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23209v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23209

**作者：** Gabriel Sarch、Declan Campbell、Nicholas Budny、Kia Ghods、Katerina Fragkiadaki、Jonathan D. Cohen、Brenden M. Lake、Taylor W. Webb

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23209v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23209v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文收集 7,109 条人类注视轨迹，比较整段注视序列与作为先验的注视引导，并提出 GGR，使 VLM 在图表理解、视觉搜索和计数中兼顾答案准确率、人类对齐与视觉定位。

**相关性简评：** 研究直接触及视觉证据筛选与探索行为塑造，其“用注视先验而非完整模仿”的结论可供核查 LVR 中视觉旁路、证据选择监督和 LOOK 成本之间的权衡。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Grounded Reasoning from Human Gaze

**abstract/A1：** Human visual reasoning is driven by systematic exploration and extraction of task-relevant visual evidence. Human gaze provides a window into this process, making gaze a useful source of guidance for visually-grounded reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs). However, human gaze also includes noise and redundancies that may transfer poorly to VLMs. In this work, we investigate how human gaze data can be effectively leveraged to improve visual reasoning in VLMs, while maintaining alignment with human behavior. We collect 7,109 human gaze trials from a diverse and challenging set of visual reasoning tasks, and train grounded reasoning models with different forms of gaze guidance. Across evaluations in chart understanding, visual search, and counting, we find that using human gaze as a prior for grounded reasoning consistently outperforms supervision using the full gaze sequence. Based on these findings, we introduce GGR (Gaze-Guided Reasoner), which achieves greater human alignment and more reliable visual grounding while matching or outperforming leading grounded-reasoning baselines in accuracy. Reinforcement learning further improves task accuracy, gaze alignment, and grounding quality despite using no gaze supervision.
