Behavioral analyses show that gaze supervised models exhibit more visual exploration before arriving at an answer. Our results suggest that the value of human gaze lies in guiding spatial exploration and shaping reasoning behavior without requiring complete imitation of human behavior.

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Chain-of-Thought Controllability Can Reduce Chain-of-Thought Monitorability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21799v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21799

**作者：** Zheng-Xin Yong、Benjamin Arnav、Miles Wang、Tomek Korbak、Olivia Watkins

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21799v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21799v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文通过「人格控制」监督微调构造 model organism：用多种语体重写数学推理轨迹，使模型获得更广泛的 CoT 可操控能力；随后在隐蔽副任务中测量 CoT 可监控性（g-mean²）的下降，并测试显式隐藏指令与透明化提示的影响。

**相关性简评：** 虽非多模态工作，但其「训练塑造推理轨迹可读性、再量化监控证据是否仍在」的评测框架，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态与生成轨迹是否真正暴露任务所需证据，以及监督方式是否会诱导信息坍缩或旁路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Chain-of-Thought Controllability Can Reduce Chain-of-Thought Monitorability

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising tool for detecting hidden goals, off-task behavior, and other failures in AI agents. One risk is that models could deliberately shape their reasoning to make it opaque to monitors. We stress-test this risk through persona-control training: supervised fine-tuning on mathematical reasoning traces rewritten in the voices of diverse personas. This induces a broader ability to control CoT, even though training contains no monitor-evasion examples. In covert side-task evaluations, traces in which a gpt-oss-20b model organism completes the hidden side task contain less monitorable evidence than the base model's. Averaged across evaluations and CoT lengths, weighted by side-task-success counts, the CoT monitorability metric g-mean$^2$ falls from 0.94 for the base model to 0.68 for the model organism without a hiding instruction, and to 0.22 when explicitly instructed to hide. In intervention evaluations, explicit hiding often drives monitorability near zero. The effect extends to a Kimi-K2.6 model organism in agentic environments, though hidden objectives requiring extended reasoning or longer trajectories remain more monitorable than simple single-step side tasks. We further discover that targeted transparency prompts can substantially recover monitorability, sometimes surpassing the base model. These model organisms provide a testbed for studying monitorability failures and potential mitigations. Our code can be found on https://github.com/yongzx/cot-control-model-organism.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 17 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21964v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21964

**作者：** Eliza Berman、Rohan Subramanian、Shreya Jha、Lohit Jagarapu、Romain Lopez、Rajesh Ranganath

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21964v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21964v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 Solver2System 数据集，用 PDE 求解器代码、方程、自然语言与数值轨迹等多表征及受控损坏，评测八个开源模型能否经跨表征一致性与物理有效性检测还原真实系统。

**相关性简评：** 其「判断正确但因果诊断错误」及证据冲突下的表征偏好，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否真被使用、答案与内部状态脱节；但对象为科学模拟而非视觉，迁移性待评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation

**abstract/A1：** Simulators based on partial differential equations (PDEs) are widely used to model physical systems, but generating and interpreting PDE solver code with large language models (LLMs) remains challenging. Scientific simulation code is a useful testbed for LLM scientific reasoning because (1) one system can be represented by code, equations, natural language, and numerical trajectories and (2) code can execute successfully while implementing invalid physics. We introduce Solver2System, a dataset of PDE solvers with multiple representations, controlled corruptions, and lexical perturbations, to evaluate whether LLMs can recover the underlying physical system through (1) cross-representation consistency and (2) static and agentic detection of physical invalidity. Across eight open-weight models, LLMs systematically favor some representations when evidence conflicts, and lexical perturbations to code alter interpretation of other unchanged representations. Execution improves physical-validity judgments, but weaker agents can judge correctly without identifying the causal defect. Thus, LLMs may reach correct judgments despite inconsistent representation reconciliation or causal misdiagnosis.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Workshop: Sim2Science: ML with Imperfect Scientific Models. 23 pages, 14 figures, 9 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21983v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21983

**作者：** Maor Farid

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、math.DS、nlin.CD

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21983v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21983v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 LLM 视为非线性动力系统，沿「深度、训练、生成」三个时钟把约 200 篇文献压缩成概念词典，并用降阶模型（softmax 作 Boltzmann 分布、注意力作 Kuramoto 模型、重复循环作加深的势井）解释现象，再用小型开源模型实测重复循环的 Arrhenius 逃逸规律与温度窗口。

**相关性简评：** 逐层迭代与重复循环的动力学刻画、逃逸势垒随长度和模型规模的变化，可能为 LVR 的循环式 latent 推理、STOP 决策与动态计算预算提供可核查的分析语言；但纯文本、无视觉证据，联系需自行迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation

**abstract/A1：** Language models are dynamical systems. A transformer is iterated over its layers, over the steps of its training and over the tokens it generates, and on each of these three clocks it shows behavior that nonlinear dynamics has studied for a century: fixed points, bifurcations, synchronization, chaos and noise-driven escape from potential wells. The two communities rarely meet, so this behavior is often rediscovered under new names. We build a bridge between them. To describe, we condense about 200 papers into the Three-Clock Dictionary, which maps fifteen concepts of nonlinear dynamics onto each clock and marks every correspondence as proved, measured or conjectured, and we show each concept in a mechanical system and in a language model side by side. To explain, we derive reduced-order models: the softmax is a Boltzmann distribution, gradient descent an Euler step on a nonlinear spring, a loss spike a ball thrown out of a stiff valley, attention on the circle a Kuramoto model, and a repetition loop a well that deepens while it is occupied. We then test these explanations on open language models, on a laptop CPU. Escape from repetition loops in GPT-2 and Pythia follows the Arrhenius law with the logit gap as the barrier, and the barrier deepens and then saturates, so the temperature needed to leave a loop falls like $1/\\ln N$ with the generation length $N$ and rises with model size. Coherent text lives in a narrow window of temperatures, which a small reasoning model shares at its recommended temperature. Training makes each Pythia layer more expansive, and a small transformer trains at a multi-mode edge of stability. The third step, solving problems of language models, is still ahead: controllers derived from the escape analysis remove loops but do not yet beat the best fixed temperature. We state twelve open problems where nonlinear dynamics could get there.

**comment/C1：** 52 pages, 19 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/maorfarid1/three-clocks

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [The Fragility of Reasoning-Importance Attribution for Pruning Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22234v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22234

**作者：** Himanshu Mishra

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22234v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22234v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在四个小型推理模型上检验「归因引导剪枝」所依赖的前提——推理重要性可被忠实测量——并指出该测量在三点上脆弱：以最终答案 token 打分会在 CoT 模型上饱和并使剪枝损失数十点准确率；单神经元因果效应处于噪声水平，需到约百个神经元的分组粒度才可复现；对比分离出的「推理专属」重要性无法与通用重要性分开。据此提出打分推理 token、粗粒度归因、以准确率而非困惑度评估的规则。

**相关性简评：** 其关于因果贡献测量在目标 token 选择、归因粒度与评估指标上的脆弱性，可供核查 LVR 中对 latent 或内部组件做贡献分配与因果归因时是否同样不可靠；但研究对象是纯文本剪枝，与视觉证据保留和 latent 状态生成无直接对应。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Fragility of Reasoning-Importance Attribution for Pruning Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Attribution-guided pruning scores components by their causal contribution and protects the important ones, and it is increasingly applied to compress reasoning language models without destroying their chain of thought. Its reliability rests on an unexamined assumption: that reasoning importance can be faithfully measured. Across four small reasoning models, we show this measurement is fragile in three ways, each of which changes pruning decisions. First, scoring final-answer tokens, the natural choice, saturates in every chain-of-thought model we test (answer-token negative log-likelihood, NLL, of $10^{-4}$--$10^{-3}$) because the model states its answer inside its own reasoning; guiding head pruning with this target costs 27.6 and 32.2 accuracy points relative to scoring reasoning tokens on the two models we power with five seeds, while perplexity barely moves and even reverses sign at 7B. Second, causal importance is not localizable to single units: single-neuron effects sit at the measurement floor, their cross-split ranking is noise, and they cancel: a group's ablation effect disagrees in sign with the summed effects of its members in 90\\% of groups. Reproducible importance emerges only at groups of $\\sim 100$ neurons. Third, contrastively isolated \`\`reasoning-specific'' importance is not separable from generic importance, and protecting it is worse than protecting raw importance. We additionally pre-registered the hypothesis that distillation-induced concentration of importance predicts the pruning cost; it is not supported, and since our grid confounds concentration with scale, we report it as unresolved rather than reversed. The result is a short set of measurement rules: score reasoning tokens, attribute at coarse granularity, and evaluate with accuracy rather than perplexity.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [MCTS Is Counting the Wrong Thing: Learning Exploration Counts for LLM Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22301v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22301

**作者：** Xuan Zhang、Zhijian Zhou、Zicheng Hu、Yan Feng、Hongyan Hao、Yuan Qi、Chao Qu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22301v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22301v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 MCTS 中按文本分支独立计数的 UCT 会让同一推理策略的不同表述重复获得探索奖励，造成计数碎片化与遗憾上升；提出 LPC-MCTS，用策略隐藏表示上的轻量伪计数网络估计表示覆盖度，使语义相关分支共享探索证据，并在多个数学推理与搜索基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「用隐藏表示判断分支是否属于同一语义区域并共享计数」的思路，可为 LVR 中 latent 相似度受内容、深度与位置影响以及探索预算与停止决策提供方法线索，但需核查该相似度是否只反映表面表述而非真实推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MCTS Is Counting the Wrong Thing: Learning Exploration Counts for LLM Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) has powered landmark successes in reinforcement learning, yet its gains in LLM reasoning can remain modest despite substantial search effort. We identify an overlooked bottleneck: what MCTS counts. Standard UCT assigns independent visit counts to textual branches, allowing different formulations of the same reasoning strategy to repeatedly receive exploration bonuses. Our theoretical analysis shows that this count fragmentation can impair exploration efficiency, as quantified by increased cumulative regret. To address this problem, we introduce Learned Pseudo-Count MCTS (LPC-MCTS), which changes only the exploration statistic in UCT. A lightweight Coin-Flipping Network head on the policy's hidden representations estimates representation coverage, enabling related branches to share exploration evidence. Across 5 mathematical-reasoning and 7 search benchmarks, LPC-MCTS consistently outperforms strong MCTS baselines. Holding each LPC-MCTS-trained policy fixed, replacing standard MCTS counts at inference improves average mean@16 accuracy across 5 mathematical benchmarks by 3.79 percentage points on Qwen2.5-7B and 2.71 points on Qwen3-4B. Applying LPC-MCTS during both training and inference yields gains of 4.96 and 4.37 points over standard MCTS, respectively. Together, our theory and experiments show that changing what MCTS counts can substantially improve its exploration efficiency and reasoning performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [InfuseLoop: Cross-Trajectory Latent Injection for Scalable Parallel Looped Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22426v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22426

**作者：** Zhaofeng Sun、Lanxiang Hu、Yu-Yang Qian、Jiaqi Leng、Yuanbo Yang、Chi Zhang、Youyou Lu、Peng Zhao、Hao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22426v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22426v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文分析并行循环 Transformer 随循环加深收益递减的原因，指出循环边界只传递同一轨迹的前一状态，进而提出 InfuseLoop：以轻量注意力残差聚合后续预测轨迹中因果可用的隐状态，在保持 token 并行训练与并发执行的前提下改善深度扩展，并用困惑度和下游准确率验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨轨迹隐状态注入」提供了迭代式 latent 状态如何获得更丰富信息的机制线索，可供核查能否用于多分支/循环式 VLM 推理的状态交换，以及注入内容是否仍保留视觉证据而非仅语言先验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InfuseLoop: Cross-Trajectory Latent Injection for Scalable Parallel Looped Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Parallel Looped Transformers (PLTs) use otherwise idle decoding compute by overlapping recurrent iterations. However, vanilla PLTs scale poorly with depth: they exhibit training-loss spikes, and their quality gains shrink as more loops are added. The benefit of additional loops depends on the information available to each iteration, so we examine whether the existing computational dependencies limit effective depth scaling. We find that only the preceding in-trajectory state is passed across loop boundaries, even though completed cross-trajectory representations are already available. Based on this finding, we propose InfuseLoop, a cross-trajectory latent injection mechanism. It aggregates causally available hidden states from later prediction trajectories through lightweight attention residuals. This design preserves token-parallel training and concurrent loop execution, and requires only $O(Rd)$ additional hidden-state storage. Experiments show that InfuseLoop eliminates training-loss spikes and improves validation-loss scaling up to eight loops. At eight loops, InfuseLoop reduces perplexity by 3.4--17.1% relative to vanilla PLTs and by 10.5--37.3% relative to the non-looped baseline. It also improves average downstream accuracy by 0.84 and 1.99 percentage points over these two baselines. InfuseLoop incurs only 0.3% decoding time over vanilla PLTs and 23.3% over the non-looped baseline, while keeping the same logical KV-cache footprint as vanilla PLTs.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Encoding, Decodability, and Use: A Five-Layer Framework for Benchmark Measurement Audits with Evidence from UAV Intent Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22434v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22434

**作者：** Zhirong Su、Tian Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22434v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22434v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出观察、表示、解码、使用、行为五层基准测量审计框架，并在 AeroIntent 无人机意图识别基准上实例化：以 RAFT 光流、边界框轨迹等特征做相关分析，配合对齐与正交注入实验确定最小可检测效应，结论是准确率与径向速度读出无关，而与程序记忆和静态线索一致。

**相关性简评：** 其「编码—可解码—是否被使用」的分层、MDE 与注入检验，可供核查 LVR 中一项关键判断：latent 状态可解码不等于答案实际使用了该内容；框架本身来自 UAV 基准，迁移到 VLM 推理需自行改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Encoding, Decodability, and Use: A Five-Layer Framework for Benchmark Measurement Audits with Evidence from UAV Intent Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Benchmark accuracy is routinely read as evidence that a model understands the construct the benchmark measures. We propose and instantiate on AeroIntent a five-layer framework that decomposes that reading into observation, representation, decoding, use, and behaviour. The framework separates encoding, decodability, and use as distinct levels. In the instantiation, the observation layer is unreliable on 7 of the 20 Drone programs and floors the direct visual correlate of radial velocity at a 0.26\\% scale change. The representation layer exposes two task-designed RGB features with nominal signals. RAFT flow radial bias reaches $r=+0.257$ ($p=0.030$), and bounding-box optical-flow divergence reaches $r=+0.203$ ($p=0.029$). No multi-feature readout gives a fold-mean test correlation above the cluster minimum detectable effect (MDE), and the largest positive global out-of-sample correlation is $r=+0.080$. The decoding layer sets the MDE at $\|r\|=0.599$ at the multi-feature Bonferroni threshold ($α=0.00714$, power 0.80), and the observed $r=+0.080$ has power 0.00. Two injection experiments establish the MDE's condition from both sides: aligned injections are detected at or above the simulated power, and orthogonal injections are not detected. The use layer finds accuracy consistent with program memory (program-identity 0.937/0.597/0.210) and multi-region static cues. The behaviour layer is consistent with accuracy not requiring a radial-velocity readout (appearance 0.6552 vs bounding-box trajectory 0.4080, cluster-level paired $Δ=+0.2471$, $p=0.0132$).

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Strong Fused Performance Can Mask Branch Collapse in a Dual-View Heart-Sound Classifier: A Three-Part Diagnostic Protocol](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22556v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22556

**作者：** Safayet Khan、Hasan Zohirul Islam、Md. Tauhid Bin Iqbal

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22556v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22556v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在双视角心音分类模型 AudioFuse 中发现：即使融合分数很好，谱图分支实际上未被使用；作者据此提出三段式诊断（删除分支是否改变融合分数、分支特征线性探针是否优于同初始化的未训练对照、单独训练能否学会本视角），并归因于早期优化失衡。

**相关性简评：** 「融合分数好但某分支塌缩」与 LVR 关注的视觉旁路、信息坍缩、贡献分配高度同构，其删除实验与未训练对照探针可供核查 VLM 中视觉分支是否真的被使用，但需注意原文为心音音频任务、无 VLM 与 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Strong Fused Performance Can Mask Branch Collapse in a Dual-View Heart-Sound Classifier: A Three-Part Diagnostic Protocol

**abstract/A1：** Fusion models are usually judged by their fused score, a practice that assumes that every branch has learned something useful. We show that this assumption can fail silently in dual-view heart-sound classification. In AudioFuse, which fuses a spectrogram transformer with a waveform CNN, the spectrogram branch is unused despite strong fused performance when both branches are trained from scratch. We introduce a three-part diagnostic for branch collapse: whether deleting a branch changes the fused score, whether a linear probe on its features beats an untrained copy of the same branch, and whether the branch can learn its view when trained alone. Deleting the branch has negligible effect, and probing its features shows little improvement over an identically initialised, untrained copy. Yet the same architecture trained alone exceeds 0.9 AUC, so its view is learnable, while the jointly trained model is near chance when it must rely on this branch. Update statistics and an initialisation experiment point to an early optimisation imbalance. Moving a strong artificial label cue from one input to the other can switch which branch the model relies on, even though the branch architectures stay the same. We observe branch collapse on PhysioNet, but the full diagnostic is not met on ICBHI or CirCor. Fused performance alone therefore cannot establish that every branch learned a meaningful representation, and we recommend reporting branch deletion and an untrained-control probe alongside it.

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Proximal Targets: Training Looped Refiners on Problems with Many Correct Answers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22737v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22737

**作者：** Syed Affan Daimi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22737v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22737v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 HRM/TRM 一类循环细化网络，指出多解任务上「固定一个标准答案」的深监督存在无法靠容量消除的损失下界、且各次迭代会把不同答案混成无效解，并提出 proximal target：让每次迭代朝距自身输入最近的正确答案训练。

**相关性简评：** 其对循环迭代的固定点性质、收敛条件与多解监督目标的设计，可能为 LVR 中循环 latent 状态的更新、避免多次迭代平均塌缩以及轨迹级监督提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Proximal Targets: Training Looped Refiners on Problems with Many Correct Answers

**abstract/A1：** Looped refiners such as HRM and TRM apply one small network repeatedly to a draft answer, and deep supervision trains every pass toward one stored answer per puzzle. We prove that when a puzzle has many correct answers this target sets a loss floor that no model capacity removes, and that the loss-minimising pass blends the answers into an invalid grid. We propose the proximal target: train each pass toward the correct answer nearest to its own input, found by scanning a list of answers, by a search solver started at the draft, or within a band of quality set by a knob; correct answers become fixed points, and a pass only has to make fewer errors than its input has for the loop to converge, which its training loss bounds. With the same 1.7M-parameter network and 5 seeds, the proximal target solves 98.8% of Sudoku puzzles with 2-64 answers (standard target: 2.5%), 93.8% of sparse puzzles with more than 10,000 answers without any list of answers (0.0%), and 90.9% of 200-node 3-colourings after training on graphs of at most 50 nodes (36.9%), and different random starts end at different answers. Against GRAM, a stochastic-latent reasoner, a single run is correct far more often (98.8% vs. 57.7%) and twenty runs find more of the answers (53.6% vs. 37.4%), while GRAM is slightly ahead on its own 10-node colouring benchmark; in Scrabble the knob becomes a calibrated strength dial.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [A Full-Ledger Counterfactual Audit of Adaptive Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22778v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22778

**作者：** Xiaopeng Chu、Wenzheng Kong、Wei Liu、Wenyi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22778v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22778v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FLCVA 全账本反事实审计：在同一冻结前缀和同一作答流程下，比较立即作答与四条定长续写的结果，并核算策略所用每个信号的成本；在 GSM8K、MATH-500 上审计自适应推理的续写价值与轻量停止控制器的实际增益。

**相关性简评：** 该文把「预测当前答案有误」与「继续计算能否改善答案」明确分开，并做信号成本核算，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的收益—成本归因与停止策略评价提供可核查的审计框架；结论来自纯文本设定，迁移到视觉证据筛选需再验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Full-Ledger Counterfactual Audit of Adaptive Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Adaptive reasoning must decide whether to answer now or spend more compute. Predicting that the current answer is wrong does not establish whether further reasoning will improve it. We introduce the Full-Ledger Counterfactual Value Audit (FLCVA), which compares an immediate answer with four fixed-length continuations from the same frozen prefix under one answer procedure and accounts for the cost of every signal used by a policy. On GSM8K and MATH-500, a random 256-token continuation recovers a wrong answer in 26.6% of Qwen3-8B cases and 18.9% of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B cases, while damaging a correct answer in 1.4% and 1.9%. Correctness labels from independently sampled continuations predict a separate continuation's outcome in both primary models. In offline replay, using these labels to allocate compute improves performance on DeepSeek; the Qwen3 gain remains inconclusive. Under full cost accounting, the tested lightweight, myopic controllers mainly recognize current errors and add little continuation-specific value beyond that shortcut. A Llama-8B replication and action/cost ablations examine the robustness of these findings. FLCVA separates measuring local continuation value, predicting it from observable signals, and using those predictions to allocate computation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to EACL 2027 (Main Conference)

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [Transfer Once, Ask Locally: Compact Audio-Visual Memory for On-Device Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23085

**作者：** Qitong Wang、Jihui Jin、Divya Kothandaraman、Vishnu Raj、Christopher Rasmussen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23085v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种免训练、与查询无关的音视频 token 剪枝框架：以时空块与音频窗为单位，用 Monte Carlo Shapley 估值并配合嵌入覆盖目标筛选 token，在预算内构造可复用的紧凑多模态记忆以降低传输与端侧开销。

**相关性简评：** 其「用重要性估值加覆盖目标在有限预算下挑选可复用多模态记忆」的做法，可为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、贡献分配与 latent 预算/缓存设计提供可核查线索，但论文本身面向压缩与端侧效率，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Transfer Once, Ask Locally: Compact Audio-Visual Memory for On-Device Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** Audio-visual question answering on lightweight user devices requires balancing speed, memory usage, privacy, and communication. One approach is to run audio-visual encoders on a server and a lightweight language decoder on the user device, keeping questions local while offloading media encoding. However, transferring and processing the full multimodal representation remains costly. We propose a training-free, query-agnostic token pruning framework that constructs a compact, reusable memory before transmission. We define video spatio-temporal blocks and aligned audio windows as players and estimate their importance through Monte Carlo Shapley valuation. We use an embedding coverage objective that combines query-independent utility with cross-modal redundancy calibration and rewards unimodal diversity through marginal coverage gains. We retain the original token embeddings of high-scoring players under a limited token budget and transfer the resulting memory once for reuse across subsequent local questions. Experiments on Video-MME, Daily-Omni, and Video-Holmes using video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) and video-SALMONN 2 Pro (4B) show that our method achieves the best or tied-best QA accuracy among the compared compression methods across all evaluated settings. On Daily-Omni with video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) at 80\\% pruning, our method improves accuracy by 12.86--14.62 percentage points over these methods while achieving the lowest device-side LLM prefill and decoding latency, with up to $1.92\\times$ decoding speedup at comparable embedding memory usage.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [Get Hint-Augmented GRPO Done Right Through Activation Steering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23124v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23124

**作者：** Khiem Le、Ting Hua、Nitesh V. Chawla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23124v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23124v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 Hint-Augmented GRPO 在训练时依赖提示、推理时提示缺失造成的上下文不匹配，论文把「提示」重新解释为对模型行为的引导，训练可学习的 steering vector 并比较有提示与无提示响应的生成概率，推理时直接将向量注入激活，以在无提示条件下复现提示带来的行为。

**相关性简评：** 这是纯文本工作，但「训练期获得额外条件、推理期以激活注入替代该条件」的做法可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据在推理阶段注入内部状态、以及训练-推理条件对齐问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Get Hint-Augmented GRPO Done Right Through Activation Steering

**abstract/A1：** GRPO has become the dominant reinforcement learning method for eliciting reasoning capability in LLMs using verifiable rewards. However, it suffers from a fundamental failure mode: when a training question exceeds the base model's current capability, all generated responses fail and receive zero reward, yielding no gradient signal and stalling further capability expansion. An emerging class of GRPO extensions, termed Hint-Augmented GRPO (HA-GRPO), adds a "hint" to the context to reduce the difficulty of the question, thereby producing a usable gradient signal. Nevertheless, existing HA-GRPO methods share a key issue: no hint is available at inference time to append to the question, creating a training-inference context mismatch that leads to inconsistent performance gains. In this work, we reinterpret the function of hinting -- not as reducing question difficulty, but as steering model behavior -- and propose ASH (Activation Steering for Hinting), a novel HA-GRPO method built on this view. During training, the model is presented with both hinted and plain questions, while a trainable steering vector is optimized to widen the margins between the model's generation probabilities for responses to the hinted version relative to the plain version, thereby capturing the question-agnostic component of the behavioral shift induced by the hint. During inference, the trained steering vector is directly injected into the model's activation, reproducing the hint-conditioned behavior without requiring any hint in the context. Experiments with four LLMs (Qwen3-1.7B/4B-Base, Llama-3.2-1B/3B-Instruct) show that ASH consistently improves pass@$k$ on competition-level mathematics benchmarks (AMC, OlympiadBench, AIME24, AIME25) and out-of-distribution benchmarks (Minerva, GPQA-Diamond). Notably, it improves average pass@8 of Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Qwen3-4B-Base by \\textbf{2.60}\\% and \\textbf{2.21}\\%.

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [WoRD: Diffusion-Based Posterior Inference for Online Goal Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23192v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23192

**作者：** Almog Anschel、Sarah Keren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23192v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23192v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** WoRD 研究如何从部分轨迹在线推断智能体的目标：以学习的轨迹扩散先验为基座，为每个候选目标运行热启动去噪链，用闭式对数似然梯度把采样引向已观测轨迹，再按贝叶斯规则重加权，得到校准的多峰目标后验，并在模拟机器人、行人与车辆轨迹上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「每个候选假设一条并行去噪链，并随证据逐步重加权」的信念维护机制，可能为 LVR 中多种 latent 假设的并行维护、视觉证据筛选及信念坍塌诊断提供可核查的类比框架，但本文面向轨迹目标识别，迁移到视觉推理需另行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WoRD: Diffusion-Based Posterior Inference for Online Goal Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Goal recognition is the task of inferring an agent's intended goal from its observed behavior. Most existing work focuses on high-level symbolic recognition, abstracting away the continuous motion through which agents reach their goal. Methods that do reason over continuous trajectories rely on an explicit dynamics model that is hard to acquire and rarely transfers across settings. Neither approach explicitly learns how agents move toward each candidate goal, limiting accuracy on ambiguous trajectory prefixes where such knowledge is most needed. We present WoRD (Waypoint Recognition with Diffusion), which uses a learned trajectory prior to infer the agent's goal from partial observations. At each step, WoRD runs one warm-started denoising chain per candidate goal, steers each chain toward the observed trajectory through a closed-form log-likelihood gradient, and reweights the resulting samples by Bayes' rule into a calibrated posterior over goals. We evaluate WoRD on simulated robot settings, pedestrian trajectories, and vehicle trajectories using four metrics targeting distinct failure modes. Across all three settings, the same inference procedure preserves calibrated multimodal beliefs through ambiguous prefixes where filter- and classifier-based observers collapse to a single mode or grow overconfident.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 40th Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [EgoLink: Egocentric Language-Vision Interactive Network Knowledge Challenge Summary](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21690v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21690

**作者：** Renhe Sun、Yueying Feng、Bohan Yu、Tong Niu、Yunqi Liu、Zitong Wang、Haolin He、Kexin Shi、Hualei Wang、Qiuqiang Kong、Chang Yao、Yuge Huang、Weiqiang Wang、Jingyuan Chen、Jian Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21690v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21690v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文总结了 ACM MM 2026 EgoLink 挑战赛：以第一人称视频为输入，设置社会情感推理选择题与对话调用工具完成服务任务两条赛道，并同时用答案正确性与过程／执行质量进行评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「候选验证、显式中间证据、状态感知纠错」等发现可能为 LVR 的证据筛选、停止决策与过程监督评价提供基准线索，但不涉及 latent 推理状态，联系较间接，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoLink: Egocentric Language-Vision Interactive Network Knowledge Challenge Summary

**abstract/A1：** The EgoLink Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 benchmarks egocentric multimodal intelligence linking first-person perception, social reasoning, and interactive action. Unlike benchmarks centered on visible objects and activities, EgoLink evaluates whether models can interpret emotional and social cues, resolve visually grounded references, and convert user requests into verified tool-mediated actions. Its two tracks cover social reasoning in egocentric video and interactive agents that complete service tasks through dialogue, tool use, and closed-loop execution. Together, they test whether perception, reasoning, and decision-making remain coupled under both offline question answering and state-changing interaction. We highlight a multi-stage Track 1 data pipeline for multiple-choice question (MCQ) generation, chain-of-thought generation, automatic quality grading, and video-contribution filtering. We summarize the task design, evaluation metrics, rules, leaderboards, and representative solutions. The benchmark combines objective answer scoring with process- and result-based evaluation, enabling analysis of both final correctness and execution quality. The best Track 1 system achieved 0.9263 accuracy, while the best Track 2 system achieved 0.844 joint success and 0.916 micro accuracy. Participant reports reveal a shared principle: strong systems make intermediate evidence explicit through candidate verification, option-robust reasoning, scenario-aware planning, tool canonicalization, and state-aware correction before answering or acting. These findings highlight the importance of process verification and evidence-grounded reasoning for reliable egocentric multimodal intelligence.

**comment/C1：** Published in ACM Multimedia 2026 (MM '26)

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [CEVA: Complementary Evidence Verification via Frame-Level Reorganization for Short-Video Fake News Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22141v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22141

**作者：** Wenshen Yang、Junfeng Hao、Jing Li、Decheng Liu、Yangkunfei、Shuyu Jiang、Haizhou Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22141v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22141v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CEVA 短视频假新闻检测框架，在融合前用双分支模块把帧分为「显式对齐」与「互补上下文」两类证据并重组，再以双向对比正则做跨模态融合；在 FakeSV、FakeTT 上取得较好结果，并显示该重组在固定帧预算下优于均匀、随机和相似度选择。

**相关性简评：** 其核心观察——按文本-帧相似度选帧会只取重复误导性主张的帧、漏掉挑战性上下文——可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与「LOOK」策略，以及相似度选择偏差是否有对应诊断价值；但方法本身不是 latent 状态推理，迁移需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CEVA: Complementary Evidence Verification via Frame-Level Reorganization for Short-Video Fake News Detection

**abstract/A1：** Short-video platforms are now a common way for the public to follow unfolding events. Their rapid, visually persuasive format also makes fake news easier to spread. Unlike text-image posts, short-video news interleaves multiple modalities and changing scenes, making it difficult to identify which frames verify a claim. Existing detectors either pool sampled frames homogeneously or select them by text-frame similarity. Both overlook that aligned frames may repeat a misleading claim, whereas less dominant context may challenge it. We present CEVA (Complementary Evidence Verification Approach), to our knowledge the first short-video fake news detection framework to explicitly organize frames by their evidential roles before multimodal fusion. Its Dual-Branch Complementary Reorganization (DBCR) module separates explicit-alignment frames from candidate complementary-context frames, and cross-modal fusion with bidirectional contrastive regularization operates on the reorganized evidence. On FakeSV and FakeTT, CEVA achieves the best overall results among the compared methods. Under fixed frame budgets and backbones, DBCR outperforms uniform, random, and similarity-based selection and improves two structurally different detectors. Together, these results establish pre-fusion evidence organization as an effective and transferable component of short-video fake news detection.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [PURE: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multi-Granularity Multimodal Identity Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22343v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22343

**作者：** Hengyu You、Xintong Wang、Jin Cao、Liang Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22343v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22343v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PURE，一个多粒度多模态身份匹配诊断基准，由真实电商匹配对构建 1457 道选择题、14 项任务，按感知、理解、推理与证据判断四个维度评测十个 MLLM，并报告基础级与变体级匹配的系统性差异。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉差异定位」与「证据支撑的变体级规则判断」弱点分析及模态消融，可诊断 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据，与研究关注的视觉细节保留和证据筛选相关，可供失败分析核查；但未涉及 latent 状态生成或训练监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PURE: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multi-Granularity Multimodal Identity Matching

**abstract/A1：** Identity matching asks whether two records denote the same identity, yet the answer depends on the target granularity. Two records may match at the base level but not at the variant level: they share an underlying design while differing in attributes such as color or size. Final matching accuracy does not separately characterize the capabilities involved. To study this problem, we use e-commerce as a challenging real-world domain. We introduce PURE, a diagnostic benchmark for multi-granularity multimodal identity matching covering four capability dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Reasoning, and Evidence-grounded Judgment. The benchmark comprises 1,457 multiple-choice questions across 14 tasks, derived from real-world matching pairs and verified by two domain experts. Across ten recent MLLMs, overall scores range from 47.1% to 86.0%. Understanding receives the highest mean score and Judgment the lowest, with weaknesses in visual difference localization and variant-level rule judgment. Modality ablations show that models benefit from multimodal evidence but can incorrectly identify conflicts between matching records. In direct matching, most models recognize matching pairs more reliably than non-matching pairs at the base level, whereas the reverse holds at the variant level. PURE therefore reveals what aggregate matching accuracy hides: not only whether models judge a pair correctly, but where they struggle to interpret evidence and apply matching criteria.

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [OVET-Consensus: Rank-Preserving Counterfactual Event Verification for Video Moment Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22637v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22637

**作者：** Alibay Osmanli、Shaogang Gong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22637v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22637v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 QVHighlights 上以固定 QD-DETR 时序专家为基座，用 oracle 诊断「候选窗口重叠目标却排序错误」的评分瓶颈，并提出保序的反事实事件校验层 OVET-Consensus：融合 Qwen3-VL 结构化事件判断、VideoLLaMA3 成对时序信号与稠密时序证据，仅允许高置信度 top-1 覆盖，R1@0.5 由 64.71 升至 65.35。

**相关性简评：** 其对多模态校验信号只做受限覆盖、且「语义兼容不足以确定事件起止与转折」的诊断，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与候选/停止决策是否真正被利用，但任务属视频时刻检索，与推理期 latent 状态生成仍有距离。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OVET-Consensus: Rank-Preserving Counterfactual Event Verification for Video Moment Grounding

**abstract/A1：** Modern video moment retrieval systems often propose windows that overlap the target event, yet still select the wrong one. We study this scorer bottleneck on QVHighlights with a fixed QD-DETR-derived temporal expert: the parent-aware temporal expert reaches 64.71 R1@0.5, while diagnostic oracles over the same candidate family reach 75.68 R1@0.5 within the parent-reranked pool and 94.97 R1@0.5 over the full 100-candidate evidence pool. We introduce OVET-Consensus, a rank-preserving counterfactual event verification layer that keeps the temporal expert's ranked list as the default and permits only high-confidence rank-1 overrides from a policy calibrated on held-out training videos. The verifier uses parent-aware interval evidence, structured Qwen3-VL event judgments, VideoLLaMA3 pairwise temporal signals, and dense temporal evidence, but never lets those signals freely reorder the top-K list. On QVHighlights validation, OVET-Consensus improves the parent-aware temporal expert from 64.71 to 65.35 R1@0.5 with a positive paired-bootstrap interval, while mAP is largely unchanged, from 42.67 to 42.74; it makes 20 overrides, with 15 helpful corrections and 3 harmful corrections. The results point to a practical constraint on verifier design: semantic compatibility is useful evidence, but temporal grounding still requires deciding where the event starts, changes, and ends inside a visually compatible scene.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 2 figures, 17 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [OmniTongue: A Comprehensive MLLM Benchmark for Automated Tongue Diagnosis in Traditional Chinese Medicine](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22652

**作者：** Liuyi Xu、Yun Guo、Shusheng Li、Xuewen Zhang、Yining Qian、An-Yang Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22652v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建面向中医舌诊的细粒度 VQA 基准 OmniTongue，含 1,351 张专家校验图像、21,616 条指令对、16 个临床维度、区域级病理定位标注与 66 个细粒度标签，并据此评测现有 MLLM。

**相关性简评：** 区域级标注与「普遍偏低的 Macro-F1 反映依赖统计捷径」这一失败分析，可供核查 MLLM 是否真正利用局部视觉证据而存在视觉旁路，与视觉细节保留相关；但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态生成与训练，联系偏外围。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OmniTongue: A Comprehensive MLLM Benchmark for Automated Tongue Diagnosis in Traditional Chinese Medicine

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have excelled in Western medical imaging, yet their potential in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) tongue diagnosis remains systematically unevaluated. Tongue diagnosis requires sophisticated global and local perception, serving as a rigorous touchstone for assessing the visual understanding depth of MLLMs. However, existing datasets are limited by single dimensions, coarse granularity, and ambiguous labels, hindering the evaluation of authentic clinical capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniTongue, the first comprehensive, fine-grained Visual Question Answering benchmark for TCM tongue diagnosis. This benchmark integrates 1,351 expert-validated images and 21,616 instruction pairs. Its core design features 16 clinical dimensions for multidimensional evaluation, region-specific annotations for pathological localization, and 66 fine-grained labels for precise diagnostic representation. Extensive evaluations of existing MLLMs reveal significant performance gaps, demonstrating that medical-specific models lack domain advantages, while universally poor Macro-F1 scores expose a reliance on statistical shortcuts rather than genuine understanding of tongue pathology. These findings not only highlight the current limitations of MLLMs in interpreting regional medical features but also underscore OmniTongue's significance for developing robust automated tongue diagnosis models. The code and dataset will be made publicly available in a future release.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [M3OPD-Reasoner: An Open Recipe for Post-Training Multimodal Reasoning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23034v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23034

**作者：** Nguyen Quang Trung、Hoang Phan、Shuo Sun、Longyin Zhang、AiTi Aw

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23034v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23034v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出全开放的多教师 on-policy 蒸馏后训练方案 M3OPD-Reasoner，构建 SFT 与可验证奖励 RL 数据，在图像 STEM、图像感知和视频推理上比较混合训练、分域教师蒸馏及蒸馏后再 RL 的效果。

**相关性简评：** 该工作直接服务 VLM 多域推理的后训练，可作为蒸馏与后续 RL 的可核查基线；但未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 机制问题的联系有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** M3OPD-Reasoner: An Open Recipe for Post-Training Multimodal Reasoning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) enables a single student to absorb the skills of several domain teachers by learning from its own generated responses. We present Multimodal Model Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation Reasoner (M3OPD-Reasoner), a fully open recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs) over three reasoning domains: image STEM, image perception, and video. We curate two datasets, M3OPD-SFT-1.35M for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and M3OPD-RL-193K for reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards. On these data, we compare post-training recipes that start from Qwen3.5-9B: training one model on the mixed data of all domains (MixSFT and MixRL), and training one teacher per domain with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), directly or after an SFT warm-up, and distilling the teachers into one student with MOPD. We evaluate on 21 image STEM, image perception, and video reasoning benchmarks after we decontaminate the training data against them. The comparison establishes MOPD of teachers trained with GSPO directly from Qwen3.5-9B as the best recipe: the student closely matches each teacher on its own domain and is on average above the mixed-training baselines on the same prompts. Since MOPD already merges the domain capabilities that MixRL would otherwise have to learn, its student is a stronger starting point for RL, and we ask whether the same data can carry the model further from there: we add MixRL on top of the MOPD student, on the same prompts and with the same settings, which, to our knowledge, no prior VLM recipe has studied. The same prompts still improve the MOPD student, with no collapse, and give our main model, M3OPD-Reasoner-9B. MOPD and MixRL can therefore work as consecutive stages that get more out of the same data. We release all data, the training code, and the checkpoints of every stage.

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Analyzing Learned Convnet Features with Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/1702.07189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 1702.07189

**作者：** David Malmgren-Hansen、Allan Aasbjerg Nielsen、Rasmus Engholm

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/1702.07189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/1702.07189v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用 Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Model 对 Convnet 各层所有节点的内部表示做无参聚类，以可视化和解释学到的特征，并讨论其在迁移学习中的用途。

**相关性简评：** 对内部表示按层做贝叶斯无参聚类，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否形成有语义的簇结构、以及这种结构与深度和位置的关系；但该文只涉及静态视觉特征可视化，与推理期状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Analyzing Learned Convnet Features with Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Models

**abstract/A1：** Convolutional Neural Networks (Convnets) have achieved good results in a range of computer vision tasks the recent years. Though given a lot of attention, visualizing the learned representations to interpret Convnets, still remains a challenging task. The high dimensionality of internal representations and the high abstractions of deep layers are the main challenges when visualizing Convnet functionality. We present in this paper a technique based on clustering internal Convnet representations with a Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Model, for visualization of learned representations in Convnets. Our method copes with the high dimensionality of a Convnet by clustering representations across all nodes of each layer. We will discuss how this application is useful when considering transfer learning, i.e.\\ transferring a model trained on one dataset to solve a task on a different one.

**comment/C1：** Presented at NIPS 2016 Workshop: Practical Bayesian Nonparametrics

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Depth-Aware Activation Steering via Attention Residuals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21655v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21655

**作者：** Shilong Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21655v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21655v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种免训练的深度感知激活引导流程：用 Attention Residuals 的可学习跨层权重把 persona 向量按层分布注入，并以 Jacobian-lens 概念读出核验内部对齐与行为变化，附带强度窗口与指标可靠性等评测发现。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本、非视觉，但其按深度权重分配注入强度、并用下游层概念余弦/秩作为「内部信息是否被使用」的诊断思路，可能为 LVR 的跨层 latent 状态传播与 latent 内容使用核验提供线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Depth-Aware Activation Steering via Attention Residuals

**abstract/A1：** Activation steering is a simple, training-free way to modulate LLM behavior, yet existing methods typically inject vectors at a single layer and lack mechanisms for depth-aware propagation or internal verification. We combine two recent ideas - Attention Residuals (AttnRes) providing learnable cross-layer pathways, and Jacobian-lens (J-space) concept readout - into a lightweight depth-aware steering pipeline. On two small open models (Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3.5-0.8B), distributing a persona vector according to AttnRes-style depth weights yields stronger internal alignment (deeper-layer concept cosine and rank, p&lt;0.0001) and stronger behavioral shifts than single-layer injection. The effect is sign-specific: reverse vectors invert all six metrics, random directions degrade alignment (p&lt;0.0001), and a short LoRA fine-tune erases the imprint. Injection rewires attention strictly downstream of the injection point (upstream JS = 0.00000). Three practical findings follow. First, a layer-wise geometric decomposition of cosine readouts: at the injection layer 123% of the increase is arithmetic, while 5-11 layers downstream 100% is dynamical. Second, an intensity window: effects are monotone over 0.3-0.7, then generation collapses (617 to 306 to 25 chars). Third, metric discipline: keyword-rule metrics fail LLM-judge validation (kappa &lt;= 0.09), and two rule-based conclusions were retracted. Training the depth weights on a steering objective (200 steps, frozen backbone) raises final alignment 10x on held-out prompts. Cross-persona vectors retain 96-99% of the effect: specificity holds against random, not semantic, directions. We contribute a practical, low-overhead depth-aware steering method with a careful evaluation protocol. Code and data are released.

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [FACE: Flow-Guided Angular Channel Estimation Framework for mmWave MIMO Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21698v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21698

**作者：** Samitha Gunarathne、Eslam Eldeeb、Nurul Huda Mahmood、Matti Latva-aho

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21698v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21698v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FACE，将 flow matching 作为生成式先验并结合 mmWave 信道的角度域稀疏性进行信道估计，在少导频条件下取得更高估计精度，且采样步数与推理开销明显少于扩散式估计器。

**相关性简评：** 其少步流匹配连续传输可作为 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态生成的效率替代线索，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理、证据筛选或停止决策，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FACE: Flow-Guided Angular Channel Estimation Framework for mmWave MIMO Systems

**abstract/A1：** Acquiring accurate channel state information (CSI) is essential for millimeter-wave (mmWave) multi-input multi-output (MIMO) systems. However, it remains challenging due to high-dimensional channels, limited pilot observations, and measurement noise. To address these challenges, we propose flow-guided angular channel estimation (FACE), a novel channel estimation framework based on flow matching, an emerging generative modeling paradigm that learns continuous transport dynamics between probability distributions. FACE exploits the inherent angular-domain sparsity of mmWave channels and incorporates the learned flow matching model as a generative channel prior. Numerical results show that FACE consistently achieves higher channel estimation accuracy than both conventional estimators and diffusion-based generative estimator across diverse channel and system configurations. Moreover, FACE maintains high estimation accuracy with fewer pilot observations and achieves an average achievable rate close to the perfect-CSI benchmark. Compared with diffusion-based estimation, FACE requires substantially fewer sampling steps while achieving superior or comparable estimation accuracy, reducing inference time and computational cost. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates the potential of flow matching for accurate, pilot-efficient, and computationally efficient mmWave MIMO channel estimation.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 11 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Regularized Score Centering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21791v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21791

**作者：** Hao Bai、Chenlu Ye、Xuanchang Zhang、Aviral Kumar、Tong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21791v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21791v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对异步强化学习中旧策略数据与训练/推理数值不一致带来的不稳定，论文先诊断 score centering（SC）的梯度行为，再提出加入反向 KL 约束使学习器下一 token 分布贴近行为策略的 Regularized Score Centering（RSC），并给出收敛性分析与多类 agentic 任务实验。

**相关性简评：** 属于纯文本 RL 训练稳定性工作，但「用反向 KL 限制学习器偏离产生数据的策略」这一线索可供核查：在异步、长轨迹的 latent/推理状态训练中，如何约束陈旧 rollout 与当前策略的偏差可能有借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Regularized Score Centering

**abstract/A1：** Asynchronous reinforcement learning increases training throughput, but data from stale policies and numerical mismatch between training and inference can destabilize learning. To stabilize off-policy learning, score centering (SC) removes the effect of reward baselines on the expected update. Our gradient diagnostics show less extreme gradients with SC than with importance sampling (IS), but SC can still fail when the learner moves far from the behavior policy that generated the data. To limit this policy mismatch, we introduce Regularized Score Centering (RSC), which adds a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) penalty to encourage the learner's next-token distribution to stay close to the behavior policy's next-token distribution. RSC works with or without truncated importance sampling (TIS), which caps token importance weights. Our theoretical analysis supports this approach, showing that KL regularization improves convergence guarantees. We evaluate dense and mixture-of-experts models on mathematical reasoning and agentic tasks, including online shopping, tool use, games, and fully asynchronous software engineering. RSC with TIS substantially improves sample efficiency across all agentic benchmarks, attaining higher training rewards with the same number of sampled responses. In final evaluations, it achieves top performance across all six agentic tasks, improving by 14.3% on AppWorld and reaching 9.9 times the strongest baseline's score on BabyAI.

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Rethinking Perceptual Similarity for Remote Sensing: Multi-Layer Attention Scoring and Human Preference Benchmarking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21816v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21816

**作者：** Eli Underwood、Marc Bosch、Neil Joshi、Jeffrey Wen、Myron Brown

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21816v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21816v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对遥感生成图像的感知相似度问题，先用逐级加重的合成扰动做控制实验，观察 RemoteCLIP 各层 Aligned Attention Score 的响应，发现不同伪影在浅层、深层被最好捕捉，据此提出不需在人类偏好标签上调参的七层平均距离 RSPD，并构建 2AFC 人类偏好基准与确定性配准误差基准来评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「不同层最能反映不同视觉差异、按注意力对齐分数分层聚合」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 与视觉特征相似度的深度选择、注意力结构影响提供可供核查的线索，但研究对象是生成图像质量评价而非推理过程本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Perceptual Similarity for Remote Sensing: Multi-Layer Attention Scoring and Human Preference Benchmarking

**abstract/A1：** Perceptual similarity metrics for natural images transfer poorly to remote sensing (RS), where severe geospatial and semantic failures can remain visually plausible while still being decisively wrong for downstream use. To address this, we develop a novel human-aligned perceptual quality metric for generated RS imagery and evaluate it across several generation tasks. We first conduct a controlled perturbation study where we progressively increase the severity of common synthetic perturbations. Using the layer-wise Aligned Attention Score (AAS) responses in RemoteCLIP, we find that different RS artifacts are best captured at different depths. Based on these findings, we introduce Remote Sensing Perceptual Distance (RSPD), a pairwise metric that averages seven independently calibrated layer distances without tuning on human-preference labels. To evaluate our metric on realistic RS generation tasks, we construct a blind, reference-conditioned two-alternative forced-choice (2AFC) benchmark with five 300-triplet subsets spanning synthetic artifacts, airborne and spaceborne super-resolution, SAR-to-optical translation, and timeseries controls. We also construct a 300-triplet ordered misregistration benchmark with deterministic labels. Using the same RemoteCLIP checkpoint, RSPD substantially outperforms simple cosine distance with the final global embedding and is competitive with the strongest generic baselines across the realistic RS generation tasks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027). 9 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [VisHead-Spec: Head-Routed Visual Attention for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21839v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21839

**作者：** Xin Guo、Arushi Rai、Xiang Zhou、Qiang Zhang、Siyu Lin、Xiangjun Fan、Lizhu Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21839v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21839v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向高并发下的多模态投机解码，分析 target 模型到 draft 模型的信息流，发现仅少数 KV 头需要直接读取 target 顶层视觉特征，据此设计固定少量 KV 头路由图像、其余头只看文本的 VisHead-Spec，在保持接受长度基本不变的同时提升吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉信息只由少数 KV 头承载、且集中在 target 高层」的稀疏路由观察，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据在内部状态中的分布、视觉旁路与信息坍缩诊断提供可供核查的线索，但论文目标是解码加速而非推理机制本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VisHead-Spec: Head-Routed Visual Attention for Multimodal Speculative Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models(VLMs) while preserving the target model's outputs, but its speedup diminishes under high concurrency serving as the system becomes more compute bounded as the batch size increases. This problem is pronounced for multimodal speculative decoding as the long range of the image tokens in the prompt can significantly increase the computational cost during draft token prediction. The block-diffusion drafters (e.g. DFlash, DSpark) can effectively speed up the draft model efficiency by predicting multiple draft tokens in one forward pass, but their speedup for VLMs under high concurrency is still modest (&lt;9%). In order to understand this gap, we examine the information flow between different modalities from target model to draft model and identify the sparsity pattern of visual attention between the target and draft models: only small set of key-value heads in the draft model need direct visual feature attention in the context and only visual features from top level layers in the target model need to be attended in the context. We therefore propose VisHead-Spec, which selects a small, fixed set of key-value (KV) heads to attend to all image features from top level layers in the target model and restricts the remaining heads to text features. On Qwen3-VL-8B and MMSpec, VisHead-Spec-8 lets 8 of the drafter's 40 KV heads read the image while keeping accepted length within 0.3% of the block-diffusion draft model baseline (i.e. DSpark). At batch size 64, its throughput gain over autoregressive decoding increases to 16.0%, compared with 9.5% for DSpark; at batch size 1, its speedup remains comparable to DSpark.

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Can a World Model Predict a Deepfake Video? A Consistency Margin Prior for Deepfake Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21846v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21846

**作者：** Pradip Gupta、Shubham Mandloi、Ravi Kumar Satzoda、Sujeeth Bharadwaj、Yassine Benajiba、Dan Roth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21846v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21846v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用冻结 V-JEPA 2.1 编码器加 rank-16 LoRA 适配的预测器，把预测输出与编码目标之差作为「预测残差」，再用轻量 Latent Consistency Head 分离残差幅度与方向，作为跨数据集 deepfake 检测的一致性边界先验。

**相关性简评：** 其在 latent 空间用预测残差作一致性信号并拆分幅度与方向，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据可信度判定与 latent 相似度/一致性度量设计提供可供核查的线索，但研究问题限于伪造检测，未涉及推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can a World Model Predict a Deepfake Video? A Consistency Margin Prior for Deepfake Detection

**abstract/A1：** Deepfake detectors often perform well on the dataset they are trained on, but their performance drops when tested on videos from different datasets. In this paper, we study whether a predictive video model can provide a signal that generalizes better across datasets. Instead of relying only on generator-specific artifacts, as many existing detectors do, we use a model trained to predict natural video and measure the difference between what it predicts and what it observes in a video. Using V-JEPA 2.1, we keep the encoder frozen and adapt the predictor using rank-16 LoRA. We define the difference between the predictor output and the frozen encoder target as the prediction residual. We hypothesize that manipulated clips produce, on average, larger prediction residuals than authentic clips, and use this as our consistency-margin prior. We process these residuals using a lightweight Latent Consistency Head (LCH), which separates residual magnitude from direction for training and classification. To test whether the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset generalization, we compare standard and reversed margin objectives while keeping the remaining training setup fixed. We match the two settings on FF++ validation performance before evaluating them on unseen data. The reversed-margin setting exhibits a larger cross dataset generalization gap on average, providing evidence that the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset transfer. Our complete model adds only 8.25M trainable parameters, which is 2.47% of the total 334M parameters. Trained only on FF++, it achieves an AUC of 90.5 on Celeb-DFv2 and performs well across several other cross-dataset benchmarks. Our ablations show that separating residual magnitude from direction, reducing dataset-specific variation, and using a low-rank projection improve cross-dataset generalization in our experiments.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [When Does Discrete Ricci Curvature Help? An Edge-Level Analysis of Curvature-Enhanced GCNs for PPI Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21849

**作者：** Clara Joswig、Elham Gholamzadeh、Ghazal Mostofi、Nico Scherf

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21849v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在四个真实 PPI 数据集上逐边比较曲率增强 GCN 与标准 GCN，发现整体 AUC/AUPR/F1 的提升几乎全部来自低度数边，而低曲率瓶颈边反而在四分之三数据集上变差，并用簇分离解释取代「缓解过挤压」的原始动机。

**相关性简评：** 其用逐边归因检验「增益是否真来自所声称机制」的做法，可能可供核查 latent 推理中聚合收益是否真源于视觉证据使用与跨区信息传递而非表层捷径；曲率加权消息传递与长程信息瓶颈、信息坍缩的类比仍需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Discrete Ricci Curvature Help? An Edge-Level Analysis of Curvature-Enhanced GCNs for PPI Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Curvature-enhanced graph convolutional networks (CGCN) incorporate Ollivier-Ricci curvature (ORC), grounded in optimal transport theory, into message passing, motivated by the claim that curvature-weighted edges alleviate "over-squashing" at structural bottlenecks. But does CGCN's improvement actually come from mitigating over-squashing? We investigate this on four real-world protein-protein interaction (PPI) datasets, comparing CGCN against a standard GCN at the level of individual edges rather than aggregate metrics alone. While CGCN improves aggregate AUC, AUPR, and F1 across all four datasets, this gain is concentrated almost entirely on low-degree edges, while performance on low-curvature (bottleneck) edges systematically degrades in three of four datasets, the opposite of what the over-squashing motivation predicts. Examining the learned edge-weight function directly, together with clustering-validity metrics, edge-distance ratios, and a layer-depth ablation across four depths, we find evidence for an alternative account: curvature-weighting sharpens the separation between clusters, which improves intra-cluster predictions at the cost of inter-cluster (bottleneck) ones. We test this account against topologically defined communities rather than the classification labels used elsewhere. Additionally, we offer practical guidance on when to prefer CGCN over standard GCN, depending on whether intra- or inter-cluster predictions matter most.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Geometric Distributional Deep Learning

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [AR-WAM: efficient streaming world-action modeling for autonomous driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21851v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21851

**作者：** Baiting Luo、Shatadal Mishra、Ahmadreza Moradipari、Nejib Ammar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21851v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21851v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** AR-WAM 提出面向自动驾驶的高效流式 world-action 模型：以因果视频骨干增量更新视觉上下文并复用历史计算，单次前向取得未来特征来条件化轨迹生成，再用强化学习按驾驶奖励优化策略，在 NAVSIM-v1 上报告 PDMS 提升。

**相关性简评：** 其因果骨干的增量潜状态复用与「单次前向预测未来特征再条件化生成」可能为 LVR 的潜状态更新、预测性工作区表示及替代迭代生成的成本问题提供可供核查的方法线索，但研究任务是驾驶轨迹而非多模态推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AR-WAM: efficient streaming world-action modeling for autonomous driving

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous driving requires anticipating how traffic will evolve while responding quickly to new observations. Video foundation models offer the first ability, but repeated history encoding and iterative video generation make them costly to use for driving. We present AR-WAM, an efficient streaming world-action model that combines autoregressive temporal modeling with single-pass future conditioning. Because its video backbone is causal, AR-WAM updates its visual context incrementally, reusing past computation and conditioning future prediction on observed motion. We further hypothesize that future features computed from recent observations can improve generalization in dynamic traffic without iterative video generation. AR-WAM therefore obtains these features in one forward pass and uses them to condition trajectory generation, and reinforcement learning then improves the policy through direct optimization of driving rewards. With a single front camera, AR-WAM achieves 91.5 PDMS after supervised training and 94.0 after reinforcement learning on NAVSIM-v1, outperforming the compared strong end-to-end world-action models. In a dynamic setting of continuous driving, supervised AR-WAM keeps its visual history across decisions and outperforms strong world-action baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [WM-Quant: Rollout-Aware Post-Training Quantization for JEPA-Style World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21882v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21882

**作者：** William Hammer、Andrew Heuer、Nikhil Krishna、Matthew Grenier、Ramtin Zand

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21882v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21882v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 WM-Quant，面向 JEPA 风格世界模型的训练后量化框架，通过保留早期编码器精度、控制通路和按整段 rollout 校准激活范围，缓解量化误差在自回归 MPC 多步推理中的累积，使低比特下的动作排序与规划成功率显著回升。

**相关性简评：** 其「量化误差随预测步累积、需保住 latent state 与细粒度控制信息」的分析，可供核查 LVR 中多步 latent 状态更新的信息保真与误差传播问题，但属控制导向的效率方法，与视觉推理机制联系间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WM-Quant: Rollout-Aware Post-Training Quantization for JEPA-Style World Models

**abstract/A1：** We present WM-Quant, to our knowledge, the first post-training quantization (PTQ) framework designed specifically for JEPA-style world models. Although world models typically feature relatively few parameters, action planning algorithms often require thousands of inferences, which can quickly become computationally expensive. PTQ is a natural way to reduce this cost. However, we show that in world models using autoregressive model predictive control (MPC) rollouts, quantization error can compound with each predictor step, resulting in action rankings from the quantized model becoming near-random. WM-Quant addresses this with three components that build on existing PTQ methods: early-encoder precision, control-path preservation, and rollout-aware scaling. Early-encoder precision protects accurate latent state and goal information to mitigate long-term error propagation. Control-path preservation allows the world model predictor to distinguish between fine-grained actions, significantly improving action rankings and execution. Rollout-aware scaling jointly calibrates encoder and per-step predictor activation ranges with respect to an entire rollout rather than a single prediction, reducing the cumulative rollout error. Across multiple world models, environments, and PTQ methods, WM-Quant substantially improves planning success at low bit-widths, approaching full-precision performance at W4A4.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures, and 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Language Models Can Rewrite Their Own Weights](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21885v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21885

**作者：** Jiajun Fan、Neil He、Changyi Yang、Andy Mao、Xi Zhu、Chaoran Cheng、Kaiqu Liang、Shuaike Shen、Zetian Li、Ruihan Guo、Ge Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21885v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21885v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 让 27B 语言模型依据评分答案、自运行结果和隐藏状态对比诊断失败，在无梯度条件下改写自身权重，并在多轮编辑中累积提升、修复受损副本。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本权重自改写不直接研究视觉推理；但其用成功与耗尽 token 答案的隐状态差异定位方向并定向修复，可能为 LVR 的内部状态诊断、失败归因与免训练持续适应提供线索，可核查其向视觉任务迁移的可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Language Models Can Rewrite Their Own Weights

**abstract/A1：** Language models are trained once, frozen and deployed: improving one again takes another training run, and a prompt, a scaffold or extra computation at inference is gone once the task ends. A frontier model has been shown to edit the weights of a much smaller one. We ask whether a model can rewrite its own weights to make itself better, and find that it can, by its own reasoning, with no gradients and no training; we call this self-rewriting. Given its weights, graded answers and the ability to run itself, a 27B model diagnoses its failure and edits the weights behind it. It surpasses itself: its own edit makes it 6.6 points more accurate on 490 problems it never saw, in a test fixed in advance, and another session's edit also makes it significantly better. It keeps getting better: it submits an edit only when its own tests support it, each edit replacing the last, and over its first eight such sessions its accuracy only rises. Unlike test-time scaling, the gain persists in its weights and adds no cost per answer; unlike training, it needs no pre- or post-training infrastructure, only an open model and hours on one GPU. It repairs a damaged copy of itself: it locates the corrupted matrix and rewrites it with values of its own, never reading the undamaged weights; found on text math problems alone, the repair lifts the copy on six image benchmarks it never saw from 7% to 70% accuracy, 83% of the undamaged model's. And the ability emerges with scale: across four sizes of one family, the 2B model makes no edit and the 4B and 9B models break themselves; only the 27B model works on itself as a human would, comparing its hidden states on answers that finish and on answers that run out of tokens, weakening the direction that separates them. Like a human, a model need not separate training from inference; improving a language model may come to take a conversation rather than a training run.

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Lost in a Few Heads: Diagnosing and Repairing Block Selection in Sparse Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21917v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21917

**作者：** Wenhe Zhang、Boxing Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21917v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21917v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用完整注意力质量选择作 oracle，定位稀疏注意力在固定预算下丢失长上下文检索能力的位置：通过教师强制答案的校准探针，找出少数「盲头」——选择器在这些头里放弃了对关键 token 的注意力，而 mean-pooled 键摘要稀释了决定性 token；只在这些头恢复完整注意力或用 4-bit scorer 即可在无训练下恢复大部分精度。

**相关性简评：** 其「少数头承载证据、池化摘要稀释决定性 token、按头沿路径归因答案对数概率」的诊断为可供核查的线索，可能启发 LVR 中视觉关键细节在压缩/摘要表示中丢失的定位与 latent 内容的贡献分配，但该方法为纯文本稀疏注意力，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lost in a Few Heads: Diagnosing and Repairing Block Selection in Sparse Attention

**abstract/A1：** Sparse attention models read a few blocks of keys for every query at a fixed budget and can lose much of their long-context retrieval accuracy at that budget. We ask where this loss is made. Using exact attention mass selection at the shipped budget as an oracle, we show that the selector under-ranks the needle rather than running out of budget. We find attention heads that miss the needle by running a calibration probe that teacher-forces the answer and reads two quantities in every head. The first is the needle attention that the selector forfeits, and the second is the share of the answer's log-probability that fixing the head's selection restores, integrated along the path from the shipped selector to full attention. Four checkpoints of the InfLLM-V2 family and NOSA-8B lose multi-key retrieval in the same three blind heads, which attend to the needle while a mean-pooled key summary dilutes its single decisive token. Full attention in those heads or a 4-bit scorer that keeps the shipped budget recovers most of the loss without training. On MiniCPM4.1-8B at 64K, either fix lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 54.1 to 97.5%, level with full attention in every head. In production serving engines, full attention in those heads keeps 84 to 89% of the time to first token (TTFT) that sparse attention saves at 32K to 128K context length. Beyond the InfLLM-V2 selector lineage, one unified rule flags attention heads above a threshold swept on each checkpoint until they close most of the gap to full attention on calibration prompts. A similar small set of blind attention heads carry the loss for the SeerAttention-R gates, NSA, and DashAttention, whereas many such heads exist in MoBA. On Qwen3-8B with the SeerAttention-R gates, full attention in 18 of 288 key-value heads lifts multi-key retrieval accuracy from 29.8 to 97.7%.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 8 figures, 20 tables. Code: https://github.com/zhangwenhe1007/blind-attention-heads

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [KVUse: Reusable-KV User Memory for Efficient LLM Recommenders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21920v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21920

**作者：** Xin Guo、Qiang Zhang、Xiang Zhou、Arushi Rai、Siyu Lin、Yifan Wu、Xiang Shen、Lei Huang、Qunshu Zhang、Xiangjun Fan、Lizhu Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21920v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21920v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 KVUse，把 LLM 推荐器中的用户画像从文本改为可复用的 KV cache 存储：用自蒸馏训练轻量 LoRA writer，在解码画像时直接产出可复用 KV，使模型能在新位置、新上下文中读取而无需重新 prefill，并配合 KV 调度器隐藏传输开销，在四个 InstructRec 数据集上保持排序质量并显著提升吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 「由 writer 生成、可脱离原位置与上下文被读取的 KV」这一机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的持久化、缓存与位置无关复用提供线索，可供核查复用时状态内容是否失真；但论文本身不涉及视觉推理或推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KVUse: Reusable-KV User Memory for Efficient LLM Recommenders

**abstract/A1：** Memory-augmented recommenders built on large language models (LLMs) maintain a natural-language profile of each user. These profiles are updated by a user profile update path that revises the profile as new interactions arrive and are consumed by downstream task paths like ranking. Because these user profiles are stored as text in the user memory, every ranking LLM call that consumes them must splice them in the prompt and calculate the KV cache of them during the prefill phase. Given these ranking LLM calls are prefill heavy (long prompt but short decoded output) and repeatedly called, these repeated prefill of user profile text dominates the serving. Prefix caching cannot remove it, because these user profiles are preceded by different text in different calls. We present KVUse, which stores the user profiles as reusable key-value (KV) caches instead of text. KVUse trains lightweight LoRA writers by self-distillation, so that the user profile update call produces the reusable KV of the user profile while decoding it, and the LLM can read these reusable KV at new positions and behind new context without recomputation. KVUse also has a customized KV scheduler that uploads the stored reusable KV of the next batch while the current batch is computed, hiding the KV transfer from host memory behind GPU computation. On four InstructRec datasets with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, KVUse shows no statistically significant difference in ranking quality from full prefill over continuous memory update rounds. Under saturated load, KVUse raises the throughput of the downstream scoring path by 4.0x-4.5x and of the profile update path by 1.4x-1.9x.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [GridDriver: On-Board BEV-Based Language Planning and Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21924v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21924

**作者：** Katharina Winter、William Engel、Zain A. Alishammat、Fabian B. Flohr

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21924v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21924v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GridDriver，以轻量时空 BEV 感知和空间结构化 token 为共享 LLM 提供场景上下文，并用帧级调度交错轨迹预测与异步语言生成，实现车载语言交互驾驶。

**相关性简评：** 其对 BEV 空间表示与 Q-Former 压缩的几何信息保留对比分析，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节是否留存于内部状态提供可核查线索；帧级调度解耦规划与 AR 生成亦可供参考，但主体为驾驶应用，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GridDriver: On-Board BEV-Based Language Planning and Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Bird's-Eye-View (BEV)-conditioned large language models (LLMs) enable language-interactive driving, but onboard deployment requires efficient perception and continuous planning alongside autoregressive language generation. We present GridDriver, an efficient BEV-conditioned LLM framework combining trajectory prediction, structured driving indicators, natural-language navigation, driving explanations, and scene question answering. Lightweight spatio-temporal BEV perception and spatially structured tokens provide scene context to a shared LLM, while frame-wise scheduling interleaves trajectory prediction with asynchronous language generation. GridDriver achieves competitive performance on public benchmarks, reaching 87.9% PDMS on NAVSIM and 57.6% accuracy on nuScenes-QA with a shared planning and QA checkpoint.
