Spatial representation analyses demonstrate improved geometric information retention and trajectory prediction compared with Q-Former compression. The system achieves 32.7 ms standalone planning latency and 9.4 plans/s in an onboard ROS 2 pipeline. We further evaluate planning on our own data recordings and demonstrate open-loop onboard operation on a research vehicle. Across public benchmarks and real-world evaluations, we identify strengths and limitations of the BEV-based approach.

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [DNeo: Your Parallel Draft Model Needs Better Target Feature Encoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21925v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21925

**作者：** Jameson Sandler、Shane Bergsma、Mostafa Elhoushi、Bin Claire Zhang、Gavia Gray、Joel Hestness、Nolan Dey、Ferdinando Fioretto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21925v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21925v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对推测式扩散解码中并行草稿模型对目标模型特征编码有损、无记忆、层采样均匀的问题，提出 MoFA 及拒绝记忆、因果卷积、前缀交叉注意力与非均匀层选择，构成 DNeo，在保持草稿模型规模与质量的同时加速生成。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本推理加速工作，但其「target 特征经投影有损（residual mixing）」「总拒绝提案的工作记忆」与「非均匀选取 target 层隐状态」三条线索，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 承载任务必要信息、被拒轨迹复用与跨层证据筛选的设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DNeo: Your Parallel Draft Model Needs Better Target Feature Encoding

**abstract/A1：** Speculative-diffusion decoding uses a small parallel drafter model to generate b draft tokens in one forward pass, which then undergo parallel verification via a larger target model. Faster inference can be achieved when multiple draft tokens pass verification simultaneously. Recent parallel draft architectures (DFlash, DSpark) improve draft quality by conditioning on rich features computed by the target, through a mechanism known as feature encoding. However, standard feature encoding mechanisms have three limitations: (1) they are lossy, applying encoding projections that are destructive to the underlying target feature-space via a discovered effect known as residual mixing, (2) they are memory-less, with no record of previously processed proposals, and (3) they utilize features sampled uniformly from across the target layers, omitting important hidden states. To address these issues, we propose Mixture-of-Features Attention (MoFA), a lightweight feature-encoding approach equipped with a working memory that encodes previously rejected proposals. Leveraging MoFA, rejection memory, and a set of further enhancements, including 2-tap causal convolutions, prefix-only cross-attention, and non-uniform target layer selection, the final architecture, DNeo, achieves 1.3x faster generation speed than SoTA (DFlash-2) without impacting drafter footprint or generation quality. DNeo marks a new state of the art in high quality, fast LLM inference.

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Physics-informed neural networks for spacecraft trajectory optimization with exact boundary conditions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21933v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21933

**作者：** Samuel Schönherr、Günther Waxenegger-Wilfing、Sascha Ranftl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21933v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21933v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用神经网络把航天器位置表示为时间的函数，以无监督方式在目标中嵌入物理定律并最小化推力来优化轨迹；提出一种利用轨迹优化问题结构的变换，将边界条件先验地精确嵌入解空间，把带约束问题化为无约束单目标优化，从而去掉边界惩罚项及其权重敏感问题。

**相关性简评：** 其「重参数化使约束按构造满足」的训练思路可能为 latent 推理轨迹的监督提供借鉴，例如以结构化映射替代加权的起止状态惩罚项；但需核查该低维边界下的构造能否迁移到高维隐状态约束。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics-informed neural networks for spacecraft trajectory optimization with exact boundary conditions

**abstract/A1：** We use neural networks to optimize trajectories by modeling the position as a function of time and minimizing the thrust with respect to the network parameters. This corresponds to an unsupervised training procedure in which physical laws are incorporated directly into the optimization objective. In standard physics-informed neural networks, initial and terminal states are enforced through additional weighted loss terms, resulting in a multi-objective optimization problem that is highly sensitive to the choice of loss weights and can lead to poor convergence and reduced accuracy. To address this challenge, we construct a simple transformation, exploiting the mathematical structure of trajectory optimization problems, to enforce boundary conditions exactly a-priori. This transformation can be interpreted as a reparameterization of the solution space that embeds the boundary conditions directly into the function. This converts the original constrained optimization problem into an unconstrained one over admissible trajectories. I.e. the transformation eliminates the boundary condition penalty terms and reduces the problem to a single-objective optimization. Crucially, trajectory optimization comes with a low-dimensional boundary, which allows particularly simple and stable constructions of distance functions compared to general domains in partial differential equations. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach meaningfully improves convergence behavior and solution accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 8 figures, for reproducibility: https://github.com/s-schoenherr/SpacePINN

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [A Log-Timescale Perturbation Bound for Selective State Space Models at Every Sequence Length](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21952v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21952

**作者：** Jack Humphries、Daniel Midgley

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21952v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21952v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文为选择性状态空间模型（Mamba）的 selective-scan 单元推导了输入扰动对隐藏状态影响的界：改在对数尺度上度量时间尺度的变化，使逐步误差不随序列长度累积，从而得到对任意长度成立、且无需时间尺度下界的稳定性保证，并在订单簿数据的小模型上对比了两类块级界。

**相关性简评：** 循环状态更新的扰动敏感性界可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态在多步更新下的稳定性与长度泛化，但论文针对 SSM 架构、与视觉证据和答案使用无关，属方法线索而非主线工作，且其界比梯度攻击实际变化宽五个数量级，实用强度待核验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Log-Timescale Perturbation Bound for Selective State Space Models at Every Sequence Length

**abstract/A1：** Mamba models deployed on streaming data, such as limit order book updates, run far past the sequence lengths they were trained on. A guarantee on how much a small change of input can move the hidden state therefore has to hold at every length. The closest existing bound we know of measures the change of the timescale additively, and it grows linearly with the sequence length unless the timescale is bounded away from zero. In this paper, we measure the change on a log scale instead. The key observation is that the decay factor of each step then moves by at most a fixed fraction of that step's input weight, so the per-step errors cannot pile up. Using this, we prove that two runs of a selective-scan cell from a common start stay within a bound that holds at every length, shrinks to zero with the size of the change and needs no lower bound on the timescale. We give a construction showing that, for small changes, this bound is within a factor of about 2 of the best possible, and we extend it to the update used in Mamba's released code when the timescale is bounded above. We then evaluate block-level bounds built from the theorem on two small models trained on one day of order book data. With a bounded timescale, a bound built on the existing analysis is tighter than ours for at most the first 8 steps. With Mamba's softplus timescale, whose certified lower bound is as small as $10^{-21}$, it is looser at every length. Our bounds hold for every input to a block, but they remain more than five orders of magnitude above the changes that gradient attacks find.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 3 figures, 1 table. Code and recorded runs: https://github.com/jackdoesjava/mamba-v-lob

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [Weight-space programming for tractor guidance controller: large language models natively write neural network weights that steer a real tractor](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21958v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21958

**作者：** Timo Oksanen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.RO、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21958v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21958v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究让 LLM 不经优化器或梯度下降，直接读取并按自然语言规格改写一个 253 参数拖拉机转向控制网络的权重，并在少量仿真评估反馈下完成「修复旧机型网络」与「从零权重写出控制器」，再与进化策略、梯度下降和 pure pursuit 在仿真与实车上对比。

**相关性简评：** 「直接写入权重、用闭环仿真反馈验证」可能为 LVR 提供一种不依赖梯度训练就构造或干预内部状态的手段，且其稀疏级联结构与控制论式闭环分析可供核查内部状态是否真正驱动输出。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Weight-space programming for tractor guidance controller: large language models natively write neural network weights that steer a real tractor

**abstract/A1：** Weight-space programming is a process where a large language model reads and writes the weights of a neural network directly, guided by a natural language specification, without an optimiser or gradient descent. This study applies it to automatic tractor guidance, where a 253-parameter neural network steers along paths of lines and arcs and must be adapted when the machine changes, such as a slower steering actuator. GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.1 (also 5.2) edited the network for our research tractor using a small budget of simulated evaluations to repair a network trained for another tractor and write a controller into a network with zero weights. We compared them with an evolution strategy, gradient descent and pure pursuit in simulation and on the real tractor. Both models read the network and analysed the closed loop with control theory correctly, and Fable's repair without simulation succeeded only occasionally. With simulated feedback, both models repaired the trained network in single sessions and wrote a working controller from zero weights, whereas from zero the evolution strategy found none. The controllers written from zero were sparse and in a classical cascaded structure; one model edited the trained network while the other replaced it. Gradient descent reached higher accuracy with far more simulation, and all language model controllers steered the real tractor without intervention, with errors of the same order as pure pursuit. Language models can program the weights of a neural controller directly, much as control engineers design one. We call this Weight-Space Engineering.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables (1 in the appendices); includes a field test on a real tractor

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [DEFTS: Data-Efficient, Training-Free Few-Shot Learning for CLIP in the Text Subspace](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21993v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21993

**作者：** Yijin Zeng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21993v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21993v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** DEFTS 提出一种无需训练、仅在文本子空间内完成的 CLIP few-shot 分类方法：将估计的图像原型投影到文本嵌入张成的线性子空间，并以贝叶斯后验均值求解少量组合系数，从而缓解模态间隔。

**相关性简评：** 把视觉原型压缩为文本嵌入子空间上的少量系数，可供核查「文本对齐瓶颈是否会丢弃视觉细节、造成信息坍缩」，但其本身不涉及推理状态生成，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DEFTS: Data-Efficient, Training-Free Few-Shot Learning for CLIP in the Text Subspace

**abstract/A1：** Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) achieves impressive zero-shot image classification performance by comparing image embeddings against class-specific text embeddings. While recent few-shot methods leverage labeled training samples to improve upon this zero-shot baseline, they often predominantly rely on image embeddings and underutilize the semantic structure of the text embeddings. In this paper, we introduce DEFTS, a data-efficient, training-free few-shot classification method strictly in the text subspace. We first demonstrate empirically that while raw text embeddings can be suboptimal as direct classifiers, the linear subspace they span remains highly discriminative. DEFTS therefore proposes to reduce the image-text modality gap by projecting estimated image prototypes directly into this subspace. Importantly, by expressing these prototypes as linear combinations of text embeddings, DEFTS reduces a high-dimensional estimation problem to the derivation of just a few coefficients. These coefficients are then computed via a Bayesian posterior mean, using a prior naturally centered on the original text embedding coordinates. Extensive experiments across 12 image classification benchmarks demonstrate that DEFTS significantly improves upon existing training-free methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [KV-LTM: Persistent Per-Session KV Caches as Long-Term Memory for Personal Assistants](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21998

**作者：** Ziv Kfir、Ido Kfir

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21998v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 KV-LTM：把每个会话的原始文本与其在固定 reader 模型下的 KV cache 一并作为记忆层存储，回答时恢复缓存并追加问题；给出存储记录、按预算的保留策略、只追加读路径与有界聚合设计，解析估算每 token 约 31K–328K 字节的存储开销与每查询成本，并设计无需真实缓存实现的评测协议（会话级 prefill 模拟缓存复用，另加小规模真实缓存回放检验数值等价）。

**相关性简评：** 其「缓存状态替代重算、以文本为退化路径、并显式核查写读之间的内容损失与计算—存储代价」的思路，可能为 LVR 中复用 latent/缓存状态时的内容保真度、以及 THINK/LOOK 相关计算成本核算提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KV-LTM: Persistent Per-Session KV Caches as Long-Term Memory for Personal Assistants

**abstract/A1：** Personal assistants accumulate months of conversations, but today's long-term memory stacks put derivation or selection between a question and its evidence. In a system-level comparison on LongMemEval, two commercial assistants scored well below the same model reading the history offline \[1\], consistent with, but not isolating, content loss between write and read. We propose KV-LTM: a memory tier storing each session's raw text plus its KV cache under a fixed reader model, answering by restoring cached sessions and appending the question. KV-LTM is complementary to, not a replacement for, retrieval and indexing. Text is the memory of record; the cache is a recompute-free prefill accelerator, degrading gracefully to text. We specify the design: storage record, budget-derived retention, append-only read path, and bounded aggregation. We size it analytically: across pinned BF16 full-attention (GQA) and latent-cached MLA configurations, per-token state spans approximately 31K-328K bytes (the pinned KDA+MLA hybrid differs: approximately 8.1K bytes/token plus a fixed per-session recurrent term), giving approximately 0.34-3.6 TB per user-year at a declared 30K-token/day scenario, against approximately 49 MB of raw text; per-query cost is modelled as regime-conditional transfer, attention, decode, aggregation, and compute. We define an evaluation protocol needing no persistent-cache implementation for logical QA evaluation: per-session prefill emulates valid cache reuse under the same model/input-state contract and ideal arithmetic, with named arms, controlled baselines, and a preregistered reader-model gate; a separate minimal real-cache replay tests numerical equivalence and measures the cost axis, with outcomes not assumed. KV-LTM trades storage for avoided re-prefill compute and write-time fidelity, within a declared retention budget.

**comment/C1：** 45 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [How Much Distribution Shift Can a Clustering Withstand? Exact Split and Merge Certificates for Structured Transports](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22000v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22000

**作者：** Alisina Bayati、Srinivasa Salapaka、Shahriar Talebi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22000v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22000v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究聚类对分布偏移的稳健性：基于确定性退火相变分析给出局部稳定性准则，用其刻画聚类「分裂」与「合并」事件，并在位移限制在给定子空间的情形下导出精确 SDP 形式的分裂/合并半径，进而组合成全聚类的稳健性证书。

**相关性简评：** 其分裂/合并阈值与 SDP 证书这一方法线索，可能可供核查 latent 状态聚类或离散化在视觉输入分布偏移下是否仍然成立，但能否用于高维视觉表征结构尚需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Much Distribution Shift Can a Clustering Withstand? Exact Split and Merge Certificates for Structured Transports

**abstract/A1：** In clustering, the distribution of the population being clustered is often uncertain or changes over time, so the true distribution may shift away from the nominal distribution used to form the clustering. Once the clustering is committed and each member is assigned to a cluster, such shifts may change the distribution of each cluster. They can make a cluster too spread out for one representative at a given resolution, motivating a split, or bring distinct clusters close enough to share a representative at that resolution, motivating a merge. We make these split and merge events precise using a local stability criterion built on the phase-transition analysis in deterministic annealing. This notion of stability enables us to quantify how robust a clustering is against such events. Furthermore, motivated by applications in which uncertainty and population change are structured, we study shifts whose displacements are restricted to a prescribed subspace. The smallest structured shifts that reach the respective thresholds define the split and merge radii, for which we obtain exact semidefinite programming (SDP) reformulations. The split SDP has only two scalar decision variables. These convex reformulations enable us to combine the radii into a robustness certificate for the full clustering that gives the size of the smallest structured shift that reaches any monitored split or merge threshold.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [MedGuardBench: A Patient-Oriented Benchmark for Personalized Guardrail Development](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22069

**作者：** Sara Khosravi、Xiaofei Wen、Majid Sarrafzadeh、Muhao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22069v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「患者条件化的用药安全判别」任务，发布覆盖 5001 个场景、87 种药物的 drug-disjoint 基准 MedGuardBench，指出现有 guardrail 依赖措辞、是否附带档案等表面线索，并给出个性化推理 guardrail：风险与患者字段关联的蒸馏、逐字段自检的 Context Unrolling 以及规则奖励的 guided GRPO。

**相关性简评：** 其逐字段 Context Unrolling 与「提供检查但不强制自检」的对照、以及删除/互换患者档案的增益归因，可能为 LVR 中证明模型确实使用视觉证据、以及显式证据筛选与监督归因提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MedGuardBench: A Patient-Oriented Benchmark for Personalized Guardrail Development

**abstract/A1：** Safety is personal: the same action can be safe for one person but unsafe for another. Yet current guardrails judge content without considering who is affected. Using medication safety, we introduce \\textit{patient-conditioned medication guarding}, which predicts a safety verdict and 17 risk categories from a patient profile and prescription. We release \\textbf{MedGuardBench}, a drug-disjoint benchmark of 5{,}001 scenarios across 87 medications. We find that standard guardrails rely on surface cues, such as wording and profile presence, rather than patient facts. To address this, we develop \\textit{personalized reasoning guardrails} with three components. \\textbf{Personalized Reasoning Distillation} links risks to relevant patient fields. \\textbf{Context Unrolling (CU)} makes models check each field themselves, reducing false alarms on most backbones; controls show that providing the check does not yield the same benefit. A \\textbf{rule-based reward (guided GRPO)} improves unsafe recall and complex-risk detection, including allergies and adverse reactions, on some backbones, but increases false alarms on others. Our models outperform base models and matched no-profile controls in accuracy, while reducing false alarms relative to no-profile controls. Profile removal and swapping confirm that gains come from patient information, not fine-tuning alone.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [When AUC Meets Generative Reconstruction: A Unified Principle for Balanced Visual Representation Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22094v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22094

**作者：** Boyu Han、Qianqian Xu、Shilong Bao、Zhiyong Yang、Qingming Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22094v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22094v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出把生成式重建改写为生成输出流形上的判别排序问题，用 AUC 与 pAUC 目标约束匹配重建得分高于不匹配对，作为可插拔损失与生成框架联合训练，并给出理论分析与实验验证。

**相关性简评：** 其把重建保真与类间判别统一在同一 latent 空间的排序监督中的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留与判别信息联合训练时的梯度冲突、表示坍缩问题；但论文不涉及推理期 latent 状态的生成或使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When AUC Meets Generative Reconstruction: A Unified Principle for Balanced Visual Representation Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require visual encoders that extract fine-grained visual features while constructing semantically disentangled and discriminative representations. Conventional methodologies encounter a fundamental trade-off: contrastive learning enhances inter-class discrimination by suppressing intra-class variability, whereas generative reconstruction emphasizes perceptual fidelity but lacks explicit comparative supervision. The naive aggregation of these objectives via composite loss functions often induces optimization conflicts and instability due to gradient interference within the shared latent space. To resolve this, we introduce a unified representation-learning paradigm that reformulates generative reconstruction as a discriminative ranking problem within the generative output manifold. Our approach maintains perceptual reconstruction as a supervisory signal and integrates a ranking-based discriminative constraint by systematically ordering matched and mismatched reconstruction outputs. This formulation embeds discriminative information seamlessly into the generative process, alleviating the risk of loss competition. Specifically, we propose AUC Generative Reconstruction (AUCGR), which optimizes the area under the ROC curve (AUC) by ensuring higher scores for matched pairs than for mismatched pairs, and Partial AUC Generative Reconstruction (pAUCGR), which addresses supervision bias by focusing on mismatched comparisons within reliable feature subspaces. The proposed objectives are designed to be plug-and-play, requiring no architectural modifications to existing generative reconstruction frameworks. Theoretical analysis establishes rigorous connections to AUC/pAUC optimization and provides generalization guarantees. Extensive experiments validate that our method advances both discriminative accuracy and fine-grained perceptual representation.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Every Measurement, Every Direction, All at Once: Multimodal Flow Matching for Molecules and Spectra](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22098v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22098

**作者：** Thorben Prein、Elton Pan、Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli、Santiago Miret

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22098v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22098v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究分子3D结构与UV、IR、Raman光谱之间的多模态生成，提出MOSAIC单一flow matching模型，通过随机掩码实现任意模态子集到任意其他子集的生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「任意子集作条件或目标」的掩码式多任务监督，可能为LVR中依据部分视觉证据更新与生成潜在状态提供方法线索，但论文未涉及视觉推理，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Every Measurement, Every Direction, All at Once: Multimodal Flow Matching for Molecules and Spectra

**abstract/A1：** Generative modeling in science is often data-limited, where experiments are expensive. However, each experiment may yield several coupled measurements of the same physical entity. Standard generative models often train only on a single direction, discarding supervision from the rest. We argue that each experiment contains more training signal than this asymmetry allows the model to exploit: random masking enables measurements to serve as either conditioning inputs or prediction targets, yielding multiple supervised denoising tasks from a single experiment. We investigate this premise with MOSAIC, a multimodal flow matching transformer over molecular 3D structure and UV, IR, and Raman spectra. MOSAIC is a single model that is capable of any-to-any generation: generating any subset of modalities from any disjoint subset of the others. On QM9S, MOSAIC achieves 88.8% molecular graph recovery, outperforming the strongest spectra-to-structure baseline by 23.2% in structure generation, while simultaneously outperforming a structure-to-spectra specialist model in spectral generation. Ablations show that the any-to-any objective improves structure generation over structure-only training across model sizes. Furthermore, MOSAIC supports arbitrary subsets of available measurements and enables molecular identification by matching generated spectra to experimental references.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 12 figures. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 77. [EvoDisco: Strategy Discovery for Evolving Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22102v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22102

**作者：** Mingyang Liu、Qingcan Kang、Shixiong Kai、Kaichao Liang、Xiaojin Fu、Tao Zhong、Linqi Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22102v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22102v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** EvoDisco 将 LLM agent 的记忆演化形式化为可学习策略问题：策略依据当前记忆状态与近期任务历史，从 Abstract、Refine、Bridge、Reframe 四种语义算子中选择并实例化，并用内层检索质量加外层策略效用的双层适应度训练该策略，在多个 agent 基准上优于 MemP、MemRL 等记忆基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「对内部状态施加何种变换算子、何时施加」的学习式选择策略与双层监督信号，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新方式与贡献分配提供方法线索，但该文面向纯文本记忆、无视觉推理实验，需核查其算子定义与适应度设计是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoDisco: Strategy Discovery for Evolving Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Large language model agents accumulate experience in memory, and how that memory evolves during interaction is as important as what it stores. Recent methods update memory online, yet both the operators that transform memory and the rules that select them remain fixed by design. We propose EvoDisco, which formulates agent memory evolution as evolutionary strategy discovery: the agent must discover which operator to apply, when to apply it, and how to instantiate it, rather than rewriting memory under a static operator schedule. A learnable policy conditions on the current memory state and recent task history, then selects and instantiates one of four semantic operators---Abstract, Refine, Bridge, and Reframe---which respectively compress related memories, decompose overloaded entries, connect weakly linked experiences, and reorganize retrieved reasoning chains. The policy is updated with a bi-level fitness. An inner signal scores each transformation using a retrieval-quality improvement regularized by memory complexity. An outer signal scores strategic efficacy over a window of past decisions, combining historical operator-utility regret, utility-prediction error, and selection diversity. Under a one-pass online plus frozen-transfer protocol on ALFWorld, WebShop, and LifelongAgentBench (OS and DB), EvoDisco improves over strong memory baselines including MemP and MemRL. The largest gains are on ALFWorld ($+8.8$ online / $+12.9$ frozen-transfer points over MemP; $+16.4$ / $+17.9$ over MemRL). Ablations attribute these gains to both fitness levels and the operator library.

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [From Sorry to QED: Refining LLM Math Proofs via Angelic Execution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22103v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22103

**作者：** Yanju Chen、Hanzhi Liu、Ruizhe Qian、Jiaming Shan、Xu Yang、Yufei Ding、Osbert Bastani、Yu Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22103v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22103v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MathEye 框架：以「angelic 检查」这一乐观执行模型把 LLM 生成 Lean 脚本中失败的片段替换为占位符，从而保留论证结构并隔离出未完成的证明义务；再在符号级数据流依赖图上做残差分析，把义务选择形式化为 MaxSAT，挑出最小代价的关键义务交由数学家以自然语言回答，并自动重建完整可验证证明。

**相关性简评：** 乐观执行加占位符残差、以及最小义务集的选取，可供核查 LVR 中中间推理状态的贡献分配与最小干预验证思路（哪些 latent 步骤真正必需、如何以最少补全修复推理链）；但其属形式化证明修复，尚无视觉或多模态证据，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Sorry to QED: Refining LLM Math Proofs via Angelic Execution

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can now generate Lean proof scripts that capture the high-level structure of many mathematical arguments. However, these scripts frequently fail on complex theorems: a single missing side condition or misapplied lemma can cause the prover to abort early and discard an otherwise viable proof outline. This paper presents MathEye, a framework that converts imperfect LLM-generated proof scripts into fully verified Lean proofs by combining symbolic reasoning with minimal human interaction. At the core of MathEye is angelic proof checking, an optimistic execution model that replaces failing proof fragments with placeholders while preserving the overall structure of the argument. This optimistic check produces an angelic proof that isolates the exact proof obligations that the LLM fails to discharge. Because these unresolved obligations are often logically interdependent, asking a mathematician to address every placeholder directly is inefficient. To minimize human effort, MathEye performs residual analysis over a proof dependence graph that captures symbol-level data flow in the angelic proof. By formulating obligation selection as a MaxSAT optimization problem, MathEye selects a minimal-cost set of essential obligations whose resolution suffices to complete the entire proof. MathEye presents these essential obligations to the mathematician in ordinary mathematical language and uses the mathematician's answers to automatically reconstruct the complete Lean proof. Experiments on 30 real-world benchmarks across analysis and algebra show that MathEye solves 93.3% of the problems while reducing the number of required user interactions by 55%.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [RetiDual-Select: Reliability-Aware Specialist-Generalist Routing for Retinal OCT under Image-Quality Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22117v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22117

**作者：** Zihao Han、Philippe De Wilde

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22117v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22117v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向视网膜 OCT 图像质量偏移下的分类，将 RETFound 专家与 MedSigLIP 编码器组合：静态 PoE 为基准，小型 gate 提出候选融合，验证集冻结的 harm guard 决定是否替换，另设分数决定是否转人工复核。

**相关性简评：** 其「候选是否替换参考」的伤害门控与复核/延迟决策可供核查能否迁移为 LVR 中潜在状态更新与停止、弃权的控制机制；但任务为医学图像分类，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RetiDual-Select: Reliability-Aware Specialist-Generalist Routing for Retinal OCT under Image-Quality Shift

**abstract/A1：** Adaptive fusion can repair a static ensemble's errors, but can also overturn correct decisions. RetiDual-Select combines retinal specialist RETFound with the image encoder of the medical vision-language model MedSigLIP, separating candidate fusion, routing safety and predictive review. Calibrated product-of-experts (PoE) fusion is the static reference, a small gate proposes an alternative for each image, a validation-frozen harm guard decides whether that candidate may replace the reference, and a separate score prioritizes review. Static PoE reached the highest or joint-highest clean accuracy on all three datasets. At a clean 2% accepted-error target the review policy accepted 94.3% of Kermany-4 images at 99.89% selective accuracy, and 92.4% of OCT-C8 at 98.65%. Both branches were correct on 95.0% and 91.8% of clean images; only 4.4% and 6.2% had exactly one correct branch. Across 15 prespecified synthetic conditions, adaptive gains were concentrated in severe contrast reduction, where this routing opportunity widened roughly ninefold; percentile rescaling largely removed the effect, linking it to compressed dynamic range. In post-hoc validation-only analyses, gain was higher when the gate followed the more reliable branch, and a frozen guard cut the worst-condition OCT-C8 loss from 1.39 to 0.36 percentage points. Adaptive routing was useful when image quality created a reliability opportunity and the gate followed it; otherwise the system retained Static PoE or deferred for review. The findings are bounded by the synthetic perturbations evaluated and do not establish robustness to natural acquisition or scanner shift.

**comment/C1：** 4 figures, 4 tables, 42 references. Submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Main article only; the separate Supplementary Information is not included

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [Text-Guided Modality Correction for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22126v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22126

**作者：** Yu Quan、Dong Zhang、Ping Gong、Peng Huang、JinHui Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22126v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22126v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向开放词汇目标检测提出 TMCNet：用文本语义引导区域特征聚合，通过跨模态对齐模块强化区域表示与类别语义的对应，并用渐进式文本修正解码器自顶向下融合深层语义与浅层视觉特征，以兼顾不同尺度目标。在 COCO-OVD 与 LVIS-OVD 上验证，并扩展到开放词汇分割。

**相关性简评：** 这是检测任务方法而非 LVR 研究，但「文本引导的查询聚合＋跨层特征修正」可能可供核查 latent 状态如何在任务语义约束下保留空间细节、以及视觉证据筛选与多尺度融合的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Text-Guided Modality Correction for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection

**abstract/A1：** Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) is essential for detecting objects beyond the categories seen during training. Establishing vision-language associations is crucial for recognizing unseen categories, while integrating complementary visual features across levels supports detection at different scales. However, existing methods often provide insufficient textual guidance for object regions, rely on limited cross-modal alignment strategies, and underuse complementary features across levels, limiting their ability to detect unseen categories and objects of varying scales. To address these limitations, we propose a Text-Guided Modality Correction Network (TMCNet) that incorporates textual guidance into region feature extraction, cross-modal alignment, and multi-scale decoding to strengthen region-category semantic correspondence and promote complementary feature fusion across levels. First, a text-guided modality encoder incorporates textual semantics into queries to guide regional feature aggregation, reducing semantic ambiguity in object representations. Second, a cross-modal alignment module strengthens the correspondence between region representations and category semantics through interactions between visual and textual features. Finally, a progressive text correction decoder combines a top-down pathway with lateral connections to progressively fuse text-guided deep semantic features with shallow visual features at the corresponding scales, preserving both semantic information and spatial detail for objects of different sizes. Experiments on two public datasets, COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD, demonstrate that TMCNet achieves strong performance in open-vocabulary object detection using simple text queries as guidance. Extending TMCNet to open-vocabulary segmentation also yields performance gains, demonstrating its cross-task applicability and extensibility.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [TempQuant: Learning Adaptive Temperatures for Quantized Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22128v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22128

**作者：** Mukund Athmaram、Liang Ma、Manoj Rao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22128v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22128v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察量化如何改变下一 token 概率分布，测得量化平均上提高生成熵（“变暖”）且温度设置对量化模型远比全精度模型敏感，进而提出 TempQuant：以无标签方式学习量化模型的温度与 top-p（全局按请求温度一组、以及逐 token 一组），使输出分布贴近全精度模型。

**相关性简评：** 其逐 token 校准采样参数以对齐参考模型分布、并用熵与排序翻转定位需干预位置的做法，可能为 LVR 中受扰 latent 状态的逐位置自适应校正与分布漂移诊断提供方法线索，但需核查该机制只作用于输出端采样，尚未触及内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TempQuant: Learning Adaptive Temperatures for Quantized Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are increasingly deployed at low precision, yet quantization can degrade their performance. We study how quantization interacts with decoding settings such as the sampling temperature and top-p by examining how it distorts the model's next-token probabilities. Measuring entropy during generation across deployed temperatures, we find that quantization warms the model on average, and that temperature matters much more for the performance of a quantized model than for its full-precision counterpart. Because quantization and temperature both shape the next-token distribution, we investigate how much the sampling settings can undo what quantization changes, through our method TempQuant, which learns the quantized model's temperature and top-p to bring its next-token probabilities closer to the full-precision model's. We learn these settings at two granularities. The first is a single global setting per requested temperature, which finds nearly the same temperatures that tuning on labeled answers would, without any labels. Model cards typically recommend sampling a quantized model with the same settings as its full-precision counterpart, while we find that different settings can substantially improve its accuracy. On 3-bit Qwen3-4B, for example, global TempQuant raises MATH accuracy from 34% under the configuration recommended for Qwen's quantized releases to 51%. The second sets the temperature and top-p for every token, motivated by our finding that tokens whose rankings flip under quantization tend to need warming, while tokens whose rankings are preserved tend to need cooling. When quantization degrades accuracy substantially, TempQuant recovers much of it, and when degradation is lighter, it still brings the quantized model's behavior closer to the full-precision model's.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [LoCaR: Localized Canvas Retrieval With Frozen Vision-Language Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22131v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22131

**作者：** Rahim Hossain、Dipanwita Saha、Ashirbad Mishra、Xinxin Shu、Svetlana Strunjas、Binbin Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22131v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22131v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的局部文本到图像检索框架 LoCaR：用 SAM2 生成对象级区域提议并以冻结视觉编码器独立编码，探索基于匹配文本区域样例的残差适配，同时发布 LOCFG 基准。

**相关性简评：** 区域级独立编码以保留小物体和局部属性的思路，可能为 LVR 的视觉细节保持与证据筛选提供线索，但其检索设定与 latent 推理状态生成仍有距离，可供核查区域表示如何进入推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoCaR: Localized Canvas Retrieval With Frozen Vision-Language Model

**abstract/A1：** Whole image embeddings can under represent small objects and local attributes that distinguish relevant images for localized queries. We present LoCaR, a training free framework for localized text to image retrieval with frozen vision language models. LoCaR constructs and utilizes independent proposals (SAM2) focusing on the object so as to get a more localized representation that independently encodes each region using the frozen visual encoder. We further study paired-support adaptation as an optional residual that uses matched text region exemplars without updating the underlying encoders. We also introduce LOCFG, a Visual Genome derived benchmark designed to evaluate localized retrieval for smaller and more challenging referents. Across several vision language backbones, our results show that improving the region representation itself is the main source of gain.

**comment/C1：** Zero-shot retrieval , Local Retrieval

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Long-Context Generation Is a Sampling Problem (When You Turn Up the Heat)](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22148v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22148

**作者：** Allen G. Roush、Minh Nhat Nguyen、Ravid Shwartz-Ziv、Judah Goldfeder、Sanjay Basu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22148v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22148v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 29 个开放权重模型和十项基准（含长链推理与编码 agent）上比较采样器在升温下的表现，发现候选池大小决定长生成是否退化，并提出 top-nsigma 等保持小候选池的策略。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本、无视觉或 latent 状态，但其「采样候选池规模随温度膨胀导致长生成崩溃」的机制可能可供核查 LVR 中 AR 推理轨迹的随机性与状态漂移，以及长生成长度泛化的成因。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Long-Context Generation Is a Sampling Problem (When You Turn Up the Heat)

**abstract/A1：** Open-weight language models are widely observed to get worse the longer they generate. Often, attempts to fix this involve more training. At high temperature, The sampler, i.e. the rule that picks the next token out of the model's probabilities, decides whether long and agentic generation survives and/or thrives. We test 29 open-weight models from 1B to 122B parameters on ten established benchmarks plus needle retrieval out to one million tokens, covering SWE-bench Verified coding agents, long chain-of-thought reasoning, long-form writing, long structured output and long-context reading. Samplers that keep their pool of candidate tokens small and steady as temperature rises don't lose accuracy. Samplers that let in low-probability tokens fall apart. At temperature 2, coding agents running top-p, top-k or Top-H resolve 0 of 100 GitHub issues, while the same agents running top-nsigma resolve 46 to 62 and win every issue the two samplers disagree on, on three Qwen3.5 models. Over all benchmarks top-nsigma keeps a median 100% of its temperature-1 score where top-p keeps 26%. Counting how many tokens each sampler lets through explains why. In one Llama-3.1-8B story, going from temperature 1 to 2 grows top-p's pool from 4 tokens to 51,845 while top-nsigma stays at 3. Across 133 combinations of sampler setting and temperature, the typical pool size predicts judged quality (Spearman correlation of -0.77), and when we pick the pool at temperature 1 and only apply temperature afterwards, Top-H, min-p and p-less stop collapsing. At the default temperature of 1 most samplers perform about the same, We release all code and data to the public.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, accepted to the 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Track on Evaluations and Datasets

</details>

### 84. [EgoHandWAM: Probing the Role of Hand Motion in Egocentric World Action Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22151v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22151

**作者：** Kailin Wang、Haoxiang Jie、Yaoyuan Yan、Zhiyou Heng、Zhaosong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22151v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22151v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作从当前图像与近期手部状态预测三维腕部/指尖轨迹及未来 DINOv2 视觉特征，在 EgoDex 大规模窗口上训练，并通过打乱手部历史、掩码消融等探针检验手部运动信息对未来视觉预测的实际贡献。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测未来视觉特征＋条件信息消融」的设置可供核查 LVR 中隐状态是否真正吸收特定证据（如打乱历史即抹去增益这类因果式检验），但任务为自中心世界建模，与推理期 latent 状态的关系需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EgoHandWAM: Probing the Role of Hand Motion in Egocentric World Action Modeling

**abstract/A1：** How much does hand motion tell us about what a first-person camera will see next? EgoHandWAM forecasts three-dimensional wrist and fingertip trajectories and a future visual feature from the current image and recent hand states. We train on 751,065 EgoDex windows using a frozen DINOv2 encoder. On held-out development sessions, the action-only model reduces two-second wrist endpoint error from 60.26 to 56.48~mm relative to a stationary forecast. It still underestimates long reaches. Adding future-feature supervision gives nearly identical trajectory accuracy. With the recorded hand history, the visual head reduces smooth L1 error by 1.32\\% relative to copying the current feature. Shuffling histories across sessions erases this gain. In a separate four-task probe, adding recorded future displacements to observed context and future tracking-validity masks reduces visual-feature error by 3.93\\%. Masks alone give little improvement. A trajectory predicted from the same observations adds little. On two simulated RoboTwin tasks, substituting aligned human examples for an auxiliary batch of robot examples gives no consistent increase in task success across three training seeds.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Unsupervised Multi-modal Medical Image Registration via Invertible Translation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22152v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22152

**作者：** Mengjie Guo、Jinming Duan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22152v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22152v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 INNReg：用基于可逆神经网络（含动态深度卷积局部注意力）的图像翻译网络把多模态医学图像转成单模态表示，再由配准网络对齐，并设计基于归一化互信息的 barrier loss 约束配准。

**相关性简评：** 其用可逆网络保证翻译前后几何一致，可能为核查 latent 变换中几何与视觉细节是否保真提供线索；但任务本身是医学图像配准，与 VLM 推理主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unsupervised Multi-modal Medical Image Registration via Invertible Translation

**abstract/A1：** In medical imaging, the alignment of multi-modal images plays a critical role in providing comprehensive information for image-guided therapies. Despite its importance, multi-modal image registration poses significant challenges due to the complex and often unknown spatial relationships between different image modalities. To address this, we introduce a novel unsupervised translation-based multi-modal registration method, termed Invertible Neural Network-based Registration (INNReg). INNReg consists of an image-to-image translation network that converts multi-modal images into mono-modal counterparts and a registration network that uses the translated mono-modal images to align the multi-modal images. Specifically, to ensure the preservation of geometric consistency after image translation, we introduce an Invertible Neural Network (INN) that leverages a dynamic depthwise convolution-based local attention mechanism. Additionally, we design a novel barrier loss function based on Normalized Mutual Information to impose constraints on the registration network, which enhances the registration accuracy. The superior performance of INNReg is demonstrated through experiments on two public multi-modal medical image datasets, including MRI T1/T2 and MRI/CT pairs. The code is available at https://github.com/MeggieGuo/INNReg

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [Centroid Spectral Coupling: Implicit Alignment and Representation Diversity in Self-Supervised Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22158v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22158

**作者：** M. Hadi Sepanj、Benyamin Ghojogh、Saed Moradi、Paul Fieguth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22158v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22158v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Centroid Spectral Coupling（CSC），用基于质心未中心化二阶矩的单一 ridge 谱目标同时实现视图对齐、表示多样性与防坍缩，并给出精确分解、全局最优几何与有限损失下对齐/多样性保证，在 CIFAR-10/100 与 Imagenette 上取得有竞争力的线性探针与 kNN 精度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接多模态推理关联，但其「对齐—多样性」单目标权衡、有效秩随参数下降的观察及常数输出坍缩为严格鞍点的分析，可供核查 LVR latent 状态训练中的信息坍缩与多样性监控是否可借用类似谱指标。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Centroid Spectral Coupling: Implicit Alignment and Representation Diversity in Self-Supervised Learning

**abstract/A1：** Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) aims to align augmented views while preserving representation diversity and preventing collapse. Existing non-contrastive methods often address these goals using multiple weighted loss terms. We introduce Centroid Spectral Coupling (CSC), a single-term SSL objective that implicitly couples view alignment, representation diversity, and collapse resistance. CSC constructs centroids from independently normalized augmented-view pairs and applies a ridge-based spectral objective to their uncentered second-moment matrix. Preserving centroid magnitudes encodes view agreement while encouraging balanced representation energy across spectral directions. We establish an exact decomposition into nonnegative alignment and spectral-imbalance components, characterize the geometry of global optima, and derive finite-loss guarantees for alignment and diversity. We also show that complete constant-output collapse is a strict saddle in normalized representation space. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Imagenette show that CSC achieves the highest linear Top-1 accuracy among the evaluated non-contrastive methods on all three benchmarks, and the highest kNN Top-1 accuracy on both CIFAR datasets. A ridge-parameter sensitivity study reveals an empirical alignment--diversity trade-off: increasing $κ$ strengthens view alignment but reduces representation effective rank. These results show that a unified spectral objective yields competitive visual representations without explicit contrastive negatives or separately weighted alignment and diversity penalties.

</details>

### 87. [What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22161v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22161

**作者：** Sushant Mehta、Edwin Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22161v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22161v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 用 CISPO 与 rank-32 LoRA 在五个专家构建任务集（软件工程、复杂约束、长程工具使用、图表理解、企业文档）上分别对 Nemotron 3.5 Super 做无监督阶段的 RL 后训练，报告十二个基准的增益，并对比各适配器相对基座的行为变化。

**相关性简评：** 其图表与文档子任务及失败模式分析显示模型更常先取数据与规则再行动、用代码量取图中标记，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据获取与「LOOK」步骤及监督设计的线索，但论文不涉及内部 latent 状态或表示层面的分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super

**abstract/A1：** We post-train NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP\_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model's on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model's failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench's spreadsheets.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 2 figures, 9 tables

</details>
