### 88. [HandRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Hand Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22164v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22164

**作者：** Zhekai Wang、Luqing Luo、Wenhao Yao、Junjie Lu、Huanan Zhu、Feifei Gao、Yuhua Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22164v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22164v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HandRSI，让编码智能体迭代改写手部姿态估计系统的推理 harness，接受标准是所有十项基准指标在留出数据上整体提升且无显著退化，再用被接受的 harness 通过选择性蒸馏更新估计器，在 HOT3D、ARCTIC 上降低误差与抖动。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但其在无 GT 帧上监督「手存在性与时序一致性」的选择性蒸馏，以及以多指标不退化作为接受准则的递归自改进，可能为 latent 轨迹的弱监督信号与贡献分配提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HandRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Hand Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Egocentric 3D hand pose estimators still miss hands at the image border, misplace occluded hands and jitter. Such failures are usually fixed by hand-written inference refinements and by fine-tuning, two interdependent steps that are tuned separately. We present HandRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework for a hand pose system that consists of a video estimator and its executable inference harness. In each round, coding agents refine the harness, and a change is accepted only if it improves a composite of all ten benchmark metrics on held-out data without significantly regressing any of them. The accepted harness then guides the estimator through selective distillation, which supervises hand presence and temporal consistency on frames without ground truth, while ground truth supervises pose. Since the updated estimator may change which refinements remain useful, the harness is re-validated on it. Starting from the released ACE-Ego-Hand model, five rounds reduce MPJPE-p by 35% on HOT3D and 11% on ARCTIC and roughly halve jitter. On HOT3D, HandRSI's MPJPE-p is 16% lower than that of ACE-Ego-Hand fine-tuned on the same ground truth. Under a unified protocol, it outperforms nine re-run published methods on all ten metrics of both benchmarks. Hypervolume selection on the accuracy-speed front yields Lite, Medium and Heavy systems running at 20.7 to 41.1 FPS. This report gives the full method, every stage of every round and the complete protocol. Code, evaluator, harnesses and weights are public.

**comment/C1：** Technical report. 25 pages, 17 figures, 18 tables. Code: https://github.com/GinKenneth/Hand\_RSI

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [Towards Optimism-Pessimism Trade-off in Model-based Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22172v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22172

**作者：** Guochen Zhou、Yijun Yang、Qiqi Duan、Qing Su、Weiming Ou、Li Shen、Shihao Ji、Yuhui Shi、Zhisong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22172v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22172v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 model-based 离线到在线 RL 中乐观—悲观权衡的分布偏移问题，提出双目标形式化，用 MOSAIC 在离线阶段构建 Pareto 策略池，并在在线微调时用分层 contextual bandit 逐步选择策略（HiP3）。

**相关性简评：** 其「离线构建多样 Pareto 策略池＋按交互步分层 bandit 选择」的机制，可能为 LVR 中维护多个 latent 假设或推理分支、并在每步决定选用哪一状态继续（THINK/LOOK/STOP 路由）提供可核查的借鉴线索，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Optimism-Pessimism Trade-off in Model-based Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Model-based offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) enables sample-efficient adaptation by leveraging offline pre-training for online fine-tuning. However, the distribution shifts between offline and online stages often hinder fine-tuning performance. Many existing methods encode the optimism-pessimism trade-off through a single-objective formulation. Selecting its trade-off coefficient through online fine-tuning can require costly bi-level optimization. We identify the balance established during offline training as a key challenge: in our motivating experiments, optimistic policies adapt better to novel tasks, whereas pessimistic policies perform better on the same online tasks. To address this challenge, we propose a bi-objective formulation that captures this trade-off, yielding a Pareto policy pool during offline training. This policy pool enables adaptive selection during online fine-tuning across diverse online tasks. To generate the pool, we introduce Multi-Objective Soft Actor-critIC (MOSAIC), which solves bi-objective problems and constructs diverse Pareto policies. After offline training, a contextual bandit algorithm hierarchically selects the most suitable policy for fine-tuning at each online interaction step. Empirically, our pipeline, Hierarchical Pareto Policy Pool (HiP3), achieves the strongest aggregate performance across both same and novel online tasks and maintains its advantage under stronger distribution shifts. Comprehensive ablations further clarify the roles of the policy pool, MOSAIC-based pool construction, and the online selection mechanism.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [RAG in a Trenchcoat: When Minimal Memory Is Enough for Agentic Systems, and When It Isn't](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22179v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22179

**作者：** Jingyu Liu、Zongze Li、Zach Xu、Zhanhui Zhou、Tahseen Rabbani、Dawn Song、Ce Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22179v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22179v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出极简 RAG 记忆 BRIDGE（链式观察笔记、BM25 与余弦混合检索、按时间顺序渲染），在 LongMemEval-S、BEAM 上与既有 SOTA 持平、在 EverMemBench 上领先，并用五个对抗探针考察记忆是否真正被 agent 用于执行规则或模式。

**相关性简评：** 其核心区分「记忆检索到什么」与「agent 能否据此行动」，以及仅在生成时判定的探针式评估，可供核查 latent 视觉内容是否被答案实际使用、而非仅被表征保存。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RAG in a Trenchcoat: When Minimal Memory Is Enough for Agentic Systems, and When It Isn't

**abstract/A1：** Agentic memory research has accumulated extraction, supersession, reflection, and graph synthesis on top of retrieval-augmented generation. However, we found that almost none of those primitives earn their cost in our tested memory benchmarks: we show this with BRIDGE, a deliberately minimal RAG memory with chained observation notes, hybrid BM25 with cosine retrieval, and chronological rendering, which reaches parity within bootstrap CI with the prior SOTA on LongMemEval-S and BEAM, and beats the best memory baseline on EverMemBench by 5.7-6.3 points at matched reader. It runs at ~5-7K reader-prompt tokens per query, using a single LLM call for each ingestion or query. We argue that this parity is itself the result: standard benchmarks score what memory retrieves, not what memory enables when the agent acts. To test the latter, we introduce five adversarial probes (Rule Application +-, Pattern Induction Style / Procedural, Dynamic Multiturn) judged at generation time by a cross vendor verification chain, where each query can only be solved by applying a remembered rule or pattern it never mentions. Across 5 probes x 20 scenarios, no architecture wins universally: atomic fact extractors (LangMem) win rule application, while BRIDGE's block-summary memory wins pattern induction. This result holds across four domains, three graders, and three reader families. Each design fails where the other wins, for reasons a recall benchmark cannot see: atomic-fact extractors discard the form a demonstrated pattern takes, while block-summary memory retrieves the rule but the reader does not act on it, a gap that presenting the rule more prominently largely closes. What separates memory designs is not what they retrieve but what they enable an agent to do, and that is what agentic memory should be evaluated on.

**comment/C1：** 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026), Main Conference

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [SRED: Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation Across Magnifications in Whole-Slide Imaging](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22189

**作者：** Zhiyuan Yang、Jiahao Cheng、Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh、Mahdi S. Hosseini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22189v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 SRED，用稀疏自编码器从 20x 高倍特征中学习事件字典，再以逻辑 OR 池化把空间对齐 patch 的事件存在性汇总为 5x 区域级目标，从而在无切片级标签条件下微调 5x 编码器，提升分类与生存预测并保持接近原生 5x 的运行时间。

**相关性简评：** 其「对齐区域内的 OR 池化证据汇总 + 稀疏事件字典作为可解释监督目标」可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、跨区域证据聚合与 latent 状态可解释性提供可核查的方法线索，但领域为病理影像，与 VLM 推理的迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SRED: Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation Across Magnifications in Whole-Slide Imaging

**abstract/A1：** In computational pathology, high-magnification whole-slide image analysis preserves tissue detail but requires encoding many patches, whereas lower magnification reduces computation by covering a higher field of view per patch while sacrificing detail. Existing cross-magnification distillation matches features that often encode detail unreachable by low-magnification input, sometimes requiring slide-level labels that limit scalability. To address those issues, we introduce Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation (SRED), which transfers regional event occurrences that encode reachable high-level interactions between details without slide-level labels. We use a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to learn an event dictionary from 20x teacher features. Logical OR pooling records whether each event is detected in any spatially aligned patch, retaining both localized and shared evidence within the corresponding 5x region. A pretrained 5x encoder is fine-tuned to predict these regional targets without slide-level labels. Visual analysis identifies dictionary components responsive to recognizable histological patterns, supporting target interpretation. Compared with native 5x features, SRED improves average macro-F1 by 1.1 points across seven classification and grading cohorts and average C-index by 1.7 points across five survival cohorts. Runtime matches native 5x, with an average 11.58x speedup over 20x.

**comment/C1：** CVPR 2027 submission

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [MIRAGE: Motion Identity Reduction via Autoregressive Generative Encoding for Privacy-Preserving Skeleton-based Motion Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22200v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22200

**作者：** Thomas Carr、Xujun Che、Depeng Xu、Shuhan Yuan、Aidong Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22200v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22200v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MIRAGE，一个无需目标骨架的因果骨骼动作匿名器：用统一因果（可选窗口）掩码的 rotary 位置 encoder-decoder 注意力对历史帧建模，为每帧预测降低身份信息的残差，并借 KV cache 逐帧流式运行；在 NTU RGB+D 60 上以自适应攻击者为主评估动作识别保留与重识别率下降。

**相关性简评：** 其因果注意力加 KV cache 的逐帧状态更新、窗口长度与状态随流长增长的取舍，可为 LVR 中 latent 状态缓存、窗口设计与长度泛化提供可供核查的工程线索；在原始坐标空间叠加残差而非替换输入的做法，也或可类比视觉旁路的信息保留问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MIRAGE: Motion Identity Reduction via Autoregressive Generative Encoding for Privacy-Preserving Skeleton-based Motion Data

**abstract/A1：** Skeleton motion identifies people through body proportions and movement style, and skeleton streams are increasingly passed to third-party analytics that run action classifiers trained on raw data. A prior adversarial anonymizer adds a per-frame residual in the raw coordinate space but sees one frame at a time; motion retargeting needs a target skeleton and complete sequences, which rules out online use. We present MIRAGE, a target-free, causal anonymizer that adds an identity-reducing residual to each incoming frame in the raw coordinate space, computed by attention over past frames. One causal, optionally windowed mask governs every attention in a rotary-position encoder-decoder, and key/value caches let the trained function run frame by frame; on all 56,578 NTU 60 sequences, batched and streaming outputs differ by at most 7.2e-6 m. Reported results use an unbounded window, so per-stream state grows with stream length. We evaluate against a passive adversary that applies raw-trained identity models and an adaptive adversary that trains on the anonymizer's output, treating the adaptive adversary as the headline, on NTU RGB+D 60 over five generator seeds, alongside the per-frame residual anonymizer of Moon et al. trained identically. When consumers retrain on the released data, MIRAGE keeps 83.9% action recognition (raw 89.1%; Moon et al. 88.7%) while a retrained identity classifier reaches 43.4% re-identification (RI) over 40 subjects, against 69.4% for Moon et al. and 75.4% on raw data. The strongest adaptive attacker still reaches 49.3% (chance 2.5%), so MIRAGE reduces identity leakage; it does not anonymize. Against raw-trained (frozen) classifiers, MIRAGE lowers RI below the strongest retargeting baseline (10.36% vs. 22.09%), but frozen action recognition falls to 37.04% (vs. 75.82%), so at full residual scale it is not a drop-in replacement for raw input to existing frozen models.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 12 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [Communicate the Change: Efficient Expert Parallelism via Activation Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22215v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22215

**作者：** Fengyuan Liu、Jay Gala、Siva Reddy、Dzmitry Bahdanau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22215v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22215v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EchoMoE：以早期层隐藏状态为参考、仅同步传输压缩后的差分，并周期性刷新参考状态，从而降低 MoE 专家并行中的通信量，仅需少量微调即可加装到已有模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「残差流逐层增量更新」观察与隐藏状态差分压缩、量化容忍，可能为 LVR 中跨层 latent 状态的缓存与传递压缩提供方法线索，可供核查压缩误差是否会损害推理所需细节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Communicate the Change: Efficient Expert Parallelism via Activation Compression

**abstract/A1：** Scaling has driven the success of large language models (LLM), with sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures enabling greater parameter capacity without a proportional increase in computation. At large scales, MoE models rely on expert parallelism to distribute experts across devices. Each MoE layer requires two synchronous collective communication steps during inference: dispatching hidden states to the activated experts and combining the resulting expert outputs. As compute throughput has outpaced interconnect bandwidth, these synchronous collectives have become a major bottleneck to MoE scaling and efficiency. We address this challenge with EchoMoE, a lightweight architecture modification that retrofits existing MoE models, requiring approximately 8,500x fewer training tokens than full pretraining. Motivated by the observation that transformer blocks update the residual stream incrementally, we use a hidden state from an earlier layer as a reference and communicate only the compressed difference between it and the current state, rather than communicating each hidden state in full. EchoMoE periodically refreshes these reference states and transmits only the compressed differences synchronously, reducing the traffic on latency-critical paths without significant computational overhead. EchoMoE reduces inter-node communication volume by 58% and improves throughput by 22.6%, while preserving average performance within 3.8 percentage points across 18 benchmarks. EchoMoE further improves inference throughput by up to 2.38x over bandwidth-constrained Ethernet, paving the way for wide-area distributed inference. EchoMoE also tolerates quantization of the communicated representations without measurable quality loss. We release an efficient implementation for applying EchoMoE to pretrained MoE models, together with an inference integration for vLLM.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Learning Optimal Transport Plans Via Autoregressive Token Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22218

**作者：** Ivan Jaen Marquez、Sotirios Panagiotis Chytas、Vikas Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22218v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ToR-OT，将离散最优传输问题重构为结构化序列生成任务，用 transformer 自回归地预测稀疏传输方案的非零项，并考察零样本泛化与摊销推理能力。

**相关性简评：** 其按稀疏结构逐项解码、摊销推断及跨成本度量泛化的自回归生成方式，可能为 LVR 中推理轨迹或计划状态的生成与稀疏监督提供可核查的方法线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Optimal Transport Plans Via Autoregressive Token Regression

**abstract/A1：** Optimal Transport (OT) provides a principled framework for finding the minimum cost coupling between probability distributions. Classical solvers remain the gold standard for this task. At the same time, OT is an ideal testbed for studying whether learning-based amortized models can internalize the structure of OT type problems. Here, ground-truth solutions are available and marginal constraints are explicitly defined. Also, exact discrete solutions have well-characterized sparsity. We describe ToR-OT, a transformer-based model that learns to predict discrete OT plans through autoregressive token regression. We reframe OT into a structured sequence generation task. ToR-OT decodes sparse transport plans as sequences instead of solving an optimization task at test time. We focus on three properties: (a) Zero-shot generalization: pretraining on a large synthetic corpus of OT instances allows a single model to generalize to unseen distribution pairs and heterogeneous cost metrics without retraining, (b) Sparse primal decoding: by exploiting the inherent sparsity in exact discrete OT solutions, the decoder predicts only nonzero entries (avoids dense coupling outputs), (c) Amortized inference: on low-dimensional discrete problems, ToR-OT gives accurate approximate plans and compares favorably with per-instance neural OT baselines. Overall, ToR-OT is not yet a replacement for classical solvers, but a step toward understanding how autoregressive transformers can learn structure of constrained optimization problems like OT.

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [ExplicitIR: Explicit Spatial Policy Fields for All-in-One Image Restoration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22222v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22222

**作者：** Dezhi Zhang、Shangrong Yang、Yudong Liang、Wenjian Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22222v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22222v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ExplicitIR 面向「一体化」图像复原，把复原策略显式表示为两张逐像素场：决定更新位置与强度的 Budget 场，以及选择窄/宽上下文窗口的 Style 场，由空间策略估计器预测、再由策略自适应解码块融合两种窗口响应，实现无需任务标签或测试时退化估计的单次前向空间自适应复原。

**相关性简评：** 其「把隐式编码在特征中的决策改为显式、可独立控制的逐位置场」这一做法，可能为 LVR 中按位置分配更新强度与注意力/上下文跨度的动态计算提供可核查的方法线索，但属于低层图像复原，与推理状态和证据筛选无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ExplicitIR: Explicit Spatial Policy Fields for All-in-One Image Restoration

**abstract/A1：** All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images blindly from inputs with various degradation types and levels using a single model. However, the adaptation policy in most existing methods remains implicitly encoded in feature representations, leaving the restoration decision neither explicitly represented nor independently controllable. Since real-world degradations vary spatially, applying uniform updates across the entire image leads to over-smoothing of clean areas and under-correction of localized degradations. To address these limitations, we present ExplicitIR, which represents the restoration policy as two explicit per-pixel fields: Budget, which determines where and how strongly to update, and Style, which selects between narrow and wide context windows. Specifically, ExplicitIR incorporates a Spatial Policy Estimator (SPE) to predict these fields, explicitly representing the restoration policy and capturing spatially-varying degradations, which are then applied by Policy-Adaptive Decoder Blocks (PADB) through adaptive fusion of narrow- and wide-window responses, enabling independent control and precise local modulation. This design enables spatially-adaptive restoration in a single forward pass, without requiring task labels, prompts, or test-time degradation estimators. Extensive experiments on three-task and five-task benchmarks demonstrate that ExplicitIR achieves performance competitive with the strongest all-in-one baselines.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [Spatially local mini-batching for implicit neural representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22236v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22236

**作者：** Minseok Choi、Swarup Dhar、Heejae Kwon、Kookjin Lee、Donsub Rim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22236v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22236v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究低秩隐式神经表示（LRNR，由共享基与小型超网络输出的系数相乘构成）能否改用空间局部 mini-batch 训练，每步只取自适应网格单个单元内的快照点，并以 Kolmogorov 宽度论证局部隐状态落在低维子空间，在六个双曲守恒律问题上报告精度与参数效率。

**相关性简评：** 其「共享基＋每层少量系数」使局部更新仅经由低维子空间触及共享基的结构，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的局部更新、避免单步更新改写共享表示提供核查线索；但研究对象是 PDE 代理建模，与视觉推理无直接证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spatially local mini-batching for implicit neural representations

**abstract/A1：** Neural surrogates based on implicit neural representations (INRs) are typically trained on mini-batches spread over the whole domain, since a generic INR loses accuracy when every point of a batch lies in one small region. We show that low-rank neural representations (LRNRs), INRs whose weight matrices are products of shared bases with coefficients emitted by a small hypernetwork, can be trained with spatially local mini-batches. Each step uses the points of one time snapshot of an adaptive-mesh numerical solution inside one small cell, with snapshots and cells drawn according to the refinement of the mesh. We posit that such training is enabled by the structural property of LRNRs, as a local step reaches the shared bases only through the $r$ coefficients of each layer, and keeps one cell from rewriting the network. By a Kolmogorov-width argument, we show that when restricted to a small cell, the hidden states of LRNRs lie close to a low-dimensional subspace, which suggests the local steps only affect low-rank subspaces of the shared bases. On six hyperbolic-conservation-law problems with solutions computed using adaptive mesh refinement, one local training recipe reaches 1.1-2.3% relative $L^2$ error with a tenth of the parameters of a dense network of the same width. Finally, we provide a comparison by training a hypernetwork-SIREN, a DeepONet, and a Fourier neural operator (FNO) of the same size with the same local batches and training budget. We observe that these baselines reach 16-78% where the LRNR reaches 4.9%. Interestingly, we also observe that local training has a significant effect on the per-cell low-dimensional structure even in a wide generic hypernetwork-SIREN.

**comment/C1：** 72 pages, 36 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [PhyVGGT: Feed-Forward Articulated Object Reconstruction from Monocular Videos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22244v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22244

**作者：** Qi Shao、Zhihao Cao、Shuhao Zhai、Anh Nguyen、Hong Liang、Baoru Huang、Liang Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22244v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22244v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PhyVGGT，在冻结的 3D 基础模型上单次前向地从无标定单目视频重建铰接物体：用部件与运动解码器融合中间层 token 与全局 token 分割三维可动部件，并以 Bingham 先验加闭式求解估计关节轴与铰点，仅用合成数据训练。

**相关性简评：** 文中诊断深层跨帧注意力会模糊可动部件边界、低估沿视线方向运动，并改用中间层 token 加静态基座几何锚点来缓解，这可能为 LVR 中如何把视觉细节与任务必要几何保留在内部状态、以及注意力结构对表示的影响提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhyVGGT: Feed-Forward Articulated Object Reconstruction from Monocular Videos

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing an articulated object with its 3D movable parts, joint parameters and unseen articulation states from an uncalibrated monocular video is essential for robot manipulation and interactive digital twins. Existing per-scene optimisation methods require minutes to hours and external depth or camera poses, whereas feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct dense point maps in one pass without segmenting movable parts or inferring joints. Even on these monocular point maps, regressing or fitting continuous joints directly fails on casual videos, since cross-frame attention blurs moving part boundaries in deep layers, underestimates motion along the viewing ray and leaves continuous axes ill-conditioned under small opening angles. To address these challenges, we propose PhyVGGT, a single-pass framework on a frozen 3D foundation model that anchors moving-part matching and closed-form joint estimation to the undistorted geometry of the stationary base. First, our Part and Motion Decoder fuses intermediate per-frame tokens with global tokens and references part queries to a static-base query to segment 3D parts, align point maps on the static base and predict learned Bingham priors. Second, our Kinematic Inference Layer fits an orthogonal Manhattan frame to static surface normals, selects the joint axis in closed form under a Bingham posterior combining pairwise motion scatter with the learned priors, and solves a linear system for the hinge pivot to drive unseen states. Trained solely on synthetic data, PhyVGGT achieves state-of-the-art monocular performance across five benchmarks, outperforming FAMOS and monocular ArtiPoint, surpassing posed RGB-D ArtiPoint in median error, and running in 6.8 s per 48-frame video against hours of per-scene optimisation in Articulat3D. Project page: https://phyvggt.github.io.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables. Project page: https://phyvggt.github.io

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [Multi-Agent Memory Inconsistency: Where Factual Conflicts Disappear in LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22245

**作者：** Hejia Zhang、Zhongming Yu、Yimin Ding、Wei-Ting Chen、Hengjia Yu、Wentao Ni、Yijun Liu、Cong Xu、Xin Chen、Matrix Yao、Fan Zhao、Jishen Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22245v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建多智能体记忆不一致的基准与「证据流」分析工具，在 Harvey LAB 文档审阅任务中注入受控事实冲突，测量冲突证据是否被检索、由 worker 上报并最终被 lead 检出。

**相关性简评：** 其证据流追踪与 handoff 损失度量可供核查，用于诊断视觉证据在中间状态或子模块交接中是否被丢弃，与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选和信息坍缩相关；但研究对象是文本多智能体，未涉及 latent 视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-Agent Memory Inconsistency: Where Factual Conflicts Disappear in LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Multi-agent delegation lets LLM systems distribute evidence collection across workers with separate contexts. This creates a consistency challenge: facts retrieved by workers may be missing from the evidence available to the lead for a joint conclusion. We introduce a benchmark and evidence-flow profiler for studying multi-agent memory inconsistency through controlled factual conflicts in Harvey LAB document-review tasks. Pooled across three models with comparable generation spending, requiring delegation increases retrieval of both conflicting claims from 50.2% to 68.7% relative to single-agent execution, yet conflict detection falls from 16.2% to 13.2%. Within this setting, workers report both claims for only 35.2% of retrieved pairs, and pooled matched comparisons show higher detection than single-agent execution when one worker reads both claims but lower detection when the claims are split across workers. Worker-to-lead handoffs are a central target for improving conflict detection in multi-agent LLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [PCGRL for Complex Platformers: Synthesizing Playable and Diverse Levels via Inflated Envelopes and Forward Curricula](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22250v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22250

**作者：** Di Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22250v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22250v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个物理感知、课程引导的 PCGRL 框架，用「Inflated Volume Envelope」把连续运动学可行性嵌入离散 MDP 状态空间，并用中间状态转移反馈、多阶段前向课程与高熵采样加事后并行拒绝过滤，在长序列平台关卡生成中兼顾可解性与多样性。

**相关性简评：** 其把几何/运动学可行约束注入离散状态表示、以及长决策序列上的中间反馈与课程式信用分配，可能为 LVR 中「几何与任务必要信息如何留在内部状态」及长轨迹贡献分配提供可核查线索；高熵采样加拒绝过滤亦与生成后验证和停止决策相关，但需判断能否迁移到多模态 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PCGRL for Complex Platformers: Synthesizing Playable and Diverse Levels via Inflated Envelopes and Forward Curricula

**abstract/A1：** Procedural Content Generation via Reinforcement Learning (PCGRL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for automated game development. However, existing PCGRL frameworks still suffer from severe exploration bottlenecks and scaling limitations when faced with long-sequence generation tasks governed by strict rigid-body rules and non-linear kinematic boundaries (e.g., platformer constraints). Consequently, traditional approaches frequently fail to maintain reliable level solvability on large-scale canvases. To address these challenges, we present a physics-aware, curriculum-guided PCGRL framework. First, we construct an "Inflated Volume Envelope" mechanism, which provides a physics-constrained representation that incorporates continuous kinematic feasibility into the discrete state space of the MDP. Second, to alleviate the sparse reward challenge induced by extremely long decision sequences, we introduce intermediate state-transition feedback and a multi-stage forward extension curriculum learning framework, enabling informative value estimation and stable credit assignment over long-horizon trajectories. Finally, to resolve the issue where deterministic policies after convergence generate highly monotonous map paths with a lack of diversity, we activate high-entropy stochastic inference during the synthesis phase, regularized by a post-hoc parallel rejection filtering mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly enhances structural diversity while maintaining high physical playability across multi-scale environments. Comparative evaluations across multi-scale canvases demonstrate that our framework maintains superior convergence stability and dimensional scalability, reliably synthesizing solvable levels where traditional PCGRL baselines degrade.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 6 figures, 1 table

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [DACE: Diversity-Driven Adversarial Co-Evolution for Robust LLM Safety Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22278v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22278

**作者：** Peng Yu、Xiaoyu Wen、Zhida He、Ziyuan Zhou、Han Qi、Shao Zhang、Qiaosheng Zhang、Ying Wen、Chaochao Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22278v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22278v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DACE，用攻击者侧二维策略空间与归一化边际覆盖增益奖励抑制策略坍缩，并用带时间衰减的 Beta–Bernoulli 贝叶斯回放池和 Thompson 采样缓解防御方遗忘，从而在安全对齐中实现多样性驱动的对抗共演化。

**相关性简评：** 其「策略层覆盖增益」与「不确定性加权的非平稳回放」线索，可能可供核查用于 latent 假设/推理轨迹训练中的多样性保持与灾难性遗忘抑制，但本文完全面向文本安全对齐，未见视觉或多模态推理验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DACE: Diversity-Driven Adversarial Co-Evolution for Robust LLM Safety Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Ensuring robust safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) is increasingly difficult as adversarial attacks evolve and outpace alignment pipelines built on pre-collected data. Existing co-evolutionary frameworks let the defender chase a moving attacker, yet their training dynamics expose two compounding pathologies: \\emph{attack strategy collapse}, where the attacker overfits to a narrow set of high-reward rewrites, and \\emph{defense adversarial forgetting}, where the defender loses competence against earlier attacks as the attack distribution drifts. Existing remedies are partial: attacker-side diversity rewards score textual novelty rather than strategy novelty, and defender-side replay relies on discrete judge scores that ignore estimation uncertainty, response stochasticity, and defender non-stationarity. We introduce \\textbf{\\method}, a diversity-driven adversarial co-evolution framework that targets both pathologies jointly. On the attacker side, \\method couples an explicit two-dimensional strategy space (risk category $\\times$ attack style) with a \\emph{normalized marginal coverage gain} reward, providing a bounded, non-vanishing exploration signal at the strategy layer. On the defender side, \\method maintains a unified \\emph{Bayesian adversarial replay pool} whose Beta--Bernoulli threat posteriors are refreshed by time decay and sampled via Thompson sampling, anchoring defender training to an evolving threat landscape. Across standard safety, automated-attacker, and general-capability benchmarks, \\method improves robustness to out-of-distribution attacks while preserving general capabilities, and yields broader attacker strategy coverage.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Learning Regions of Attraction for Transverse Feedback Linearization under Controller Singularities](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22286v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22286

**作者：** Abdullah Waqar Malik、Hamza Tariq、Adeel Akhtar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22286v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22286v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究横向反馈线性化（TFL）控制器在奇异集上无定义时的受约束吸引域，即能安全激活控制器并收敛到路径的初始状态集合；作者将状态约束下的 Zubov 值函数分解为解析已知的二次 cost-to-go 与待学习的安全值函数，用无需无限时域轨迹标签的拟合值迭代学习，并以区间分支定界验证的内层加采样测试的外层构成估计。

**相关性简评：** 其「控制器何时可安全启用」的可容许集刻画与学习式安全值函数，可能为 LVR 中 STOP／激活决策与 latent 状态有效性判断提供线索；「已验证内层＋学习外层」的认证结构与免轨迹标签的值迭代训练可供核查是否可迁移到 latent 推理状态的判定与监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Regions of Attraction for Transverse Feedback Linearization under Controller Singularities

**abstract/A1：** Transverse feedback linearization (TFL) achieves path following by exactly linearizing the error dynamics, but its controller is undefined on a singular set, such as zero speed for a unicycle or zero thrust for a quadrotor. The constrained region of attraction, the set of initial states from which the closed loop converges to the path without violating state or regularity constraints, determines where the controller can be safely activated. Exploiting the exact linear error dynamics, we show that this region is the largest forward invariant subset of the admissible set. We then decompose its state-constrained Zubov value function into an analytically known quadratic cost-to-go and a safety value function. We learn only the latter, by fitted value iteration without infinite-horizon trajectory labels. The resulting estimate combines an inner layer verified by interval branch and bound with a learned outer layer tested on samples. On a unicycle following a circle and a sinusoid and on a planar quadrotor, it covers on average 72.0%, 83.4% and 19.1% of the simulated region of attraction. In comparison, the quadratic cost-to-go and two physics-informed neural network (PINN) solvers of Zubov's equation cover at most 11.3%, 4.0% and 0.0% on average. Code and animations are available at https://gradslab.github.io/roa\_tfl

**comment/C1：** 27 pages (10 main + references + appendix), 1 figure, 8 tables

</details>

### 102. [Perception into Imagination: Executable Memory for World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22294v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22294

**作者：** Xianyao Li、Ruitong Tian、Rui Min、Fang Xu、Jing Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22294v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22294v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「可执行感知记忆」(EPM) 世界模型框架：在想象/开环 rollout 中让显式感知状态与神经 latent dynamics 并行演化，其读出用于奖励预测、价值估计与策略学习，并在 Memory Maze 与 MiniWorld 上验证控制收益。

**相关性简评：** 显式记忆状态与 latent 动态并行、读出驱动下游预测，以及把奖励输入对齐到产生它的转移并在开环 rollout 上监督读出，可能为 LVR 中「内部状态是否保留视外视觉细节」与轨迹监督/贡献分配提供核查线索，但属 RL 世界模型，迁移价值待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Perception into Imagination: Executable Memory for World Models

**abstract/A1：** Physical agents must use what they previously saw or touched after visibility or contact is lost. Their actions change the implications of these remembered facts. We introduce executable perceptual memory (EPM), a world-model framework that advances explicit perceptual state alongside neural latent dynamics during imagination. Its evolving read-out informs reward prediction, value estimation and policy learning. We align reward inputs with the transitions that produced them and supervise the reward head on open-loop rollouts of recorded actions. On Memory Maze $13\\times13$, EPM reaches 30.37 return at 1M interactions, compared with 23.37 when the same evolving read-out informs only policy and value on a common perceptual front end: a gain of 7.00 \[3.80, 10.21\]. The complete method also improves control on $9\\times9$ and in MiniWorld. Probes of remembered, out-of-view arrivals show stronger reward anticipation in both visual implementations. Concentration, tactile recovery and real tactile frames demonstrate complementary roles of remembered facts in imagined-policy training, motion-dependent decisions and sensory-content retention. EPM connects persistent perception to the predicted consequences and learning signals of a world model for Physical AI.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 6 figures, 15 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Streaming On-Policy Distillation: Decoupling Three Clocks for Stage-Wise Supervision Consumption](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22298v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22298

**作者：** Yun Zhu Da Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22298v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22298v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出流式 on-policy 蒸馏，把响应生成、监督投递与参数更新这三种粒度解耦，用一套消费协议组织监督预算、位置选择、陈旧度处理与更新调度，并在 GSM8K 上分别评估质量恢复与数据供给等待时间。

**相关性简评：** 其「按位置选择性投放监督、控制版本滞后与更新节奏」的协议，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的逐步贡献分配与监督粒度设计提供可核查线索，但纯文本蒸馏向多模态 latent 推理的迁移需自行判断，摘要中多数增益亦未确立稳定优势。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Streaming On-Policy Distillation: Decoupling Three Clocks for Stage-Wise Supervision Consumption

**abstract/A1：** In on-policy distillation (OPD), response completion, supervision delivery, and parameter updates need not share the same granularity. We develop streaming OPD by decoupling these three clocks: a response repeatedly delivers newly generated segments while generation continues, retaining the full causal prefix at each delivery and computing loss only on new positions. A consumption protocol then organizes supervision budgets, position selection, staleness handling, and update scheduling, allowing control over quantity, coverage, and version lag across training stages. In GSM8K experiments distilling Qwen3-4B into Qwen3-0.6B with VERL, supervised-budget matching reduces the early gap between best test accuracies over seven common candidates from 4.55 to 2.42 points. Synchronous continuation from the same checkpoint reaches 73.39% best test accuracy over seven common candidates at u=384,...,576, versus 74.30% synchronous and 71.95% budget-matched (differences -0.91 and -2.35 points); an unpaired extended record reaches 76.12%. Freshness filtering and disagreement-overlap selection improve local peaks but do not establish sustained advantages. A system prototype shows lower data-supply waiting times without establishing an equal-update speedup. Quality recovery and supply-side benefits are assessed separately; end-to-end gains must account for continuation cost.

**comment/C1：** 37 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [Harnesses That Improve Themselves: A Unifying Framework for Self-Improving Agent Harnesses](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22299v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22299

**作者：** Deep Shah、Nehal Kathrotia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22299v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22299v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出自改进 agent harness 的统一架构：四个可编辑运行时组件配以「诊断失败—提出代码补丁—验证候选—部署更新」四阶段循环，并在 SWE-bench Lite 上比较模块化门控演化与提示反思、整体重写。

**相关性简评：** 其发现的 pre-stop verifier、loop breaker 及验证恢复模块，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策与停止前证据核验提供借鉴；但工作属纯文本 agent 运行时工程，与视觉 latent 状态生成无直接联系，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Harnesses That Improve Themselves: A Unifying Framework for Self-Improving Agent Harnesses

**abstract/A1：** Whether a foundation model succeeds as an autonomous agent depends as much on its runtime software as on its weights. This runtime, the agent harness, manages context and memory, steers multi-turn control, executes tool actions in sandboxes, and verifies work before stopping. Because models differ in how they use tools and how they fail, a static harness tuned for one model or task often stalls, wastes tokens, or regresses on another. This bottleneck has driven a shift from prompt tuning to self-improving agent harnesses: systems that inspect their own execution traces, separate runtime bugs from model limits, and rewrite their own code. We present a unifying architectural and design framework for self-improving agent harnesses. The framework pairs four editable runtime components (context and state, agent control, action interfaces, and verification and recovery) with a four-stage loop that diagnoses failures, proposes code patches, validates candidates, and deploys updates. Using this framework, we show how optimizers trace errors across long trajectories and discover reusable runtime mechanisms such as pre-stop verifiers, loop breakers, and persistent memory. We further show why full-harness rewrites overfit or break across modules, and how modular updates with validation gates generalize across models while bounding token cost. To keep self-modification safe, we establish practical defenses against recurring failure modes including prompt bloat, benchmark gaming, and evaluator tampering. Finally, experiments on SWE-bench Lite validate this framework, showing that gated modular evolution outperforms prompt reflection and full-harness rewrites across models and repositories at a fraction of the token cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [Learning to Edit IFC Building Models: Verifiable Checkers as the Training Signal for Dependency-Aware Editing Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22307v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22307

**作者：** Jiaxin Zhang、Yao Luo、Yuze Dan、Yunqin Li、Bowen Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22307v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22307v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 IfcGym 代码编辑环境，用有效性、意图、依赖传播与无关实体保持四个 checker 的联合判定作为严格成功信号，将教师轨迹经 SFT 蒸馏到 Qwen3.5-9B 后再用 GRPO 优化，并在自建与公开 IFC 编辑基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「依赖需传播、无关需保持」的多检查器联合奖励与严格成功判据，可能为 LVR 中区分任务必要信息保留、无关信息不被改写以及监督与贡献分配提供可核查的奖励设计线索；但论文本身不涉及视觉 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Edit IFC Building Models: Verifiable Checkers as the Training Signal for Dependency-Aware Editing Agents

**abstract/A1：** Editing a building information model in the Industry Foundation Classes (IFC) schema requires propagating changes to dependent elements while preserving unrelated entities. Existing language-model agents for IFC editing use prompted models without learning from edit outcomes. Target-file comparisons do not specify which alternative edits are allowed or which unrelated entities must remain unchanged. We present IfcGym, a CodeAct environment in which a language model edits IFC through executable code and four checkers verify validity relative to the input model, intent, dependency and preservation against a task specification. Their conjunction is strict success. IfcEdit distils verified teacher episodes into a Qwen3.5-9B student through supervised fine-tuning (SFT), then applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with strict success as the reward. On IfcEditBench, 1,990 editing tasks on 13 held-out real projects, the final student reaches 86.78% strict success, from 79.15% after SFT. The paired confidence interval for the difference from its teacher includes zero; the same recipe on a 27B model reaches 91.76% and exceeds the teacher. On the public benchmarks BIM-Edit and BIBIMBAP the final student scores 55.19 and 54.50. On IfcEditBench, the students outperform all prompted models except the teacher under matched tool access; both evaluated frameworks also score lower. Rewarding all four checkers yields 4.83 points more strict success than rewarding intent and validity alone.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [VRLLM: Turning LLMs into Powerful Tabular Classifiers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22308v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22308

**作者：** Yingtong Dou、Shubham Jain、Junpeng Wang、Hadi Abdullah、Vineeth Rakesh Mohan、Yuzhong Chen、Liang Wang、Yiwei Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22308v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22308v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文报告在数十亿行带标注表格数据上微调 Llama 3.2 1B，得到在工业表格分类上超过多种树模型与自有生产深度模型的 VRLLM，并给出数据准备、训练配方、设计消融、部署验证及前沿 LLM 对比分析。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其按组件/FFN 层消融并用线性可解码性定位任务信息出现的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 表示随层深出现与贡献分配的问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VRLLM: Turning LLMs into Powerful Tabular Classifiers

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text, but whether pretrained LLMs are effective for large-scale tabular classification remains unclear, as many previous works demonstrate that LLMs' tabular data classification performance falls behind tree-based models or neural architectures designed for tabular data. At Visa Research, we managed to fine-tune a pretrained Llama 3.2 1B LLM on billions of rows of labeled tabular data and make it outperform a wide array of tabular classifiers and even our competitive production deep learning model. For ease of reference, we refer to the model as the Visa Research LLM (VRLLM). In this paper, we share how we achieved this exciting result, covering data preparation, training recipe, ablation studies of design choices and deployment validation. We also benchmark frontier LLMs on the same task and analyze their prediction behavior across model families and generations. Our interpretability experiments suggest that VRLLM learns feature-specific predictive associations, while component ablations indicate that the earliest two FFN layers contribute to the emergence of linearly decodable task information. Our paper provides an engineering recipe and a focused empirical case study on adapting compact LLMs to industrial tabular prediction tasks.

**comment/C1：** Technical Report. WIP

</details>

### 107. [Efficient Wide-Beam Generative Retrieval with Model-Native Trie Decoding and Shared-Context Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22309v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22309

**作者：** Vedant Jhaveri、Arashpreet Singh Mor、Satyam Kumar、Alex Tsun、Qing Lan、Shaunak Mishra、Sriram Vasudevan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22309v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22309v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向生成式检索的宽 beam 服务效率，提出 Trie-Head：将 SID trie 存为经校验的 GPU 张量实现模型内约束解码，避免 Python 约束回调与全词表候选缓冲，并用 shared-context attention 在 beam 间复用 prompt KV、保留各自私有解码尾，在 SGLang 中实现并报告吞吐提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「共享上下文 KV + 私有解码尾」的宽 beam 机制，可能为多假设 latent 推理中共享前缀缓存与各假设状态隔离的开销结构提供核查线索，但任务是检索服务优化，与视觉推理无直接关联，迁移价值待判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Wide-Beam Generative Retrieval with Model-Native Trie Decoding and Shared-Context Attention

**abstract/A1：** Generative retrieval (GR) predicts catalog entries as short semantic identifiers (SIDs). Trie-constrained beam search keeps these identifiers valid, but wide beams expose two serving costs: vocabulary-wide score tensors that are mostly discarded, and repeated attention over the same prompt. We present Trie-Head, a model-native output interface that stores the identifier trie as validated GPU tensors. Compared with a Python custom logit processor (CLP), it reduces CPU--GPU coordination and memory traffic by avoiding Python constraint callbacks and vocabulary-wide candidate buffers. It scores compact codebook candidates and streams the full-vocabulary normalizer, preserving the original model's valid-token scores in real arithmetic without retraining. Shared-context attention complements this output path by reusing prompt key/value blocks across beams while preserving their private decode tails. We implement both mechanisms in SGLang and evaluate candidate sourcing for LinkedIn Hiring Assistant and qualification retrieval for the Qualification Retrieval Platform. The combined system improves closed-loop throughput by up to 5.57x over the CLP baseline and up to 2.63x over BF16 FlashRec at a common beam width of 200 on the Qualification Retrieval Platform workload.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [EviDARL: Evidence-Centric Dual-Agent Reasoning via Alternating Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Table Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22310v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22310

**作者：** Dunhan Li、Leilei Gan、Lirong Gao、Wenyan Fan、Yongwei Wang、Sheng Guo、Fei Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22310v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22310v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EviDA 双智能体框架：证据构造智能体在完整表格上生成并执行表格操作程序以产出问题相关证据和可核验中间轨迹，推理智能体基于该证据完成多跳问答；并用 EviDARL 的智能体专属细粒度奖励与交替强化学习（轮流固定一方）优化二者协作，在 MMQA 与 TQA-Bench 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 「先选证据、再推理」的分离结构与可验证中间轨迹、细粒度奖励和交替优化，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与答案生成的分工及贡献分配提供借鉴；但属纯文本表格场景，与视觉内部状态无直接关系，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EviDARL: Evidence-Centric Dual-Agent Reasoning via Alternating Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Table Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** Despite advances in LLM-based table reasoning, most studies focus on single-table settings. Multi-table QA requires multi-hop reasoning over complex table relationships and evidence dispersed across long contexts, often without verifiable intermediate traces. To address these challenges, we introduce EviDA, an evidence-centric dual-agent framework comprising an Evidence Construction Agent and a Reasoning and Answering Agent. The Evidence Construction Agent generates and executes table-manipulation programs over complete tables to produce question-specific evidence and verifiable intermediate traces, simplifying subsequent reasoning. The Reasoning and Answering Agent then performs multi-hop reasoning over this evidence to derive final answers. We further introduce EviDARL, which combines agent-specific fine-grained rewards with parameter-efficient alternating reinforcement learning to improve collaboration between the agents. By optimizing each agent in turn while holding the other fixed, EviDARL enables bidirectional adaptation between evidence construction and answer reasoning. Experiments on MMQA and TQA-Bench demonstrate that EviDARL substantially outperforms competitive open-model and multi-table reasoning baselines under constrained model and training budgets.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [M$^3$-Test: Coverage Gaps in Joint-Perturbation Testing of Visual Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22329

**作者：** Enhui Wan、Xiaowan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22329v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 M3-Test，将每个干净输入与其单模态变体以及 5×5、4×4 强度联合扰动库对齐，构成可重放的覆盖审计，在四个 VLM 的约 260 万条回答上刻画「通过全部单模态测试、却在联合扰动下失败」的请求比例与分布。

**相关性简评：** 其「对齐扰动网格＋请求级风险排序分配查询」可能可供核查 LVR 在视觉与问题条件同时变化时 latent 状态是否真正使用视觉证据，以及如何在有限验证查询下优先暴露失败；但论文不涉及内部表示或推理状态生成，仅为评价方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** M$^3$-Test: Coverage Gaps in Joint-Perturbation Testing of Visual Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** A visual question answering (VQA) model can pass every image-only and question-only test yet fail when the same changes occur together. Standard robustness reports average over conditions and therefore cannot say which accepted requests remain vulnerable. We introduce M3-Test, a coverage audit that aligns each clean input with its single-modality variants and a complete 5x5 family, 4x4 strength joint library, so that any reduced test suite can be replayed on the same requests. Across 2.6 million responses from four vision-language models, joint failures affect 26-29% of the Qwen questions that pass all 40 single-modality tests, although fewer than 0.6% of their joint records fail. Passing the 25 maximum-strength corners does not close this gap: 11-14% of the accepted questions still fail elsewhere in the library. Re-executing the archived failures reproduces about 90% of them, against a 0.3-0.6% flip rate among matched passing controls, and re-checking the most fragile failure records supports 80-89% of them. On the evidence examined, neither execution noise nor grading error can fully account for the gap. The same aligned grids make previous evaluations reusable. On held-out images, a risk order learned from other models discovers about 80% of affected Qwen questions within 25 additional queries, compared with 65-68% for maximum-first and 53-57% for pairwise covering arrays. M3-Test thus turns a passing robustness suite into a request-level account of what remains untested, supporting acceptance, regression review and test allocation.

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [Multistage Contextual Optimization for Vehicle Repositioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22330v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22330

**作者：** Jinyu Zhang、Di Huang、Zhiyuan Liu、Nikolas Geroliminis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22330v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22330v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究网约车闲置车辆再定位的多阶段线性规划问题，提出以决策表现而非预测误差训练预测模型的多阶段上下文优化方法，给出含状态继承项的精确梯度与伴随反向计算，并提出并行分块求解与有限轮内恢复串行轨迹、提前停止时梯度误差有界的分析。

**相关性简评：** 其按决策效果训练预测器、梯度中显式分解「前序决策经继承状态影响当前决策」的贡献分配，以及分块并行迭代并分析提前停止误差的做法，可能为 latent 多步推理的轨迹监督与并行/停止机制提供可供核查的线索，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multistage Contextual Optimization for Vehicle Repositioning

**abstract/A1：** Ride-hailing platforms reposition idle vehicles to improve spatial supply-demand balances. This problem is prediction-driven and multistage: at each stage, the platform forecasts future demand, executes current decisions, and uses the resulting state to initialize the next stage. We formulate a multistage linear program (LP) with a macroscopic fundamental diagram (MFD) linking accumulation to trip completion. We then develop a multistage contextual optimization approach that uses decision performance to train the prediction model. The learning problem combines multiple coupled stages, context that depends on earlier decisions, and predictions that affect the objective, the constraint matrix, and the right-hand side of the LP. We derive the exact gradient, which has three parts: parameters change forecasts and hence decisions; earlier decisions change the current decision through the inherited state; and this state also changes forecasts as context. An equivalent adjoint uses reverse vector-Jacobian products, avoiding full solution and state-parameter Jacobians. Finally, forward propagation solves many stage LPs one after another, which makes training slow. We propose a parallel block evaluation algorithm that solves blocks of consecutive stages in parallel and repeatedly feeds each block output to the next block. We prove that the algorithm recovers the serial trajectory within a finite number of passes and that its gradient error remains bounded when it stops early. On New York City ride-hailing data, multistage contextual optimization yields 7.7% to 9.1% more business benefit than the same predictor trained by mean squared error, outperforms all compared baselines from five paradigms, and even outperforms realized demand fed to the same policy. With eight workers, parallel block evaluation shortens training by a factor of 1.86 without loss of decision performance.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [DAP-MR: Degradation-Aware Prompt-Magnitude Routing for Real-World Ultrasound Super-Resolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22331v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22331

**作者：** Ngoc-Hoang-Lam Le、Thi-An Le、Dinh-Hung Le、Manh-Hung Nguyen、Kai-Lung Hua、Ching-Chun Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22331v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22331v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DAP-MR，为每类图像退化设置专用 token，并另用 magnitude router 预测连续的、依赖输入的强度来调制该 token，从而把「退化类型」与「退化严重程度」解耦，可嵌入一步或多步扩散式超声超分辨率框架。

**相关性简评：** 其将「注入什么」与「以多强强度注入」分离为离散 token 加连续调制的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据内容与证据强度/置信度的分流调制提供可核查的线索，但论文本身属图像复原，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DAP-MR: Degradation-Aware Prompt-Magnitude Routing for Real-World Ultrasound Super-Resolution

**abstract/A1：** When super-resolution models designed for natural images are used on ultrasound scans, relying on semantic text prompts can lead to unreliable results. The tags these models generate often do not match the actual restoration needs, and specialized medical terms may be misunderstood by general-purpose text encoders. Instead, prompts focused on common image degradations are more directly useful for guiding restoration. However, using a single prompt to describe both the type and severity of image degradation can entangle these factors, making it hard for the model to know how strong the degradation is. To address this limitation, we introduce Degradation-Aware Prompt--Magnitude Routing (DAP-MR). Each degradation type is represented by a dedicated token initialized from the contextualized EOS embedding of a degradation-oriented prompt and subsequently adapted during ultrasound SR training. A separate magnitude router predicts a continuous, input-dependent magnitude that modulates the corresponding token. Thus, language provides a degradation-aware initialization for what should be restored, while magnitude routing supplies an explicit signal for how strongly it should influence restoration. DAP-MR can be added to both one-step and multi-step diffusion super-resolution frameworks without changing their core structures. Our experiments--on synthetic datasets with both in-domain and under anatomical-domain shift, as well as unpaired real-world ultrasound data--show that DAP-MR delivers better restoration quality and generalizes more consistently across different domains.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Scaling Laws for Vision-Language Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22351v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22351

**作者：** Seng Pei Liew、Teppei Suzuki、Seiya Tokui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22351v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22351v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统分析视觉-语言预训练的学习动力学，将验证损失拆分为基础学习轨迹（不同数据类型以不同速率贡献）与模态竞争引起的分布偏移，并据此导出统一的 scaling law，用于预测不同混合比例、训练步数和模型规模下的损失，刻画 early fusion 与 late fusion 的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其「基础轨迹＋模态竞争偏移」的损失分解与预测框架可供核查是否能用于建模 VLM 训练中视觉与文本贡献的分配及预算取舍，但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态生成与使用，与 LVR 主线联系较外围。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Laws for Vision-Language Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as central foundation models combining multimodal perception with text comprehension. While VLMs are traditionally built via late fusion by continued pre-training an off-the-shelf language model on multimodal (image-text) data, mixing modalities from the start of pre-training (early fusion) has recently gained traction. However, the fundamental trade-offs between these paradigms remain poorly understood, resulting in expensive and heuristic-driven training recipes. In this paper, we systematically analyze the learning dynamics of these multimodal pre-training processes, showing that validation losses during vision-language fusion can be modeled by separating the base learning trajectory, where different data types contribute at distinct rates, from a distribution shift driven by the competition between modalities. We derive a scaling law that unifiedly describes these dynamics, enabling accurate loss predictions across mixture ratios, training steps, and model sizes. Crucially, our law characterizes the trade-off between early and late fusion: under a fixed data budget, early fusion preserves text performance while late fusion favors vision performance, yielding a trade-off that increasingly tilts toward early fusion as model size scales. Extensive experiments confirm that our scaling law reliably captures multimodal learning dynamics and governs fusion decisions.

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [SandBAR: A Band-Asymmetric Architecture for Underwater Image Restoration with Removable Frequency Scaffolding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22355v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22355

**作者：** Yanzi Liu、Haoran Hou、Jiachi Tong、Shen Chen、Shuang Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22355v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22355v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向水下图像复原，提出按频带非对称组织的统一架构 SandBAR：低频段做通道感知的大核校正，高频段做轻量局部细化，并把低频引导注入深层编码器，同时配套可移除的训练期脚手架 PaST，使推理阶段不增加参数与计算量。

**相关性简评：** 其「训练期加容量、推理期移除」的脚手架训练与低频引导注入深层表示，可供核查是否适用于 LVR 中训练监督与内部状态保留视觉低频/颜色信息的问题，但论文本身不涉及推理或 latent 生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SandBAR: A Band-Asymmetric Architecture for Underwater Image Restoration with Removable Frequency Scaffolding

**abstract/A1：** Light scattering, absorption and turbidity degrade underwater imagery through complementary frequency tendencies: large-scale, channel-dependent appearance distortions and local high-frequency detail loss. This band asymmetry motivates different forms of restoration for global colour and local structure. Our primary contribution is SandBAR, a novel unified band-asymmetric architecture for underwater image restoration. Rather than treating frequency processing as an isolated module, SandBAR organises the restoration pipeline around band asymmetry at the block, encoder and objective levels. Within this architecture, we introduce a novel band-asymmetric sandwich block with a reserved post-attention repair slot and the Split-band Parallel Asymmetric Repair Kernel (SPARK), a novel module that applies channel-aware large-support correction to the low band, which predominantly carries large-scale appearance distortions, and lightweight local refinement to the detail-bearing high band, thereby restoring global colour while preserving fine structure. To deliver the global cast to the depth at which the network represents global structure, a learnable low-frequency guidance path injects a low-pass view of the input into the deep encoder, and a three-scale spatial-frequency objective supervises the same axis throughout the decoder. Building on the reserved post-attention slot, we further introduce Post-attention Scaffold Training (PaST), a novel removable nonlinear scaffolding scheme that trains the permanent SandBAR graph under band-specific capacity and runs it at inference without that capacity. SandBAR reaches 29.89 / 28.92 / 22.77 dB on EUVP / LSUI / UIEB and establishes a new three-domain mean state of the art on the benchmark family, exceeding the strongest published result by approximately 1.0 dB. PaST adds no parameters or FLOPs at inference and preserves matched latency.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. Camera-ready version. 9 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [Leveraging Segmentation Representations for Efficient Classification: A Self-Configuring Framework Based on nnU-Net](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22380v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22380

**作者：** Renjun Cai、Antonio Javier Gallego、Félix Fuentes-Hurtado

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22380v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22380v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文基于 nnU-Net 构建自配置框架，把 3D 医学分割编码器特征与预测分割概率送入轻量分类器，比较「冻结骨干＋ROI、前景体积、分割置信度」与「末段部分微调＋辅助分割损失」两种信息共享策略，并在 FLARE 2026 Task 2 排名第一。

**相关性简评：** 把分割概率与置信度作为显式证据特征、并以辅助分割损失抑制信息流失，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据保留与内部状态被答案监督冲淡的问题提供可核查线索，但该工作属医学分割的下游分类应用，未涉及 latent 推理状态的生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Leveraging Segmentation Representations for Efficient Classification: A Self-Configuring Framework Based on nnU-Net

**abstract/A1：** We propose a self-configuring framework that extends the nnU-Net workflow to both segmentation and classification in 3D medical imaging. Building on nnU-Net's self-configuring method, the framework reuses representations learned by a segmentation model for image-level classification. Its encoder features and predicted segmentation probabilities are used by a lightweight classifier. Two information-sharing strategies are investigated. In the frozen-backbone branch, features are augmented with region-of-interest information, predicted foreground volume, and segmentation confidence. In the adaptation branch, the final encoder stage is partially fine-tuned for classification, with an auxiliary segmentation loss to preserve segmentation-related information. Additional five-fold experiments showed that more complex configurations did not consistently improve classification performance. On the public validation leaderboard, our selected framework achieved an Overall score of 0.8056. In the official FLARE 2026 Task 2 final evaluation, our framework ranked first overall, achieving the highest DSC, NSD, and AUROC across all four testing tasks, including two hidden testing tasks. Local inference measurements also remained below the challenge limits of $60 \\mathrm{s}$ per case and $4 \\mathrm{GB}$ GPU memory. These results demonstrate the practical value of reusing segmentation representations for classification while maintaining a simple and efficient framework.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [SignAligner: Dual-Granularity Span Generation for Sign Language Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22409v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22409

**作者：** Jongbhin Woo、Youngjoon Jang、Joon Son Chung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22409v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22409v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SignAligner，一个由提示条件驱动的视觉-语言 span 生成器，输入手语视频与字幕句或目标词查询，输出起止帧区间；先联合字幕 span 与词 span 训练，再用基于重叠度的奖励微调。

**相关性简评：** 其用时序重叠奖励替代交叉熵继续训练区间预测，可能为 LVR 中证据定位与停止决策的监督、奖励设计提供可核查线索，但任务本身是手语视频时间定位，与 latent 推理状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SignAligner: Dual-Granularity Span Generation for Sign Language Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Large sign-language corpora benefit from temporal labels at two scales: subtitle spans for subtitle alignment and word spans for sign spotting. Sign spottings already locate words in time at corpus scale, but training subtitle alignment jointly on subtitle and word spans remains underexplored. We present SignAligner, a prompt-conditioned visual-language span generator that maps a sign-language video and a subtitle-sentence or target-word query to a textual start/end frame interval. It is first trained jointly on subtitle spans and word spans from sign spottings, then refined with overlap-based rewards while retaining auxiliary word examples. On the subtitle test set of the British Broadcasting Corporation-Oxford British Sign Language (BOBSL) corpus, the refined model reaches 66.68 F1@0.50 (F1 with a temporal intersection-over-union threshold of one half), the best among compared systems without external sign-video resources. This overlap-reward refinement outperforms cross-entropy continuation from the same jointly trained model. Joint training with word spans improves subtitle alignment in a pair of our generators matched in code, labels and maximum update budget. The advantage remains after both models of the pair are refined with overlap rewards. For word queries, the refined model follows the queried word, not a fixed position in the video: when the word's sign lies away from the video centre, it is far more accurate than predictions that use position alone. Together, these findings support one shared span generator refined with overlap rewards, aligning subtitles and producing word spans for human review.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [Think Once, Act Fast: Cost-Effective Recursive Self-Improvement for Decision Model Harness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22432v2>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22432

**作者：** Siqi Lai、Haitao Li、Hao Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22432v2>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22432v2>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 JevRSI：轻量 System-1 模型负责低延迟动作选择，异步 System-2 LLM 构建含流程、状态记忆与世界模型的模块化 harness，并按上下文相关性与历史效用检索模块，借环境反馈递归改进。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其「按历史效用检索外部模块、把重型 LLM 推理摊薄为可复用决策上下文」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的计算预算分配与停止判据设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Think Once, Act Fast: Cost-Effective Recursive Self-Improvement for Decision Model Harness

**abstract/A1：** Fast System-1 models enable responsive actions but struggle with complex or unfamiliar tasks. Large language model (LLM)-based System-2 provides strong reasoning capabilities, yet repeated invocation incurs substantial latency and computational costs. To bridge this gap, we propose JevRSI, a System-1/System-2 collaboration framework that transforms LLM reasoning into transferable decision support through recursive harness improvement. A Jev-style System-1 model enables low-latency action selection, while an asynchronous System-2 LLM develops a modular harness. The harness integrates procedures, state memory, and world models to provide decision contexts spanning the past, present, and future. Utility-driven retrieval selects modules based on both contextual relevance and their utility on previous tasks. Environmental feedback guides targeted revisions and enables recursive self-improvement. Experiments across three datasets show that JevRSI consistently improves decision quality, matching or surpassing LLM agents in several settings. It preserves low-latency execution with favorable performance-cost trade-offs. Moreover, System-2 development interventions decrease as the harness evolves, amortizing System-2 costs through continuous improvement. Our project is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/JevRSI.

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [As Student Rollouts Continue, Visual Backdoors Keep Growing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22446v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22446

**作者：** Zane Fu、Yanzhao Zheng、Jinze Liu、Ziting Qin、Qianshan Wei、Jihuai Zhu、Yipeng Yu、Baohua Dong、Hangcheng Zhu、Ruohui Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22446v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22446v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 On-Policy Distillation（OPD）中视觉后门从教师到 VLM 学生的传播：发现低毒化率下后门罕见出现、超过临界阈值后攻击成功率骤升，传播由教师持续累积的异常概率质量驱动，并据此提出无需显式注入触发器的攻击设计。

**相关性简评：** 其「教师概率分布在线监督学生自生成轨迹」的设定，与 LVR 中轨迹训练、监督信号如何塑造学生对视觉证据的使用可能相关，可供核查；但论文未触及 latent 状态或推理机制，联系偏弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** As Student Rollouts Continue, Visual Backdoors Keep Growing

**abstract/A1：** Conventional knowledge distillation can facilitate the propagation of backdoors from teacher models to student models. However, how this risk manifests in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains insufficiently studied. OPD has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Unlike traditional offline distillation, OPD does not require teacher-generated responses. Instead, it uses the teacher's probability distributions to supervise response trajectories generated online by the student. Since the initial student model typically generates benign responses and receives no explicit supervision from malicious target responses, OPD appears to offer some inherent resistance to backdoor propagation. In this paper, we challenge this intuition and reveal a more subtle risk of visual backdoor propagation in OPD. Through experiments across multiple model families and datasets, together with theoretical analysis, we identify three main findings. First, unlike the relatively gradual propagation observed in traditional offline distillation, backdoors rarely emerge in OPD at low poisoning ratios. However, the attack success rate rapidly approaches 100% once a critical threshold is exceeded. Second, backdoor propagation does not depend on explicit malicious responses. Instead, it is driven by the continuous accumulation of anomalous probability mass assigned by the teacher. Finally, based on these findings, we propose a novel trigger design that enables visual backdoor propagation with zero explicit trigger injection. These findings further reveal an overlooked mechanism of backdoor propagation in OPD and highlight its potential security risks.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [How Many Training Labels Are Wrong? Auditing a Fitted Model through Gradient Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22449v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22449

**作者：** Yuchen Li、Zongqi Fan、Xintong Li、Nguyen H. Tran、Ken-Tye Yong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22449v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22449v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用单个已训练模型的逐样本梯度，归一化为「梯度分配」后与各类平均分配比较，无需阈值、干净数据或额外训练即可标出错误标签并估计噪声率；在 CIFAR-10N 等强噪声设定下与 confident learning 等方法对比。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关系，但其用逐样本梯度分配做贡献归因与监督质量审计的思路，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的样本/步骤贡献分配提供可核查线索，需判断能否从分类标签迁移到 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Many Training Labels Are Wrong? Auditing a Fitted Model through Gradient Allocation

**abstract/A1：** How many labels in a training set are wrong, and which ones? The usual answers read a held-out set or the model's outputs. A held-out reading depends on how the held-out set was drawn, and an output-based reading fails once the model has fitted its wrong labels, because the loss of a fitted wrong label is small and the prediction agrees with it. We read the answer from the per-example gradients of a single trained model. Each example, together with its label, asks the network to change in a particular way. We normalise the magnitudes of its gradient into a gradient allocation and compare it with the mean allocation of every class. An example is flagged when relabelling it as its nearest other class makes its allocation fit better than under its own label, and the share of flagged examples is the reading of the noise rate. The rule has no threshold and needs no clean data, no held-out set and no further training. After the model has fitted 99-100% of its wrong labels, the allocation still separates wrong from right labels with an AUC of 0.94-0.96 on the noisiest human labels of CIFAR-10N, where a feature-space margin falls to 0.79-0.84 and the loss, the gradient norm and the self-influence of an example to 0.73-0.76. On such a fitted model, readings of the noise rate from predictions or feature margins fall to 0-6% at true rates of 8-58% on CIFAR-10 with ViT-S, while the audit reads 54-96% of the true rate, and at least 93% of the examples it counts carry wrong labels. On a briefly trained model, before the wrong labels are fitted, the reading lies within 1.7 percentage points of the true noise rate on CIFAR-10 from 0% to 58% noise. Confident learning, which trains four further models, separates wrong labels about as well (AUC 0.925-0.998 against 0.900-0.995) and mostly reads the noise rate more closely, at a lower precision. An audit of 5,000 examples takes 14 to 27 seconds on one GPU.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 8 tables, 1 figure. Preliminary version

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [LeViT-UNet++: Towards Balanced Fine-Grained Details and Context for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22482v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22482

**作者：** Guoping Xu、Jinling Zhu、Liuyue Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22482v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22482v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出用于高效医学图像分割的编码-解码架构 LeViT-UNet++：以 Haar 小波多尺度下采样把特征分解为高低频分量以减轻下采样时的细节损失，并用 depthwise 卷积解码器融合 Transformer 全局表示与局部空间特征，在四个 2D/3D 基准上以较小参数量和较快推理取得有竞争力的精度。

**相关性简评：** 其小波高低频分解以缓解细节丢失的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉编码器是否能把任务必要的高频细节保留到内部状态；但论文本身不涉及推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，与主线的联系较弱，需人工判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LeViT-UNet++: Towards Balanced Fine-Grained Details and Context for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Efficient medical image segmentation is essential for clinical applications requiring fast and accurate delineation, such as surgical navigation, radiation therapy, and real-time image-guided intervention. Despite substantial advances in convolutional neural networks and Transformers, attaining high accuracy under limited computational resources remains difficult, because reducing model complexity often sacrifices fine-grained structural details and global context modeling, yielding an unfavorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. To address this, we propose LeViT-UNet++, an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that better balances local detail preservation and global context modeling. Specifically, we introduce a Haar Wavelet Multi-Scale Downsampling (HWMD) module that decomposes feature representations into low- and high-frequency components to extract multi-scale structural information, thereby mitigating detail loss during downsampling. We further develop a depthwise convolution-based decoder that integrates multi-scale global representations from Transformer blocks with local spatial features, enabling progressive feature refinement while reducing computational overhead. Evaluated on four benchmarks (Synapse, ACDC, LA2018, and MSD Heart) under both 2D and 3D settings, LeViT-UNet++ achieves competitive accuracy with a compact parameter footprint and fast inference. On Synapse, for instance, it attains a DSC of 76.57% with only 8.025M parameters and 241.72 FPS, confirming a favorable accuracy-efficiency balance. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining wavelet-based multi-scale feature extraction with lightweight local-global feature fusion for efficient medical segmentation. The source code is available at https://github.com/apple1986/levit\_unet\_pp.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 5 figure

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [Motion-Aware Reward Modeling for Instruction-Guided Video Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22495v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22495

**作者：** Li Li、Xiangbo Gao、Bo Ni、Samyadeep Basu、Viet Dac Lai、Jing Shi、Seunghyun Yoon、Gang Wu、Yu Wang、Thien Huu Nguyen、Tyler Derr、Franck Dernoncourt、Zhengzhong Tu、Yue Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22495v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22495v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究指令引导视频编辑奖励模型的「motion collapse」：用受控的运动抑制变体诊断复合奖励、学习式视频奖励与 VLM 评判均偏好退化候选，并提出融合 RGB 帧差流、以运动抑制与伪造运动为负样本做偏好训练的 motion-aware 奖励模型，辅以蒸馏锚点保持与人类判断对齐。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其「代理奖励偏好退化输出、并可被 latent 优化与微调利用」的诊断及帧差显式动态输入，可能为 latent 推理中视觉证据保留、奖励/验证器设计与退化状态检测提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Motion-Aware Reward Modeling for Instruction-Guided Video Editing
