**abstract/A1：** Reward models for instruction-guided video editing can favor temporal consistency at the expense of motion. We study this failure, termed \\emph{motion collapse}, using controlled motion-suppression variants of real edits. On VE-Bench, the evaluated composite reward prefers a motion-suppressed candidate in $97\\%$ of cases. Learned video rewards and a VLM judge also struggle with mild suppression, while latent optimization and reward-guided fine-tuning exploit this weakness to increase reward as motion declines. A linear probe of our base reward backbone performs near chance on mild suppression, motivating an explicit inter-frame dynamics input. We propose a motion-aware reward model that combines an RGB frame-difference stream with preference training against motion-suppressed and fabricated-motion negatives. A distillation anchor preserves alignment with human quality judgments. On the VE-Bench motion ladder, our model reduces the rate of selecting motion-suppressed candidates to $0\\%$ while achieving a Spearman correlation of $0.57$ with human quality ratings. It also detects unseen suppression variants and preserves motion during reward-guided fine-tuning on two editing pipelines. These results support explicitly modeling motion in rewards for video editing.

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [Towards Data-Efficient, Real-Time World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22497v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22497

**作者：** Raluca Stevenson、David Bignell、Jennifer C. White、Tabish Rashid、Emanuele Aiello、Mariya Hendriksen、Junha Hyung、Sadegh Aliakbarian、Abdelhak Lemkhenter、Marko Tot、Katja Hofmann、Sarah Parisot

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22497v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22497v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向游戏画面世界模型，用两阶段离散扩散替代 next-token prediction：先粗生成，再由轻量 refinement transformer 做迭代掩码预测，从而在数据量约 28 小时的条件下实现约 79 倍推理加速并保持适度质量损失。

**相关性简评：** 其「先粗后细的迭代掩码精化」与扩散式并行状态更新，可能为 LVR 中替代逐 token AR 的并行/迭代 latent 状态生成及迭代步数带来的计算成本权衡提供可核查线索，但该工作在像素级游戏序列上，与推理语义无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Data-Efficient, Real-Time World Models

**abstract/A1：** State-of-the-art world models have been used to produce sequences of gameplay that accord with provided user-input actions. However, high quality, consistent gameplay generation often comes at the cost of inference speed, making real-time interactive play and ideation challenging. The substantial quantities of data used to train state-of-the-art models have also made custom training of these models appear impractical, limiting many of their potential uses in creative fields. In this work we demonstrate a major step towards efficient world models: achieving both fast high-quality inference, and improved data efficiency. This is done by replacing the next-token prediction paradigm with a two-stage discrete diffusion-based approach, introducing a lightweight refinement transformer which carries out iterative masked predictions. Building on an autoregressive world model capable of generating highly consistent and complex sequences over minutes (Kanervisto et al., 2025), we achieve substantial model speed-up (79x) with a modest quality trade-off. Additionally, we demonstrate that this model can be trained to reproduce high-quality, consistent gameplay using a dataset containing approximately one day (28 hours) of gameplay data. Our results demonstrate progress towards removing barriers to the use of world models in settings where real-time inference and data efficiency are essential.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 12 figures, 1 table. Includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Reliability-Driven Feature Correction for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22508v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22508

**作者：** Lianming Zhang、Xiaolu Wang、Qiya Song、Jinyang Liu、Renwei Dian、Xudong Kang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22508v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22508v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向高光谱与 LiDAR 联合地物分类，提出 ReDF 框架：用类原型相似度把各模态特征转成类别证据以估计逐空间位置的可靠性，据此进行双向跨模态特征纠正，并按可靠性重加权各模态对融合表示的贡献。

**相关性简评：** 遥感的双模态融合与 VLM 推理主线不同，但其「用原型证据强度估计可靠性并据此调节模态贡献」的做法，可能可供核查用于 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态门控。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliability-Driven Feature Correction for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Classification

**abstract/A1：** Hyperspectral image (HSI) and LiDAR data provide complementary spectral and elevation information for land-cover classification. However, the spatially varying class-discriminative reliability of modality features is rarely modeled explicitly, allowing weak or ambiguous features to adversely affect cross-modal interaction and fusion. To address this issue, we propose ReDF, a reliability-driven framework that quantifies the reliability of each modality's features at each spatial location through the strength of class-related evidence they provide, and uses it to guide feature correction and fusion. Specifically, a Reliability-Guided Feature Correction module introduces a class prototype bank for each modality and converts feature-prototype similarities into class evidence for reliability estimation. The estimated reliabilities are used to construct a reliability-weighted shared representation and to guide bidirectional cross-modal feature correction based on neighborhood agreement, refining modality features with weak class evidence through information from the other modality. Building on these outputs, a Reliability-Guided Feature Reweighting module conditions a low-rank modulation on the estimated reliabilities and the cross-modal disagreement, and applies it to the corrected modality-specific descriptors to adaptively rescale each modality's contribution to the fused representation used for final classification. Experiments on three public HSI-LiDAR datasets show higher mean overall accuracy and Kappa than the compared methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Right Labels, Wrong Reasons: Signed Feature Attribution for Multi-Label Explainability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22515v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22515

**作者：** Kenton White

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22515v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22515v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FLEX，以单个直接多标签 Fisher LDA 作为全局代理模型，在全标签矩阵上统一拟合，为每个标签输出带符号特征归因，并用判别权重幅值标记「标签预测正确但依据可疑」的情形。

**相关性简评：** 其全局代理加带符号归因、以权重幅值界定可分性的做法，或可作为诊断 VLM 答案是否依赖任务必要视觉证据的核查线索，但需核实从表格特征到视觉 token 或区域的迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Right Labels, Wrong Reasons: Signed Feature Attribution for Multi-Label Explainability

**abstract/A1：** When a multi-label classifier assigns the right labels, it may do so for the wrong reasons. Diffuse feature combinations may vary across classifier families and lack a dominant feature. This paper proposes FLEX (Fisher LDA Explainer), a global surrogate that fits a single direct multi-label Fisher LDA over the full binary label matrix, producing signed feature attribution lists for each label in a single coherent coordinate system. Across the evaluated benchmarks and classifier families, FLEX reproduces the black box's predictions with consistently high fidelity. Pooling covariance across labels outperforms a per-label alternative on most datasets, with the advantage growing largest where labels are numerous and positive examples are scarce. FLEX's fidelity-complexity Pareto curve is monotonically increasing, giving practitioners a predictable path to higher-fidelity explanations as more features are used, and its in-sample fidelity matches or leads Binary Relevance alternatives once a modest feature budget is allowed. The magnitude of each discriminant weight vector bounds its label's separability, flagging labels that may be predicted correctly for the wrong reasons. The evaluated classifiers largely agree on which predictions to make, but reach those decisions through different feature subsets when no single feature dominates.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 1 figure, 5 tables. Accepted at the 2027 IEEE Symposium Series on Computational Intelligence (IEEE SSCI 2027), Gold Coast, Australia, February 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Testing World Models on Actions They Have Never Seen: Held-Out Plans, Forecast Accuracy and Price Rules in Retail](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22537v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22537

**作者：** Karthik Iyer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22537v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22537v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在零售定价场景中对 2853 个动作条件世界模型做「未见动作」留出测试，检验预测精度能否预示新计划上的误差、JEPA 预训练与 latent 一致性训练是否改善该误差，以及结构化价格规则带来的增益。

**相关性简评：** 其「以 JEPA 预训练和 latent 一致性检查学习动作效应却未见收益」的线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正承载动作与证据、以及训练监督是否有效；但该文非视觉、非推理任务，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Testing World Models on Actions They Have Never Seen: Held-Out Plans, Forecast Accuracy and Price Rules in Retail

**abstract/A1：** A world model is trusted to answer "what if": how its world responds to an action it has never seen. We give a test of that trust for any action-conditioned world model: actions held out of training by design and scored against a known answer; whether forecast accuracy predicts accuracy on those actions; and the same structure given to simpler models. We run it in retail, where the action is a price and promotion plan, across 2,853 fitted models. First, forecast accuracy is a fair guide between kinds of model (rank correlation with error on new plans 0.32 to 0.66) and a poor guide between versions of one world model: where merchants plan into high-demand weeks the link reverses, down to -0.69. Second, training the world model to learn the effect of actions (JEPA pretraining, a latent consistency check, adaptation at the plan date) leaves its error on new plans unchanged. Third, in generated worlds the recorded regular price and three price rules improve it. Given the same rules, a log-linear model is best when its curve can match the truth; in a world whose curve lies outside the rules, the world model with the rules is ahead of the log-linear model with the rules in 36 of 40 worlds, and does as well without its recurrent cell. On real data (Breakfast at the Frat, The Complete Journey and M5) the rules lower the world model's error on pure price cuts deeper than any it saw (0.63 to 0.56 on the Frat; 0.45 to 0.44, within chance, on The Complete Journey), but not on lasting price moves. Fourth, a small share of random test plans helps every model a little. In a generated dosing world, a stress test in a second domain, the first finding repeats, and the built-up drug level lowers the world model's error (2.86 to 1.91) though it still predicts only 0.12 to 0.32 of the true gain. The test carries to any domain where a world model is asked about actions outside its history.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Companion to arXiv:2611.20811

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Fast Training-Free Low-Light Video Enhancement with a Frozen Image Diffusion Prior](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22551v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22551

**作者：** Hon Ming Yam、Chun Pong Lau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22551v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22551v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究无训练的低光照视频增强，在冻结图像扩散先验上加入时序输入融合与关键帧偏移传播（非关键帧只反演低噪声段并叠加经光流扭曲的增强偏移），较 ZVRD 提速 261 倍且多数指标更好，但低分辨率下 LPIPS 反而下降。

**相关性简评：** 其「关键帧走完整采样轨迹、其余样本只做短程反演并搬运偏移」的 latent 复用与校正思路，可能为 LVR 中廉价复用推理期状态、减少扩散式 latent 更新成本提供核查线索，但该偏移能否跨问题迁移及误差累积尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast Training-Free Low-Light Video Enhancement with a Frozen Image Diffusion Prior

**abstract/A1：** Pretrained image diffusion models can enhance low-light video without low-light training data and without retraining for a new camera. ZVRD, the only published training-free method for low-light video, is too slow to use: our implementation needs over a minute per $256 \\times 256$ frame. We present a causal, training-free pipeline that adds two temporal components to a frozen MR. Illuminate image prior. Temporal input fusion recursively averages motion-compensated low-light frames before diffusion inversion. Late-start offset propagation runs the full sampling trajectory only on key frames. Each non-key frame inverts the low-noise part of its own trajectory and adds an enhancement offset warped from the latest key frame. At ZVRD's own resolution the pipeline is $261\\times$ faster than ZVRD and scores better on all five metrics we report. On 13 held-out SDSD clips at $1280 \\times 720$, it improves on framewise eight-step processing by 0.55 dB in PSNR and 0.034 in LPIPS and halves the warping error, in 10% less time. Measurements inside the model show why, and a linear-Gaussian model of inversion explains them. Fusion removes frame-to-frame noise before inversion amplifies it. A flow error perturbs the transported offset ten times less than it perturbs a transported state. The perceptual gain does not transfer to low resolution: at $346 \\times 260$, LPIPS is worse by 0.028. At 0.65 frames per second, the pipeline is an offline method.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 12 tables, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [MillBill: A Machining Agent with Geometric Feedback](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22565v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22565

**作者：** Gennadii Savrasov、Artem Slavin、Soslan Kabisov、Andrey Kuznetsov、Anton Konushin、Dmitrii Zhemchuzhnikov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22565v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22565v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MillBill 把加工决策与几何执行、验证分离：由中型开源模型对给定零件、毛坯与材料提出工序和切削参数，几何 harness 生成刀路、模拟去料并回传反馈以迭代改进方案，最后按仿真后目标表面落在容差内的面积比例评估。

**相关性简评：** 「模型只做决策、外部几何执行器与验证器提供反馈供反复精修」的架构，可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 中把数值执行外置、用证据反馈驱动推理状态更新与停止判断；原文未涉及视觉 latent 表示，联系仍属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MillBill: A Machining Agent with Geometric Feedback

**abstract/A1：** Turning a CAD model into a machining program requires both process knowledge and geometric precision. Language models can generate such workflows by writing geometry and toolpath code for each part, but this makes numerical execution part of the model's task. We introduce MillBill, an agent that separates machining decisions from their geometric execution and verification. Given a boundary-representation part, stock, and material, a locally hosted, mid-sized open model proposes operations and cutting parameters for indexed three-axis machining. A geometric harness generates toolpaths, simulates material removal, and returns feedback for plan refinement. Setup planning accounts for what the available cutters can physically reach, rather than visibility alone. We evaluate the resulting programs by the fraction of the target surface area within the specified tolerance after simulated machining. With Qwen3.8-27B, MillBill outperforms frontier-model approaches that construct their own geometry and toolpath workflows on the evaluated machining tasks. Code: https://github.com/kulibinai/millbill

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 8 figures. Code: https://github.com/kulibinai/millbill

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [ReMaRL: Learning to Unmask and Remask in Frozen Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22576v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22576

**作者：** Egor Shibaev、Danila Biktimirov、Ivan Klimov、Artem Makoian、Viacheslav Meshchaninov、Dmitry Vetrov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22576v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22576v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReMaRL：在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型上训练约 0.3M 参数的轻量策略，学习对每个 token 选择解掩、保留还是重掩，并用 GRPO 以最终答案奖励优化，在数学与代码任务上优于已有解掩／重掩策略。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本扩散任务，但「冻结主干＋小策略决定继续生成、保留或修正已生成状态，并以结果奖励训练」与 LVR 中的计算预算分配、中间状态修订和贡献分配有结构上的相似性，可供核查其是否可迁移到视觉推理的 latent 状态更新与停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReMaRL: Learning to Unmask and Remask in Frozen Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Masked diffusion language models generate tokens in parallel, and the order in which tokens are unmasked affects final-answer accuracy. Several methods learn unmasking policies from task rewards, but these policies cannot remask earlier predictions. Remasking lets the model correct those predictions using newly generated context. Heuristic rules guide both unmasking and remasking, while supervised remaskers learn to detect token errors. Neither approach directly optimizes these decisions for final-answer correctness. To address this gap, we introduce ReMaRL (Remasking with Reinforcement Learning), a lightweight policy that learns which tokens to unmask, retain, or remask. We train this roughly 0.3M-parameter policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) from final-answer rewards while keeping the diffusion model frozen. With LLaDA-8B-Instruct, ReMaRL achieves the highest scores among the compared unmasking and remasking policies within the tested inference budgets of 4-32 model calls on math and 8-16 calls on code.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [Distilling World Action Models for Efficient Bimanual Manipulation via Anchored Path Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22592v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22592

**作者：** Jialong Liu、Junjie He、Yandu Chen、Bin Zhou、Heng Su、Peijun Tang、Jianan Wang、Changhao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22592v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22592v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Anchored Path Matching，将迭代去噪的世界动作模型蒸馏为少步联合生成：从学生模型实际到达的状态出发，以教师零噪声终点与首个中间状态为双重目标，训练模态路由适配器，在梯度冲突时保留终点梯度；单一检查点支持 1/2/4 步采样。

**相关性简评：** 其「终点＋短程转移」监督、按学生轨迹锚定目标及部署时选择步数预算的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中多步 latent 推理压缩为少步生成与自适应计算的问题；但原文面向双臂操作与视频-动作生成，尚无视觉推理层面的直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Distilling World Action Models for Efficient Bimanual Manipulation via Anchored Path Matching

**abstract/A1：** World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative denoising. Few-step distillation reduces the number of model evaluations, but its coarse updates can move the student away from the teacher's trajectory. We propose Anchored Path Matching (APM), which supervises endpoint and short-transition predictions from states reached by the student. From each state, we continue the teacher to zero noise and use its endpoint and first intermediate state as targets. Modality-routed adapters learn to match both targets from the same starting state. When the two gradients conflict, anchor-priority updates preserve the endpoint gradient and remove the opposing component of the local gradient within each modality. One checkpoint supports 1-, 2-, and 4-step joint generation. The sampling budget can be chosen at deployment without retraining or teacher queries. On RoboTwin 2.0, we train only on Clean demonstrations and evaluate directly on the domain-randomized Random setting. At one step, APM improves closed-loop success over continued base distillation at matched inference budgets by 2.0 percentage points on Clean and 1.4 on Random. Video peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) rises from 30.169 to 32.317 dB, with gains in structural similarity (SSIM) and learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS). On two real-robot tasks, APM improves success over the one-step teacher and reduces control-cycle latency by $21.3\\times$ relative to the eight-step teacher.Project page: https://jialonglong.github.io/Anchored-Path-Matching/.

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Propose with the Gradient, Accept with the Loss: One Hedged Attack for Pixel-Sparse Robustness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22596v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22596

**作者：** Hon Ming Yan、Chun Pong Lau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22596v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22596v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 sPGD 与 Sparse-RS 统一为「提议分布+接受规则」的局部搜索，依据 Taylor 界与搜索中实测给出五条梯度使用规则（符号定值、排序松跟随、接受只看损失等），据此提出单次运行的 GRS 像素稀疏攻击，在 CIFAR-10 与 ImageNet 上以更少前向与更快速度接近或超过 sAA。

**相关性简评：** 其将搜索拆为「梯度指引提议、仅用损失接受」的结构可供核查能否迁移到 latent 假设的提议与验证分工；对少数像素的选取与移除排序也可能类比视觉证据筛选与贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Propose with the Gradient, Accept with the Loss: One Hedged Attack for Pixel-Sparse Robustness

**abstract/A1：** A pixel-sparse adversary may change a few pixels of an image to any value. Robustness to such attacks is currently measured with an ensemble, sparse-AutoAttack (sAA), because its members fail in different places: sparse projected gradient descent (sPGD) trusts a first-order model that a full pixel change breaks, and sparse random search (Sparse-RS) ignores the gradient even where it is informative. We show that both attacks are local searches, each a proposal distribution plus an acceptance rule, and ask what such a search can learn from the gradient. A Taylor bound says what to measure, and measurements at the states the search visits give five rules. The sign of the gradient is its most reliable use at a single pixel and sets a new pixel's value. Its ranking of candidate pixels should be followed loosely, and its ranking of the pixels to remove, which is often more reliable, can be followed more closely. A pixel should not be proposed twice before a move is accepted, and acceptance should be decided by the loss alone. These rules define Gradient-guided Random Search (GRS), which runs once and costs one forward and one backward pass per step. On 13 CIFAR-10 configurations and 5 ImageNet models, GRS matches or beats the best single member of sAA and is within one point of sAA itself or stronger on 17 of 18 settings, and it is significantly stronger than sAA on 8. It uses 3 instead of up to 7 forward-pass units, and on CIFAR-10 it runs 3.7 to 5.0 times faster than the members of sAA run in full.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 13 tables, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [Simplicity Goes Far: Auditing Prompt Optimizers with Demonstration-Conditioned Prompt Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22605v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22605

**作者：** Bohe Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22605v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22605v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把 prompt 优化中的「生成—选择」步骤单独抽成 DCPS：冻结生成器、以随机示例为条件、在验证集上打分并保留最优候选，作为机制归因对照，在 StablePrompt 与 DSPy 系统中与 PPO、GEPA 等重型优化器做同预算比较。

**相关性简评：** 其「用算力匹配的生成—选择基线分离机制增益与采样预算」的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 训练中 latent 状态、轨迹采样或额外 rollout 的贡献分配是否被算力优势混淆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Simplicity Goes Far: Auditing Prompt Optimizers with Demonstration-Conditioned Prompt Search

**abstract/A1：** Automatic prompt optimization is growing more elaborate. Recent methods stack policy-gradient updates, trace reflection, and evolutionary search. These methods share a generate-and-select step; without controlling for it, reported gains may conflate the contributions of optimizer mechanisms and larger sampling budgets. We isolate this step as Demonstration-Conditioned Prompt Search (DCPS), repurposing the Automatic Prompt Engineer (APE) recipe as a mechanism-attribution control: it freezes the generator, conditions it on randomly sampled demonstrations, scores candidates on validation data, and keeps the best---no learned update, reflection, or evolution. Across two optimizer families, DCPS replaces Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in StablePrompt, matching classification accuracy within 0.5 pp at roughly one-third the measured cost; in DSPy-based compound AI systems, under the reported budgets, it matches GEPA's macro accuracy on GPT-4.1-mini while using 55% fewer rollouts at 32% lower measured API cost on average. The audit reveals a task-dependent pattern: proposal and selection recover much of heavier optimizers' performance on structured and instruction-following tasks, while additional mechanisms retain advantages in some open-ended and reasoning-heavy settings. We recommend benchmarking prompt optimizers against generate-and-select controls and reporting rollout counts, token usage, and dollar costs alongside accuracy. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/bhc6/DCPS.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [DLS: Dual-Logic Graph Neural Networks with Chaos-Fuzzy Residuals for Robust Link Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22607v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22607

**作者：** Mohammad Mehdi Keikha、Abolfazl Nadi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22607v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22607v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DLS（Dual-Logic SAGE）归纳式链路预测框架，用 Logistic 映射的混沌注入打散特征流形以区分拓扑相近但语义不同的节点，再用模糊逻辑门控软阈值滤噪，以「受控不稳定」缓解深层 MPNN 的过平滑，在异配图上优于基线。

**相关性简评：** 其混沌注入加门控残差的动态状态更新管线，可能为 LVR 多步 latent 状态中的表示坍塌与高置信信号筛选提供可核查的方法线索，但任务为图链路预测，与视觉多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DLS: Dual-Logic Graph Neural Networks with Chaos-Fuzzy Residuals for Robust Link Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Link prediction is a fundamental task in graph mining, critical for applications ranging from biological interaction discovery to recommender systems. While Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) have achieved state-of-the-art results, they are fundamentally limited by the inadvertent suppression of high-frequency signals, as the recursive aggregation that stabilizes structural learning inherently leads to over-smoothing, rendering node representations indistinguishable in deep layers. This limitation is particularly severe in heterophilous graphs, where structural proximity does not imply feature similarity. In this paper, we propose DLS (Dual-Logic SAGE), a novel inductive framework designed to break the over-smoothing barrier via a "controlled instability" mechanism. Unlike conventional methods relying on static structural heuristics, DLS introduces a dynamic residual pipeline. First, a Chaotic Injection Module utilizes the Logistic Map to diversify the feature manifold, exploiting sensitivity to initial conditions to separate topologically similar but semantically distinct nodes. Second, to mitigate the aleatoric uncertainty introduced by chaotic dynamics, a Fuzzy-Logic Gating Mechanism applies soft-thresholding to filter noise and retain only high-confidence signals. Experimental results on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DLS effectively balances feature discrimination with robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in heterophilous settings while maintaining competitive efficiency on standard homophilous graphs.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to World Wide Web (Springer Nature) on January 5, 2026. Preprint version, currently under peer review

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [PlaceTally: A Test-Time Memory Model for Constructing Place Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22608v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22608

**作者：** Alibay Osmanli、Shaogang Gong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22608v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22608v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PlaceTally：一个 question-blind 的测试时记忆模型，为每段视频构建 place graph，每个片段并入最佳历史匹配的地点或新建地点，再由冻结 VLM 对每个地点最多读 128 帧完成计数，并在真实房间重录基准 RevisitCount 上超过整段视频读取。

**相关性简评：** 其「匹配强度决定复用已有状态还是新建状态」及定长帧读取带来的有界证据输入与缓存管理，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、状态更新和计算预算分配提供可核查的结构线索，但论文本身不涉及 latent 表示或推理轨迹。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PlaceTally: A Test-Time Memory Model for Constructing Place Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Counting distinct objects in a video is hard when the camera returns to places it has seen. A vision-language model that reads the whole video answers with a few small numbers, and one that reads it in parts counts an object again in every part that shows it. Object trackers need the category before the video is read, or a fixed vocabulary. We observe that a memory for counting does not need to know what it is looking for, only where it has been: objects rarely leave a room between visits, so parts of a video grouped by place share almost no objects, and a place can be recognised before any question is asked. Building on this, we introduce PlaceTally, a question-blind test-time memory model that constructs a place graph for each video. Each video fragment joins the place of its best earlier match or opens a new place, because a strong match is rarely wrong while a true return often matches weakly. A frozen vision-language model counts each place once from at most 128 of its frames, so every read has a bounded input and the memory grows with the number of places; the graph is built 15 times faster than real time. On RevisitCount, our benchmark of real room re-recordings, it scores 62.5% mean relative accuracy on test rooms and 61.2% on held-out rooms, against 51.2% and 44.3% for the same model reading the whole video. It stays level as rooms return more or less often, while a fixed correction of summed visits moves from 38.5% to 61.4%.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Code, data and supplementary material: https://alibayosmanli.github.io/placetally

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [Right Diagnosis, Unsafe Route: Auditing Clinical AI Decision Trajectories and Corrective Oversight for Validity and Potential Harm](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22612v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22612

**作者：** Guanqiao Chen、Wei Jiang、Shu Zhang、Qian Lin、Guanxu Yu、Siran Li、Chunhui Wang、Tianyu Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22612v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22612v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把患者安全作为逐步评价目标：按检查点重放真实入院病例，让系统只用已释放证据决策，对每项检查、治疗与处置分别评估潜在危害和临床有效性，并在首个可纠正错误处分叉，检验纠正反馈既修复错误又是否引入新风险。

**相关性简评：** 其「轨迹审计＋首错分叉干预」的评价设计可能为 LVR 提供核查线索，例如正确答案是否经由不可靠的中间 latent 状态、纠正性干预是否反而引入新失败；但对象是临床文本决策，迁移可行性尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Right Diagnosis, Unsafe Route: Auditing Clinical AI Decision Trajectories and Corrective Oversight for Validity and Potential Harm

**abstract/A1：** A clinical AI system can reach a correct final diagnosis through intermediate decisions that could have harmed the patient, and can repair an error while introducing new risks. Most evaluations score final diagnoses; even stepwise benchmarks, which release a case's evidence over time, score whether systems reach the right diagnosis or gather the right information, not whether the decisions a patient passes through are safe. We make patient safety the target of stepwise evaluation. A trajectory audit replays real admissions checkpoint by checkpoint, lets each system decide with only the evidence released so far, and rates every test, treatment and disposition for its potential harm to the patient and, separately, for clinical validity, against the documented final diagnosis and physician-written disease rubrics yet on the evidence available when the decision was made. An LLM judge scores every decision, and a physician reference standard (triple annotation and senior adjudication of a stratified sample) calibrates the rates. An oversight audit branches each trajectory at its first actionable error and measures, on the same branches, whether corrective feedback repairs the error and whether it introduces new harm. Across 300 cases from a hospital cardiology cohort, MIMIC-IV and the public ASCENT benchmark, with up to seven AI systems per source, 55.2% of hospital trajectories reaching an acceptable final diagnosis passed through a decision with moderate or severe potential harm and 24.8% through a clinically unacceptable one; the harm rates were 76.2% on MIMIC-IV and 39.6% on ASCENT. Structured corrections resolved 73.6% of first errors versus 24.8% after bare rejection, yet new potentially unsafe decisions followed 30.2% of corrections versus 19.7% of rejections. A correct final diagnosis does not certify a safe trajectory, and a successful correction does not certify a safe recovery.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Sub-Meter Canopy Height Synthesis from Sentinel-2 via Anchored Residual Flow](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22636v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22636

**作者：** Kiarie Ndegwa、Tony Chang、Andreas Gros、Vincent A. Landau、David D. Diaz、Luke Zachmann、Nathan E. Rutenbeck、Guy C Bayes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22636v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22636v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用免费的 10 m Sentinel-2 影像合成 0.5 m 冠层高度图：先训练确定性回归器 Anchor 追求低保真度误差，再在学习到的 CHM latent 空间中对 Anchor 的残差做条件 flow matching（AR-flow），以提升感知保真度，并在 197k 瓦片训练、2530 瓦片评测中对比八种基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「确定性锚点 + latent 残差随机流」的双工作点设计，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的采样式更新与不确定性表达提供核查线索；而作者明确指出的「生成细节是合理结构而非实测树木」也提示生成式 latent 细节可能与视觉证据脱耦，值得在信息坍缩讨论中对读。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sub-Meter Canopy Height Synthesis from Sentinel-2 via Anchored Residual Flow

**abstract/A1：** Canopy height models (CHMs) - per-pixel maps of vegetation top height - are essential for forest carbon accounting, wildfire fuel mapping, and tree-level ecology, yet sub-meter CHMs require airborne LiDAR refreshed on multi-year national cycles. We produce 0.5 m CHMs from free 10 m Sentinel-2 imagery, a 20$\\times$ cross-modal super-resolution task where optical reflectance must predict geometric height it does not directly observe. Motivated by the perception-distortion trade-off, we derive two operating points with shared conditioning. A deterministic regressor (Anchor) targets low distortion; a conditional flow matching model, trained on the Anchor's residual in a learned CHM latent space (AR-flow), targets perceptual fidelity. Trained on 197k LiDAR-paired tiles spanning 69 ecoregions across the contiguous United States and evaluated on 2,530 held-out tiles against eight baselines, the Anchor achieves 2.83 m MAE using only free satellite input. The AR-flow reduces sliced Wasserstein distance from the Anchor's 1.13 to 0.208, at a higher MAE of 3.68 m. Crown-scale statistics improve, but strict individual-tree detection remains weak: generated sub-pixel detail represents plausible structure rather than measured individual trees. Quarterly continental refresh becomes tractable, letting satellite monitoring inform timing and extent for more expensive data acquisitions.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [EDA: Observe, Verify, and Assess for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Depression Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22648

**作者：** Changzeng Fu、Ming Shang、Xinyi Zhang、Jiadong Wang、Runhao Zeng、Shiqi Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22648v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EDA 框架，把抑郁评估拆为 Observe（将声学与视觉线索定位为带时间区间和测量的、可溯源 Evidence Record）、Verify（冻结多模态代理核查媒体是否支持该观察，仅保留通过验证的记录）、Assess（统计描述与时间事件做参照比较，并结合 PHQ-9 文本分析）三步，在 D-Vlog 与 LMVD 上评估线索一致性与下游效用。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据需可溯源并被视觉核查后才准入」的门控设计，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容贡献分配提供可供核查的参考；但该工作属特定任务应用，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EDA: Observe, Verify, and Assess for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Depression Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Existing multimodal depression assessment methods often summarize long recordings into global behavioral descriptions. This leaves the source and decision relevance of individual observations difficult to inspect. We propose EDA, an evidence-grounded framework organized around Observe, Verify, and Assess. In Observe, literature-informed acoustic and visual cues are localized into source-linked Evidence Records with temporal intervals and behavioral measurements. In Verify, frozen multimodal agents examine whether the cited media support each observation. The simplified Support admission policy retains positively verified records and their review grounds. In Assess, statistical descriptors and temporal events support reference-based behavioral comparison, while PHQ-9-guided text analysis provides complementary symptom evidence. Cross-modal disagreement triggers review of the original text before final selection. Experiments on D-Vlog and LMVD evaluate human-referenced cue agreement and downstream assessment utility separately. AI cue annotations achieve 91.02% accuracy against human consensus labels on shared source segments. With a frozen 7B backbone, EDA Support improves F1 over direct prediction by 7.61 and 9.88 percentage points on D-Vlog Leave-Query-Out and LMVD, respectively. These findings support explicit, source-linked behavioral evidence for inspectable multimodal depression assessment.

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Must You See Fakes to Catch Fakes? Language Says What to Look For, Real Images What to Ignore](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22650v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22650

**作者：** Tianchi Xu、Yuxuan Zhou、Yuheng Zhang、Yanan Guan、Tao Dai、Shu-Tao Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22650v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22650v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SaySee，一种无需训练的 AI 生成图像检测器：用仅在真实性上不同的成对描述构造「生成方向」，按图像嵌入在该方向上的投影打分，再用真实图像校正格式、图像类型与内容带来的分数偏移。

**相关性简评：** 其「用文本指明该看什么、用真实图像指明该忽略什么」的做法，可能与 VLM 推理中的视觉证据筛选、以及 latent 表示里任务相关信号与格式/内容混淆因素的分离可供核查；但该文属检测任务，并非推理期状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Must You See Fakes to Catch Fakes? Language Says What to Look For, Real Images What to Ignore

**abstract/A1：** Must a detector see generated images in order to catch them? Detection of AI-generated images has long faced two problems. The cues a detector learns from the few generators in its training set carry traces specific to those generators and transfer poorly to new ones, which limits generalization. The real and generated training images also differ in format and content, so the detector takes these differences as evidence of authenticity, and its decisions change with factors that have nothing to do with authenticity, which harms robustness as well as generalization. Both problems stem from training on generated images. A detector never trained on them can largely avoid both at the source. Vision foundation models have already learned the concept of "AI-generated" during pretraining, and text can point to it directly. Building on this, we propose SaySee, a training-free detector: Say builds a generation direction from sets of paired descriptions that differ only in authenticity and scores an image by the projection of its embedding onto it, and See uses real images to correct the score offsets caused by format, image type and content. On 24 evaluation sets that span standard benchmarks, in-the-wild data and the latest generators, SaySee reaches a mean AUC of 98.2% with MoE-ViE, 4.2 points above the best existing detector. When format is decoupled from authenticity, it remains the best of all methods. Code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [The Between-Round Decision in Autonomous Research: An LLM Controller That Reads the Whole Trajectory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22661v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22661

**作者：** Caoxin Wang、Shengkai Gan、Xiang Hu、Lili Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22661v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22661v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BRED，一个基于 LLM 的控制器：每轮实验后读取当前最优解（Champion）与历史轮次日志，在无搜索结构的情况下决定是继续精修、换方向、以哪个解为基础以及设什么目标，并对比不读累积历史时的增益，在多个研究型 agent 基准及语音抑郁检测任务上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「需要读取多少累积证据、以压缩状态而非外部搜索结构支撑逐轮决策」的设计与消融，可供核查 LVR 中证据保留、状态更新与停止/取舍决策的对照线索；但任务并非视觉多模态，迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Between-Round Decision in Autonomous Research: An LLM Controller That Reads the Whole Trajectory

**abstract/A1：** Executing an idea often changes how promising it looks, so the next step in autonomous research should rest on results, not on proposals. After each round of experiments, the system must decide whether to refine the best solution or start a new direction, which solution to build from, and what goal to set. We call this the between-round decision. Existing systems wrap it in machinery such as rules, scores, search trees, and schedules; where an LLM makes it, what the LLM needs to read is left open. We ask whether an LLM can make this decision from accumulated evidence, without a search structure around it, and how much of that evidence it needs to read. We present BRED (Between-Round Evidence-based Decision), an LLM-based controller that maintains the best verified solution so far (the Champion) and a log of past rounds, and makes one decision per round. On three widely used research-agent benchmark tasks, BRED improves on the task baselines by 4.80% in MAE on ETTh1 (to 0.4172) and by 3.44 and 3.43 points of accuracy on SST-2 (to 95.07%) and CIFAR-100 (to 84.63%), ahead of the published results. When the controller does not read the accumulated evidence, the same loop gains less: with no history it reaches 0.4299 on ETTh1 and 83.71% on CIFAR-100, whereas the full controller's MAE is 2.95% lower and its accuracy 0.92 points higher. On a new task, speech-based depression detection under a leakage-free protocol, it reaches a macro-F1 of 0.6164 on seeds held out from selection, 5.5 points above its baseline and 4.0 above the strongest published system. Code is available at https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22666v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22666

**作者：** Suho Yoo、Youngjoon Jang、Hyebin Cho、Taeyeop Lee、Jaewook Jeong、Joon Son Chung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22666v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22666v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** VSpark 针对逐 token 生成机器人动作的自回归 VLA，采用轻量草稿模型提议、VLA 单次验证的投机解码，并结合动作噪声 rollout、隐藏特征对齐和被拒草稿续用来加速且保持无损。

**相关性简评：** 其草稿—验证、隐藏特征对齐与拒绝候选复用可能为 latent 推理状态的并行提议、验证及回退更新提供方法线索，但面向机器人动作解码，能否迁移到多模态推理尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models

**abstract/A1：** Our aim is to accelerate autoregressive vision-language-action models (VLAs), which generate robot actions one token at a time. Speculative decoding makes this possible: a lightweight draft model proposes several tokens at once, and the VLA verifies them in a single pass, accepting only those that match its own predictions, so decoding remains lossless. The speedup, however, is limited in robot control, where many different tokens encode nearly the same motion and a draft close to the correct action is still rejected. To address this, our approach, VSpark, is built upon three design choices: (i) rollouts collected with calibrated action noise, which expose the draft to a wider range of robot states; (ii) feature alignment, which trains the draft to match the VLA's hidden features rather than its output tokens alone; and (iii) continuation reuse, which keeps the predictions the VLA has already computed along rejected drafts as candidates for the next verification step, instead of discarding them. On four VLAs with different action tokenizers and four manipulation benchmarks, VSpark achieves 1.28x to 2.10x speedup while keeping the VLA's decoding lossless.

**comment/C1：** Preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [CascadeJEPA: Enhancing 3D Geometric Representations via Spatially Coupled Cascaded Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22686v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22686

**作者：** Kailai Zhou、Ziqi Chen、Xiaoning Xu、Yuefeng Wu、Zixuan Fu、Zijun Lin、Zhihao Li、Youwei Pang、Xiaoqi Zhao、Xun Cao、Bihan Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22686v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22686v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文分析 I-JEPA 中语义识别提升伴随 3D 几何表示退化的问题，提出 CascadeJEPA：以全网格上下文编码和跨阶段交叉注意力实现空间耦合的级联 latent 预测，在几何任务与语义识别上均优于 I-JEPA。

**相关性简评：** 其"上下文不完整导致预测偏向语义规律、丢失细粒度几何"的诊断与级联多阶段状态更新，可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留和多步 latent 推理监督的设计；但属纯视觉自监督预训练，与 VLM 推理仍有距离，迁移需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CascadeJEPA: Enhancing 3D Geometric Representations via Spatially Coupled Cascaded Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for predictive visual representation learning. However, our analysis of I-JEPA reveals a growing discrepancy between semantic and geometric learning: improved semantic recognition during pretraining is accompanied by degraded 3D geometric representations. We hypothesize that this partly stems from predicting highly abstract latent representations with incomplete spatial context, favoring semantic regularities over fine-grained geometric information. To address this issue, we introduce CascadeJEPA, a spatially coupled cascaded prediction framework that promotes richer patch-level interactions through full-grid context encoding and cross-attention between prediction stages, while largely preserving the original I-JEPA architecture. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements over I-JEPA across multiple 3D geometric tasks while maintaining strong semantic recognition performance. Furthermore, its advantages extend to future feature forecasting and world model planning, highlighting the potential of enhanced geometric representations to support predictive world modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [TANGO: Real-World Unregistered RGB-Guided Hyperspectral Super-Resolution with Two-Camera Acquisitions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22699v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22699

**作者：** Matteo Kolyszko、Marco Buzzelli、Simone Bianco、Raimondo Schettini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22699v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22699v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了双相机采集的真实未配准 RGB–HSI 超分数据集 DUET，并提出 TANGO：先以端到端全局对齐补偿跨相机主位移，再用由粗到细的局部特征对齐修正残余不一致，并用学习到的门控在双视角无法一致匹配处削弱 RGB 引导。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其依据跨模态一致性学习门控、在不可靠处抑制引导信号的做法，可能可供核查为 VLM 潜在推理中视觉证据筛选与视觉信息注入权重的设计线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TANGO: Real-World Unregistered RGB-Guided Hyperspectral Super-Resolution with Two-Camera Acquisitions

**abstract/A1：** Unregistered hyperspectral image super-resolution aims to recover a high-resolution HSI from a low-resolution hyperspectral observation and an unregistered high-resolution RGB reference. Although recent methods have begun to address the geometric misalignment between the two modalities, their evaluation is still constrained by the lack of a large-scale public benchmark based on real two-camera RGB--HSI acquisitions with a high-resolution HSI reference. To address this gap, we introduce DUET, the first large-scale public dataset for unregistered RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution, comprising 403 indoor and outdoor scenes captured with two separate RGB and hyperspectral cameras. We further propose TANGO (Two-camera AligNment with Gated Occlusions), which first compensates for the dominant cross-camera displacement through end-to-end global alignment and then refines the remaining inconsistencies with a coarse-to-fine local feature alignment. A learned gating mechanism further attenuates the RGB guidance where the two views cannot be consistently matched. Experimental results show that TANGO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both DUET and REAL across all evaluated scale factors, outperforming the strongest competitor on DUET by up to 2.29 dB in PSNR while requiring 2.5$\\times$ fewer FLOPs. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/FagiolottoConSombrero/TANGO-DUET

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 11 Figures, 10 tables. Submitted to CVPR 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [PCRG: Training-Free Pruning and Caching of Reference Tokens for Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22717v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22717

**作者：** Siyuan Wang、Haoran Li、Yi Yue、Tak L. Fung、Quan Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22717v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22717v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练、可插拔的 PCRG，用 VAE latent 的时间/邻域冗余度与 VLM 条件器给出的注意力重要性两个信号，把参考视频 token 分为剪枝、KV 缓存与保留三档，以缩短全注意力序列加速参考条件视频生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「冗余度＋VLM 注意力重要性」的分层筛选与 KV 复用，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据 token 的保留/缓存决策提供线索，也可供核查视觉旁路是否只保留低价值内容；但目标是视频生成加速，未涉及推理状态与答案使用，需核查注意力分数与推理必要性的对应关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PCRG: Training-Free Pruning and Caching of Reference Tokens for Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Reference-to-video diffusion transformers pack the variational autoencoder (VAE) latents of all references and the generated tokens into one full-attention sequence, in which reference tokens make up about half. Because attention cost grows quadratically with sequence length, these tokens take up an even larger share of the attention computation, yet most of them are static, smooth or irrelevant to the output. To accelerate reference-conditioned generation, we propose pruning and caching of reference tokens for video generation (PCRG), a training-free, plug-and-play method that uses two signals the model already computes to assign each reference token to one of three tiers, namely pruned, cached or kept. The first is a low-level redundancy r from VAE latents, since a token whose latent barely changes over time or across its neighbors adds little new content. The second is a high-level importance g from the vision-language-model (VLM) conditioner, where the attention that the prompt and any first or last frames pay to a token tells whether the output needs to read it. Tokens that are not redundant are kept, and redundant tokens are cached if important and pruned otherwise. Pruned tokens leave the sequence before the first step with all rotary position embedding positions unchanged. Cached tokens have their keys and values computed once and reused, and kept tokens are processed as usual. On MiniMax-H3, already distilled to 8 timesteps and quantized to int8, PCRG is 1.57x faster end to end on our V2V-Bench and 1.49x faster on the public VACE-Benchmark, while the quality of VBench drops by only 0.24% and 0.17%, respectively. PCRG shortens the sequence rather than changing how attention is computed or which steps are run, so it also stacks on step caching and sparse attention. On V2V-Bench it reaches 1.86x with TeaCache, 1.82x with Spark at nearly unchanged fidelity and 2.15x with Veda.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [HADVQ: Hessian Aware Directional Vector Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22721v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22721

**作者：** Daniil Li、Mars Davletshin、Vadim Klyzhenko、Artem Danilishin、Valery Sandul、Daniil Ryazanov、Ekaterina Muravleva

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22721v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22721v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 HADVQ，一种面向大模型超低位宽量化的向量量化流程：先用随机预条件正则化权重空间，再在极坐标下解耦方向与幅值的量化，方向部分用基于 E₈ 格的结构化码本，并以代理 Hessian 识别极敏感通道加以保留（额外约 0.16 bit）。

**相关性简评：** 其「方向/幅值解耦 + 格码本 + 敏感通道保留」可能为 LVR 中连续 latent 状态的离散化与任务关键维度保护提供可供核查的压缩思路，但原文只处理权重压缩，与推理期状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HADVQ: Hessian Aware Directional Vector Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Ultra-low-bit quantization of large language models is a challenging task because aggressive compression severely distorts model parameters and disproportionately harms a small number of highly sensitive channels. We present a vector quantization method tailored for this regime. Our approach begins with random preconditioning, which regularizes the weight space and makes it more amenable to quantization. We then represent weights in polar coordinates and decouple the quantization of directions and magnitudes. To quantize directions, we leverage the structure induced by random preconditioning and design a structured codebook based on the $E\_8$ lattice, yielding a compact yet expressive representation. To further reduce degradation, we detect extremely important channels using a proxy Hessian and preserve them with an additional cost of only 0.16 bits. Together, these components provide an effective quantization pipeline for LLMs in the extremely low-bit regime. Empirical results demonstrate improved accuracy, highlighting the benefit of combining geometric structure, lattice-based codebooks, and selective channel preservation.

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [Evolve in Panorama, Render in View: Panoramic World State Evolution for Video World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22733v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22733

**作者：** Longtao Jiang、Mingfei Han、Jianmin Bao、Tingjun Dai、Jinjie Wei、Zhiran Zhang、Xin Tao、Zhihui Li、Salman Khan、Xiaojun Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22733v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22733v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PanoEvo，在全景视频扩散模型的 latent 空间中持续演化世界状态，并用透视渲染器经 latent 投影与方向受限 cross-attention 生成清晰视角，同时构建事件级 360° 数据用于训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「未被观察方向仍在 latent 中持续更新、再按视角定向读取」的机制，可能为 LVR 中视域外视觉证据的保留、筛选与读取（含注意力结构对 latent 读取的影响）提供借鉴线索，但属视频世界模型，与推理任务的关联待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evolve in Panorama, Render in View: Panoramic World State Evolution for Video World Models

**abstract/A1：** Video world models should maintain a world that keeps evolving as the viewpoint changes. Yet when the camera turns away and returns, current models often freeze the unobserved scene, lose objects, or change their appearance, because content outside the view has no representation that keeps changing. We present PanoEvo, which uses a Panoramic Evolver to evolve the world state in the latent space of a panoramic video diffusion model, where every viewing direction is present at every time step, so the scene evolves whether or not it is observed. Existing panoramic video models are not trained to evolve a scene from a single image or the objects within it, so we build PanoEvo-Data, an event-centric dataset from real 360° videos, and train the Panoramic Evolver coarse-to-fine as an image-to-panoramic-video model. Since each view covers only a small part of the low-resolution panorama, directly projected views are blurry and often show low-quality details or structural errors. A Perspective Renderer, built on a perspective video diffusion model, reads this state via latent-space projection and direction-restricted cross-attention to render sharp, high-quality views. PanoEvo achieves the best overall score among 23 publicly evaluated models on WRBench and the highest total score on STEVO-Bench. Code, models, and data will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Can Activation Steering Substitute for Prompt-Based Affective Priming in LLM Digital Twins?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22738v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22738

**作者：** Junyi Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22738v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22738v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文检验激活引导能否替代提示式情感启动：在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 的 100 个人格上，将人类写作中提取的均值差方向注入残差流，并与真实写作提示路径比较效果及剂量反应。

**相关性简评：** 其「向残差流注入方向、按强度检验下游测量是否等价」的干预范式，可供核查 latent 状态是否真正承载任务信息及 latent 与显式推理轨迹的替代关系；但情感启动任务与 LVR 距离较远，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Activation Steering Substitute for Prompt-Based Affective Priming in LLM Digital Twins?

**abstract/A1：** Affective priming -- asking participants to write about a recalled event, such as a time they felt grateful or lacked control, and then measuring the induced state and its spillover onto related judgments -- is a standard way to manipulate emotion in the social sciences, and it also shifts the responses of LLM-based digital twins. The writing task, however, requires generating a full text. We test whether activation steering can replace it. Using Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on 100 personas in two domains (gratitude, lack of control), we compare a real-prompt path replicating the original Twin-2K-500 Mega Study (J2) with an injection path that skips the writing and adds a mean-difference direction, extracted from human writing, to the residual stream at varying strengths. The real-prompt effects fall between the original human and digital-twin effects in 5 of 6 comparisons. Injection shifts the manipulation check in a dose-dependent way and matches the real-prompt effect at one strength in the gratitude domain (beta ~ 2.7), but overshoots beyond it, on the proximal measure as well, and asymmetrically across the two directions. A structure-matched No-Injection baseline separates two sources of difference: omitting the writing task raises the proximal measure in both domains, whereas the injected direction itself moves a distal measure in the lack-of-control domain. Substitution is therefore measure-dependent: validity established for one way of inducing a state does not carry over uniformly to another.

**comment/C1：** Code, generation logs and analysis scripts: https://github.com/JunyiTim/twin-activation-injection

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [LoopPrune: Recurrent Pruning for Looped Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22757v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22757

**作者：** Weizhong Huang、Huaijia Kou、Yan Zhang、Fei Chao、Xiawu Zheng、Liujuan Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22757v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22757v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** LoopPrune 研究循环复用共享块的扩散 Transformer 的结构化剪枝，提出结合共享移除效应与循环位置差异的重要性估计，以及逐循环位置的恢复训练，并在四个开源模型上评估精度保持与显存、去噪速度收益。

**相关性简评：** 该文区分「共享移除效应」与「循环位置差异」来归因循环迭代的贡献、并按位置恢复，可能为 looped latent reasoning 中按迭代分配参数容量与贡献分配提供可供核查的线索，但其属图像生成剪枝，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoopPrune: Recurrent Pruning for Looped Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Looped diffusion Transformers reuse shared blocks to deepen computation and match larger models with fewer parameters, reducing storage and deployment burdens. Yet repeated attention and MLP computations remain costly. Pruning reduces storage and computation by removing redundant parameters or units. Training-free methods struggle to resolve recurrent contributions, while training-based methods lack explicit constraints on position-specific errors affecting later inputs and final outputs. We propose LoopPrune, a pruning and recovery framework addressing these requirements. Recurrent importance estimation combines shared-removal effects and position variation to select a shared structure; shared reconstruction pools recurrent input statistics to compensate weights. Loop-position recovery compares complete denoising predictions at identical student states and matches text guidance, updating all student parameters against a fixed dense teacher. Extensive experiments across four open-source models, six benchmarks, and three pruning paradigms demonstrate effectiveness. At 30% structured parameter pruning, Looped-DiT B/16 retains 93.14% of dense six-benchmark average accuracy, exceeding the best baseline by 9.49%. Across PyTorch, vLLM-Omni, and SGLang Diffusion, pruned SDXSv3 reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 34.36-44.64% and accelerates denoising 1.46-1.74x over dense models.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 11 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [PANO: Parallel Latent Space Neural Operator for Solving PDEs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22782v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22782

**作者：** Zijiang Yang、Xiaomeng Wu、Chen Kuang、Dongmei Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22782v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22782v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PANO，一种用于求解 PDE 的神经算子：用独立映射把观测压缩成多个不同的 latent 表示，在多个并行 latent 空间中学习物理交互，再融合解码结果预测物理场；并配套双向一致的编解码投影 CAP 与跨空间自适应更新的 PaLA 层。

**相关性简评：** 其「多种压缩模式并行 latent + 跨空间注意力更新」针对单一聚合导致信息不可见的失败，与 LVR 中多 latent 假设、视觉信息坍缩的担忧结构相似，可供核查是否能迁移为并行 latent 推理状态的构造与相互校正机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PANO: Parallel Latent Space Neural Operator for Solving PDEs

**abstract/A1：** Aggregating observations into a limited number of latent tokens and learning physical interactions in the latent space has emerged as an effective approach for Transformer-based neural operators to solve Partial Differential Equation (PDE) problems involving large-scale observations. However, existing methods typically compress observations using a single aggregation pattern, making physical information not retained by that pattern invisible to the latent space and thereby constraining physical interactions within it. To address this limitation, we propose PArallel Latent Space Neural Operator (PANO), a novel neural operator for solving PDEs that formulates physical interactions among observations as an additive combination of interaction results from parallel latent spaces with shared parameters. Specifically, PANO transforms observations into multiple distinct compressed representations through independent mappings, learns physical interactions in parallel based on these representations, and finally fuses the decoded results to predict the target physical field. Within PANO, we further propose Coupled Assignment Projection (CAP) to construct bidirectionally consistent encoding and decoding mappings, and Pairwise Latent Space Attention Layer (PaLA) to jointly update latent representations of each latent space through adaptive cross-space interactions. Extensive experiments on nine benchmarks spanning diverse physical systems, geometries, and observation scales demonstrate that PANO achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, PANO reduces equivalent plastic strain prediction errors on Unilateral Stamping by 28.3% (or more) and lift-coefficient prediction errors on AirCraft by 21.4% (or more) compared with existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/ZijiangY1116/PANO.

**comment/C1：** 39 pages, 24 figures, 23 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [OICycle: Observe, Infer, and Intervene Cycle for Belief-State Learning in Longitudinal Clinical Decision Making](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22826v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22826

**作者：** Jitindra Fartiyal、Pedro Freire、Sergei K. Turitsyn、Sergei G. Solovski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22826v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22826v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 OICycle：在临床回顾轨迹的每个 episode 上用循环算子把上一轮信念采样与新的多模态观察、相应干预融合，学习高斯信念状态，并以预测医生下次干预、下一 episode 间隔与死亡率作为训练信号，同时发布基于 MIMIC 系列的时序 episode 基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「动作条件下的循环信念更新 + 任务监督」以及历史增益集中在依赖先前发现的干预上这一分析，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态如何随证据与动作更新、旧表示如何保留必要信息；临床 POMDP 领域与视觉推理距离较远，迁移需检查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OICycle: Observe, Infer, and Intervene Cycle for Belief-State Learning in Longitudinal Clinical Decision Making

**abstract/A1：** Clinical care is a closed loop: clinicians observe partial evidence, infer the patient's state, and intervene, and each intervention determines what is observed next. This makes clinical decision making a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), in which a belief over a latent patient state must be updated from irregular, multimodal, and incomplete records. Yet most deep clinical models predict from a single snapshot or from coarsely aggregated history, while POMDP-based clinical methods need hand-specified discrete state spaces and transition models that real electronic health records cannot supply. We introduce OICycle (Observe, Infer, and Intervene Cycle), which learns a stochastic belief state directly from retrospective trajectories. At every episode, a learned recurrent operator fuses a sample of the previous belief with the new multimodal observations and the intervention that produced them. The resulting Gaussian belief is trained to predict clinicians' next interventions, the time to the next episode, and next-episode mortality. To enable reproducible evaluation, we release a benchmark derived from MIMIC-IV, MIMIC-IV-Note, and MIMIC-CXR that organizes 72,075 hospital admissions (5.4M laboratory, microbiology, radiology, prescription, and note records) into temporally ordered therapeutic--diagnostic episodes. Compared with an otherwise identical history-free model, OICycle improves next-intervention adaptive recall from 0.253 to 0.347, time-to-next-episode macro-F1 from 0.293 to 0.351, and next-episode mortality macro-F1 from 0.585 to 0.649. Analysis shows that the learned belief encodes intervention type and trajectory position and evolves smoothly across episodes, and that the benefit of history is concentrated in interventions whose ordering depends on prior findings.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Verifying Agents in Rubric-Graded Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22841v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22841

**作者：** Markus Dücker、Vaibhav Kumar、Yi Liu、Ronak Chaudhary、Andreas Plesner、Francisco Guzmán、Anish Athalye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22841v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22841v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统研究「rubric 评分」型 agent 环境的验证器：构建含 2,464 条人工判定评分标准的投资银行任务基准 BVB，归纳出九类验证能力与三项设计原则，并实现开源验证器 Gandalf，在多个基线与另一类环境上以更低成本取得相当或更高 F1。

**相关性简评：** 其「反应式验证」「与环境状态对齐」的多准则核验方法，可能可供核查如何判断 VLM 推理产出是否真正依据视觉证据、以及为推理轨迹构造 rubric 式监督与评价信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifying Agents in Rubric-Graded Environments

**abstract/A1：** As AI agents take on increasingly open-ended and complex tasks, verifying their outputs has proven to be difficult. Rubric-graded agent environments, where a verifier judges multiple natural-language criteria against the deliverables and environment state changes produced by the agent, have emerged as a popular paradigm. We conduct the first systematic study of verifiers for rubric-graded agent environments. First, we create BankerVerifierBench (BVB), a meta-evaluation dataset of 2,464 human-judged rubric criteria across 24 investment-banking tasks. Next, we develop a methodology that derives from any rubric corpus the capabilities a verifier should possess, yielding a nine-capability taxonomy that we distill into three general design principles---reactive verification, environment alignment, and domain guidance---which we implement in Gandalf, an open-source verifier. Finally, we evaluate Gandalf against three existing verifiers on BVB. Gandalf matches or exceeds every baseline's F1 at lower cost: with Gemini 3 Flash it matches the strongest baseline's F1 (0.864 vs. 0.852) at one-fifth of its cost, and with GPT-5.4 models it improves F1 by over 0.12 at lower cost. Gandalf also generalizes to OpenClaw, a structurally different personal-productivity environment, where it again achieves the highest F1 (0.955 vs. 0.934 for the best baseline). We release BVB, Gandalf, and the code and data to reproduce our experiments.

**comment/C1：** Presented at NeurIPS E&amp;D 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [SilverSpoon-KD: A General-Purpose Toolkit for Knowledge Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22842v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22842

**作者：** Xaver R. Davey、David Broman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22842v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22842v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出开源 PyTorch 工具包 SilverSpoon-KD，用统一 API 覆盖多种知识蒸馏范式与师生架构关系，并提供首个通用 blockwise 蒸馏实现，在视觉、掩码语言模型与因果语言模型上验证吞吐和 GPU 显存等性能。

**相关性简评：** 其异构架构与逐块蒸馏实现可能为把教师中间表示或 latent 推理状态迁移到结构不同的学生模型提供工程线索，但是否支持推理轨迹级监督需核查。论文本身不涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SilverSpoon-KD: A General-Purpose Toolkit for Knowledge Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge distillation transfers deep learning representations from a teacher model to a student model, typically reducing model size or inference cost. However, knowledge distillation tooling has not kept pace with the breadth of research methods: existing toolkits typically support only a limited subset of techniques and use cases. We present SilverSpoon-KD, an open-source PyTorch toolkit that unifies different distillation paradigms and architectural relationships across workloads and applications behind a single API. Moreover, SilverSpoon-KD offers the first general-purpose blockwise knowledge distillation implementation with unique scalability features. We validate its versatility across vision, masked language model, and causal language models on tasks spanning homogeneous, heterogeneous, and quantization distillation across paradigms. We also evaluate SilverSpoon-KD by comparing its performance, including throughput and GPU memory usage, with existing toolkits. Despite extensive support for convenience, metric calculation, and tracking, we have found SilverSpoon-KD's performance to be on par with or better than existing toolkits.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 10 figures, 11 tables. Code: https://github.com/silverspoon-dev/silverspoon-kd

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [Keeping Parts Honest: Isolated Part Graphs for Faithful Vision Transformer Explanations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22853v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22853

**作者：** Bernard Tomczyk、Gail Collyer-Hoar、Bryan Williams

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22853v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22853v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出可解释 ViT 分类器的部件级解释因自注意力跨 patch 混合而被全局上下文污染，提出将图像表示为「孤立部件图」，各部件仅由自身 patch 编码、边由部件对计算，并与每类学到的原型图匹配，使预测与解释由节点/边相似度直接构成。

**相关性简评：** 其「孤立编码」消融显示模型准确率大幅依赖跨区域信息泄漏，可作为核查 LVR 中视觉旁路、证据泄漏与 latent 内容是否真被使用的诊断线索；但该工作面向解释保真度而非推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Keeping Parts Honest: Isolated Part Graphs for Faithful Vision Transformer Explanations

**abstract/A1：** Part and prototype-based explanations for Vision Transformers (ViTs) present a decision as evidence from individual image regions. However, self-attention mixes information across patches, so part-level features absorb context from the whole image and consequently fail to faithfully reflect the decision. We show that existing interpretable ViT classifiers depend heavily on this leakage: when each explained region is encoded in isolation, their accuracy collapses, and retraining them under isolation recovers only part of the lost accuracy. We propose a classifier that represents each image as a graph of isolated parts. Each part is encoded only from its own patches by a ViT whose last blocks are fine-tuned under isolation, preventing evidence leakage from elsewhere in the image. Parts are discovered without annotation and refined hierarchically through their sub-parts, making them more consistent and stable across images than those of competing methods. Each edge is computed from part pairs, and recovers evidence that attention previously shared across the object without affecting the parts. Matching the image graph to a learned prototype graph per class makes the prediction a direct combination of node and edge similarities, each computed only from the patches it is credited to, so every explanation is faithful by construction. Across 11 fine-grained and general-object benchmarks, our method exceeds the accuracy of existing explainable methods that rely on leakage, while providing explanations that reflect what the model actually uses.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables. Supplementary material will be added in a later version

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [Beyond Co-Generation: Rethinking Jointness in Joint Audio-Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22857v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22857

**作者：** Jihoo Jung、Ji-Hoon Kim、Suyeon Lee、Gia Thinh Pham、Joon Son Chung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.AS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22857v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22857v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「intrinsic jointness」概念，用语义欠指定的 prompt 检验 T2AV 模型在音视频细节上是否产生一致的实现，发现现有模型该能力有限，并提出基于表示对齐的后训练方法 JointREPA 及对应基准 UNSAIDBench。

**相关性简评：** 其以预训练表示对齐约束跨模态细节一致性的后训练思路，可能为 latent 推理状态的训练监督与视觉细节保持提供可核查线索；但论文聚焦音视频生成，不涉及推理期状态生成或答案对 latent 内容的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Co-Generation: Rethinking Jointness in Joint Audio-Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Our aim is to advance the understanding of genuine audio-visual coordination in joint text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation, with particular emphasis on semantic consistency. Evaluating audio-visual consistency in T2AV generation is nontrivial, as detailed text prompts obscure whether such consistency stems from genuine cross-modal coordination or merely from independent text following. To isolate the former, we introduce intrinsic jointness: the ability of audio and video to produce the same realization for details left underspecified by the prompt. Using semantically underspecified prompts (e.g., "musical instrument playing" rather than "electric guitar playing"), we investigate four recent models and reveal their limited intrinsic jointness. To address this limitation, we propose JointREPA, a simple representation alignment-based post-training method to facilitate cross-modal coordination. Since existing benchmarks use semantically detailed prompts, we introduce UNSAIDBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate intrinsic jointness under semantic underspecification. Experiments across two distinct models and three benchmarks, including UNSAIDBench, demonstrate that JointREPA consistently improves semantic consistency.

**comment/C1：** Preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22859v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22859

**作者：** Christoffer Löffler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22859v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22859v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对长尾多标签分类，提出标签依赖的 within-label 估计量、一条只承认伴随性召回下降的判读规则，以及仅用训练标签即可在训练前给出召回上限的统计界，并在 23 个语料、7 种训练目标和 5 类标签相关机制上测量尾部标签被共现标签「代答」的程度。

**相关性简评：** 其把报告指标拆成「依赖捷径所得」与「独立能力所得」的度量与事前上界思路，可能可供核查 LVR/VLM 中答案究竟由视觉证据还是语言先验等旁路支撑；该工作属单模态分类测量，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification

**abstract/A1：** A rare label seldom occurs alone, and a classifier can recover it from the frequent labels it co-occurs with, its companions, without having learned the label itself. Existing diagnostics of this need a trained model: none says before training whether a corpus admits the failure, or what the standard aggregate hides. Macro-F1, like any measure built from per-label confusion counts, is unchanged by how a label's recall divides between occurrences with and without a companion. We introduce a within-label estimand of that division, a reading rule under which a fall in dependence counts only if companion-free recall rises, and a statistic of training labels that bounds, before training, how far reported recall can exceed companion-free recall. Across 23 corpora, 7 training objectives and five families of label-correlation mechanisms, companion-free recall falls short on all six corpora where the estimand is defined. Up to 18.4% of the tail true positives macro-F1 credits would vanish at the companion-free rate, within the training-label bound on every corpus. No long-tail objective reduces the dependence, but architecture moves it. Models whose tail score is computed from other labels' predictions are strongly dependent.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 3 figures, 13 tables. Submitted to Information Sciences

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [LOCUS: Causal Support Windows for Attribute Control in Autoregressive Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22867v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22867

**作者：** Zijun Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22867v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22867v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究自回归图像生成中属性干预效果的序列位置依赖性，提出「因果支持剖面」测量各窗口内 patch image-token 状态能改变渲染属性的频率，并据此将测试时 latent 优化路由到对应窗口（LOCUS）以修复多属性失败。

**相关性简评：** 其按窗口测量 latent 状态 patch 对输出的因果影响、并区分「结构属性靠前窄窗口、外观属性靠后宽窗口」的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中干预位置与深度如何影响视觉信息的因果作用与贡献分配；但研究对象是图像生成而非 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LOCUS: Causal Support Windows for Attribute Control in Autoregressive Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Unified autoregressive (AR) image generators struggle to satisfy multiple attribute constraints in a single prompt. Although these attributes are decodable from image-token latents, editing those latents at test time remains difficult. Adding a fixed attribute direction to the prefix can reinforce a requested attribute but rarely overrides a conflicting one, such as turning a car red when the prompt specifies blue. We investigate how the effect of an intervention depends on where it acts in the image-token sequence. We introduce the causal support profile, which measures, for each attribute, how often patching image-token states within a given window flips the rendered attribute. Structural attributes (count and position) are most responsive in early, narrow windows, while appearance attributes (color, size, and material) respond over later, broader windows. A budget-matched comparison of early and middle windows shows that the effect of window location depends on the target attribute, and two-dimensional support maps show that the responsive regions track spatial content. Based on these profiles, we propose LOCUS (Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows), which diagnoses which attribute failed and routes test-time latent optimization to its corresponding support window. With the generator, optimizer, reward, verifier, and token budget held fixed, support-routed windows outperform late windows by 23.6 percentage points in GenEval++ macro accuracy. With the generator frozen, LOCUS raises Janus-Pro's GenEval++ accuracy from 24.6% to 46.8% overall and from 19.5% to 40.5% on multi-attribute tags. On GenEval, LOCUS repairs 48.1% of failed generations in one rollout, compared with 37.4% for prefix optimization, and needs 14% fewer rounds on average to succeed.

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Outcome-Balanced PPO and Policy-Induced Flow Rectification for Flow-Based VLA Policies](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22878v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22878

**作者：** Yuhan Wang、Ruiyang Wang、Jiaguan Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22878v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22878v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个面向 flow-based VLA 策略的强化学习后训练框架，将按任务成功率重加权优势的 Outcome-Balanced PPO 与周期性用当前策略生成的 ODE 端点对重拟合线性桥 flow 的「策略诱导校正」结合，在仿真操作基准及一项 Real2Sim2Real 任务上考察性能、优化行为与 flow 一致性。
