**相关性简评：** 策略更新会使速度场几何与用于随机转移的 score 近似失配，因而需周期性重拟合生成表示，这一「生成器随策略漂移而重校准」线索可供核查是否适用于 latent 推理轨迹训练中的分布偏移与监督更新；但该文针对机器人动作生成，与视觉推理状态本身联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Outcome-Balanced PPO and Policy-Induced Flow Rectification for Flow-Based VLA Policies

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) enables post-training of flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies by optimizing expected task return through environment interaction. However, this optimization raises two challenges: policy updates can alter the velocity-field geometry underlying the score approximation used for stochastic transitions, and sparse multi-task rollouts can yield uneven learning signals across tasks and advantage signs. We introduce an RL framework that combines Outcome-Balanced Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with policy-induced flow rectification. Outcome-Balanced PPO reweights positive advantages using task success-rate estimates and rescales positive and negative advantages while preserving their signs. Policy-induced rectification periodically fits a linear-bridge flow to ODE endpoint pairs generated by the current policy, refreshing the representation used in subsequent stochastic optimization. We evaluate the framework on simulated manipulation benchmarks and additionally study a component combination in a Real2Sim2Real task, examining task performance, optimization behavior, and flow consistency. The proposed framework improves success rates over the evaluated baselines across the simulated benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Beyond Attribute-Centric Fairness: Modelling and Mitigating Unified Bias Representation in Omni-modal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22889v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22889

**作者：** Runqi Lin、Suqin Yuan、Zihao Fu、Chris Russell

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22889v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22889v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 OmniDebias，面向全模态大模型，利用某属性在不同语义上下文中的激活不一致性把偏见与合法知识分离，并将多属性、多模态偏见归并为共享表示，再用后处理编辑与微调统一缓解。

**相关性简评：** 其「按上下文激活差异定位内部子空间再编辑」的表示级线索，可供核查能否用于区分 LVR 中任务必要的视觉证据与干扰内容、以及剖析 latent 状态构成；但论文目标是公平性而非推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Attribute-Centric Fairness: Modelling and Mitigating Unified Bias Representation in Omni-modal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Omni-modal large language models (OLLMs) provide joint multimodal understanding and generation capabilities, making them well-suited to complex environments and promising backbones for next-generation AI. Despite these advances, the harmful stereotypes encoded in such models manifest in increasingly diverse forms, leading to broader and more severe societal consequences. Existing debiasing methods typically impose separate fairness objectives for each sensitive attribute and modality to minimise demographic-dependent variations. However, in omni-modal settings, this attribute-centric paradigm struggles to jointly address diverse social biases with distinct fairness objectives and to generalise beyond predefined attributes. To this end, we propose OmniDebias, one of the first methods for promoting fairness in OLLMs, which employs a bias-centric formulation to extract and mitigate a shared bias representation across attributes and modalities while preserving attribute-related knowledge. Specifically, we exploit activation inconsistencies of a given attribute (e.g., gender) across semantic contexts (e.g., occupations) to disentangle bias from legitimate knowledge. We further consolidate multiple extracted biases into a shared representation, formulating a unified objective that captures the underlying structure across social biases and generalises to unseen attributes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that eliminating unified bias with both post-hoc editing and fine-tuning can consistently improve fairness across diverse attributes and modalities in OLLMs while maintaining favourable utility.

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [Impact of Pretraining Length, Model Size, and Annotation Budget on Downstream Clinical Tasks of Foundation Models in Radiotherapy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22896v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22896

**作者：** Alejandro Cortina Uribe、Sidsel Højklint Poulsen、Ivan Richter Vogelius、Jens Petersen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22896v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22896v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在约15万张原始3D放疗CBCT上以masked image modeling预训练ViT编码器，考察预训练时长、模型规模、LoRA与全量微调及标注数据量对下游临床分类（喂养管、性别、分期等）的影响，并检验预训练期验证损失、effective rank、linear probing与下游表现的相关性。

**相关性简评：** 其以effective rank和linear probing作为预训练表示质量诊断的做法，可能为核查LVR中latent状态的信息坍缩及内容是否被下游使用提供可借鉴的度量线索，但这些指标跨模态迁移的可靠性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Impact of Pretraining Length, Model Size, and Annotation Budget on Downstream Clinical Tasks of Foundation Models in Radiotherapy

**abstract/A1：** Cone-beam CT (CBCT) scans acquired during radiotherapy capture changes such as tumor shrinkage and radiation-induced tissue changes, offering great potential for foundation models that help monitor individual treatment responses. However, to our knowledge, no large-scale pretrained models for radiotherapy CBCT exist, and evaluating modeling choices is difficult since downstream tasks are not known in advance. We examine how pretraining length, model size, and finetuning strategy relate to downstream clinical performance, and how metrics tracked during pretraining correlate with it. We pretrain vision transformer encoders with masked image modeling on about 150,000 raw 3D radiotherapy CBCT scans, tracking representation quality via validation loss, effective rank, and linear probing. Downstream tasks are classification of feeding tube presence ($n=51,135$ CBCTs, $1,562$ patients) and clinical characteristics (T-, N-stage, sex, age, tumor site; $n=1,278$ patients) in head and neck cancer. We compare low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and full finetuning against no pretraining, varying annotated dataset size. Pretext validation loss was the most consistent predictor of downstream performance; effective rank and linear probing were also associated, but not consistently across model sizes and finetuning methods. LoRA performed comparably to full finetuning while training only 0.5-2.5% of the parameters. Fully finetuned models consistently outperformed models without pretraining across all sizes, with AUROC 0.91-0.97 vs. 0.58-0.85 for feeding tube classification and 0.99-1.00 vs. 0.71-0.97 for sex classification. Model size had only a small, inconsistent effect, suggesting smaller models suffice at lower computational cost. Code and pretrained models (non-commercial license): https://github.com/alexCortinaU/RT-CBCT\_SSL

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables. Supplementary Material included

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [You Only Encode Once: Sub-Pixel-Shifted Frames for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution with 2D Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22898v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22898

**作者：** Canjie Liu、Liuqian Wang、Qi Ming、Wei Lu、Xiaohan Hao、Yuefei Wu、Wentao Chao、Lei Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22898v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22898v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SPS-GS，一种「只编码一次」的遥感任意尺度超分框架：用单幅高分辨率图合成多相位低分辨率观测，先经傅里叶对齐与相位感知残差融合把多帧信息合并，再做一次深度编码，并以多观测反投影迭代精化 2D 高斯中心与颜色以支持任意尺度渲染。

**相关性简评：** 其「编码前融合多帧互补信息、避免重复编码」的路线可供核查是否能降低视觉细节进入内部状态的编码成本，而反投影式高斯状态精化也可能为多证据整合与状态更新提供线索，但需确认其表示是否可服务推理而非仅重建。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** You Only Encode Once: Sub-Pixel-Shifted Frames for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution with 2D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Arbitrary-scale super-resolution (ASSR) of remote sensing images remains challenging due to the limited high-frequency information in a single low-resolution (LR) observation. While multi-frame SR can exploit complementary sub-pixel information, existing approaches often require real multi-frame training sequences and repeated feature encoding, increasing data acquisition demands and computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose SPS-GS (Sub-Pixel-Shifted Gaussian Splatting), an encode-once framework that learns from multi-phase LR observations synthesized from individual high-resolution images, without requiring real multi-frame training sequences. Specifically, Phase-Coherent Aliasing Demodulation (PCAD) exploits shift-dependent aliasing through Fourier-based alignment, phase-aware residual fusion, and least-squares aliasing estimation, enabling multi-frame information to be integrated before a single deep encoding pass. Gaussian Projection Alignment (GPA) then iteratively refines Gaussian centers and colors using multi-observation back-projection to promote observation consistency. The refined Gaussians support continuous rendering at arbitrary scales. Extensive experiments on five remote sensing datasets demonstrate strong reconstruction performance against single-image and multi-frame SR baselines, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency at common scales. The code will be publicly released.

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Code World Model 2.0: Towards Infinite Controllable Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22925v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22925

**作者：** Ruoyu Wang、Hao Zhong、Yanming Yang、Jiahao Tian、Lin Liu、Biao Gong、Guoshan Lu、Yiding Tian、Chunhua Shen、Zhenzhong Lan、Chi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22925v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22925v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Invoker-Render，以结构代理、外观参考和语言为共享条件接口，将双向创作模型蒸馏为四步因果流式渲染模型，并用 Progressive Forcing 从训练视频历史过渡到自生成 rollout，实现长时稳定的可控视频生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「先以真实历史蒸馏、再转入自生成轨迹」的训练安排，可能可供核查 LVR 多步推理轨迹训练中的误差累积与监督分配；但主体是写实视频渲染与流式部署，与视觉推理状态本身联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Code World Model 2.0: Towards Infinite Controllable Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Visual proxies decouple explicit world-state simulation from photorealistic video synthesis, offering a controllable interface for both offline clip creation and real-time interactive streaming. Yet a practical renderer must address two challenges. Upstream proxies vary from coarse layouts to fine articulated geometry depending on the interaction, and streaming inference must operate in a few causal steps without accumulating drift across self-generated history. We present Invoker-Render, pairing a bidirectional creation model with a causal four-step streaming model under a shared conditioning interface over structural proxies, an appearance reference, and language. Trained on an aligned approximately 200-hour multi-granularity corpus, Invoker-Render honors fine geometric constraints when supplied while synthesizing plausible local motion under coarse proxies, enabling runtime selection of input detail. To bridge bidirectional creation and real-time streaming without compounding step compression and rollout drift, we introduce Progressive Forcing (ProForcing). ProForcing first trains a causal predictor and distills it into a four-step renderer using histories derived from training videos. Training then shifts to continuous self-generated rollouts. A pipelined serving stack supports efficient streaming inference. Experiments show versatile control across proxy families, granularities, and camera paths, consistent gains over recent streaming baselines, and stable 30-minute racing rollouts that preserve scene and subject appearance.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://www.inclusion-ai.org/CodeWorldModel/

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [The Kernel Reality Check: Benchmarking and Distilling Efficient Attention at Scale](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22947v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22947

**作者：** Fırat Öncel、Cem Subakan、Mirco Ravanelli、Çağatay Yıldız

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22947v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22947v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对 kernelized（线性）注意力做统一评测与蒸馏实验：发现相比 FlashAttention 的效率优势多数难以复现，统一蒸馏协议下与 softmax 存在 2–10 分质量差距，并提出用自适应提案分布重要性采样的 LARA++，最多恢复约 99% 的 softmax 精度，但开销仍高。

**相关性简评：** 其「统一蒸馏协议＋近似 softmax 分布」可供核查近似注意力是否会丢失视觉证据细节，且论文给出的训练/推理时间与内存口径可能用于 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK 阶段的计算成本核算；但论文不含 latent 推理状态设计，仅为方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Kernel Reality Check: Benchmarking and Distilling Efficient Attention at Scale

**abstract/A1：** Kernelized attention methods, which aim to replace the $O(N^2)$ complexity of softmax attention with $O(N)$ alternatives, have been the focus of intense research in the last five years. Despite growing interest, systematic evaluations and fair comparisons across approaches and scales are still missing. We analyze kernelized attention in two halves and highlight prominent limitations on each. On efficiency, FlashAttention achieves lower memory consumption and faster wall-clock time than SOTA kernelized methods during training and in vision transformers across most sequence lengths (with inference-time memory in causal language models the only clear exception), contradicting the core assumption for pure kernelized attention used as a drop-in replacement for softmax. We also discover that reported efficiency gains often fail to reproduce against modern baselines. On quality, current kernelized methods leave gaps of 2-10 points relative to softmax under a unified distillation protocol, and model scala alone does not close these gaps. We show this gap is not intrinsic to kernelization: LARA++, a principled extension of LARA that approximates softmax via importance sampling with adaptive proposal distributions, recovers up to 99% of softmax accuracy. We establish that the gap is closable; however, the computational overhead remains substantial, leaving the efficiency wall intact. Code is available at https://github.com/firatoncel/KernelRealityCheckNeurIPS2026

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 20 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [Is model self-assessment enough for diverse LVLM ensembles?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22956v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22956

**作者：** Michael Smith、Frank P. Ferrie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22956v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22956v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文建立统一的候选答案选择流程，在 34 种置信度估计、24 种集成配置、20 个数据集与两种 prompt 下评测 LVLM 集成，发现无需训练与参数拟合的自我评估式选择即可优于单个成员模型，但数据、成员构成与措辞等混杂因素会显著影响收益。

**相关性简评：** 其“仅凭各模型自评置信度做候选选择”的免训练方案，可供核查 LVR 中如何从多个 latent 候选或推理轨迹中挑选答案、以及自评信号何时可靠；所揭示的评价混杂变量也提示 latent 选择的对照设置需谨慎。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Is model self-assessment enough for diverse LVLM ensembles?

**abstract/A1：** Ensembles of diverse Large Vision Language Models have demonstrated performance beyond that of single models across multiple datasets and tasks. However, the necessary aggregation process usually relies on training, parameter fitting, or complex task-specific pipelines. Furthermore, evaluation is limited, making a fair comparison difficult. We create a unified pipeline where we perform candidate response selection: we build an ensemble by selecting the best answer from a set provided by different models. We comprehensively evaluate across 34 different means of confidence estimation, 24 ensemble configurations, 20 datasets, and two prompt specifications. We show that simple training and parameter-free approaches can be sufficient, even for ensembles of different architectures. These simple self-assessment approaches rely solely on each constituent model's confidence, without explicit calibration, yet are able to perform better than said constituent models. They are also competitive with more involved alternatives on standard metrics such as accuracy, including on open-ended problems. However, we also discover that dataset, ensemble composition, and prompt wording are key confounding variables insufficiently explored by prior work that significantly affect ensemble performance, such that the ensemble can perform worse than its best constituent model. These must be optimized for to get the most out of ensembles, at the cost of limiting their generalizability. We recommend that practitioners consider training-free ensembles only if deployment conditions can be precisely characterized, with Best-of-N configurations of strong models being a good starting point.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [MI-Hyper: Generating Multiple Instance Models for Weight Space Transfer Learning in Histopathology via Mixed Tasks Pretrained HyperNetwork](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22968v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22968

**作者：** Xitong Ling、Yousef Kotp、Weiming Chen、Yining Li、Jiawen Li、Minxi Ouyang、Xinyi Guo、Mingxi Fu、Yizhi Wang、Tian Guan、Lianghui Zhu、Yonghong He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22968v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22968v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MI-Hyper：在 52 个病理数据集、308 个混合任务上预训练超网络，按任务描述生成 MIL 模型的聚合与分类权重，先联合训练生成器与共享 MIL 基座、再冻结基座微调生成器，从而在零样本 WSI 预测上超过 MI-Zero。

**相关性简评：** 任务条件化的权重生成被用于长序列 patch 证据的聚合与判别，可能为 LVR 中「按任务筛选并汇聚视觉证据」提供线索；但其生成的是分类器权重而非推理期 latent 状态，且无动态计算与停止机制，联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MI-Hyper: Generating Multiple Instance Models for Weight Space Transfer Learning in Histopathology via Mixed Tasks Pretrained HyperNetwork

**abstract/A1：** Zero-shot whole-slide image (WSI) prediction aims to generalize to new pathology tasks without parameter updates at deployment. MI-Zero makes these predictions by pooling patch-text similarities. Conditional weight generation, combined with supervision from diverse pathology tasks, offers an alternative: learning to construct predictive rules from task descriptions. We introduce MI-Hyper, a hypernetwork pretrained across mixed tasks to generate the aggregation and classification weights of multiple instance learning (MIL) models. We first train the generator and a shared MIL basis jointly using source labels. We then freeze the basis and refine the generator. The generated task weights are reused across cases. Across 308 tasks from 52 pathology datasets, MI-Hyper improves mean AUC, balanced accuracy and macro-F1 over MI-Zero by 1.85, 1.17 and 0.57 percentage points, respectively. On some molecular prediction tasks, both AUC and balanced accuracy improve by more than 20 points. These results support task-conditioned weight generation as a promising approach to weight-space transfer for long-sequence modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [SpikWM: Efficient End-to-End Spiking World Modeling for Visual Planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22981v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22981

**作者：** Shuhan Ye、Hongbin Yu、Chong Wang、Hui Lu、Jiaming He、Yi Yu、Jiangbo Qian、Xudong Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22981v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22981v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SpikWM：一个端到端的脉冲神经网络 JEPA 世界模型，通过区域池化保留空间线索、双脉冲动作编码区分正负响应，并用带可学习时间常数的膜电位预测在 latent 历史间传递阈下证据，在无奖励的图像—动作轨迹上联合训练编码器与预测器，用于目标条件视觉规划。

**相关性简评：** 其动作条件的 latent 状态转移与跨历史膜电位累积可能为 LVR 的循环状态更新与 latent 轨迹训练提供线索，稀疏脉冲计算与能耗估算也可供核查「THINK/STOP」的计算成本维度；但工作面向世界模型规划，未涉及 VLM 推理的视觉证据筛选与停止决策，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpikWM: Efficient End-to-End Spiking World Modeling for Visual Planning

**abstract/A1：** Goal-conditioned planning with image observations and goals requires predictions that distinguish visual states and action consequences. Spiking neural networks offer sparse computation, but single-step spikes can obscure motion cues and merge actions with different physical outcomes. We propose the Spiking Latent World Model (SpikWM), an end-to-end joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) with one simulation step per image and one neural update per latent position. Spiking Regional Encoding retains spatial cues through regional pooling. Bi-Spike Action Encoding combines positive and negative binary responses, while Causal Membrane Prediction carries subthreshold evidence across the latent history with learned group-wise time constants. The encoder and predictor are trained jointly on reward-free image--action trajectories using next-embedding prediction and SIGReg. With a common planner, SpikWM achieves mean success rates of $88.67\\%$, $97.33\\%$, $99.33\\%$, and $86.67\\%$ on Push-T, Two-Room, Reacher, and OGBench-Cube, respectively, outperforming two spiking baselines. Push-T ablations support each module, and controlled replays examine responses to appearance and physical state changes. Under a matched planning budget, SpikWM reduces estimated arithmetic energy by $75.6\\%$ relative to LeWM. Code will be released at https://github.com/ShawnYE618/SpikWM.

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [RagHarnessDebugger: Jev-Guided Agentic RAG in Contract Inference Repair Auditing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22991v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22991

**作者：** Moriya Dechtiar、Daniel Martin Katz、Michael J. Bommarito、Hongming Wang、Sylvain Jaume

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22991v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22991v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RagHarnessDebugger，用于 agentic RAG 合同推断：记录每次修复决策，审计轨迹完整性、验证与候选的对应关系及接受规则的覆盖范围，并借助标注错误分析与重放追溯缺失修复的根因。

**相关性简评：** 其“是否、何时、如何修复”的接受规则审计与反事实重放，可能为 LVR 的证据筛选、停止决策和逐步贡献归因提供可核查的检查思路；但对象为纯文本 RAG，不涉及视觉 latent 状态。｜DSML｜ parameter&gt;

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RagHarnessDebugger: Jev-Guided Agentic RAG in Contract Inference Repair Auditing

**abstract/A1：** Agentic systems using retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must repair their own answers by gathering more evidence, testing alternative policies and deciding if, when, and what repairs are needed. Yet how do we know if a system's repairs are working when metrics like accuracy can be misleading? We present RagHarnessDebugger, a framework that records every repair decision in a Jev-guided harness for contract inference and audits the record to facilitate complete debugging of all decisions taken during a RAG repair journey. RagHarnessDebugger checks that each trace is intact, that each validation applies to the candidate it is counted for, and which answers the acceptance rule can reach. We tested our approach on ContractNLI non-disclosure agreements with two reader models and a replication set of separate contracts. Our debugger's audit layer exposed failure patterns that were hidden under aggregate metrics, policies acceptance cost of repairs vs regressions and policy acceptance gap of 77% and 76% of available but unrealised repairs on development and replication sets. Our audit, together with a labeled error analysis and replay mechanism, allowed following the execution path all the way to the terminal decision - uncovering where missing repairs became lost. Our revised harness improved accuracy over the initial BM25-only baseline by 3.6 points on development contracts and 3.7 points on the replication set, and even improved our full contract access reference score by 1.1 on development and 2.4 on replication set. While our work here demonstrates how the debugger can conduct decision root-cause analysis for missed repairs, the true value lies with providing this debugger for judgment visibility serving continuous improvement of RAG decisions and their explainability. Keywords: agentic RAG, AI evidence repair, harness explainability, counterfactual replay, Jev, ContractNLI

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [CAST: Calibrating Correspondences for Training-Free Biomedical In-Context Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22995v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22995

**作者：** Mingze Jiang、Jiashu Han、Haonan Chen、Zhiyi Li、Yilun Du、Kunzan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22995v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22995v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CAST：一个基于冻结 DINOv3 的无训练生物医学 in-context 分割框架。论文用稳定性测试在全局与 patch 级校准特征可靠性，并用一致性测试判断候选对应是否得到局部结构上下文支持，以改善跨结构、跨受试者与跨模态的细粒度对应；在八个生物医学基准上报告 Dice 提升。

**相关性简评：** 该文本身是无需训练的分割对应方法，但其中「稳定性筛选特征、局部上下文一致性校验对应」的线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 相似度可靠性判断的做法；尚未涉及推理期状态或答案使用 latent 内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAST: Calibrating Correspondences for Training-Free Biomedical In-Context Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Biomedical image segmentation must keep up with new anatomical structures, subjects, imaging modalities, and acquisition protocols, yet dense expert annotations are scarce and models trained on one cohort often struggle to transfer. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers a way out: a new structure is specified by a few annotated exemplars at inference time. However, reliable correspondence remains challenging in biomedical images, where adjacent structures often share similar appearances, and variations across subjects and imaging conditions further obscure their distinctions. We introduce CAST, a training-free framework built on a frozen DINOv3 backbone to calibrate fine-grained correspondences. Its stability test improves feature reliability at both global and patch-specific levels, while its coherence test assesses whether candidate correspondences are supported by consistent local structural context. Together, these tests enable more reliable correspondence across diverse anatomical structures and imaging conditions. Across eight biomedical benchmarks spanning 2D and 3D segmentation, cross-subject, and cross-modality transfer, CAST outperforms the strongest overall baseline by 12.34 percentage points in Dice and ranks first across all six volumetric settings. The versatility of CAST further opens up opportunities for challenging biomedical applications, including 2D-to-3D segmentation and atlas label transfer.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Residual-Guided Lightweight Reinforcement Learning for Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection with a Frozen World Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22998v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22998

**作者：** Jiaqi Wu、Yongqi Dong、Yijie Zhong、Maple Shao、Yide Tao、Linsen Cai、Weize Cai、Hai L. Vu、Alvaro Garcia Hernandez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.ET、cs.LG、cs.RO、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22998v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22998v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在冻结世界模型与固定候选库下，用 PPO 联合学习候选轨迹选择与有界残差，并训练上下文条件排序网络，从所选轨迹局部扰动的评估反馈中学习候选偏好；部署时只保留约 1.2 万参数的排序网络，在 nuScenes 开环评测中降低 L2。

**相关性简评：** 其「对选中候选做局部扰动、依评估反馈学习偏好排序」以及训练期残差/评委只用于训练的做法，可能为 LVR 中候选 latent 假设的筛选、贡献分配与轻量化部署提供可核查线索，但论文本身面向驾驶轨迹选择，不涉及多模态内部推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Residual-Guided Lightweight Reinforcement Learning for Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection with a Frozen World Model

**abstract/A1：** In automated driving, world models provide future scene predictions for evaluating candidate trajectories, but fixed selection rules may not capture scene-dependent trade-offs. We propose a residual-guided reinforcement learning (RL) framework for lightweight trajectory selection with a frozen world model and a fixed candidate bank. The framework uses proximal policy optimization (PPO) to fine-tune a joint policy over candidate selection and bounded trajectory residuals. A context-conditioned ranking network learns candidate preferences from feedback obtained by evaluating local perturbations of the selected trajectories. At inference, the selector combines learned preference and predicted risk ranks using fixed weights and returns the selected candidate trajectory without modification. Fine-tuning updates 16,106 parameters across the actor and critic, while the deployed selector retains only the 11,725-parameter ranking network, with the residual branch and critic used exclusively during training. In open-loop evaluation on the nuScenes validation set, the method reduces average Euclidean trajectory error (L2) by 3.99% relative to the RiskWorld model baseline without RL-based selection. Under matched budgets for feedback accesses and actor updates, residual exploration reduces L2 by 0.83% relative to the categorical PPO variant trained without residuals. The method also maintains lower L2 than RiskWorld when risk features are removed or shuffled.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [ForecastGym: A Live, Controlled, and Replayable Benchmark for Forecasting Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23045v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23045

**作者：** Yicheng Tao、Yuante Li、Zipei Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23045v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23045v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ForecastGym：定期记录商业搜索 API 对预测市场问题的返回结果，并在结果揭晓后离线重放固定证据，以比较模型、搜索源和记忆策略。

**相关性简评：** 其固定证据获取与证据状态重放的设计可供核查如何分离视觉证据获取与 latent 推理的贡献；「持久笔记」与重读全部证据的对比也可能为压缩推理状态及证据筛选评价提供线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ForecastGym: A Live, Controlled, and Replayable Benchmark for Forecasting Agents

**abstract/A1：** Agents built on large language models (LLMs) increasingly forecast real-world events by searching the Web and reasoning over what they find. How well such an agent forecasts depends on both the retrieved evidence and the model, making control over that evidence essential for evaluation. Live evaluations can mix retrieval with reasoning when each system searches on its own, while retrospective search can leak later information through date filters or today's ranking of historical pages. Comparing models, search sources, and memory strategies requires both fixed evidence and a record of how it evolved before resolution. We introduce ForecastGym, which records what four commercial web search APIs return for open prediction-market questions every two days and replays this frozen evidence offline once outcomes are known, comparing models, memory, and captured search sources under a fixed evidence-acquisition policy. Its first release covers 150 questions, 614 evidence states, one per question and collection time, and 131k search results. Rerunning the same queries after settlement returns results that all but state the outcome for up to 92% of the questions, despite the tested date filters. On the frozen evidence, all tested LLMs trail the market in forecasting accuracy. Our diagnostic analysis suggests that missed public information contributes more to the gap in high-gap states than misreading does. Switching the search API changes a model's Brier score by up to 0.043, more than the spread across models, while persistent notes score similarly to rereading all evidence at half the input tokens. The evidence a forecaster sees can matter as much as the model that reads it.

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Refin3R: Geometry-Guided Refinement for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23051v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23051

**作者：** Jongmin Lee、Jaeyeong Kim、Seungryong Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23051v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23051v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Refin3R，用 SfM 稀疏点云精化前馈式点图：2D 分支借助几何感知骨干特征把 SfM 几何扩展到其覆盖范围之外，3D 分支在所有视图的点集上建模多视图结构，两支路双向交互以输出稠密准确点图。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏几何证据在稠密网格上传播 + 跨视图双向状态交换」的结构，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据注入与 latent 状态更新的设计提供核查线索，但论文本身不涉及推理或答案生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Refin3R: Geometry-Guided Refinement for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models predict dense point maps from multiple images in a single pass. Without explicit geometric constraints, however, their predictions are often inconsistent across views and lack fine-grained precision. Recent methods incorporate geometric priors such as camera pose or depth, but use them merely as auxiliary inputs, process each view independently, or refine the geometry only where the priors are available. We present Refin3R, which refines feed-forward point maps with Structure-from-Motion (SfM) points, propagating SfM geometry over the image grid. In its dual-branch architecture, a 2D branch extends SfM geometry beyond its coverage using geometry-aware backbone features, while a 3D branch reasons over the SfM points of all views, capturing multi-view structure that the image grid alone cannot represent. The two branches exchange information bidirectionally to jointly refine each view, yielding dense and accurate point maps. Across ScanNet++, ETH3D, Tanks\\&amp;Temples, and out-of-domain human and surgical scenes, Refin3R consistently outperforms feed-forward and geometry-guided methods in point map accuracy while running faster than the strongest geometry-guided baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [The Resolution Hypothesis: Agent Evolution's Key Is Identifying Improvements](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23068v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23068

**作者：** Yuanhao Zeng、Shaocheng Lan、Ao Lu、Kuang Yan、Heng Lin、Xinang Xu、Yuan Luo、Yueting Wang、Yexin Li、Ruo Shan、Kan Ren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23068v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23068v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「Resolution Hypothesis」，认为 agent 自我进化停滞并非因为提不出更好的 artifact（prompt、skill、memory），而是因为带噪评估无法可靠识别改进；作者在候选选择与方法排名两个层面，用七种进化方法与六个模型（超千万次 LLM 调用）检验该假设。

**相关性简评：** 其候选级选择接近随机、方法级差距小于同方法重跑波动的诊断，可供核查 LVR 训练轨迹筛选与 latent 策略比较中「真实增益是否可分辨」，提示小幅度性能提升可能只是评估噪声而非机制有效。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Resolution Hypothesis: Agent Evolution's Key Is Identifying Improvements

**abstract/A1：** Agent evolution follows a simple loop: a proposer writes candidate artifacts (prompts, skills, memories) and selection accepts those that score higher. This loop has shown promise, yet practitioners face a puzzle: methods trade wins across papers, and evolution often fails to bootstrap on certain tasks. We trace this puzzle to the Resolution Hypothesis: agent evolution stalls not because methods fail to propose better artifacts, but because noisy evaluations cannot reliably identify them. Across seven evolution methods and six models (over 10M LLM calls), we find support for the hypothesis at both levels where evaluation scores are used. At the candidate level (choosing artifacts), helpful candidate artifacts exist, but selection is no better than random at telling them from harmful ones, so errors accumulate across rounds. At the method level (ranking methods), typical margins of a few percentage points are smaller than the difference between two independent runs of the same method, so published rankings often rest on luck. Together, these findings indicate that agent evolution is often bottlenecked by reliably identifying improvements. This calls for a shift in how we develop and evaluate self-improving agents, from designing ever more candidate improvement systems to making true improvements detectable and accumulable.

**comment/C1：** 38 pages, 12 figures, 13 tables. Project page: https://lineshogan.github.io/resolution-hypothesis/

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Learning to Undo: Transfer Reinforcement Learning under State Space Transformations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23081

**作者：** Mridul Mahajan、Aldo Pacchiano、Xuezhou Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23081v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究源 MDP 与目标 MDP 之间存在「undo map」时知识迁移何时可证明有益，提出通过匹配两个 MDP 的状态特征统计量来学习该映射，再用它把源策略迁移到目标，并在线性映射与线性 Q\* 可实现假设下给出优于从零训练的样本复杂度分析，最后在连续控制与 Atari 上验证样本效率。

**相关性简评：** 用特征统计匹配学习状态空间之间的对齐映射，这一方法线索可能可供核查于跨任务或跨模型的 latent 推理状态对齐与迁移；但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Undo: Transfer Reinforcement Learning under State Space Transformations

**abstract/A1：** Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong empirical success. In this work, we take a more principled perspective by studying when and how transferring knowledge between MDPs (a source and a target) can be provably beneficial. Specifically, we consider the case where there exists an undo map such that applying this map to the target's state space recovers the source exactly. We propose an algorithm that learns this map by matching state feature statistics gathered from both MDPs, and then uses it to transfer the source policy to the target. We theoretically justify the algorithm by analyzing the setting when the undo map is linear and the source is linearly-$Q^\\star$ realizable, where our approach has strictly better sample complexity than tabula rasa RL in the target MDP. Empirically, we demonstrate that these benefits extend beyond this regime: on challenging continuous control tasks and Atari games, our method achieves significantly better sample efficiency. Overall, our results highlight how shared structure between tasks can be leveraged for efficient transfer of policies across environments.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS'26 paper

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [Event-Based Person Detection for Maritime UAV Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23088v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23088

**作者：** Zijie Ning、Patrick Vandewalle

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23088v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23088v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用 v2e 将海上搜救视频转为合成事件流，比较循环视觉 Transformer RVT-T 与 YOLOv8n、RT-DETR-R18 在长曝光模糊下的人员检测表现，并在 Jetson Orin Nano 上测得延迟与能耗。

**相关性简评：** RVT-T 以循环隐状态随事件流累积视觉证据，其在曝光退化下优于单帧模型的对比及逐样本时延/能耗核算，可能为 LVR 的状态更新与计算成本权衡提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Event-Based Person Detection for Maritime UAV Search

**abstract/A1：** Maritime search and rescue requires locating people in the water quickly, motivating the use of UAVs to survey the search area. During flight, camera motion and the longer exposures used in low light can blur small targets and hinder person detection. Event cameras record brightness changes as they occur, reducing the motion blur associated with longer exposure times. We investigate their use for maritime person detection by generating synthetic events using the v2e event simulator from public MOBDrone rescue videos and retaining the existing annotations. We compare the recurrent vision transformer model RVT-T with YOLOv8n and RT-DETR-R18 trained on exposure-matched images. On 843 test samples from held-out recordings, RVT-T achieves 15.63% person AP50:95, compared with 6.89% for YOLOv8n and 9.44% for RT-DETR-R18 under 100 ms synthetic exposure. Increasing exposure times from 50 to 200 ms progressively reduces frame-based detection accuracy, widening the gap to the event model. To evaluate the feasibility of on-board processing, we deploy our detector on a Jetson Orin Nano platform. The RVT-T model retains its accuracy and requires approximately 136 ms and 2.02 J per sample, allowing real-time processing on the UAV.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the 5th Workshop on Maritime Computer Vision (MaCVi), WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [Hidden-State Similarity Predicts Re-Elicitation After Inoculation Prompting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23096v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23096

**作者：** Rose N. Kwon、Samy Mammeri、Sabyasachi Sahoo、Christian Gagné、Thomas Jiralerspong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23096v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23096v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 inoculation prompting 后被抑制的有害行为在评测中复现的触发条件，发现与 inoculation prompt 的文本相似度不足以预测复现，而 prompt 诱发的激活状态相似性更能预测行为是否重新出现。

**相关性简评：** 以隐状态相似度而非表层文本相似度预测行为复现，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 相似度是否捕捉内容而非表述，并为判断被旁路的视觉证据是否仍「可及」提供方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hidden-State Similarity Predicts Re-Elicitation After Inoculation Prompting

**abstract/A1：** Fine-tuning on narrow harmful tasks can cause emergent misalignment, where models generalize harmful behavior beyond the training distribution. Inoculation prompting can reduce this effect by explicitly eliciting the undesired behavior during training, but recent work shows that the behavior can reappear when evaluation prompts contain cues from the training context. We study what makes such prompts effective triggers. We find that, surprisingly, textual similarity to the inoculation prompt is an incomplete predictor. Instead, prompts are more likely to re-elicit suppressed behavior when they induce activation states similar to those produced by the inoculation context. These findings advance our understanding of how inoculation prompting modulates conditional misalignment, and suggest that activation space analysis can help identify when suppressed behaviors remain accessible under eval-time prompts.

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [FMT: Lossless Image Compression in a Fixed Number of Parallel Steps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23108v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23108

**作者：** Ammar Ali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23108v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23108v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FMT 是面向无损图像压缩的图像格式与编解码器族：它把图像组织成粗到细的树，使每一步的数值相互独立并由 GPU 并行预测，再用携带跨步状态的并行 rANS 通道解码，从而让任意尺寸图像在固定数量的串行步内完成解码。

**相关性简评：** 「逐步数值独立＋状态跨步携带」的并行解码设计可能为 LVR 中并行生成与更新 latent 推理状态、把串行深度固定化提供可供核查的方法线索，但该文不涉及视觉推理或答案对视觉证据的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FMT: Lossless Image Compression in a Fixed Number of Parallel Steps

**abstract/A1：** Learned lossless codecs make smaller files than common classical codecs, yet the images we archive and train on still use classical codecs. A main reason is time: a learned codec takes seconds to decode a photo that a classical codec opens in milliseconds, and their faster variants give up most of their gain in size. We argue that much of this time is spent waiting, not computing: the network waits for the values it conditions on, and the arithmetic decoder reads them one at a time. We introduce FMT (Fixed-step Multiscale Tree), a lossless image format whose decoding order and entropy coder are designed together so that neither wait grows with the image. FMT stores an image as a coarse-to-fine tree whose steps hold independent values: a GPU predicts each step at once and decodes it in parallel rANS lanes that carry their states from step to step, so an image of any size decodes in a fixed number of sequential steps. On this tree we build a family of bit-exact codecs, from a rule with no network to large networks, and no codec we ran on the same GPU beats any of them on both size and speed. Without a network, FMT beats NVIDIA's HTJ2K on size and speed; with a large one, it decodes Kodak photos 9 to 61X faster than learned codecs we ran on the same A100, at the same size or smaller.

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Event-Based Dynamic Lip Region Estimation During Speech](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23111v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23111

**作者：** Hamze Hammami、Omar Eldardeer、Francesco Rea

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23111v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23111v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从完整事件「帧」出发，用极性分离体素网格表示，经两条连续时间循环网络（无状态快速通路与有状态慢速通路，时间常数在对数空间学习）预测唇部区域的稠密热图，并在真实传感器上验证跨域表现。

**相关性简评：** 其快慢双通路状态与「细化—锚定」的更新方式，可能为 LVR 中多时间尺度 latent 状态更新、动态计算与证据累积提供结构线索，但该文不涉及 VLM 推理，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Event-Based Dynamic Lip Region Estimation During Speech

**abstract/A1：** Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and low power consumption, making them promising for speech-related applications. These include automatic lip reading, audiovisual speech analysis, and human-robot interaction, particularly in challenging environments characterized by low illumination or high dynamic range. Event-based lip reading, speaker recognition, and voice activity detection, however, mostly operate on a cropped region of interest (ROI), by hand or by the dataset, and no event-based method supplies that ROI as a spatial region; existing event-based face analysis gives the mouth as landmarks, which presents a mismatch between application and localization. The present work predicts a dense heatmap over the lip region from the full event "frame". Events are encoded as a polarity-separated voxel grid and processed at every location by a two-pathway continuous-time recurrent network (CT-RNN) with learned, log-space bounded time constants acting on the real elapsed time of each bin: a stateless fast pathway and a stateful slow pathway, utilised for refining and anchoring the prediction. The model reaches 69.5% on lips on four unseen speakers and switches target only 2.1 times per second, against 8.9 and 11.0 for convolutional baselines with more parameters. Adding a spatial encoder improves precision but fails on a new face scale. On a real event sensor, trained only on simulated events, the proposed model reaches 58.0% on lips against 43.2% for the CNN, with fewer than half its target switches.

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [Large-scale Neuron Community Discovery Reveals Neuron-Sparse Behaviors in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23112v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23112

**作者：** Igor Shilov、Thomas Fel、Tal Haklay、Lucius Bushnaq、Owen Lewis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23112v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23112v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出无监督的「神经元社群发现」方法，结合梯度归因、神经元共现图与社群检测，在 Llama-3.1-8B 与千万级 prompt 上找出跨输入复用的稀疏神经元组，并用必要性、充分性等因果检验验证其功能。

**相关性简评：** 其跨层稀疏子网络与因果充分性检验可能为 LVR 中「latent 状态是否被答案使用」的稀疏贡献定位与干预验证提供可核查工具，但研究的是语言模型内部电路而非视觉推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Large-scale Neuron Community Discovery Reveals Neuron-Sparse Behaviors in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** What reusable units of computation can we discover within a language model's neurons? Prior work has shown that individual forward passes can often be sparsified post-hoc to small groups of neurons, but it has been unclear whether these groups form coherent computational entities reused across inputs. We introduce neuron community discovery, an unsupervised method for finding neuron groups that recur across prompts and are causally necessary and sufficient, within the layers they span, for common computations. The method combines gradient-based attribution, a graph connecting neurons that are repeatedly attributed together, and community detection. Applied to Llama-3.1-8B using ten million prompts, it discovers thousands of candidate communities. We evaluate them on held-out prompts through three causal tests: community necessity, sufficiency within a layer subset, and layer necessity. These tests identify groups whose removal disrupts predictions and whose retention preserves predictions despite ablating the remaining MLP neurons in the tested layers. Discovered communities support functions like grammatical agreement, token-probability calibration, fixed phrases, in-context copying, addition, and multiplication. With communities capped at 128 neurons across all layers, 10.3% of held-out prompts have at least one layer's contribution explained on a neuron-sparse basis. Coverage remains 8.7% when requiring at least four layers and 3.3% when requiring at least twelve. These results show that small groups of existing neurons can provide reusable computational components across prompts, and that such groups can be discovered at scale.

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23115v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23115

**作者：** JunYeong Park、Shijing Wang、Seonwook Park、Michael Stengel、Shalini De Mello、Sang-Il Choi、Hyung Jin Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23115v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23115v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SORBET 基准（1,578 个片段共 3,340 个样本，含同步语音、转写与被注视者标签）及 Nunchi 框架：把转写与 gaze、说话人模型输出构造成文本提示并关联到候选人，由 VLM 权衡几何线索与对话线索来判定被注视者。

**相关性简评：** 其把视觉几何线索序列化为文本、并与语言线索冲突仲裁的做法，可供核查 VLM 是否转而依赖文本旁路、弱化视觉证据使用，与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选和信息坍缩问题相关，但属多 party 注视推断任务，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations

**abstract/A1：** Identifying whom a person looks at is central to understanding multi-party conversations, yet gaze geometry alone becomes ambiguous as more people appear. Conversations offer cues that geometry lacks, such as who is speaking and what is said, but no gaze dataset provides them for many multi-party scenes. We introduce SORBET, a benchmark of 3,340 samples from 1,578 clips with synchronized speech, transcripts, and attended person labels, covering more multi-party scenes than existing gaze datasets. Prior gaze models struggle to use these cues, which come in different forms, must be linked to the right person, and can conflict with gaze geometry. We propose Nunchi, a framework that constructs a text prompt from the transcript and the outputs of gaze and speaker models, links every cue to the candidates with shared labels, and lets a vision-language model (VLM) weigh gaze geometry against conversational cues to identify the attended person. Nunchi outperforms the strongest existing method by 4.8 percentage points. On SORBET, conversational cues help whereas additional visual cues do not, and the gains hold across three VLMs, with automatic transcripts, and on two existing datasets.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Correcting Flows with Marginal Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23134v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23134

**作者：** Meital Bojan、Nadav Bojan Sellam、Ev Zisselman、Aviv Tamar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23134v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23134v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为 flow matching 生成模型提出可训练的 predictor-corrector 框架：在前向 ODE 的特定时间步上，将样本朝后向 ODE 的匹配边缘分布进行修正（Marginal Matching），并给出两种修正函数实现，在 CIFAR-10 与 ImageNet-64 上以较小计算开销提升样本质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「在轨迹中间时刻对状态做边缘分布对齐式纠正」的思路，可能可供核查 latent 推理轨迹中状态纠偏与轨迹训练监督的设计，但论文本身是图像生成采样加速，与视觉推理没有直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Correcting Flows with Marginal Matching

**abstract/A1：** Flow matching models, ODE-based generative models, generate samples by gradually morphing a simple source distribution into a target distribution. However, these models still face challenges in optimally balancing computational efficiency with generation fidelity. While predictor-corrector schemes have been proven to help address this issue in diffusion models effectively, their application to flow matching models remains under-explored. In this work we present a novel training-based predictor-corrector framework for flow matching, which exploits the fact that the ODE can be easily reversed. Our key idea, termed Marginal Matching, is to correct samples at a particular time step in the forward ODE towards a matching marginal distribution of the backward ODE. This scheme proves to reduce a bound on the distance between the data distribution and the learned distribution, motivating two different implementations for the correction function. We show that our proposed method improves sample quality on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-64, with minimal overhead in computation time.

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [RSI-Prime: Training Models to Train Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23138

**作者：** Dewu Zheng、Yang Ye、Hongyu Zhang、Jun Yu、Yanlin Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23138v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 RSI-Prime 数据集，包含七类任务共 350 条完整训练实验轨迹，并对决策做细粒度识别与质量标注，用决策级 loss masking 屏蔽低质量行为，使 LLM 学会自主开展模型训练。

**相关性简评：** 其决策级质量标注与 loss masking 思路或可供核查 LVR 长推理轨迹中的监督分配与贡献分配问题，但工作本身面向纯文本自主训练，与视觉状态表示无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RSI-Prime: Training Models to Train Models

**abstract/A1：** Enabling large language models (LLMs) to autonomously conduct training and improve target models is an important foundation for recursive self-improvement (RSI). As LLMs expand their role from being trained to conducting training, their model-training capability warrants dedicated study and systematic development. To this end, we construct RSI-Prime, a dataset for improving model-training capability, comprising 350 complete experimental trajectories across seven task categories. To help LLMs learn to make high-quality experimental decisions from training practice, we perform fine-grained decision identification and quality annotation, then apply decision-level loss masking to exclude supervision on low-quality behavior. Experiments with GLM-5.3 on PostTrainBench, a benchmark for autonomous post-training, show that training on RSI-Prime yields a 31.95% relative improvement in overall performance over the original GLM-5.3, highlighting a path toward future RSI systems in which models autonomously sustain their own advancement.

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [$π^4$: A Foundation Model for 4D Scene Geometry and Motion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23144v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23144

**作者：** Yifan Wang、Weiguang Zhao、Ni Yang、Haoyu Guo、Junting Dong、Weinan Zhang、Chunhua Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23144v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23144v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出统一的 4D 场景感知基础模型：从无标定 RGB 观测（单图、无序图像集或视频）恢复稠密几何、相机位姿与度量尺度，并对多图输入输出 2D/3D 点轨迹和运动物体掩码；以几何分支加几何条件运动分支、约 0.5B 参数共享骨干，单次前向给出联合重建与轨迹。

**相关性简评：** 不属 LVR 主线，但其「训练仅用至多 64 视角、推理可扩展到上千张输入」的长上下文外推现象，以及用几何条件把 2D 对应关系提升到 3D 的分支设计，可能为 latent 工作区的长度泛化与几何证据注入提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $π^4$: A Foundation Model for 4D Scene Geometry and Motion

**abstract/A1：** $π^4$ is a unified foundation model for 4D scene perception. From diverse, uncalibrated RGB observations in the wild---a single image, an unordered collection, or a video sequence---it recovers dense geometry, camera poses, and metric scale, together with 2D/3D point tracks and moving-object masks for multi-image inputs. It couples a geometry branch with a geometry-conditioned motion branch, which predicts image-space correspondences that are lifted into 3D using reconstructed target-view geometry. Across diverse benchmarks, $π^4$ achieves the best overall average rank in geometry evaluation, alongside competitive performance in single- and multiview 2D/3D point tracking and moving-object segmentation. With a shared backbone, the full geometry-and-motion system contains approximately 0.5B parameters---less than half the size of most baselines. It produces joint reconstructions and tracks in one forward pass, including large-scale scenes with thousands of input images despite training with contexts of at most 64 views. We further present the data and training recipe underlying these capabilities, together with empirical studies of key design choices. Code and models are available at https://github.com/yyfz/Pi4.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [Learning Stratified Policies for Inference-Time Objectives](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23154v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23154

**作者：** Mridul Mahajan、Xuezhou Zhang、Aldo Pacchiano

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23154v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23154v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究把推理时采样预算分配给 k 个独立 LoRA 策略组成的「策略群体」，每个策略贡献一条回答以优化 pass@k，并用线性设定分析说明群体可取得单一策略无法达到的 best-of-k 近似误差，在多类推理任务上验证收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「把采样预算分配到互补候选源」的思路可供核查是否能用于 VLM 多候选推理轨迹的生成与验证预算安排；但该工作只在纯文本输出层做多次采样，未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理表示，向 LVR 的迁移需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Stratified Policies for Inference-Time Objectives

**abstract/A1：** Inference-time scaling improves the performance of large language models by sampling multiple responses and evaluating their collective utility. However, many existing approaches obtain these samples by repeatedly sampling from a single policy. In this work, we study if the sample budget at inference time can instead be allocated across a population of policies. We introduce stratified policies, where each of $k$ learned policies contributes one response to a $k$-sample inference-time objective. We realize this policy population using separate LoRA adapters on a shared language model backbone and optimize it for pass@$k$. We theoretically motivate the approach by analyzing the linear setting; a population of LoRAs can achieve zero best-of-$k$ approximation error in settings where every single LoRA has strictly positive prediction error. Empirically, we demonstrate that stratified policies consistently improve pass@$k$ over a single-policy baseline across multiple reasoning tasks. Overall, our results highlight how distributing samples across complementary policies can be a more effective use of sample budget than repeated i.i.d. sampling from a single policy at inference time.

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [Attribution Through the Decoder: Prediction-Space Explanations for Structured Outputs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23161v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23161

**作者：** Zaer Bin Zaqy、Brinnae Bent

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23161v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23161v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出角度、视线与旋转等结构化输出由固定解码器（如归一化）从原始坐标映射而来，归因应透过解码器微分（对归一化即为缩放后的切空间投影）进行；据此构造切空间归因变体，并在合成任务、MPIIGaze 与 PASCAL3D+ 上以删除位移衡量效果，同时区分径向污染与标量化损失两类失效来源。

**相关性简评：** 「经解码器微分归因、区分解码丢弃的信息」这一线索可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态经解码器读出答案时的贡献分配与视觉证据筛选问题，但其对象是几何回归输出而非推理轨迹，迁移性需阅读正文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Attribution Through the Decoder: Prediction-Space Explanations for Structured Outputs

**abstract/A1：** Networks that predict angles, gaze directions, and rotations do not output these quantities directly: they emit raw coordinates that a fixed decoder, such as normalization, maps to the final prediction. Attribution methods are usually applied to the raw coordinates, so they can credit inputs for changes the decoder discards, such as rescaling a vector when only its direction matters. The resulting explanation can highlight inputs that have no effect on the prediction. We instead attribute through the decoder differential, which for normalization decoders reduces to a scaled tangent projection. We characterize invariance to output rescaling, show that every fixed smooth scalar target on a sphere has points of zero tangential sensitivity, and show that an endpoint-projected variant of Integrated Gradients can still attribute to scale-only inputs, whereas direct angular Integrated Gradients cannot. On synthetic tasks with analytically known relevance, ambient attribution assigns up to 72% of its mass to an irrelevant region. On MPIIGaze validation data, tangent Grad-CAM produces 44% greater prediction displacement under 10% pixel deletion. On PASCAL3D+ with a 6D rotation decoder, the full differential improves mean deletion displacement by 14.1 degrees over the ambient coordinate sum and by 13.8 degrees over a decoder-invariant scalar. Results for a two-polarity tangent LayerCAM variant are mixed: it exceeds ambient-sum LayerCAM on circular regression but no difference is detected on quaternion pose. These results separate two failure sources, radial contamination and scalarization loss. The approach uses existing attribution methods with decoder-aware targets, although some variants also require changes to attribution aggregation. An invariant attribution can still miss inputs that drive the prediction, so invariance and deletion performance should each be checked.

**comment/C1：** Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [Any2Building: Unifying Point Clouds and Images for Building Reconstruction via Shared Spatial Queries](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23187v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23187

**作者：** Hongye Hou、Zhuoxiao Li、Liu Xiangyu、Tengxi Wang、Jing Ou、Dongli Wu、Shuai Zhang、Tongyan Hua、Wufan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23187v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23187v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向 LoD2 建筑重建，提出 Any2Building 统一框架，可从部分点云、俯视图像或两者重建建筑，并以占用监督的共享空间查询在公共 3D 网格中组织可用观测，再由体素条件生成与低模重建流程输出紧凑模型，同时构建了约百万样本的 BuildingWorld-Image 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其「占用grounded 共享空间查询」把多模态部分证据绑定到统一 3D 栅格并由完整表面监督，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、缺失信息与内部状态表示的关系提供可核查的结构化线索，但任务本身是几何重建而非多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Any2Building: Unifying Point Clouds and Images for Building Reconstruction via Shared Spatial Queries

**abstract/A1：** Abstracting structured buildings from limited observations is essential for 3D urban modeling and digital twins. Partial point clouds and overhead images of the same building provide incomplete 3D surface measurements and visual cues to its footprint, roof geometry, and rooftop structures, respectively. This motivates a unified framework that aligns geometric and visual cues in 3D and reconstructs buildings from either or both modalities. Building on this connection, we present Any2Building, a unified model for Level of Detail 2 (LoD2) building reconstruction from partial point clouds, overhead images, or both, with optional coarse geometric priors. At its core is an occupancy-grounded shared spatial queries representation that organizes available observations within a common 3D grid. Supervised by complete building surface voxels, these queries connect observational evidence to target geometry, enabling a shared generator to learn structural priors across tasks and providing spatial guidance for recovering missing structure. A trained voxel conditional surface-generation and low-poly remeshing pipeline then converts the predicted structure into compact LoD2 building models. To support this framework, we introduce BuildingWorld-Image, comprising approximately one million paired samples of partial point clouds, overhead images, and LoD2 building models across 18 cities. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Any2Building outperforms existing methods in geometric reconstruction accuracy across unimodal and multimodal point-cloud completion, single-image building generation, and prior-assisted generation, while maintaining detailed and robust recovery when point cloud input modality is degraded.

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [Retrieval-Augmented Correction for Generalist Medical Image Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23189

**作者：** Vincenzo Marcianò、Xiaoming Zhang、Michela Antonelli、Sebastien Ourselin、Maria A Zuluaga

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23189v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GRACE：一个检索增强的分割掩膜修正器，从共享的已验证病例知识库中取回上下文来编辑输入掩膜，一次跨多器官训练，并用留一器官实验测试冻结模型下的索引扩展。

**相关性简评：** 用检索到的已验证病例作为形状先验注入修正过程，以及将嵌入相似度映射到掩膜形状一致性的轻量投影头，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、支持样本选择以及「检索内容是否真被使用」提供可核查线索，但任务本身属分割后处理，不含推理生成过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Retrieval-Augmented Correction for Generalist Medical Image Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Automated segmentation is routinely used as the first step in quantitative image-based clinical workflows, where predicted masks are subsequently reviewed and corrected by human operators. Automated correction of the residual structural errors is currently addressed one anatomy at a time, so the number of trained correctors scales with the number of segmentation targets. We present GRACE, a retrieval-augmented refiner that edits an input mask under context drawn from a shared knowledge base of verified prior cases. The refiner is trained once on a multi-organ corpus spanning compact organs, cavities, branching vessels, and tumour lesions. In the held-out dataset-transfer evaluation, three held-out datasets query a fixed knowledge base built from training data only. A separate leave-one-anatomy-out experiment tests parameter-free index extension: verified cases of an unseen solid organ enter the knowledge base, and the refiner stays frozen. A light shape-aware projection head on a frozen multimodal encoder maps embedding similarity to the shape agreement of the masks, so the knowledge base returns cases that the refiner can use as a shape prior. We evaluate GRACE on 18 public datasets spanning CT, MRI and ultrasound, using nine refinement configurations and seven upstream segmentors that span weak, mid, and strong operating regimes. We report each change with the DSC of its input mask. On the weak tier, the input DSC is 0.386 and GRACE adds up to +0.35 DSC. On a strong segmentor, the input DSC is 0.932 and the mean change is small. Transfer to an imaging modality that is absent from training and from the index is partial and depends on the anatomy, and remains an open research direction. Our results suggest retrieval-conditioned refinement is a practical alternative to anatomy-specific correction models. Code: https://github.com/robustml-eurecom/grace

**comment/C1：** Accepted in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (10/2026). Reviewed on OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=PeDqE0SQRe

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [Multimodal Masked Diffusion Autoencoder for Modeling Impaired Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23211v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23211

**作者：** John Gideon、Anshul Gupta、Megan Applegate-Kenton、Kayli Battel、Todd J Rowell、Patricio Reyes Gomez、Laporsha Dees、Emily Sumner、Avinash Balachandran、Guy Rosman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23211v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23211v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ID-MAE：一种多模态掩码扩散自编码器，在把受损标签作为掩码 token 预测的同时，自监督重建被掩码的注视与轨迹，并发布 DIVERT 驾驶模拟数据集，探查输入模态、掩码方案、位置编码、注意力与解码方式的设计空间。

**相关性简评：** 掩码扩散解码与多模态掩码重建目标，可能为 LVR 中并行/扩散式 latent 状态生成及重建监督的贡献分配提供可核查线索；但任务本身是驾驶受损推断，与视觉推理主线距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Masked Diffusion Autoencoder for Modeling Impaired Driving

**abstract/A1：** Human cognitive state (e.g., distraction, stress), gaze, and behavior interact with the driving environment in subtle ways that AI could use to assist drivers, but ground-truth labels for human cognitive state levels are costly to obtain at scale. We propose a multimodal masked-diffusion autoencoder, ID-MAE, that adds a self-supervised objective to impairment prediction: alongside predicting the impairment label as a masked token, it reconstructs masked gaze and trajectory. To probe the effects of different modalities and types of impairments on model inference, we introduce DIVERT, a driving-simulator dataset containing 103 hours of driving data (n = 109) with ten impairment inductions spanning cognitive, visual, and emotional states, recorded with gaze, vehicle state, and scene video. We demonstrate how ID-MAE affords significant improvements in impairment inference results on both the DIVERT dataset and existing datasets, and enables strong results in additional tasks, such as trajectory and gaze prediction. For location-matched pairs, our model achieves a paired AUC of 0.706 for impairment detection on DIVERT, compared to 0.590 without reconstruction loss, improving over existing baselines. We probe the design space in terms of input modalities, masking scheme, positional encoding, attention mechanism, and decoding. We also investigate the interaction between various impairments in training and inference, demonstrating that visually demanding distractions are separable from cognitive distractions. Code and data will be available upon acceptance.

**comment/C1：** Equal contribution: John Gideon and Anshul Gupta. 8 pages, 6 figures. Submitted to CVPR 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [EviSlide-MIL: Multi-Query Pooling and Shared Prediction for Class-Incremental Whole-Slide Image Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23212v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23212

**作者：** Zhouhan Qian、Sicheng Chen、Chad Wong、Tianyi Zhang、Enhui Chai、Zeyu Liu、Fei Xia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23212v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23212v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向全切片病理图像的类增量分类，提出 EviSlide-MIL：为每个类别设置多个可学习 query，将冻结的 patch 特征聚合为类特定表示，并共享预测模块，配合经验回放与知识蒸馏缓解遗忘。

**相关性简评：** 冻结旧类 query 以固定相同输入下的注意力模式，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 表示的稳定性、增量更新下状态一致性提供可核查的方法线索，但该文不涉及多模态推理生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EviSlide-MIL: Multi-Query Pooling and Shared Prediction for Class-Incremental Whole-Slide Image Classification

**abstract/A1：** Histopathological analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs) plays a critical role in cancer diagnosis, and multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely adopted for WSI analysis under weak supervision. As new tumor subtypes are introduced, extending existing MIL models requires learning new classes while retaining knowledge of previously learned ones. This presents a class-incremental learning challenge, where models must acquire new classes with limited access to historical training data and recognize all learned classes without task identifiers at inference. A major obstacle is forgetting, as updates to feature aggregation and prediction modules can degrade performance on previously learned classes. To address this challenge, we propose \\textbf{EviSlide-MIL}, a class-incremental WSI framework that integrates multiple learnable queries per class with a shared prediction module. The queries aggregate frozen patch features into class-specific representations, while the shared prediction module limits parameter growth across incremental stages. By freezing queries associated with previously learned classes, EviSlide-MIL preserves their attention patterns for identical inputs, while experience replay and knowledge distillation further mitigate forgetting as shared parameters are updated. Experiments on eight tumor subtypes from The Cancer Genome Atlas demonstrate that EviSlide-MIL achieves improved performance and reduced forgetting compared with competing methods across most learning settings, demonstrating its potential for scalable and knowledge-preserving WSI classification. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [Do LLM Agents Still Need to Be Told How to Self-Improve?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23219v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23219

**作者：** Shobhita Sundaram、Cansu Sancaktar、Phillip Isola

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23219v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23219v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文让冻结的 LLM agent 在给定算力预算与工具（收集经验、更新文本文档）的条件下自行设计基于上下文的自我改进算法，以游戏为试验台检验其能否自发形成自调节学习策略，并测试所学文本产物向其他游戏与 3D 导航任务的迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其「用外部文本产物承载经验、由 agent 自建学习循环与自生成课程」的机制，可能为 LVR 中推理状态的更新与轨迹监督来源提供核查线索，但该文不含视觉表征或 latent 状态，迁移性需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do LLM Agents Still Need to Be Told How to Self-Improve?

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents increasingly drive their own learning. Self-improvement methods delegate various components of model optimization to the models themselves. As models become more capable, this motivates a basic question: do these increasingly capable models still need us to specify how they should self-improve? In this paper we study an autonomous paradigm in which, given basic primitives, resources, and a general goal for self-improvement, agents construct their own self-learning algorithm for context-based optimization. Using the setting of gameplay as a testbed, a frozen LLM agent is given a compute budget and tools for collecting experience and updating text documents, and allowed to freely experiment without an externally imposed loop or access to real-world data. We find that sufficiently capable models do self-improve under these conditions, demonstrating strategies that align with classic cognitive frameworks for self-regulated learning and constructs from machine learning literature on self-generated curricula. Furthermore, we study the extent to which the learned text artifacts transfer to external benchmarks, observing improvement across different game horizons, mechanics, and 3D navigation.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://ssundaram21.github.io/self-org/

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [FastFrames: Video Generation at Interactive Latency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23226v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23226

**作者：** Gene Chou、Guandao Yang、Fujun Luan、Lars Mescheder、Wei Dong、Stephan Richter、Vladlen Koltun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23226v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23226v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文剖析自回归视频扩散模型的推理管线，发现 VAE 解码与 KV cache 更新等被视作固定实现细节的操作占端到端延迟一半以上，并以轻量替代方案实现单帧 36–45 ms 的交互级生成。

**相关性简评：** 其关于 KV cache 增量更新与 latent 解码开销的分解方式，可能为 LVR 中多步 latent 状态维护、缓存结构与逐步解码成本核算提供借鉴线索，可供核查是否可迁移为跳过或轻量化状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FastFrames: Video Generation at Interactive Latency

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive video diffusion models generate frames based on new observations and inputs, making them a promising medium for interactive visual experiences. However, existing models are orders of magnitude too slow for fluid interaction. This paper introduces FastFrames, a family of fast autoregressive video diffusion models that achieves interactive latency without compromising quality. We profile the inference pipeline of state-of-the-art models and find that operations often treated as fixed implementation details, such as VAE decoding and KV cache updates, in fact account for over half of end-to-end inference latency. We challenge the assumption that these components are indispensable and replace them with lightweight alternatives. Our 1.3B variant runs at 36 ms per frame, or 28 responses per second (RPS), on a single B200 GPU while matching the generation quality of a state-of-the-art model that is nearly 8x slower. Our 14B variant runs at 45 ms per frame, or 22 RPS, on an 8x B200 GPU node and yields significantly better visual quality and realism. We show that FastFrames enables real-time interaction in applications such as camera-conditioned and physics-conditioned video generation.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-fastframes/

</details>

<a id="paper-187"></a>

### 187. [Spool: Low-Latency Streaming Inference for Hybrid Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23228v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23228

**作者：** Rui Ning、Om Chabra、Hari Balakrishnan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23228v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23228v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Spool：注意到混合 VLM 中线性循环层对状态的更新是仿射且可结合复合的，于是缓存每帧的状态变换，在滑动窗口推进时无需重放帧即可精确移除过期帧，实现免训练的低延迟流式视频推理。

**相关性简评：** 「按帧可组合的仿射状态变换」为在 latent 状态中有选择地保留或剔除视觉证据提供了机制线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中的证据筛选与状态内容保持问题；但原文仅评测时延与准确率，未涉及推理过程分析，迁移需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spool: Low-Latency Streaming Inference for Hybrid Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Applications such as visual accessibility agents and robotics require answering questions about recent video content promptly, over a bounded window of past frames (e.g., the last two minutes). Hybrid vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave attention with linear recurrent layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), are attractive for this setting, but maintaining a sliding window is expensive. The reason is that recurrent layers combine all past frames into a shared state, so expiring old frames normally requires re-running the entire window through the model. We observe that the recurrent update is affine in the state with input-dependent coefficients, so the effect of each frame can be captured as a transformation that composes associatively. We present Spool, a training-free framework that caches per-frame state transformations and composes them efficiently as the window advances, removing expired frames from each recurrent layer's state without replaying frames through the backbone. Removal is exact within each layer; as with key-value eviction in attention, expired frames can retain residual influence through the cached inputs computed by earlier layers. We design a dedicated data structure and GPU kernels to achieve low caching and composition overhead. Across five open-source hybrid VLMs and four video reasoning benchmarks, Spool achieves the lowest p95 time-to-first-token in all 20 model-benchmark tests, reducing it by a geometric mean of $6.6\\times$ over the faster of two recent vLLM baselines, while improving accuracy by 2.3 percentage points on average.

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505v3>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.14505

**作者：** Daniel Lenton、Yusha Arif、Haris Mahmood

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14505v3>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.14505v3>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Continual-ARC 终身学习基准：以三个 ARC-AGI 版本构造任务流，旧任务在休眠间隔后以新实例重现并持续加入新任务；学习者可付费获取示范、提交答案或在 harness 内操作，按反馈预算消耗计分，并据此在同一尺度上比较记忆型与权重型方法及 harness 持久化的效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨实例可保留什么」与「按证据获取和重试成本计分、流结束后冻结测记忆与泛化」的评测设计，可能为 LVR 的状态保留、证据筛选与停止决策评价提供参照；但核心是持续学习而非 latent 视觉推理，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continual-ARC: Measuring Skill Acquisition over a Working Lifetime

**abstract/A1：** Agent harnesses have emerged as a promising mechanism for continual learning, alongside test-time parametric learning. However, the current benchmarks have several limitations: they generally focus on a single applied domain, serve each instance only once, and assume a single family of learner, so memory-based and weight-based systems are rarely compared on the same scale. We propose Continual-ARC, a lifelong stream built from the three ARC-AGI editions, whose tasks need no domain knowledge. The same tasks return after dormant gaps with fresh instances, while new tasks keep arriving. On each instance, the learner may pay for demonstrations, submit answers, or act inside the harness, with retries permitted on incorrect submissions, which are paid for. Each instance is scored based on the share of this feedback budget consumed. Since a learner may keep anything between instances, memory-based and weight-based systems are scored on the same scale. After the stream, the learner is frozen and tested on fresh instances of the tasks it met, which measures memorization, and on unseen tasks, which measures generalization.
