We run an agent harness with frozen weights, with and without persistence, on Qwen3.5-9B and GLM-5.3 Flash, comparing them with two prompting floors, a program-induction method and Qwen3.5-9B's weights trained to imitate GLM-5.3 Flash. Persistence in the harness generally does not improve memorization unless provided with explicit expert guidance. Most harness-based methods still learn less than a floor that simply caches each task's demonstrations, across all models we tested. Harness-based methods gain little or nothing on unseen tasks, whereas weight-based learning shows substantial gains. This opens the door to future weight-based continual learning that could teach models to drive harnesses in new domains.

**comment/C1：** 54 pages, 14 figures. Code and run records: https://github.com/unifyai/continual-arc

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [NEBench R1: a Reasoning Benchmark for Evaluating Nuclear Domain Competence in Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21682v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21682

**作者：** Liyan Wang、Longze Li、Mohamed Hassan、Isshu Lee、Dajie Sun、Ruimin Feng、Aleksandar Vakanski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21682v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21682v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 NEBench R1，从美国核管会反应堆操作员执照考试中抽取 7,546 道多选题，配套官方解析，并用 LLM 评判按多维量规评估 6 个模型答案正确性与推理质量。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本领域基准，与视觉或多模态 latent 状态无直接关联；但其「推理链更长反而准确率更低、token 消耗或可作为不可靠回答信号」的观察，可能为 LVR 中的停止决策与推理长度/计算预算评价提供间接参考。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NEBench R1: a Reasoning Benchmark for Evaluating Nuclear Domain Competence in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for knowledge-intensive and safety-critical applications in nuclear engineering. However, existing nuclear-domain benchmarks provide limited content coverage or focus primarily on answer accuracy without systematically evaluating the quality of the underlying reasoning. This study introduces NEBench R1, a reasoning-oriented benchmark comprising 7,546 unique multiple-choice questions extracted from U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) reactor operator licensing examinations covering 18 nuclear facilities. Each question is classified according to the NRC Knowledge and Abilities catalogs and accompanied by an official explanation serving as a reference for reasoning evaluation. Six LLMs were evaluated, with overall accuracy ranging from 34.0% to 73.9% across three distinct performance tiers. To assess reasoning quality, an LLM-based judge evaluated correctly answered responses using a multidimensional rubric. Although responses generally demonstrated strong problem comprehension, they scored lower in factual and procedural accuracy and distractor analysis, indicating that correct answers were not necessarily supported by technically sound justifications. Response clarity was also weakly indicative of technical quality. Furthermore, longer reasoning traces were associated with lower accuracy in both frontier reasoning models, suggesting that reasoning length may reflect question difficulty rather than improved problem-solving performance. Reasoning-token consumption may therefore provide an additional signal for identifying potentially unreliable responses. NEBench R1 establishes a benchmark for evaluating both answer accuracy and reasoning quality, providing a foundation for assessing the domain competence and reliability of LLMs in nuclear engineering.

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [DeltaHarness: Self-Evolving Harnesses for Resource-Adaptive Tool Execution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21688v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21688

**作者：** Lecheng Yan、Jianze Lin、Xicheng Han、Xiantao Xu、Wenxi Li、Chenyang Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.PF

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21688v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21688v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DeltaHarness，一种从冷启动实测数据出发的工具执行 harness 自适应方法，通过 QHI 循环依据观测响应更新运行时调度、修订物化计划并挑选后续实验，并由独立确认面板把关策略替换，以降低任务完成延迟。

**相关性简评：** 其「用真实执行测量消解计划优劣的不确定性、按成本挑选探测实验并按实测延迟替换策略」的框架，可能与 LVR 中证据获取的收益成本权衡及 LOOK/STOP 决策相近，可供核查；但该文面向工具调度延迟，不涉及视觉推理状态或表示，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeltaHarness: Self-Evolving Harnesses for Resource-Adaptive Tool Execution

**abstract/A1：** Tool-using agents rely on execution harnesses to organize and schedule tool operations. The same task and resource description can conceal runtime conditions that reverse the latency ordering of valid execution plans. Resolving this uncertainty requires firsthand execution in the target setting. We introduce DeltaHarness, an execution-guided policy-search method that adapts agent harnesses from cold-start measurements. Its QHI loop uses the observed response to update runtime dispatch, revise physical plans, and select experiments for later proposals. An independent confirmation panel controls policy replacement. With fixed dispatch, a revised materialization policy reduces complete-delivery latency by 30.0% on one video-editing task across three fresh pairs. On held-out tasks, the evolved policies reduce latency by 8.2% relative to the best fixed plan in one seed and by 4.6% relative to the initial proposal in another.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

### 191. [AdaFlowLite: Scalable and Non-blocking Inference on Asynchronous Mobile Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21691v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21691

**作者：** Sicong Liu、Fengmin Wu、Yuan Gao、Bin Guo、Zimu Zhou、Hongkai Wen、Zhiwen Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21691v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21691v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** AdaFlowLite 面向移动端异步多模态传感器数据，用分层归一化的跨模态亲和矩阵和轻量多模态 Swin Transformer，实现「部分数据到达即开始推理」的 opportunistic inference，并支持无需重训的实时数据填补，降低时延与能耗。

**相关性简评：** 与主线无直接交集，但其「证据未齐即推理 vs 等待」的权衡及对跨模态亲和的显式计算，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据是否充分、何时继续 LOOK 或 STOP 的判据提供概念类比，可供核查其亲和度量能否转用于证据筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AdaFlowLite: Scalable and Non-blocking Inference on Asynchronous Mobile Data

**abstract/A1：** The rise of mobile devices equipped with numerous sensors, such as LiDAR and cameras, has driven the adoption of multi-modal deep intelligence for distributed sensing tasks, such as smart cabins and driving assistance. However, the arrival time of mobile sensory data vary due to modality size and network dynamics, which can lead to delays (if waiting for slow data) or accuracy decline (if inference proceeds without waiting). Moreover, the diversity and dynamic nature of mobile systems exacerbate this challenge. In response, we present a shift to \\textit{opportunistic} inference for asynchronous distributed multi-modal data, enabling inference as soon as partial data arrives. While existing methods focus on optimizing modality consistency and complementarity, known as modal affinity, they lack a \\textit{computational} approach to control this affinity in open-world mobile environments. % \\sysname pioneers the formulation of structured cross-modality affinity in mobile contexts using a hierarchical analysis-based normalized matrix. This approach accommodates the diversity and dynamics of modalities, generalizing across different types and numbers of inputs. Employing an multi-modal lightweight Swin Transformer (MMLST), \\sysname facilitates real-time and flexible data imputation, adapting to various modalities and downstream tasks without retraining. Experiments show that \\sysname significantly reduces inference latency by up to 80.4\\% and enhances accuracy by up to 62.1\\%, while achieving nearly a 50\\% reduction in energy consumption, outperforming status quo approaches. Also, this method can enhance LLM performance to preprocess asynchronous data.

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [TIBECG: Temporal Information Bottleneck for Lead-Specific Demographic Privacy in ECG Waveform Release](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21694v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21694

**作者：** Tsai-Min Chen、Jun Seita

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CR、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21694v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21694v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文评估一种可在单导联 ECG 波形上变化 latent 长度的时序信息瓶颈处理器，衡量其在保留诊断宏 F1 的同时抑制年龄/性别推断的表现，并与降采样等传统处理方式作比较。

**相关性简评：** 可变 latent 长度下的「效用—泄漏」权衡，以及「固定长度时 latent KL 无法可靠排序残留泄漏」这一负结果，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 是否保留任务必要信息、以及 latent 相似度指标能否反映内容；但研究对象是心电时间序列，迁移线索较远，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TIBECG: Temporal Information Bottleneck for Lead-Specific Demographic Privacy in ECG Waveform Release

**abstract/A1：** Transmitting an electrocardiogram (ECG) waveform can expose demographic information beyond an intended diagnostic task. We evaluate TIBECG, an architectural temporal information bottleneck, on individual leads extracted from hospital twelve-lead recordings. A 2,302,337-parameter processor varies its latent length while retaining the same nine-score consistency objective, without age or sex labels in protector fitting. Across 6,386 recordings, ten folds, 12 leads and 21 lengths, retrospective selections retained at least 95% of each lead's clean macro-F1. Their weaker age/sex interval gain exceeded the best eligible conventional transformation on all leads, by a median 22.08 percentage points. The implemented sampling-rate comparison shows that direct downsampling usually suppresses demographic inference more strongly but at a larger loss of diagnostic utility, whereas TIBECG favors utility preservation. Selection stability also varies by lead. Lower latent Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence does not reliably rank residual sex leakage at a fixed length. These findings support lead-specific evaluation of waveform processing, not validated consumer deployment or expert-readable reconstruction. When the waveform-input diagnostic classifier is hosted as part of a proprietary cloud service rather than deployed on the device, waveform transmission is required to obtain its scores.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 9 figures, 2 tables. Corresponding author: Tsai-Min Chen

</details>

### 193. [Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21782v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21782

**作者：** Sheng Lu、Guangpin Tao、Tong Lu、Ying Tai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21782v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21782v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EyeHeight 基准，用多视角相机高度估计检验六个开源交互视频世界模型在转身行走时能否保持初始相机高度，发现普遍向人眼高度「回归」，并将其归因于以人为中心的视频先验，尝试用无引导采样与高度均衡微调缓解。

**相关性简评：** 相机高度这种需在生成过程中持续维持的几何状态被先验覆盖，可能为 LVR 中「任务必要视觉/几何信息是否在 latent 状态中被先验冲淡」提供可核查的评测与归因思路；视角漂移改变视觉导航策略预测路径，也提示状态误差会传导至决策，但该文并非 VLM 推理方法。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models

**abstract/A1：** Interactive video world models serve as simulators for embodied agents: from a starting image and an action trajectory, they generate the views the agent would see. The starting image fixes how high the camera is, for example 0.15 m for a floor robot or 2.2 m for an overhead camera, and a faithful simulator must keep this height as the camera moves. We show that six recent open-weight models fail to do so: as motion replaces the starting view with generated content, they move the camera toward human eye level, a failure we call camera-height regression. Our benchmark, EyeHeight, pairs six rendered elevations (0.15 m to 2.2 m) and four calibrated real-world rig heights with scale-free multi-view height estimation. All six models lose their initial height during a $180^\\circ$ turn-and-walk rollout: initialized at 0.15 m, only 0-20% of clips end within $1.3\\times$ of the starting height as inferred viewpoints converge to human heights of 1.1 m to 1.5 m, whereas ground-truth renders remain within a few percent. In Matrix-Game models, homography-tracked floor motion stays consistent with constant elevation during rotation even as newly outpainted room geometry yields a higher reconstructed viewpoint. Regression grows with rotation angle, begins while the start frame remains in context, and largely reverses in SANA-WM when a $360^\\circ$ turn restores the initial view. We trace this pull to human-centric video priors in which floor-level views are nearly absent: in SANA-WM, textual height cues and dense floor-disparity maps do not prevent it, whereas classifier-free text guidance during the first half of denoising strengthens it. Combining unguided sampling with height-balanced fine-tuning recovers more retention but costs 5 MUSIQ points, a loss fixed during those same early steps. Viewpoint drift alone shifts the paths predicted by visual navigation policies.

</details>

### 194. [Data-Driven Variable-Length Compression of 3D Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21802v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21802

**作者：** Yvette Y. Lin、Sai Karthikey Pentapati、Sarvesh Sundaram、Guandao Yang、Sara Fridovich-Keil

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21802v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21802v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种学习式变长压缩方法，利用训练数据集中的共享结构，并在仅用快速前馈推理的情况下按单个形状的复杂度自适应调整压缩表示的大小，在网格与 SDF 上改善率失真。

**相关性简评：** 其按样本复杂度分配变长潜表示、并以率失真权衡决定表示规模的做法，可能为 LVR 中按题目难度动态决定 latent 长度或停止计算的问题表述与评价提供可供核查的编码视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-Driven Variable-Length Compression of 3D Representations

**abstract/A1：** Compressing 3D shapes is important for the efficiency of many applications, yet existing methods either cannot leverage dataset-level statistics or use a fixed compression ratio for all shapes. We propose a learned variable-length compression method that exploits structure across a training dataset while adapting compressed representation size to individual shape complexity using only fast feed-forward inference. Our method operates on general latent representations and improves rate-distortion for both meshes and signed distance fields. Code is available at https://github.com/voilalab/varlen-3D.

</details>

### 195. [Urban Representation Learning for Identifying Potential Disparities in Crime Reporting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21825v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21825

**作者：** Jorge Valverde-Rebaza、Carlos Paez De La Cruz、Pedro Pérez-Collado、Daniel Sánchez-Cataño、Eashley Martínez-Vergara

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21825v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21825v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出自监督城市表示学习框架：由小时、周、静态犯罪上报画像学习多尺度地理自监督表示并融合为统一 latent 空间，再用邻域画像偏离度（NPD）度量各区与其检索到的相似区的上报模式差异，用于发现异常上报模式。

**相关性简评：** 「在统一 latent 空间中检索相似对象并度量其偏离程度」这一做法可能为判断 latent 推理状态何时偏离同侪预期、从而触发额外视觉证据获取或停止决策提供评价线索，但领域相距较远，需核查其相似度是否受内容与位置结构混淆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Urban Representation Learning for Identifying Potential Disparities in Crime Reporting

**abstract/A1：** Open crime data support urban safety analysis but reflect reported incidents rather than the complete distribution of criminal activity, potentially introducing spatial distortions in downstream analysis. We propose a self-supervised urban representation learning framework for identifying unusual reporting patterns relative to representation-similar peers using only georeferenced crime records. The method constructs complementary hourly, weekly, and static reporting profiles, learns scale-specific representations under geographic self-supervision, and integrates them into a unified latent space. Neighbor Profile Divergence (NPD) is then used to quantify disagreement between each region's reporting profile and those of its retrieved peers. Experiments on more than 1.8 million crime records from Mexico City, aggregated into 1,058 H3 regions, show that the proposed representation achieves a mean Silhouette score of 0.4052, compared with 0.2863 for Hex2Vec. NPD rankings remain highly stable across neighborhood sizes, with Spearman correlations from 0.9720 to 0.9951, while most high-divergence candidates persist across configurations. The retrieved peers also extend substantially beyond immediate geographic neighborhoods, indicating that the learned representation captures structure beyond local spatial proximity. High-divergence regions exhibit spatially organized patterns and, in several cases, differences in incident volume relative to peer expectations. These results support NPD as an internal auditing and prioritization mechanism for identifying regions that warrant further investigation, rather than as direct evidence of underreporting.

**comment/C1：** Accepted for publication in International Conference on Artificial Intelligence, Computer, Data Sciences, and Applications (ACDSA 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [How Much of SAR Vehicle Recognition Is the Vehicle? Background Shortcuts, Angle Gaps and Partial Libraries on MSTAR and ATRNet-STAR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21827v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21827

**作者：** Mousa Shaya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21827v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21827v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 MSTAR 与 ATRNet-STAR 上用 ConvNeXt-Small（3 个种子）和 SAR 基础模型 SARATR-X 做「去掉目标」与「只留目标」的对照，并固定训练数据量改变训练俯仰角，发现高分很大程度来自背景捷径，且性能主要由与最近训练角度的间隔决定，据此建议同时报告 background-only、target-only 与角度间隔。

**相关性简评：** 其「背景-only / 目标-only」控制与分布位移下的部分类别覆盖分析，可能为核查 VLM 答案是否真正依赖任务相关视觉证据（而非上下文或语言先验捷径）提供评测设计参照；但对象为 SAR 非生成式模型，与 latent 状态生成和使用机制无直接联系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Much of SAR Vehicle Recognition Is the Vehicle? Background Shortcuts, Angle Gaps and Partial Libraries on MSTAR and ATRNet-STAR

**abstract/A1：** Deep networks score near 99% on the MSTAR vehicle benchmark, and the newer ATRNet-STAR benchmark was built to be harder. We ask how much of these scores comes from the vehicle itself. With a modern network (ConvNeXt-Small, 3 seeds) we first reproduce the known MSTAR result: with the central 96x96 pixels zeroed out, accuracy is still 73.3% (chance 10%). The same shortcut is present in ATRNet-STAR, which was collected across several scenes: with the vehicle removed, accuracy is 63.3% on the 40-class SOC split (chance 2.5%). Under the official scene-change split, the background stops helping and starts hurting. Accuracy is 22.8% on full chips and 30.8% when only the vehicle is shown. A SAR foundation model, SARATR-X, leans on the background more than ConvNeXt does (+4 to +6 points with the vehicle removed, in every seed) and does not solve the scene change. We then hold the amount of training data fixed and vary the depression angles used for training. Accuracy is set mainly by the gap to the nearest training angle: 57% at 15 degrees, 39% at 30 degrees and 26% at 45 degrees. Adding more training angles does not help. Finally, when most classes have training images at the test angle and a few do not, the missing classes do worse than when no class has them: 9.0% instead of 22.4% at a 45-degree gap. Most of their images are assigned to known classes. We recommend reporting background-only and target-only controls, and the angle gap, next to any accuracy figure. Code is public.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Code: https://github.com/mousashaya/sar-atr-study

</details>

### 197. [Auditors Struggle to Catch Sabotage In End-to-End Transcripts of AI Safety Research](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21832v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21832

**作者：** Keshav Shenoy、Anthony Zang、Eric Gan、Jacqueline Tay、Amy Ngo、Sebastian Prasanna、Julian Stastny、Vivek Hebbar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21832v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21832v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文让人类专家充当「破坏者」在AI安全研究中植入蓄意破坏，构建红蓝对抗审计场景，测试人类审计员与前沿模型能否从完整研究记录中判断哪些实验被破坏及具体手法。

**相关性简评：** 该工作实质在检验「仅凭完整推理记录能否还原真实计算意图」，这与LVR中显式trace的可监督性、以及把推理转入latent状态后证据可审计性与评价成本的问题可能相关，可供核查其审计设定是否能迁移为trace/latent状态忠实性的评测思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Auditors Struggle to Catch Sabotage In End-to-End Transcripts of AI Safety Research

**abstract/A1：** As AI systems are increasingly used to automate AI R&amp;D, misaligned systems could sabotage research intended to improve model safety and alignment. To stress-test defenses against such sabotage, we use a human expert as a proxy saboteur on AI safety research. We conduct a red-team/blue-team game where a human researcher runs AI safety experiments from scratch and auditors try to tell which were sabotaged and how. Given access to the full research transcript and the ability to run experiments, human auditors mostly fail to distinguish sabotaged from benign research: they achieve an AUROC of 0.59, not significantly better than chance, and fully identify the specific sabotage in only 17% of cases. Despite not being allowed to run experiments, the best frontier models outperform human auditors (Fable 5.1: AUROC 0.73, 32% full identification) within noise and weaker models perform comparably. When the saboteur chooses which experiments are attacked, rather than being required to sabotage every question given, sabotage identification drops further. Our results suggest that research sabotage by expert-level agents is difficult to detect with current auditing methods and provide a new setting for studying research sabotage.

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Inventa: Decentralized Self-Improving Agents for Autonomous Scientific Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21847

**作者：** Prithwish Jana、Nikos Kanakaris、Purak Jain、Anoop Deoras、Sahika Genc

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Inventa，用多个自治研究单元配合 mutator 生成候选想法、local orchestrator 在停滞时调整 harness 或重启演化，并由验证器将改进与违规记入唯一的单元间共享知识库，在五个科研流程基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉 latent 推理无直接关系；但「进度停滞即重启或换模型」的元控制与「验证器筛选后才写入共享记忆」的机制，可能为 LVR 的停止决策与视觉证据筛选提供概念借鉴，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Inventa: Decentralized Self-Improving Agents for Autonomous Scientific Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents can generate research ideas and improve scientific discovery through iterative experimentation. However, increasing the number of agents or iterations does not automatically translate to producing more diverse or feasible ideas. Agents may converge on similar candidates, propagate unsupported claims through shared memory components and repeatedly apply search strategies that no longer yield any improvements. We introduce Inventa, a framework for automated scientific discovery that employs autonomous research units and verified shared knowledge. Each unit creates a lineage of candidate ideas using a mutator agent. A local orchestrator agent periodically adapts the mutator's harness. When progress stalls, the orchestrator agent replaces its model or restarts the idea evolution process. Verifiers evaluate each candidate idea and record verified improvements, useful components and constraint violations in a shared knowledge, the only channel between units. We evaluate Inventa on five benchmarks spanning the research cycle, covering retrieval (ScholarCatalyst), ideation (IdeaBench), proposal writing (IdeaScientist), and scientific law discovery (EurekaBench, SciLaws-Bench). It leads all five, achieving 73.6% on EurekaBench versus 56.2% for CORAL. On IdeaBench, it discovers 26.0 novel ideas per paper, compared with 18.6 for ResearchAgent, with 82% fewer model calls.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21879

**作者：** Sreejeet Maity、Sukanya Modak、Leonardo F. Toso

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21879v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在部署间模型仅稀疏变化（至多 ε 比例奖励与转移项改变）的折扣 RL 设定下，提出按状态-动作对自适应选择复用多少历史观测：由近及远逐个加入旧部署数据，用均值与下一状态分布的一致性检验确定可用历史长度，再在拟合 MDP 上求策略并给出长期平均差距界。

**相关性简评：** 「以一致性检验决定历史复用长度」这一思路可供核查是否能对应 latent 推理中的证据/状态缓存淘汰、分布漂移检测与更新窗口选择；但论文属表格型 RL 理论、不含视觉表示，迁移仅为推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Memory for Online Reinforcement Learning under Sparse Model Changes

**abstract/A1：** We study the discounted reinforcement learning (RL) problem in a setting where the underlying environment changes between deployments. Each deployment provides $H$ observations from a fixed MDP. The reward function and transition kernel are fixed within each deployment. Across deployments, at most an $ε&gt;0$ fraction of the reward and transition kernel entries may change. In this setting, learning each model to deploy from scratch may discard useful observations from past deployments, whereas pooling all past observations can bias the estimates of entries that have already changed. We then propose an algorithm that for each state and action pair, selects how much history to reuse to estimate the reward and probabilities of the next state. We start with observations from the current deployment and add earlier deployment observations one at a time, the most recent first. After each addition, we check whether a single average reward agrees with the estimates from all histories considered until that point. We apply the same check to the distribution of next states. For each estimate, we leverage the last history that passes these aforementioned checks. The optimal control policy is then computed on top of the fitted MDP. We characterize the average gap between the optimal return and the return of the learner's policies over a finite number of deployments. We demonstrate that when $0&lt; \|S\|ε\\leq 2$, with $\|S\|$ being the cardinality of the state space, our algorithm needs only the observations from the most recent $O(1/ε)$ deployments, and achieves a long-run average gap of at most $\\widetilde{O}(\\sqrt{ε/H})$.

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [Cancellation-Free Constrained SquareCB for Safe Controller Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21880

**作者：** Kartik Pandit、Sourav Ganguly、Arnob Ghosh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21880v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究带预算约束的上下文 bandit，用对成本做修正加权的 SquareCB 替代拉格朗日乘子式约简，给出 regret 与不可抵消的违规界，并在四旋翼与楼宇控制选择任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「每轮违规不可由其他轮松弛抵消」的约束度量与预算内动作选择，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的计算成本约束及过程性评价提供参照，但联系较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cancellation-Free Constrained SquareCB for Safe Controller Selection

**abstract/A1：** We study constrained contextual bandits, in which a learner chooses one of $K$ actions in each round to minimize loss while keeping the expected cost of its play below a budget. Existing reductions to online regression weight the cost by a Lagrange multiplier learned from past constraint residuals, so slack in some rounds can offset violations in others. We instead run SquareCB proposed in \\citep{foster2020beyond} with a rectified weight on the cost: it is zero when the estimated cost is within the budget, and otherwise just large enough to bring it back to the budget. Under realizability and Slater's condition with margin $δ$, the algorithm attains regret $O(\\sqrt{KT\\,\\mathrm{Reg}\_{\\mathsf{Sq}}(T)}/δ)$ and cancellation-free violation $\\sum\_t(V\_c^{p\_t}-b)^+=O(\\sqrt{KT\\,\\mathrm{Reg}\_{\\mathsf{Sq}}(T)})$, where $\\mathrm{Reg}\_{\\mathsf{Sq}}$ is the error of the regression oracle. The same bounds hold when the budget must be met in every context, potentially adversarially chosen contexts. In controller selection for a safe-control-gym quadrotor and a BOPTEST building, the violation is 6 to 9 times lower than that of primal-dual and virtual-queue baselines, with a lower or comparable regret.

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [REEL: Brain-Guided Video Generation and Selection by Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear Decoding from fMRI data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21907v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21907

**作者：** Fabrizio Spera、Tommaso Boccato、Moein Khajehnejad、Nicola Toschi、Matteo Ferrante

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21907v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21907v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 REEL 框架，用线性语义解码器从 fMRI 信号预测预训练视频生成器的嵌入，并以贝叶斯式的「提议—重排」方式生成多个视频候选再加以筛选，在 CC2017 与 BMD 上报告 50 路视频识别准确率的提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「多候选生成 + 借助读出信号重排」的结构与 LVR 中多种 latent 假设的生成、视觉证据筛选问题形式相近，可能为候选评估与选择机制提供旁证；但研究对象是脑信号解码而非 VLM 推理，实际可迁移程度需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** REEL: Brain-Guided Video Generation and Selection by Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear Decoding from fMRI data

**abstract/A1：** Visual brain decoding from functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data has made remarkable progress in recent years, driven by increasingly powerful artificial intelligence (AI) models and the growing availability of large-scale public datasets. However, despite the impressive advances achieved in static image decoding, the reconstruction of videos from fMRI signals remains challenging due to the temporal mismatch between rapidly changing video stimuli and the slow dynamics of the blood-oxygen-level-dependent (BOLD) response. We evaluate a Bayesian-inspired proposal-and-rerank strategy on CC2017 and the BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD), using a linear semantic decoder, a pretrained video generator, and three neural encoding streams with ridge readouts and learned pooling in two streams. We refer to this framework as REEL (Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear decoding). Compared with a single generated candidate, reranking increases mean Video 50-way accuracy from 0.2359 to 0.2434 across three CC2017 participants and from 0.3653 to 0.3920 across ten BMD participants. These findings suggest an alternative and conceptually simpler strategy for improving video brain decoding, highlighting the potential of our approach as a complementary direction to increasingly complex pipelines.

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [A Temporal Algebraic Framework for Data Stream Evaluation Under Delayed Labels and Distribution Shifts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21914v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21914

**作者：** Ronaldo C. Prati、Elaine Ribeiro de Faria

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.DB

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21914v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21914v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把可获得 micro/macro 指标的代数混淆矩阵框架扩展到数据流场景，用指数衰减或滑窗加权计数，并以观测掩码处理延迟或选择性到达的标签，同时用无先验坐标和一阶 Jacobian 分解区分先验漂移与概念漂移。

**相关性简评：** 其「标签未到先用校准概率计数」的掩码机制与漂移/性能退化的分解，可能可供核查 LVR 中证据或标注延迟、部分监督下的推理评价协议，以及把 latent 贡献下降与分布变化区分开的问题表述。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Temporal Algebraic Framework for Data Stream Evaluation Under Delayed Labels and Distribution Shifts

**abstract/A1：** In data streams, labels often arrive late or only for some instances, class prevalence drifts, and rare classes vanish from short windows. Standard evaluation then confuses a degraded decision boundary with a change in prevalence, and is biased when labels are delayed or selected by the model. We extend an algebraic framework that obtains pooled (micro) and per-class (macro) measures from one confusion matrix to streams, weighting the counts in time by exponential fading or a sliding window; the update costs O(m^2) per step, whatever the stream length, and each pooled measure stays a weighted combination of the per-class ones. Delayed and selective feedback enter as an observation mask: until its label arrives, an instance is counted with the model's calibrated class probabilities, and the bias of this interim estimate is driven by their calibration error. Audited conventions report no value for a class without verified instances. We separate prior shift from concept drift by monitoring prior-free coordinates (recall and specificity) and attribute a change in a measure to the two sources with a first-order Jacobian decomposition. On synthetic streams and four real datasets, a detector on these coordinates with a memory per class raises no excess false alarms under label shift, unlike the Jacobian alarm and error-rate detectors. The correction for delayed labels recovers recall in some real settings and is only partial for precision, specificity and F1. Twenty-five statements are checked in Lean 4, and the code is in the aggmetrics package.

**comment/C1：** 53 pages, 25 tables, 2 figures. Code: https://github.com/rcprati/data-stream-evaluation (experiments), https://github.com/rcprati/aggmetrics-datastream-lean (Lean 4 formalization), https://github.com/rcprati/aggmetrics (aggmetrics &gt;= 0.4.0, subpackage aggmetrics.stream)

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [Wrong Input, Wrong Threshold: Re-evaluating Adversarial Facial Privacy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21923v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21923

**作者：** Tunahan Parlayıcı、Erchan Aptoula、Yücel Saygın

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21923v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21923v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文重新审视人脸隐私对抗方法的评测协议，指出输入前端（检测、对齐、裁剪）与判定阈值均存在缺陷，导致成功率指标饱和，进而改用成对余弦位移来度量防护效果并给出可微对齐的修正。

**相关性简评：** 其对相似度度量中阈值依赖、成功率差值饱和以及预处理/重采样不一致的关注，可能为 LVR 中基于 latent 相似度的比较（阈值选择、图像前端对齐对内部表示的影响）提供可供核查的评测线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Wrong Input, Wrong Threshold: Re-evaluating Adversarial Facial Privacy

**abstract/A1：** Published face-privacy methods report protection above 90% against black-box recognition models. That number comes from an evaluation protocol with two independent defects, reported in a measure that cannot size either one. The input is wrong: methods published since 2022 feed the network the whole photograph, or a detector's box, where deployed systems detect, align to a landmark template and crop. Re-scoring the same files through a realistic front end costs a standard transfer attack 56% of its dodging protection and a budget-allocating attack 7%, with no image regenerated. The measure is wrong: that loss is a difference of success rates, so it saturates, moving by 8.2x over a budget sweep while the effect beneath it moves by 1.34x. We therefore report the paired cosine shift, in which both thresholds cancel algebraically, so the input defect holds without committing to a calibration. Measured that way it is 2.2x and 10.7x larger on three unseen recognizers, and at matched budget it reverses the L-infinity transfer ranking: the one surveyed method that aligns at evaluation gains 0.18 where MI-FGSM loses 0.05. The cause is a resampling mismatch, not perturbation lost outside the crop; optimising through a differentiable alignment operator largely removes it, adding +24 to +58 points on unseen recognizers and a commercial system, with a residual on one of them. The thresholds are wrong: the four constants inherited since 2021 hold the false-accept rate near its nominal 1% on aligned crops but admit 2.3-5.4% on whole images, with an opposite-signed bias across dodging and impersonation, the two tasks reported side by side. Differentiable alignment and gallery scoring are not new: the first generation of this literature used both, and we show what dropping them cost.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages main text and 15 pages supplementary material. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Information Forensics and Security. This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Wheelchair Configuration Classification in Accessible Automated Vehicle Applications](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21945v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21945

**作者：** Samir Ahmed、Shaaz Saleem、Devin Dhami、Wesley Maia、Angel Martinez-Sanchez、Parthib Roy、Jannatul Ferdous Srabonee、Ahmed Sabbir Arif、Ross Greer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21945v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21945v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 WCID 轮椅构型图像数据集（467 张、仅用于评测，标注手动/电动、子类型、占用、视角、遮挡、光照等），并在外部数据上训练三种 CNN backbone 与一个冻结 VLM probe，考察跨数据集泛化及高斯模糊下的鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其冻结视觉语言 probe 与遮挡、模糊条件标注，可能可供核查 VLM 内部状态是否保留细粒度视觉属性及其证据筛选的脆弱性；但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态或停止决策，仅属外围线索，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Wheelchair Configuration Classification in Accessible Automated Vehicle Applications

**abstract/A1：** Making automated vehicles accessible requires consideration of vulnerable road users, including wheelchair users. To offer a more tailored experience that supports independence for this group, vehicle design needs to accommodate the wide range of wheelchair configurations. However, wheelchairs are individualized, varying in frame geometry, propulsion, and visual appearance. Moreover, occupants or accessories may obscure visual cues. Existing mobility-aid datasets only label the presence of a wheelchair, rather than describing its specific configuration. To fill this gap, this paper introduces the Wheelchair Configuration Image Dataset (WCID), comprising 467 face-blurred images across 192 capture groups solely for evaluation. Each image is annotated with (i) a two-class label of manual or powered, (ii) a chair subtype, (iii) occupancy, viewpoint, setting, lighting, occlusion, propulsion, and control, with a 273-image validation split and 194-image test split. Three convolutional neural network backbones and a frozen vision-language probe are trained on an external image dataset and evaluated on WCID to measure cross-dataset generalization, while also testing robustness using Gaussian blur. Convolutional models do well on clean images, but performance drops under strong blur, leaving a gap before deployment. Code, split assignments, labels, trained weights, and the face-blurred images is available at: https://github.com/Mi3-Lab/MAID

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [DiscoveryAgent: A Framework for Scientist-centric Agentic AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21950v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21950

**作者：** Owen Queen、Ioana Ciuca、Emma Dann、Jean Somalwar、Carine You、Steven Dillmann、Viraj Manwadkar、Evan Gorrell、Harrison G Zhang、Jonathan K Pritchard、Risa H Wechsler、James Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21950v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21950v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DiscoveryAgent——一个领域无关、以科学家为中心的 agentic 研究框架：科学家在每轮之间选择或替换假设，audit agent 借助 checkpoint 记录追溯论断、复现分析并纠错，并在天文分类、蛋白变异效应预测及三个领域案例中验证。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线距离较远，但其中对中间 checkpoint 的审计式追溯可供核查 latent 轨迹的贡献归属与证据核验，而「图像与光度互补失效」的多模态融合案例可能为视觉证据筛选提供线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DiscoveryAgent: A Framework for Scientist-centric Agentic AI

**abstract/A1：** AI agents can assist scientific research, but balancing autonomous investigation with scientists' ability to inspect and redirect ongoing work remains a central design challenge. We present DiscoveryAgent, a domain-agnostic, scientist-centric system that combines autonomous investigation with scientist-directed hypothesis steering. Scientists select, refine, or replace hypotheses between research rounds, while audit agents interrogate checkpointed records to trace claims, reproduce analyses, and inform subsequent decisions. Across two predictive modeling tasks, DiscoveryAgent produced the best variable-star classifier among evaluated baselines and, with scientist steering, a protein variant-effect predictor surpassed by only one of 106 ProteinGym baselines. We develop a rigorous checkpointing system that enables highly capable audit agents, a scalable form of oversight for agentic research. Audit agents autonomously identify errors at higher rates than previous AI scientist agents, and scientists collaborating with audit agents identify 14x more valid errors versus scientists performing manual review. In three case studies led by domain experts across physical science, social science, and life science, we developed a multimodal photometric-redshift estimator that resolves complementary failures of images and photometry, achieving 0.971 AUROC for low-redshift classification; documented an 83% rise in Florida homeowners-insurance premiums from 2013 to 2023, with a growing, regressive burden; and identified CD4+ T-cell relay genes, which both receive and exert broad transcriptional regulation, enriched for autoimmune disease associations. Together, these results show how autonomous research can expand scientists' empirical reach while centering their authority over research direction and their ability to understand and challenge the evidence.

**comment/C1：** Supplementary information is available as an ancillary file (supplementary\_information.pdf)

</details>

### 206. [How Many Persistent Features Does a Biometric Need? A Law Linking Equal Error Rate to Feature Count and Persistence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21973v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21973

**作者：** Oleg Komogortsev、Mehedi Hasan Raju

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21973v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21973v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为生物特征识别推导解析规律：在独立高斯特征与余弦打分下，等错误率 EER 由特征数、特征间相关所决定的有效特征数 N\_eff，以及跨比较的特征持久性离散度 τ 共同决定，并指出 τ 会形成特征数无法突破的误差地板；作者用已发表模拟与多种生物特征数据做无拟合参数验证，并给出「加特征何时仍有效」的设计规则。

**相关性简评：** 其「有效特征数」「持久性波动造成的误差下限」「增加特征收益递减」的定量表述，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的有效维度、跨步骤状态稳定性与可用信息上限提供类比和评价线索；论文不含视觉或多模态推理实验，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Many Persistent Features Does a Biometric Need? A Law Linking Equal Error Rate to Feature Count and Persistence

**abstract/A1：** A biometric designer controls the number of features and their temporal persistence. Earlier empirical work linked the equal error rate (EER) to persistence and to the required number of features by straight lines. We derive the law behind these regularities. For $N$ independent standardized Gaussian features with test-retest correlation $ρ$, scored by cosine similarity, Fisher's $z$-transform turns the genuine and impostor scores into two normals, giving $\\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-\\frac{1}{2}\\sqrt{N}\\,\\operatorname{atanh}ρ)$ and, inversely, $N=(2q/\\operatorname{atanh}ρ)^2$ with $q=Φ^{-1}(1-\\mathrm{EER})$; intercorrelated features enter through an effective count $N\_{\\mathrm{eff}}$. The central extension lets persistence vary across comparisons with spread $τ$ on Fisher's scale: $\\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-m/(\\sqrt{τ^2+v}+\\sqrt{v}))$ with $v=1/(N\_{\\mathrm{eff}}-2)$, which for a normal spread has a floor $Φ(-m/τ)$ that no number of features can lower. With no fitted parameter the simple law reproduces published simulations and the EER-ICC line, and predicts the KCC-EER relation of eye-movement embeddings on GazeBase ($R^2=0.88$), though less well on GazeBaseVR ($0.63$). Read from the genuine and impostor scores, the general form matches the EER of eye movements, keystroke dynamics, the ECG and face embeddings over 101 conditions with $R^2=0.98$ and a median error of $0.6$ points, against $0.87$ and $1.9$ points for the simple law. The 61 eye-movement, keystroke and ECG conditions check consistency on the same scores; the 40 face conditions use held-out subjects, with the same accuracy. The product $τ\\sqrt{N\_{\\mathrm{eff}}-2}$ gives a design rule: adding or decorrelating features pays fully while it is well below one and little once it exceeds about three, where only higher or more uniform persistence helps.

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Pathology Randomization for Synthetic Priors and Zero-Shot Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21975v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21975

**作者：** Alexandra G. Roberts、Tonmoy Hossain、Mark Olchanyi、Stefan Brandstätter、Jonathan Williams Ramirez、Ana Lawry Aguila、Matthew Rosen、Annabel Sorby-Adams、W. Taylor Kimberly、Juan Eugenio Iglesias

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21975v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21975v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PathoDraw，在健康脑部解剖标签图上条件生成病灶，再经对比度无关渲染合成带病理的 MRI，用单一 3D UNet 同时估计健康解剖与带符号强度偏差，从而在未接触真实病理的情况下实现跨对比度、跨分辨率的零样本异常检测。

**相关性简评：** 「健康先验 + 带符号偏差」的分解与合成先验训练，或可供核查是否能类比 LVR 中参考状态与偏离量的 latent 表示划分，但论文不涉及推理轨迹、证据获取或停止决策，联系较为间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pathology Randomization for Synthetic Priors and Zero-Shot Detection

**abstract/A1：** Hospital brain imaging archives hold a diverse array of pathology at sample sizes beyond prospective studies, particularly for underrepresented populations and diseases. To unlock uncurated clinical data and scale analysis, methods are needed to detect abnormalities across contrasts and resolutions. Synthetic data has provided robust, contrast-agnostic models for a variety of human neuroimaging tasks in healthy anatomy, but pathology detection and other tasks in clinical settings still require real patient data, contrast-specific models, or disease-specific generators. We propose PathoDraw, a generative model of brain MRI with pathology. A conditional distribution of lesions given the healthy anatomy is drawn into the label map before contrast-agnostic rendering, and a single network estimates healthy anatomy with exact signed intensity deviation of any input scan. The contrast-agnostic 3D UNet learns the healthy labels under the lesion and the signed deviation directly and identifies pathology via robust tissue statistics and learned features, despite no training on real pathology. We show zero-shot detection on public stroke, tumor, and white matter hyperintensity datasets as well as archived clinical data, spanning $T\_1$w, FLAIR and $T\_2$w at various resolutions under a recent unsupervised anomaly detection benchmark protocol. Our single model exceeds or performs comparably to contrast-specific anomaly detectors trained on real scans and keeps healthy anatomy intact. The same generator trains networks for super-resolution and for registration in the presence of pathology. Our model, robust across acquisition and disease, enables the use of uncurated, archived data for clinical analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/agr78/Pathology-Randomizer.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 9 figures, 13 tables (8-page main text with appendices). Code: https://github.com/agr78/Pathology-Randomizer

</details>

### 208. [Beyond Appearance: Where Forest Visual Place Recognition Actually Fails](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21996v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21996

**作者：** Blake Matheson、Ben McShanag、Isabella Moffat、Michael Milford、Peyman Moghadam、Dana Kulić、Somayeh Hussaini、Kavindie Katuwandeniya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21996v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21996v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在森林视觉地点识别任务上，以 MegaLoc 为仅外观基线，在 WildCross 基准上系统比较序列匹配、视觉语言描述与重排、场景图及深度主干图等多种时序/语言/结构/几何上下文表示，并分析各自失效的原因。

**相关性简评：** 其负结果提示「增加上下文表示并不必然提升判别力」，且重排受初始候选列表限制，可能可供核查 latent 推理中视觉证据筛选与候选取证瓶颈的类比问题，但任务与机制均相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Appearance: Where Forest Visual Place Recognition Actually Fails

**abstract/A1：** Visual place recognition (VPR) in forest environments remains challenging because repetitive vegetation, and changes in illumination, season, and viewpoint can make distinct places appear similar and revisited places appear substantially different. In this paper, we investigate whether temporal, linguistic, structural, and geometric context can improve on an appearance-only descriptor, and what limits each representation when it does not. Using MegaLoc as the appearance-based baseline on the WildCross benchmark, we evaluate score-space and descriptor-space sequence matching, vision-language captions and reranking, scene-graph representations, and depth-derived stem graphs across same-direction revisits, extended routes, and reverse traversals. On a same-direction revisit, both sequence matching strategies reach similar Recall@1 (94.1% vs. 91.5%), but recall at 100% precision rises from 50.9% (single-frame MegaLoc) to 71.6% in score space and falls to 36.5% in descriptor space. The remaining context representations provide no consistent improvement over the appearance-based baseline. Same-place captions show limited place-specific discrimination, reranking is constrained by the initial candidate shortlist, and stem-graph matching remains weak even with LiDAR depth.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures, accepted to the Australasian Conference on Robotics and Automation (ACRA)

</details>

### 209. [On Formal Counterfactual Explanations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22010v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22010

**作者：** Alexey Ignatiev、Peter J. Stuckey、Joao Marques-Silva

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22010v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22010v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在形式化 XAI 框架下把 closest-world 反事实解释刻画为所有极小距离反例实例的析取范式，并提出由特征文字合取构成、可保证预测翻转的反事实解释子类 CFXps，给出其与 CXps、AXps 的结构联系及复用现有 AXp/CXp 机制的模块化提取方法，并在树集成与神经网络基准上评估效率。

**相关性简评：** 其极小充分特征集（AXp）与极小命中集对偶，可能为 LVR 中「答案是否依赖必要视觉证据」的最小证据筛选与贡献分配提供形式化刻画，可供核查；但论文面向表格分类器，迁移到多模态 latent 状态仍需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On Formal Counterfactual Explanations

**abstract/A1：** Counterfactual explanations are often seen as counterfactual conditionals under closest-world semantics, requiring one to reason over all minimally distant counterexamples. In contrast, formal XAI typically relies on feature-based abductive explanations (AXps) and contrastive explanations (CXps), which admit efficient computation and a minimal hitting-set duality. This paper bridges these perspectives by (i) characterizing closest-world counterfactual explanations explicitly as a disjunctive normal form over all minimally distant counterexample instances, and (ii) introducing conjunctive counterfactual explanations (CFXps), a subclass of counterfactual antecedents restricted to conjunctions of feature literals that guarantee prediction change. The paper establishes structural results connecting CXps, AXps and CFXps: every CXp induces an AXp for its witness instance, and every CXp together with a witness AXp induces a CFXp whose support is precisely determined by that AXp. These theoretical results yield a modular approach for computing CFXps using existing AXp/CXp machinery. Experiments on a representative set of benchmarks using tree ensembles and neural networks show that CFXp extraction is practically efficient. Compared with popular point-based counterfactual explainers, CFXps generally require no more feature changes and can guarantee the counterfactual prediction beyond a single instance.

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22017v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22017

**作者：** Gengyue Dong、Ye Wei、Zheng Zhu、Yidong Han、Jiale Miao、Xiaojun Jia、Guanghui Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22017v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22017v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SkillBuffBench 基准与配对评测协议，将 agent skill 的获取方式设为三级因素，并与记忆开关、多种 harness 与模型交叉，在材料科学与生物学专家任务上衡量技能的真实贡献，报告确定性验证得分及成本、轮次与耗时；当前版本仅描述设计与协议，结果待补。

**相关性简评：** 其「配对因素交叉、分离技能与记忆来源、成本单列」的评测设计可供核查，可能为区分 latent 推理步骤、视觉证据与记忆对 VLM 推理收益的各自贡献提供方法模板，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory

**abstract/A1：** Agent Skills, file-based packages of procedural knowledge that a coding agent can discover and load on demand, are now shipped by every major agent harness, yet the evidence for how much they help comes almost entirely from settings in which the right Skill is hand-picked for the task and the agent starts from a blank slate. Neither holds in deployment, where an agent chooses from whatever library happens to be installed and frequently carries memory from earlier work. We present SkillBuffBench, a benchmark and paired evaluation protocol that treats Skill access as a three-level factor (no Skill, a curated Skill, and automatic invocation from an installed library), crosses it with memory (off and on), with four agent harnesses and with multiple models (the state-of-the-art proprietary models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI, and the state-of-the-art open-weight models from Chinese providers), every model being run under every harness, and evaluates the result on research tasks contributed by domain experts together with the Skills they use, currently in materials science and biology, with the suite extended on a rolling basis. Every run is marked by a deterministic verifier; uncertainty is reported with a paired, task-level bootstrap; and cost, agent rounds and wall-clock time are reported alongside the score rather than folded into it. This version of the paper describes the design of the benchmark, its evaluation protocol and its current tasks and Skills. The evaluation is in progress: results will be added once the runs are complete, and the task suite, results and analysis will continue to be updated on a rolling basis.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Work in progress: benchmark design and protocol; results to be added in a later version

</details>
