### 211. [An AI-Based OSSE Framework to Evaluate Learned Physical Consistency: A Multi-Model Study Using FourCastNet, Aurora, Prithvi WxC, and GraphCast](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22024v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22024

**作者：** Yash Diggikar、Milton Halem、Aishwarya Raman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22024v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22024v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于 AI 天气模型的 OSSE 框架：在 60 天自回归预报中插入 ERA5 温度或风场观测，比较 FourCastNet、GraphCast、Aurora、Prithvi WxC 对同一观测信息的响应与物理一致性，并报告 GraphCast 高层级配置下的数值不稳定。

**相关性简评：** 其「注入观测后自回归状态是否按预期改变、不同架构响应差异与反馈失控」的设置，可能为 LVR 中潜状态是否真正消费视觉证据、架构对证据贡献分配的影响提供类比性核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An AI-Based OSSE Framework to Evaluate Learned Physical Consistency: A Multi-Model Study Using FourCastNet, Aurora, Prithvi WxC, and GraphCast

**abstract/A1：** Observing System Simulation Experiments (OSSEs) are a central methodology for evaluating future atmospheric observing systems. We present the first AI-based OSSE framework to test whether different AI weather models can reproduce comparable physical impact from new observations that traditional physics based models generate. We evaluate four distinct architectural AI models on the Charney conjecture that incomplete historical observations can infer the present state of the atmospheric. We considner- FourCastNet V2, GraphCast, Aurora and Prithvi WxC and integrate each autoregressively over a 60-day forecast horizon. After a 30-day spin-up, we insert ERA5 temperature fields to infer winds and related atmospheric fields. Similar OSSE experiments are conducted for wind insertion. We measure the performance of each model's response to observational information on a 30-day data assimilation cycle forecast with and without data insertion. Distinct architectural AI weather models respond differently to the same OSSE observations. Foundation models (i.e. Prithvi WxC and Aurora), produce the strongest impact. For example, while GraphCast is the weakest, all three of the four models capture the temperature-to-geopotential height response. The two foundational models retain a positive, non-trivial, tropical zonal wind at 500 hPa response to temperature insertion. Various data insertion experiments can vary significantly even within a given architecture. We identify an architecture-specific numerical instability in GraphCast's full 37-level configuration that produces a runaway feedback concentrated at the uppermost levels with ERA5 temperature insertion correctable by excluding levels at or below 100 hPa.

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [BATMoS: Budget-Aware Guidance Allocation for Efficient Video Diffusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22028v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22028

**作者：** Zhixu Du、Kuo Yang、Jingwei Sun、Hai "Helen" Li、Yiran Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22028v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22028v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究视频扩散模型固定推理预算在去噪时间轴上的分配问题，提出 BATMoS：依据幅值的时间规则在新鲜 classifier-free guidance 与缓存 guidance 之间路由，用单一整数控制预算，并在 CogVideoX-2B 的 VBench 十六维上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「沿轨迹不同阶段的算力价值不相等、可缓存复用少量评估」的预算分配框架，可能为 LVR 中动态计算预算与 latent 状态复用的策略设计和评价提供类比线索，但对象是视频生成 guidance，迁移性需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BATMoS: Budget-Aware Guidance Allocation for Efficient Video Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** The effectiveness of a video diffusion model's inference budget depends on where computation is spent along the denoising trajectory. Across eight equal-budget inference configurations, VBench total scores span 4.80 points, highlighting the importance of explicit allocation design. Our key insight is that guidance evaluations are not equally valuable across diffusion time: retaining the sampling updates while selectively reusing guidance can preserve generation quality with fewer denoiser evaluations. We introduce BATMoS (Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies), which routes between fresh classifier-free guidance and cached guidance using a single pretrained denoiser. A magnitude-based temporal rule assigns guidance reuse to low-magnitude blocks and reserves fresh guidance for the remaining blocks, with a single integer controlling the computation budget. The resulting schedule requires no additional router training or per-budget threshold search. Evaluated on CogVideoX-2B with 4,720 videos per configuration across all sixteen official VBench dimensions, BATMoS achieves the highest total score among the eight allocation policies and comes within 0.10 points of full classifier-free guidance using 20\\% fewer branch evaluations. BATMoS also composes with feature reuse, reducing computation from 80 to 62 effective branch evaluations with a 0.11-point decrease in total score. These findings make temporal guidance allocation a practical mechanism for extracting more generation quality from a fixed inference budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [What Does an Agent Need? Bundle-Aware Agent Resource Composition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22030v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22030

**作者：** Yunxiao Shi、Tingwei Chen、Dannong Xu、Ahad N. Zehmakan、Jialie Shen、Zhendong Chu、Qingsong Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22030v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22030v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Task-Conditioned Agent Resource Composition 问题，即联合选择 backbone 模型与工具技能包以最大化端到端执行效用；方法检索相似任务的候选资源与历史配置，用微调生成器加约束束搜索重组候选，并以 LLM 教师和历史成功配置作为执行代理训练 critic，在有真实执行反馈时继续适配。

**相关性简评：** 其「用执行代理与历史成功配置替代真实执行来排序候选」以及少量目标域查询即可适配的做法，可能为 LVR 中缺少直接监督时的 latent 假设筛选与验证提供参照，但原文并不涉及视觉推理状态，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does an Agent Need? Bundle-Aware Agent Resource Composition

**abstract/A1：** An agent resource's value depends on its backbone and companion resources and is known only after execution. Token-based generators remain tied to their training catalog, while independent resource scoring misses complementarities and reused bundles target other tasks. We formulate Task-Conditioned Agent Resource Composition (AgentComposition): jointly selecting a backbone model and a tool-skill bundle to maximize end-to-end execution utility. Our Retrieval-Grounded Bundle-Aware Agent Generation retrieves candidate resources and past configurations of similar tasks. A fine-tuned generator reads resource descriptions and recombines candidates through constrained beam search, allowing new resources without retraining. A bundle-aware critic ranks complete configurations using a large language model (LLM) teacher and historically successful configurations as execution proxies, and adapts to execution feedback when available. We evaluate reference recovery, offline execution with judged and native rewards, and online recommendation with real execution, building AgentSelect-Exec with 5,060 executed configurations. Our method recovers the complete reference configuration within ten candidates for 67.73% of AgentSelect queries, nearly twice the strongest baseline, and leads all baselines on SkillsBench. The proxy-trained critic raises the top-ranked agent's execution score from 7.58 to 8.37 out of 10; execution feedback increases it to 8.78, above the strongest baselines. Four target-domain queries suffice to adapt the generator to SkillsBench. Agents recommended online outperform the strongest baselines under both an LLM judge and native verifiers. These results support learning and transferring joint resource composition from task experience.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, including appendices. Code: https://github.com/Ancientshi/AgentComposition

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [GP--RL--MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control with Gaussian Process Postdecision State-Value Functions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22038v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22038

**作者：** Jon Li、Jay H. Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22038v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22038v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GP-RL-MPC：用高斯过程学到的 critic 作为 MPC 终端代价以计入时域外成本，在乐观近似策略迭代中数据高效学习，并以可调风险系数加权 GP 认知不确定性、用 postdecision state 处理终端转移的偶然不确定性，再用稀疏 DTC 后验降低在线评估开销；在 CSTR 扰动仿真中相对匹配基线平均降低 80.52% 的 SSE。

**相关性简评：** 其「对价值预测的认知不确定性显式加权」与后决策状态更新，可能为 LVR 中基于置信度的证据筛选、停止决策及 latent 状态更新的不确定性建模提供可供核查的思路，但属控制仿真域，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GP--RL--MPC: Uncertainty-Aware Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control with Gaussian Process Postdecision State-Value Functions

**abstract/A1：** We propose Gaussian Process Reinforcement Learning-Augmented Model Predictive Control (GP--RL--MPC), which uses a learned critic as the MPC terminal penalty to account for costs beyond the prediction horizon. Gaussian process regression (GPR) enables data-efficient critic learning from limited, noisy rollouts within optimistic approximate policy iteration (OAPI), with soft updates mitigating policy chattering. A tunable risk coefficient weights GP epistemic uncertainty in the terminal cost, regulating sensitivity to uncertain critic predictions. The postdecision state formulation shifts disturbance averaging offline to account for aleatoric uncertainty from the terminal transition onward. Under matching objectives and admissible actions, a one-step horizon recovers greedy value-space RL. After fitting GP hyperparameters by exact marginal likelihood, we construct a sparse deterministic training conditional (DTC) posterior using inducing points selected by pivoted Cholesky (PC), reducing online evaluation costs as the training dataset grows. In a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) subject to exogenous disturbances, GP--RL--MPC with an appropriately selected risk coefficient and policy screening reduces the sum of squared errors (SSE) by an average of 80.52% relative to an otherwise matched baseline MPC across five random seeds (sample standard deviation: 3.48%).

**comment/C1：** 19 pages including references and appendices, 3 figures, 2 tables. Submitted to L4DC 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [DSADiff: Data Structure-Aware Diffusion for Crystal Structure Prediction via Mitigating Complexity- and Imbalance-Induced Biases](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22050v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22050

**作者：** Jiang Xiaobo、Dinghong Lai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22050v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22050v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究扩散模型用于晶体结构预测时的生成偏差问题：提出 LAS 分层分析框架，将晶体复杂度与元素组成不平衡识别为影响生成难度的两个关键因素，并据此提出 DSADiff，把二者统一为「生成难度表示」以联合驱动特征建模与训练策略优化，在 Carbon-24 等三个基准上提升 Match Rate 与 RMSE。

**相关性简评：** 其「复杂度/不平衡→统一难度表示→条件化控制表示与训练」的思路，可能可供核查用于按任务难度调节 latent 推理的计算与监督强度，以及对生成偏差来源做机制性拆解；但领域为材料生成，与视觉推理无直接联系，属较远启发线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DSADiff: Data Structure-Aware Diffusion for Crystal Structure Prediction via Mitigating Complexity- and Imbalance-Induced Biases

**abstract/A1：** Crystal Structure Prediction (CSP) is an important step in accelerating the generation and discovery of new materials. In recent years, diffusion-based CSP methods have made significant progress. However, existing models still suffer from generation bias when facing diverse materials spaces, while systematic approaches for tracing the sources of such performance variations remain limited. To address this issue, we propose LAS (Layered Analysis Strategy), a mechanism analysis framework that identifies crystal complexity and elemental composition imbalance as two key factors affecting generation difficulty and further reveals their relationships with model generation performance. Building on these findings, we further propose DSADiff (Data Structure-Aware Diffusion), a data structure-aware diffusion model that transforms the key factors identified by LAS into a unified generation difficulty representation and uses it to jointly drive feature modeling and training strategy optimization. This enables the model to adapt its representation and generation capability to samples with different data structures and difficulty levels, thereby mitigating learning bias across samples. Experiments on three CSP benchmark datasets, including Carbon-24, demonstrate that DSADiff consistently improves both Match Rate and RMSE, with a maximum Match Rate improvement of 9%, surpassing existing state-of-the-art methods and achieving new state-of-the-art performance. This work deepens the understanding of diffusion-based CSP models from the perspective of generation bias and provides a new direction for designing less biased generative models for complex materials spaces.

</details>

<a id="paper-216"></a>

### 216. [RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22052v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22052

**作者：** Song Yan、Chenfeng Wang、Wei Zhai、Xingchen Wang、Tao Zhang、Yawen Shao、Shi Pan、Min Li、Zheng-Jun Zha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22052v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22052v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RiDEx，在扩散模型去噪速度场中把局部探索集中到与奖励相关的子空间，并在同一生成状态施加等量反向扰动，以终端奖励差作为有符号局部监督，再结合全局 DiffusionNFT 学习。

**相关性简评：** 「同状态反向扰动—终端奖励差」的局部信用分配思路可能可供核查 latent 推理中单个状态或方向对最终答案的因果贡献；但对象是图像生成，与 VLM 推理的联系尚需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reward-based diffusion post-training updates model parameters from task rewards. While existing approaches improve sampling and policy optimization, they still rely largely on isotropic random perturbations in a high-dimensional latent space for exploration. Such variation often spends the perturbation budget on changes with little influence on the target reward, making exploration inefficient. We study where diffusion models should explore and how to learn from that exploration. We introduce Reward-Informed Directional Exploration (RiDEx), which concentrates local exploration in reward-relevant subspaces of the denoising velocity field. Conditional residuals or differentiable reward gradients propose the subspace, and equal-and-opposite perturbations at the same generation state measure its terminal reward response. The reward difference supplies signed local supervision in the explored coordinates, while all generated images contribute to global DiffusionNFT learning. RiDEx thereby couples exploration-space selection with feedback about the changes it produces. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, RiDEx attains the highest peak rewards among the evaluated methods on four alignment and preference tasks. Its gradient-direction instantiation achieves 15x and 29x target-attainment speedups over DiffusionNFT on HPSv2 and PickScore, measured in H200 GPU hours. Controlled experiments confirm that reward-aligned directions elicit stronger responses at equal perturbation magnitude, and that applying their measured credit improves curve-average reward across all four tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22055

**作者：** Hyunjong Ok、Jaeho Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22055v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22055v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察「weight-space programming」：在无梯度训练、仅给定少量标注样例和沙盒的条件下，让三个前沿 LLM 直接写出固定架构的权重或自行设计架构，发现自由设计时它们都收敛到稀疏查表加固定查询流程，以约 1/1800 的参数量超过同一 LLM 手写的 Transformer，并据此提出联想记忆式模型。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本多选题、无视觉与推理轨迹，与 LVR 主线联系弱；但「稀疏表 + 固定查表过程」这种可读出权重含义、删去一行只改变约一个答案的显式状态结构，可能为 latent 状态的可解释性、贡献分配与计算成本比较提供参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same LLM wrote while using about 1,800x fewer parameters. Without gradients, they choose a table, in which the LLM can state what each weight means and can find and rewrite a wrong one. Each wrong answer points to one row, and removing a row changes about one other answer. From this, we propose an associative-memory-based model up to 17x faster on GPUs than a same-sized Transformer. We also test pretrained weights, other benchmarks, and tasks where models generate answers. Pretrained LLMs remain more accurate; the claim concerns which structure to build when writing weights.

**comment/C1：** Preprint (Work in progress)

</details>

<a id="paper-218"></a>

### 218. [Can You Undo It? Reversibility Classes for AI Agent Actions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22075v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22075

**作者：** Deepak Waghmare、Shaad Sufi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22075v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22075v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「可逆性等级」这一五级声明式词汇（含参数敏感规则、保守默认与前置状态捕获契约），覆盖20个MCP服务器、536个工具并附证据链接注册表，通过规则一致性检查、AI辅助评分、2158次记录运行的会话守护评估，以及文件、Git、SQLite与浏览器实验考察恢复是否可行。

**相关性简评：** 「动作是否可撤销」可作为一种行动代价信号，可能与 latent 推理中的停止/继续取证决策及前置状态捕获的表示要求相关，但该文关注 agent 安全分类而非视觉推理，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can You Undo It? Reversibility Classes for AI Agent Actions

**abstract/A1：** Tool-using AI agents change files, databases, access rights and public information, yet permission to call a tool does not establish whether a mistake can be undone. We present Reversibility Classes, a declared five-level vocabulary with argument-sensitive rules, conservative defaults and an explicit prior-state capture contract. The pinned implementation and evidence-linked registry cover 536 tools across 20 MCP servers. All 738 rule-case checks conform to declared expectations, while native annotation mapping differs from draft registry labels for 118 of 481 comparable tools. One AI-assisted reviewer approves 148 ratings, 75 matching the registry; this is not independent agreement. On 2,158 recorded runs, the implemented session guard gates the associated action in all 29 attributable attack-positive runs, compared with 18 for a read/write proxy, while adding nine approval points across 1,209 benign runs. Controlled file, Git, SQLite and three-browser experiments show that recovery depends on captured state, later changes, editor storage and recovery route, and expose a Git merge-state exception. The evidence supports making recovery assumptions explicit and testable. It does not establish registry-wide accuracy, reduced production harm or general recovery reliability.

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [ATLAS-Finance: Benchmarking AI Agents in Realistic Banking Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22085

**作者：** Elaine Lau、Antoine Pangas、Alexandre Finot、Collin Schweiker、Amir Bouzidi、Hui Wen Goh、Andrew Peter Martin、Jonas Mueller

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22085v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ATLAS-Finance 基准：13 家模拟公司中的 100 个端到端投资银行任务，agent 需在邮件、聊天、日历、笔记与文件中检索需求、核对冲突与更新并搭建财务模型，由专家 rubric 评分，前沿模型通过率不足 11%。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本长程办公 agent 基准，与视觉表示和 latent 状态无直接联系，但其「信息分散、跨源冲突与随时间更新、需自判何时停止检索」的任务设计，或可为 LVR 的证据筛选与停止决策评价提供参照线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ATLAS-Finance: Benchmarking AI Agents in Realistic Banking Environments

**abstract/A1：** Professional work involves under-specified assignments, where relevant information is spread across workplace applications and may conflict or change over time. To evaluate how well frontier agents can perform economically valuable work in such settings, we introduce ATLAS-Finance, a benchmark of 100 end-to-end investment-banking tasks across 13 simulated firms. As in real banking workflows, agents must search email, chat, calendars, notes, and files to identify requirements, gather information, reconcile conflicts/updates, and then build complex financial models. Tasks were developed by experienced bankers based on their real deal work and take them 15-30 hours to complete. Agent deliverables are evaluated with expert-authored rubrics (median: 124.5 criteria) covering financial rationale and results, spreadsheet construction, completeness, and formatting. Despite completing substantial portions of the requested work, current agents rarely produce correct deliverables. Across today's frontier models, the best ones (Claude Opus and Fable) pass fewer than 11% of tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [CueSplat: Learning to Schedule Sparse Deformation for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting on Edge Devices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22095v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22095

**作者：** Qingyuan Tang、He Zhu、Shixuan Sun、Anbang Wu、Jingwen Leng、Minyi Guo、Yu Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22095v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22095v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CueSplat 面向边缘设备上的动态 4D 高斯泼溅，在冻结预训练骨干的前提下，用历史帧预测高斯属性，并通过轻量学习型 router 在离线标定的预算内挑选需要精确形变更新的高斯，配合索引复用与漂移触发的全量更新帧来控制累积误差。

**相关性简评：** 其“按预测可靠性与渲染贡献决定哪些单元获得精确更新、并周期性全量重算”的调度思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的选择性刷新、缓存复用与停止/重建时机提供概念参照，但该文并无多模态推理证据，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CueSplat: Learning to Schedule Sparse Deformation for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting on Edge Devices

**abstract/A1：** Deformation-based 4D Gaussian splatting (4DGS) enables dynamic scene rendering. However, evaluating deformation networks for all Gaussians at every frame is compute-intensive on edge devices. We find that Gaussian trajectories are often locally predictable, yet prediction reliability varies across Gaussians and time. Thus, we propose CueSplat, a post-training framework that exploits local temporal regularity while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen and retaining its Gaussian set. Specifically, CueSplat predicts Gaussian attributes from recent history and uses a lightweight learned router to select exact deformation updates within a scene-specific budget calibrated offline. Selection accounts for prediction reliability and rendering contribution. Selected indices are reused across nearby frames to reduce routing overhead, while selected Gaussians still receive exact updates each frame. To control accumulated prediction error, we use drift signals to schedule three consecutive full-deformation frames that rebuild the prediction history. Evaluated across four mainstream 4DGS algorithms, CueSplat achieves up to $6.74\\times$ and $8.15\\times$ speedups on Jetson AGX Orin and Orin NX, respectively, with a negligible quality drop (at most $0.16$ dB in dataset-average PSNR).

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures. Main paper only

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [Diagnosing and Correcting Skin-Tone Color Casts in Ultra-Lightweight Photo Retouching Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22106v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22106

**作者：** Choulhyouc Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22106v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22106v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文诊断超轻量照片修饰网络在肤色区域残留的绿色偏色，指出合成自监督会静默引入该偏色而全图 PSNR/SSIM 几乎无变化，并提出零参数的逐像素、肤色掩码、非对称绿色过量损失，将偏色人像从 52/70 降至 1/70。

**相关性简评：** 其「全图指标掩盖局部区域系统性失效、需区域掩码监督」的观察，可能为 LVR 中整体答案指标掩盖局部视觉证据使用失败、以及区域化监督与诊断提供类比线索，可供核查是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diagnosing and Correcting Skin-Tone Color Casts in Ultra-Lightweight Photo Retouching Networks

**abstract/A1：** Color casts are expected to come from the scene or camera, and a retouching model removes them. We report a case where the model leaves one: an ultra-lightweight retouching network renders human skin greener than the original because it under-restores the skin chroma that a degradation removes and, even on clean inputs, adds a small hue shift of its own. A common paired-data-free recipe, synthetic self-supervision (degrade high-quality images, learn to restore them), silently produces this cast while whole-frame PSNR/SSIM stay essentially unchanged. In a representative 6,825-parameter histogram-conditioned network, the cast appears in 52 of 70 portraits under a ground-truth-anchored, white-balance-free protocol. Both a histogram-conditioned and a CNN-conditioned network develop the cast under synthetic self-supervision, yet both are near-neutral in-domain when trained on MIT-Adobe FiveK expert pairs. Image-level objectives miss it: a global-mean green loss worsens the cast (52/70 to 57/70). With zero added parameters, our per-pixel, skin-masked, asymmetric green-excess loss reduces the cast to 1/70 across stratified skin tones (68.1% to 2.2% over the 70,000-image FFHQ corpus; 64.4% to 0.8% on held-out CelebA-HQ photos). In-domain FiveK PSNR/SSIM are unchanged (+0.13 dB, +0.004), at the cost of a small whole-frame CIEDE2000 increase (8.77 to 9.69) and a magenta over-correction where skin is nearly neutral (skin Delta a\* -1.5 to +4.4 and CIEDE2000 5.6 to 6.4 on clean inputs): the loss targets the signed green-magenta axis, not total skin-color fidelity. The same loss suppresses the cast across three ultra-light architectures. A whole-frame green metric correlates with skin-region CIEDE2000 at the method level (Spearman rho = 0.89). The final network exports to a 35 KB TFLite model.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ACCV 2026. 32 pages including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [RegimeCouncil: Paired-Evidence Routing For Memory Conflict Governance In LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22113

**作者：** Qingcan Kang、Mingyang Liu、Shixiong Kai、Tao Zhong、Mingxuan Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22113v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把记忆失效后负反馈应局限于同一事实还是跨事实传播，形式化为 memory conflict governance，提出 RegimeCouncil：以 Local/Global 两个 advisor 加 Meta Governor 在线仲裁共享范围，不重训 LLM，并在 LongMemEval 更新流与真实历史漂移数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 「保留两种策略、依延迟反馈在线仲裁切换」的结构可能与 LVR 中是否采信 latent 内容、以及 THINK/STOP 的证据与计算取舍形成类比，但论文不含视觉推理，迁移可行性待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RegimeCouncil: Paired-Evidence Routing For Memory Conflict Governance In LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Language agents rely on long-term memory to personalize responses across extended interactions, yet stored facts quietly go stale as the world changes, and an agent often learns that using memory hurt only through delayed feedback after acting on an outdated record. Before the memory store is updated, how far should that negative feedback propagate: should it suppress memory use only on future queries for that same fact (a local policy), or also across other stored facts (a global policy)? Existing memory systems implicitly fix one of these two sharing scopes at design time. We formulate this online scope choice under hidden validity and delayed feedback as memory conflict governance, and prove that neither fixed policy is sufficient on its own. We then propose RegimeCouncil, a lightweight paired-evidence router that retains both scopes as advisors and arbitrates between them online without retraining the LLM. Using revealed comparisons between answering with memory and answering without memory (the no-memory anchor), a Local Advisor tracks the latest outcome on the queried fact alone, while a Global Advisor pools recent outcomes across all stored facts. A Meta Governor follows the Local Advisor by default to avoid cross-fact interference and switches to the Global Advisor only when global pooling accumulates a strictly positive recent utility margin, guaranteeing finite-time switching under delayed feedback. Across LongMemEval update streams and real-world historical drift on TempLAMA and TAQA with Qwen3-30B-A3B and GPT-4o, RegimeCouncil outperforms the better fixed-scope policy by 5.2-7.3 points on primary disagreement episodes and across all 30 held-out settings, while ablations show that removing any of its three modules reduces accuracy by 2-7 points.

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [FrameSeg3D: Learned Local Frames for Segmenting Small Parts on High-Resolution 3D Scans](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22124v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22124

**作者：** Xinwen Zhang、Tongyan Wang、Huawei Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22124v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22124v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对高分辨率 3D 扫描上小部件（指甲、牙齿）分割，先从低分辨率整体扫描预测每个部件的局部坐标系（中心与朝向），再沿法向在原始分辨率下重新取景分割，使单一网络可跨部件类型工作且少标签可训。

**相关性简评：** 其「精度受限于如何看（方向与尺度）而非点数」的主张及局部帧规范化加按需原分辨率重采样，可能为 LVR 中「LOOK」阶段证据的取景、尺度选择与局部几何保留提供启发，但领域距离较远，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrameSeg3D: Learned Local Frames for Segmenting Small Parts on High-Resolution 3D Scans

**abstract/A1：** Segmenting small parts on high-resolution 3D scans, such as nails on hand scans and teeth on intraoral scans, poses three challenges: 1) each part covers a tiny fraction of the scan, 2) its boundary is only a subtle change of height and curvature, and 3) it faces its own direction. Existing methods fall into two groups, and both fall short. The first group processes the whole scan at once and sees each part too coarsely. Point and mesh networks must shrink the scan to fit in memory, so a small part keeps only a few of its points. Multi-view methods render images of the whole object, so each part fills only a few pixels and a tilted part is seen from the side. The second group first finds each part and then segments it up close. But these methods find the parts with knowledge about one kind of part, for example the shape of the dental arch for teeth. Such knowledge does not carry over to other kinds of parts. We find that accuracy is limited by how a part is seen, from which direction and at what scale, not by how many points land on it. We therefore present FrameSeg3D. It first predicts a local frame for every part, its center and the direction it faces, from a low-resolution view of the whole scan. It then segments the part in views around its frame normal at the scanner's resolution, so covering the whole scan no longer costs resolution. Because every part arrives centered, facing the camera, and at a fixed scale, one segmentation network can serve different kinds of parts and learn from few labeled masks. We test FrameSeg3D on two kinds of parts, nails and teeth. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods on hand scans (mean Dice 0.970 on nails) and on the Teeth3DS+ test fold (per-tooth IoU 96.5), with the most accurate tooth boundaries (boundary IoU 70.5).

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 6 figures, 16 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Reconfiguring Procedural Knowledge for Compositional Robot Skill Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22174v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22174

**作者：** Daehee Lee、Dongsu Lee、Sanghyun Ahn、Minjong Yoo、Honguk Woo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22174v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22174v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RecPo，用多种 procedure view 以任务无关方式表示技能，维护技能引发的转移知识库，并在部署时把任务与过程规格解析为组合查询，绑定出可执行技能序列；在 Franka Kitchen 长程任务上以每回合约 1 ms 推理取得 91.9% 有序完成率，实机可通过局部记忆编辑适配新任务而无需重训。

**相关性简评：** 其「因子化视图＋在存储关系上做组合查询＋局部记忆编辑」的状态抽象与稀疏监督下绑定组合思路，可能为 latent 推理状态的可重组、任务条件绑定及低成本计算提供概念类比，但属机器人技能领域、无视觉推理证据，可供核查线索较少。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reconfiguring Procedural Knowledge for Compositional Robot Skill Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** General-purpose behavior models increasingly internalize broad procedural knowledge during training, making their adaptation to new goals and skill interfaces less a problem of learning entirely new behaviors than of reconfiguring existing competence. We study this capability as reconfigurability, the ability to realize new behaviors by cost-efficiently restructuring existing components and relations under sparse governing information, where task specifications provide high-level goals and procedure specifications expose a limited set of reusable factors, leaving the concrete bindings and compositions required for execution underdetermined. We propose RecPo, a framework that represents skills task-agnostically through multiple procedure views and materializes executable skill sequences by composing stored transitions to realize task-specific changes. RecPo maintains a procedure knowledge base of skill-induced transitions, with each view exposing a reusable factor and preserving local skill connectivity. At deployment, it interprets task specifications through the same views and resolves the resulting compositional queries over the stored relations to bind skills for execution. On long-horizon tasks in an extended Franka Kitchen benchmark, RecPo achieves 91.9% ordered completion with 1.0 ms of task inference per episode across varying procedure support and task-record sparsity. Real-robot studies further demonstrate adaptation through local memory edits without task-specific retraining.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [The Only Edge Was Memorized in Training: LLM Trading Agents Around Earnings and Bad News](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22177v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22177

**作者：** Jackson Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22177v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22177v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在无前视偏误的基准上，考察多个 LLM 交易 agent 在财报公告前和坏消息暴跌后的决策，与单次调用、简单规则和逻辑回归对比，并设置是否提供股票名称的对照，发现表现未超过简单押注，收益主要来自模型训练中记住的信息。

**相关性简评：** 其「去掉关键输入的对照＋简单基线＋训练截止前后盲测」的评价思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中的答案是否真正使用了视觉或 latent 证据，还是依赖语言先验与记忆，而非推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Only Edge Was Memorized in Training: LLM Trading Agents Around Earnings and Bad News

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) trading agents are tested on news, but news reaches stock prices within minutes, and a benchmark that replays headlines at their publication time finds no alpha in trading on them. Money can then remain in two places, before news whose date is known, such as earnings announcements, and after bad news, if the market's fall went too far. However, studies of LLMs and earnings read documents published with or after the announcement, and studies of reversal sort monthly portfolios, so neither tells whether an agent can trade these moments after costs. In this paper, we test LLM agents at both moments on the same look-ahead-free benchmark, before 598 earnings announcements and after 409 bad-news drops of large US stocks. Every input is cut at the decision close, staged agents are compared with single calls, simple rules and a logistic regression, and every decision is made with and without the stock's name. Across five models from four vendors, no agent beat the simple bets. Before earnings, the agents mostly bought and earned what buying before every announcement earned, and after bad news, a Gemini-based agent called almost every fall an overreaction while no agent picked better dips than buying them all. The only gains came from what the models had memorized in training. Given only the ticker and the dates, a GPT-based model called the direction of 59.2% of the announcements before its training cutoff and 53.3% after it. Agent builders should therefore compare an event agent with the simple bet and test it blind after the training cutoff.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 9 figures, 12 tables. Original empirical study of LLM trading agents before scheduled earnings announcements and after bad-news drops, run on the rta-bench-1 benchmark (arXiv:2611.19530)

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [An Optimization Framework for Denoising Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22202v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22202

**作者：** Xinyu Zhou、Jiawei Zhang、Stephen J. Wright

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22202v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22202v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文把无噪声条件的去噪生成解释为在固定对数密度景观上的梯度上升，在高斯混合目标下证明轨迹几乎必然收敛到参考流形附近，并给出源—汇恒等式以论证有限分辨率下的区域覆盖、初始化的多样性以及避免记忆训练样本的带宽区间。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及多模态推理，但其关于扩散轨迹是否抵达有意义数据、不同初始化是否保持多样性、覆盖与记忆的判据，可能为评价 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态的生成与更新提供可供核查的类比框架。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Optimization Framework for Denoising Generation

**abstract/A1：** Noise-unconditioned denoising learns a single score, so generation can be viewed as gradient ascent on a fixed log-density landscape. This optimization interpretation leaves two questions: do trajectories approach meaningful data, and do different initializations retain diverse outcomes? We establish both phenomena for empirical and population multi-scale Gaussian-mixture objectives. Under explicit posterior and landscape conditions, ascent converges almost surely within $O(mσ\_{\\min}^2/τ)$ of an $m$-dimensional reference manifold with reach at least $τ$. We derive a source--sink identity for the same fixed field and use it to prove finite-resolution regional coverage. In an affine construction, we further identify a positive-bandwidth regime where even empirical-score optimization can avoid training-sample memorization while preserving diversity at the training resolution. The same fixed objective naturally supports penalty-guided controllable generation. Experiments support our predicted mechanisms, show improved robustness under large-step guidance, and demonstrate downstream gains consistent with finite-data generative generalization.

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Sample-efficient Personalization of Large Language Model Alignment with Multidimensional User Preference Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22210v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22210

**作者：** Zhen-Yu Zhang、Jerry Huang、Gang Niu、Takashi Ishida、Masashi Sugiyama

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22210v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22210v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MPG-DPO，用自动验证器对用户历史比较做低成本多维评估，构建个性化偏好锥，先剔除被明确支配的回复，再选择在偏好锥内仍存在未解决分歧的回复对进行在线偏好对齐。

**相关性简评：** 其「多维验证信号+剔除被支配候选+优先选择高分歧样本」的主动选择思路，可能为 latent 推理训练的监督分配、轨迹贡献判定或视觉证据筛选提供线索，但与视觉推理状态本身无直接联系，需核查可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sample-efficient Personalization of Large Language Model Alignment with Multidimensional User Preference Data

**abstract/A1：** Preference alignment has become a central tool to align large language models (LLMs) with human values and desired behavior, and its online variant can further advance their capabilities beyond what static data alone offer by repeatedly generating and labeling new responses with human feedback. But costly, time-consuming annotations limit its scalability, making sample efficiency essential, and existing methods typically seek to improve it by expanding coverage of the response space or selecting pairs with the highest uncertainty for comparison. Although they are effective, these methods quantify uncertainty from the perspective of the LLM, which may differ from the uncertainty perceived by the user: a pair can appear highly uncertain to the LLM even when past comparisons from the user already imply a clear ordering. In this paper, we propose multidimensional preference-guided online direct preference optimization (MPG-DPO), which leverages low-cost multidimensional evaluations from automated verifiers on the user historical comparisons to construct a personalized preference cone guiding response selection for online preference alignment. Specifically, it first discards responses that are confidently dominated under the estimated user preference, then selects two responses that are competitive under the retained preference cone yet exhibit the strongest unresolved disagreement. Theoretical analysis shows that, with high probability, the fraction of queries spent on cone-dominated responses vanishes as learning progresses. Experiments across multiple LLM families show that MPG-DPO outperforms strong contenders under matched query budgets.

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [SOTA or Luck? The Winner's Curse in LLM Leaderboards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22227v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22227

**作者：** Sikun Xu、Raphael Thomadsen、Dennis J. Zhang、Heng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、stat.ME、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22227v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22227v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 LLM 公开排行榜因对噪声基准分数排序而产生「赢家诅咒」与排名诱导偏差，提出 m-out-of-N bootstrap 校正分数高估，并给出模型合理排名区间与分层，汇总为 RULER 报告。

**相关性简评：** 其排名不确定性与选择偏差校正可能可供核查 LVR/VLM 推理基准中「最优方法」的收益是否被采样运气夸大，但论文本身不涉及 latent 状态或推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SOTA or Luck? The Winner's Curse in LLM Leaderboards

**abstract/A1：** Public LLM leaderboards guide model evaluation and adoption, yet the reported performance of top-ranked models can be systematically overstated. This leaderboard winner's curse arises because ranking noisy benchmark scores rewards sampling luck alongside genuine ability. The same selection effect can distort scores throughout the leaderboard, a phenomenon we call the rank-induced bias. We develop an $m$-out-of-$N$ bootstrap to correct for this bias. It assumes a fixed model pool with independent, identically distributed bounded tasks while allowing arbitrary within-task correlation among models. Under exact population tiers separated by positive gaps, we prove that the bootstrap reduces $Θ(N^{-1/2})$ ranking bias to expected residual bias $o(N^{-1/2})$. Across 14 benchmarks from 5 benchmark families, the rank-1 adjustments range from approximately 0.09 to 3.86 percentage points. In our SWE-bench Verified snapshot, the tied leaders receive an adjustment of 0.90 percentage points. Such overstatement is large enough to make the apparent winner statistically fragile. Thus, we use the same bootstrap to further quantify the intervals of plausible rank positions for each model and group models into tiers. We establish sufficient conditions for consistent recovery of population tiers. On SWE-bench Verified, this procedure places 13 of 173 models in the leading tier. We combine the score adjustment and rank intervals in the Rank Uncertainty and Leaderboard Evaluation Report (RULER). We validate its finite-sample performance using sample splitting and calibrated simulations, and advocate publishing RULER alongside raw rankings so readers can assess both reported performance and the precision of the ordering. RULER also provides guidance for benchmark design that accounts for selection effects and ranking uncertainty.

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [What Does Weight-Space Programming Add to Diffusion Editing? Program Construction versus Score-Guided Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22228v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22228

**作者：** Zitian Gao、Jingyi Xu、Ziqi Han、Chengkai Lei、Ye Liu、Liangliang Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22228v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22228v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 Stable Diffusion 1.4 的概念编辑上比较由语言模型生成的权重空间程序（指定训练语境、保持约束与更新方向）与已发布编辑器、手写基线及预算匹配搜索，发现预注册的重写流程未胜过强基线，而一次性完整程序在 VLM 联合成功率上提升约 10 点，且分数引导的修订会反转该收益，据此主张程序构建与修订应分别评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「在受保护的文本状态子空间中组织可调方向」的做法与「完整程序构建、事后修订」分离的发现，可能为 LVR 中在 latent 更新时保护任务必要信息、抑制表征坍缩提供概念类比，具体子空间约束实现可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Does Weight-Space Programming Add to Diffusion Editing? Program Construction versus Score-Guided Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Weight-space programming (WSP) lets a language model construct executable weight updates for another network. We study diffusion concept editing through an interface in which programs define training contexts, preservation constraints and update directions, while a shared numerical fitter selects their coefficients. We compare generated programs with published editors, a shared-panel hand-written baseline and budget-matched search over conventional edits on 24 TIMED test edits for Stable Diffusion 1.4. The preregistered rewrite pipeline shows no advantage over the strong baseline. In an exploratory comparison, one-shot Astra programs improve fresh-scene VLM joint success, which requires subject presence together with whole-image target matching, by +10.0 points over the shared-panel baseline, while CLIP relative match shows no detectable change. These programs organise adjustable directions around protected text-state subspaces. Probing and rewriting the Astra programs then raises CLIP by +6.2 points while joint success changes by -9.7. On a fixed 144-image sample, five additional evaluators give point estimates in the same directions under whole-image target matching; checks of subject-local change give less decisive differences. Together, these results identify useful behaviour in complete generated programs and show that score-guided revision need not preserve it: program construction and revision should be evaluated as distinct stages of a WSP pipeline.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 7 figures, 13 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [Fidelity Regret: Measuring Simulator Misspecification in Verifiable Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22263v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22263

**作者：** Noah Flynn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22263v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22263v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在约两万个基因电路设计上量化「保真度遗憾」，即廉价模拟器选出的设计相对真实物理性能参考的损失，并比较不同预算分配下的补救策略。

**相关性简评：** 「验证器通过但真实目标未达」以及预算在廉价与昂贵评估间的分配，可能类比 LVR 中答案验证与实际推理质量脱节、THINK/LOOK 计算取舍的问题，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fidelity Regret: Measuring Simulator Misspecification in Verifiable Design

**abstract/A1：** RLVF assumes the verifier evaluates the quantity of interest. In genetic-circuit design, a topological verifier can establish that a program is a valid circuit computing the target function, whereas ranking its physical performance requires a simulator. We measure fidelity regret, the reference simple regret incurred when a cheap simulator selects the design, over a 21,362-design combination subspace spanning 5 measured gate libraries and 3 organisms. Exact ordered-assignment checks retain positive regret in every tested cell while shifting magnitudes in both directions. Mean normalized regret is 0.369 over 72 cells and only 6 have zero regret. The discrepancy depends strongly on the reference axis: the yeast library SC1C1G1T1 has mean regret 0.113 for cytometry quality and 0.801 for growth burden, and every evaluated library--circuit-size pair has positive burden regret. Topological verification ties all valid gate assignments, while an oracle whitelist subject to retaining at least 25% of the design space leaves positive regret in 30 of 52 cells. Design-space optimization and feedback-guided search with frozen language models reach the high-regret regions; on a matched zero-regret control, additional optimization instead reduces regret. At a matched modeled-cost budget, mitigation is budget- and library-dependent. An in-sample fixed rule spending $α{=}0.34$ of the budget re-scoring top cheap-simulator designs achieves mean excess regret 0.016, compared with 0.305 for cheap-only and 0.034 for reference-only selection, improving on cheap-only in 330 of 360 cells. More broadly, any workflow optimized through a simulator or digital twin can satisfy its verifier yet perform poorly on the intended physical objective. Fidelity regret provides a transferable template for detecting this gap before optimization and comparing budget-aware remedies across scientific domains.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Workshop "Sim2Science: ML with Imperfect Scientific Models"

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [Decoding Without a Decoder: Direct Pixel Prediction from Diffusion Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22287v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22287

**作者：** Hansheng Chen、Shengqu Cai、Jan Ackermann、Rayhan Zirvi、Gordon Wetzstein、Xinchen Yan、Leonidas Guibas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22287v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22287v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DWD：在预训练扩散 transformer 上仅微调最后几个 block 并新增线性头，在采样最后一步直接预测像素，从而去掉解码器并降低推理成本，在图像与视频生成上保持或提升像素质量。

**相关性简评：** 其把「驱动采样的状态更新头」与「末端读出头」分离、并用锚定约束保持原采样轨迹的做法，可能与 LVR 里答案读出是否扰动 latent 推理轨迹、读出是否需要独立轻量头的问题相关，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decoding Without a Decoder: Direct Pixel Prediction from Diffusion Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Latent diffusion models rely on a decoder to map denoised latents to pixels, adding complexity and inference cost. We introduce Decoding Without a Decoder (DWD), which lets a finetuned diffusion transformer produce final pixels directly through a linear head. Unlike previous methods, where the denoising output leads to final pixels, DWD separates the two roles: the original denoising head continues to drive sampling, then the new DWD head predicts the pixels at the final step. We extend pretrained latent models with DWD efficiently by finetuning only the last few transformer blocks, training the DWD head with pixel quality losses while anchoring the denoising head to the pretrained model to preserve the sample trajectory. Across latent image and video generators, DWD removes the decoder and reduces inference cost while maintaining or even improving pixel quality. On Wan video generation, it visibly reduces detail distortion and flickering, lowering warping error by 23%. Remarkably, the same separation also improves pixel diffusion models that do not even use a decoder: a final-step DWD head produces substantially better texture detail in text-to-image generation, and improves ImageNet FID from 1.55 to 1.43, a new state of the art among pixel flow models.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://hanshengchen.com/dwd

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [Inertial Video World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22314v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22314

**作者：** Yuan Gao、Fan Xu、Yu Guo、Yuxu Lu、Zhengru Fang、Hao Wu、Xiaomeng Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22314v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22314v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 InertialWorld 视频世界模型，以「惯性」为自回归预测的结构先验：用 Inertial Memory 把场景证据以稠密 Eulerian 网格与持久 Lagrangian 载体两种形式跨时间携带，依动作条件搬运载体内容并预测下一帧状态，再用学习门控合并搬运内容与新预测内容。

**相关性简评：** 该工作的「携带而非逐步重建证据＋门控合并新旧状态」可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的持续更新、减少重复重建导致的信息坍缩提供可核查线索，但属视频世界模型，未涉及视觉推理任务。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Inertial Video World Models

**abstract/A1：** The physical world has inertia: it evolves from what already exists rather than being recreated at every instant. However, many existing video world models lack an explicit mechanism for inertial state evolution, leaving the continuity of motion and structure implicit in video prediction. During autoregressive rollouts, repeated reconstruction can accumulate errors and destabilize the predicted dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce InertialWorld, an Inertial Video World Model that makes inertia a structural prior for autoregressive prediction: existing scene evidence is carried across time rather than reconstructed at every step. Central to this formulation is Inertial Memory, which turns memory from passive predictive context into an evolving video state with two complementary views: a dense Eulerian grid preserves scene-aligned evidence, while persistent Lagrangian carriers bind local content to learned canonical coordinates. At each step, the model transports the carrier content according to action-conditioned motion, predicts the complete next video state from the transported carriers and dense grid, and uses a learned gate to combine transported and newly predicted content into the memory for the next step. Extensive experiments across multiple domains, including robot manipulation, 3D game simulation, and open-world navigation, demonstrate that InertialWorld achieves competitive autoregressive prediction performance against strong video world model baselines. Codes link: https://github.com/YuanGao-YG/InertialWorld

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [SIDUMN: Self-Supervised Image Denoising Under Symmetric Multiplicative Noise](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22317v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22317

**作者：** Nyan Htet Naing、Gyeongha Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22317v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22317v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SIDUMN：针对均值一对称乘性噪声，从同一噪声分布采样噪声因子及其关于 1 的反射，构造两张互补的加噪图像，训练网络由其一预测另一，推理时对 K 对反演重建取平均，并分析训练目标的总体偏差与反演平均不增大期望平方误差的性质。

**相关性简评：** 其「成对互补采样＋重建平均」可作为降低 Monte Carlo 估计方差的采样聚合思路，可能为多种 latent 假设或视觉证据的采样组合与平均提供核查线索，但与 VLM 推理无直接关系，迁移价值需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SIDUMN: Self-Supervised Image Denoising Under Symmetric Multiplicative Noise

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised denoising is challenging when the corruption is multiplicative and signal-dependent. In this letter, we propose SIDUMN, a symmetric noisier framework for self-supervised denoising under mean-one symmetric multiplicative noise. Given noisy observations and the noise law, the method samples an independent synthetic noise factor from the same law and constructs two complementary noisier images by applying the factor and its reflection about one. The denoising network is trained to predict one noisier image from the other, and at inference the reconstructions of $K$ such antithetic pairs are averaged. We show that the population target of the training loss is the clean conditional mean minus a multiplicative cross-term. We further prove that, for any trained network, antithetic averaging never increases the expected squared error of a single noisier-input reconstruction. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings and the proposed method.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. Submitted to IEEE Signal Processing Letters

</details>

### 234. [Reliable Evaluation of AI Agents for Investment Research: A Framework for Performance and Validity Assessment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22338v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22338

**作者：** Dongnuan Tian、Yan Yu、Yangfan Liu、Wenjun Tang、Junting Kan、Xiang Wang、Sicong Liu、Yangyang Zhang、Shuanghong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22338v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22338v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向投资研究任务的 AI agent 评估框架，把结果质量与执行过程质量分开，结合任务特定标准、确定性校验器和独立 LLM 评审，并引入对评估输入、参考信息、证据与评分结果的有效性核查及专家复核。

**相关性简评：** 该文与视觉推理无直接关系，但「过程质量与结果质量分离」及「证据与评分有效性核查」的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 轨迹是否被使用、视觉证据是否真正支撑答案以及评判环节的可靠性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliable Evaluation of AI Agents for Investment Research: A Framework for Performance and Validity Assessment

**abstract/A1：** We consider the problem of evaluating AI agents undertaking investment-research tasks, where agents interact with external information sources and computational tools to produce financial analyses and research reports. An important challenge is that the quality of an agent's final output does not necessarily reflect the quality of its execution process, while the reliability of the resulting assessment also depends on the validity of the evaluation procedure. Furthermore, investment-research tasks differ considerably in their requirements for numerical accuracy, information retrieval, financial analysis and supporting evidence, making it difficult to assess agent performance using a uniform set of criteria. We propose a domain-oriented evaluation framework that distinguishes outcome quality from execution quality and accommodates these differences through task-specific criteria, deterministic validators and independent large language model judges. We also introduce an evaluation-validity mechanism that examines the integrity of evaluation inputs, reference information, supporting evidence and grading outputs, with expert review used to resolve identified deficiencies and significant grading disagreements. Numerical results show that the proposed framework can distinguish differences in agent performance across evaluation dimensions, while identifying potential reliability issues in the evaluation process. The associated implementation and evaluation materials will be made available on GitHub.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 2 figures

</details>

### 235. [HealthCLBench:Evaluating Context Learning in Health Conversations through Controlled Case Clusters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22362v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22362

**作者：** Ting Yang、Zijing Hong、Chengjie Wang、Peng Wei、Chunxiao Guo、Yun Yue、Xurui Li、Jinqun Guan、Zixiao Chen、Ant Group

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22362v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22362v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 HealthCLBench 中文健康对话基准，包含 328 个受控案例簇、1,103 个案例与 4,931 条成员级评分细则：成员共享背景，但在证据、患者状态或任务目标上发生变化，用于评测八套系统的全上下文与仅查询表现及人工一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其「受控案例簇＋成员级细则」的可控变体设计可能可供核查，用于判断答案是否真正依赖某一具体证据（类似视觉证据消融与贡献分配），但本文无视觉输入与 latent 状态，联系仅为评价思路层面的类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HealthCLBench:Evaluating Context Learning in Health Conversations through Controlled Case Clusters

**abstract/A1：** Complex health conversations require answers grounded in individual information and currently valid evidence. We evaluate health context learning: acquiring, integrating, and applying relevant context during inference with fixed model parameters to answer health questions and respond appropriately to changes in facts, evidence, or task goals. To our knowledge, Chinese-language medical benchmarks have yet to systematically combine explicit contextual difficulties, controlled case variations, and member-specific medical criteria for open-ended answers. We introduce HealthCLBench, comprising 328 controlled clusters, 1,103 cases, and 4,931 rubric criteria across medical consultation and everyday health management. Members share backgrounds while evidence, patient states, or task goals change; each has its own medical scoring requirements. A 70-person medical team annotates and reviews the simulated cases and their rubrics. Human review of 700 full-context responses from the original evaluation yields 90.5% pooled agreement with the original automated judgments at the rubric-item level. We report full-context and query-only results for seven general-purpose models and Ant Afu. Across the eight systems, full- query differences range from 11.83 to 31.76 case points and from 10.87 to 38.97 geometric cluster points. GPT-6-Astra leads both full-context metrics at 75.03 and 60.42, followed by Claude-Opus-5-5 and Ant Afu. Cross-condition differences describe score changes under the reported evaluation settings. Response comparisons reveal failures in checking historical information, expressing uncertainty when warranted, and ensuring recommendations respect required conditions. The resource supports model comparison and targeted follow-up evaluation.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 7 figures, 18 Tables

</details>

### 236. [AutoE2E: Don't speak - Language free end-to-end autonomy A Real-Time Driving Model for L2++ ADAS and L4 Robotaxis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22374v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22374

**作者：** Muhammad Zain Khawaja、Zaynab El Mawas、Ryota Yamada、Gabriela Cordova、Arseni Lysak、Intisar Chowdhury、Bharat Jayaprakash、Apostolos Georgiadis、Shri Harsha Adapala、Jatin Gautam、Christoph Bank、Alejandra Solarte Uscategui、Atanasko Boris Mitrev、Hassan Ali Ansari、Nishad Sahu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22374v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22374v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文发布 AutoE2E 开源端到端驾驶模型：以多相机、HD 地图和路线条件构建时序 BEV 表示，结合时序特征融合与轻量循环轨迹规划器预测未来车辆运动，并在 KITScenes 上报告开环 ADE/FDE 结果与初步实车试验。

**相关性简评：** 其「无语言」的时序 BEV 加循环规划器需在内部持续维护和更新场景状态，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新与证据累积提供结构参照；但主体是自动驾驶预测任务，不含显式推理、生成或视觉证据筛选机制，联系较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoE2E: Don't speak - Language free end-to-end autonomy A Real-Time Driving Model for L2++ ADAS and L4 Robotaxis

**abstract/A1：** AutoE2E is an open-source end-to-end driving model developed by the Autoware Foundation Robotaxi Working Group. It introduces a temporal bird's-eye-view (BEV) architecture that combines multi-camera perception, HD-map information and route conditioning to predict future vehicle motion. The evaluated model uses a pretrained camera-BEV backbone, temporal feature fusion and a lightweight recurrent trajectory planner. It contains approximately 79.9 million parameters, of which approximately 1.45 million are trainable in the evaluated configuration. The v1.0 release provides model checkpoints, training and evaluation code, and DataModelConsole, a dashboard for visualizing and inspecting model experiments. On the KITScenes validation benchmark, the fine-tuned checkpoint achieves an average displacement error (ADE) of 0.745 m and a final displacement error (FDE) of 2.020 m at a three-second prediction horizon. The paper also reports preliminary real-vehicle research conducted with the Technical University of Munich's EDGAR platform, alongside ongoing collaboration with the Karlsruhe Institute of Technology. AutoE2E v1.0 is an early open research release. Its quantitative evaluation is open-loop, and the model has not yet demonstrated validated closed-loop autonomous driving. The release is intended to provide the autonomous-driving community with an inspectable baseline for further research, experimentation and collaborative development.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 17 figures, 6 tables. Code, model checkpoints and evaluation pipeline released at https://github.com/autowarefoundation/auto\_e2e(v1.0 tag, Apache-2.0)

</details>
