### 237. [Symbolic Abstraction Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22404v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22404

**作者：** Daniel Bryce、Robert P. Goldman、David J. Musliner、Jeremy Gottlieb、John Skovbekk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22404v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22404v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SYMAR，把概率时间自动机验证中的符号状态改为携带「时钟子区域→概率界」的符号值函数，使精化表现为函数内新子区域的出现，避免分裂状态与重建转移，并用启发式前向搜索和部分精化备份把计算限制在与性质相关的片段上，在 PRISM 基准上显著加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「按需细化抽象粒度、以界限引导只展开相关片段、保持区间可证包含真值」的思路，可能与 LVR 中动态分配推理计算、决定内部状态表示在何处细化的问题相通，可供核查；但研究对象是形式化验证而非视觉或多模态推理，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Symbolic Abstraction Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous systems choose actions whose outcomes are uncertain and are subject to time constraints. Probabilistic timed automata (PTAs) model exactly this, and verifying one answers a question about the system's decision-making: the maximum probability of reaching a goal, or the minimum probability of reaching a hazard, that a controller can guarantee. Verification normally works over symbolic states, each pairing a discrete location with a region of clock values, and its difficulty is that the best behavior generally varies within such a region. The PRISM verifier's most capable PTA method responds by splitting regions until each is uniform, rebuilding the transitions that reach them and propagating the split backward through the state graph of a stochastic game, a cascade of graph reconstruction that partitions regions the property never depends on. We present SYMAR, which instead makes the value function symbolic: each symbolic state carries a function from sub-regions of its clock region to probability bounds, so refinement is the appearance of new sub-regions in that function, and previously generated states are never split nor their incoming transitions reconstructed. Both sources of PTA nondeterminism, the timing and the choice of actions, are captured implicitly by the preimage computation that produces those sub-regions. Heuristic forward search then bounds how much refinement is done at all, expanding an And-Or graph from the initial state along the most promising behaviors so that effort is confined to the property-relevant fragment, guided by bounds and preferred actions from a single unrefined run of the existing method; a partial-refinement backup keeps the otherwise combinatorial sub-region count lean, and every stored interval provably contains the true probability. On the PRISM PTA benchmarks, whose subproblems recur in autonomous systems (distributed agreement under contention, leader election, and self-configuration without central coordination), SYMAR is up to 108x faster, solves an instance on which the existing method exhausts the time limit, and is markedly less sensitive to the deadline constant, resolving one instance in a 37-node search graph, unchanged as the deadline triples, where the existing method needs 86,380 abstract states and 73 refinement rounds.

**comment/C1：** In Proceedings FMAS 2026, arXiv:2611.21375

</details>

<a id="paper-238"></a>

### 238. [Do Diffusion Models Learn to Generalize Basic Visual Skills?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22427v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22427

**作者：** Amish Sethi、Boya Zeng、Wenhao Chai、Zhuang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22427v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22427v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Visual Generative Lab 可控框架，用显式规则生成合成数据从头训练 diffusion 模型，按规则化指标测量 size、position、rotation、count、color、shape 及组合的泛化，发现外推普遍受限、组合泛化更依赖技能组合多样性而非数据量，且各技能多独立失败（position 除外）。

**相关性简评：** 属图像生成而非 VLM 推理，但其把视觉能力拆成可规则化度量的单项技能与组合、并检验外推与组合泛化的实验设计，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留与组合泛化诊断的评测构造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Diffusion Models Learn to Generalize Basic Visual Skills?

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion models generate high-quality images, but how well they generalize is hard to measure. Current evaluations rely on human preference and prompt-following benchmarks for text-to-image models and on distribution-level metrics such as Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) for class-conditional models, but these cannot tell whether models learn basic visual skills or largely match training patterns. To address this gap, we introduce the Visual Generative Lab (VGL), a controlled experimental framework for studying how diffusion models learn and generalize basic visual skills, including size, position, rotation, count, color, shape, and their compositions. By generating synthetic training data for each skill from explicit rules, VGL allows precise measurement of generalization with task-specific, rule-based metrics. Training models from scratch on these data reveals three patterns in this controlled setting: (1) with our default design, extrapolation is limited: size and position degrade within a few pixels of the training range, count reaches only about 50% accuracy on out-of-range counts, and rotation extrapolation accuracy varies widely across seeds; (2) for compositional generalization, the number of distinct basic visual skill combinations matters more than dataset size; (3) skills mostly fail independently, except position. Code and data-generation scripts: https://github.com/AmishSethi/visual-generative-lab.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026. Project page: https://amishsethi.github.io/visual-generative-lab/

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [When Neural Collapse Fails to Route: Routing Drift in Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22429v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22429

**作者：** Nicolas Larue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22429v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22429v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在持续学习中提出「路由漂移」失败模式：基于神经坍缩 ETF 分类器的模型任务内可分，但全局 argmax 常落到错误任务块；作者用路由准确率分解任务内判别与跨任务分数可比性，并提出免重训练的轻量后处理校准方法 RCR。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但「表示可分性不等于读出可比性」的分解、以及小回放缓冲因样本被反复优化而掩盖问题的观察，可能为判断 latent 内容是否真正被读出、以及评测样本过拟合造成的掩盖效应提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Neural Collapse Fails to Route: Routing Drift in Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Progressive neural-collapse classifiers can learn strong task-local decision boundaries while failing the global Class-IL decision. We identify the failure as routing drift: under an expanding equiangular tight frame (ETF) classifier, a held-out sample can remain separable within its true task block, yet receive its largest global score from another block. This produces high Task-IL accuracy but low Class-IL accuracy, and small replay buffers can mask the problem because stored samples are optimized repeatedly and often remain well routed. We formalize route accuracy directly from the global class argmax and use it to separate within-task discrimination from cross-task score comparability. This decomposition suggests a classifier-level correction: preserve the global ETF frame, but add class-local reliability evidence when transported class measures deviate from the neural-collapse ideal. We develop Reliability-Calibrated Routing (RCR), a lightweight post-hoc classifier that composes standardized ETF alignment with radial plausibility around transported, NC-shrunk class representatives. Across three datasets and two fixed replay-buffer budgets, this NC-consistent correction gives the best Class-IL FAA on five of six settings and reduces Class-IL forgetting on all six. The largest gains occur in harder routing regimes: up to $+6.75$ FAA over the reported reference, and up to $+8.03$ FAA and $+8.62$ final route accuracy in paired evaluations, while preserving Task-IL performance. These results position routing drift as a measurable failure mode of ETF-based continual learning and show that reliability-calibrated class evidence can repair part of the lost global routing without retraining the representation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://towzeur.github.io/RCR/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at BMVC 2026. 36 pages, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-240"></a>

### 240. [Where Demand Meets Supply: Spatial Elastic Consensus for Low-Bandwidth Collaborative Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22439v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22439

**作者：** Linshan Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22439v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22439v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Spatial Elastic Consensus 框架：自车生成内容感知的需求先验、协作者评估本地供给，单轮共识把有限带宽分配给自车被遮挡而缺失的证据，并以 refine-before-mask 与可变形采样器保证预算严格合规，在 64KiB 下提升协同 3D 检测精度。

**相关性简评：** 其「需求—供给」双边稀疏证据选择、抑制自车已见冗余及预算精确约束，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、LOOK 的成本控制与信息价值判断提供问题表述参考；但对象是 3D 协同检测而非 VLM 推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Demand Meets Supply: Spatial Elastic Consensus for Low-Bandwidth Collaborative Perception

**abstract/A1：** Occlusions and limited sensor range make ego-only 3D perception insufficient for safe driving. Collaborative perception addresses this by enabling connected vehicles to share complementary observations, yet it remains constrained by tight vehicular communication budgets. While spatial selection is more flexible than fixed-rate feature compression, existing selectors remain largely sender-driven and waste scarce bandwidth on ego-visible targets. Furthermore, applying convolutions after masking spreads isolated tokens into their neighborhoods, silently violating low-bandwidth constraints. To address this, we propose Spatial Elastic Consensus (SEC), a bandwidth-adaptive framework centered on bilateral demand-supply consensus. Instead of unilateral token selection, the ego vehicle formulates a content-aware demand prior while each collaborator evaluates its local supply. This single-round consensus allocates the token budget to critical ego-missed evidence rather than ego-visible redundancy, without transmitting dense maps. To guarantee exact budget compliance, an anti-dilation refine-before-mask pipeline refines features prior to discrete token selection, preventing receptive-field leakage. A lightweight deformable sampler then aggregates these sparse messages with learned local offsets, compensating for the spatial context lost to token selection and mitigating inter-agent pose drift. Evaluated at a stringent 64KiB budget, SEC consistently achieves the best AP@0.7 on the simulated OPV2V and V2XSet datasets and the real-world DAIR-V2X dataset. These results demonstrate that combining bilateral consensus with budget-exact transmission is key to efficient low-bandwidth collaborative perception.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-241"></a>

### 241. [RehearsalBench: Can AI Predict What Happens When You Change a Company?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22480v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22480

**作者：** Mika Siddiqui、Tharaka Perera

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22480v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22480v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RehearsalBench，把模型置于贴合真实呼叫记录的模拟银行客服中心，考察其能否诊断运营、选择干预并预测后果，用 200 条随机未来路径与实际货币化评分比较十个模型，并以 33 个已知结局的真实组织变革检验外部效度。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉/多模态推理主线没有直接交集，但其中「方向对、效应量系统性高估」的校准问题，以及没有任何模型在「不作为更优」时选择观望的停止/弃权失败，可能为 LVR 的停止决策与预测不确定性评价提供外部参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RehearsalBench: Can AI Predict What Happens When You Change a Company?

**abstract/A1：** Organizations increasingly ask AI systems what will happen if they change how they operate: which work to automate, which team to resize, what a voice agent will do to waiting times. We introduce RehearsalBench, a benchmark that measures whether AI models can diagnose an operation, choose an intervention, and forecast its consequences. Each model is placed in a simulated bank contact center fitted to real call-level records, sees only what a manager would see, and submits a plan and forecasts that are then played out across 200 seeded futures and three plausible versions of the organization. Decisions are scored in dollars at the stakeholders' stated valuations; forecasts are scored on the effect of each change relative to doing nothing. Ten models made 280 attempts across 14 organizations. The best model, GPT-6 Astra, improved on doing nothing by 6.8% of the no-change cost and made 77% less forecast error than a forecaster who always predicts no effect. On average, every model forecast the direction of every specified effect correctly, but models erred on the size of the same effects in the same direction, overstating a voice agent's effect on repeat contacts about five-fold. No model ever recommended holding off when doing nothing was the right choice. To test external validity, twelve models forecast 33 real organizational changes with known outcomes. Under verdict rules fixed in advance, the benchmark's forecast ranking agreed with the real-world ranking (Spearman $ρ= 0.92$, 95% lower bound 0.50). We report the result alongside an inconclusive first test, the analysis choices made after outcomes were seen, and evidence that part of the agreement reflects how well models size their uncertainty.

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [SciWorkBench: A Benchmark for Real Scientific Workflows](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22490v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22490

**作者：** Yuchen Zhang、Taolin Han、Jinghang Wang、Jinxin Wang、Yun Wu、Wai Yuet Chiu、Xiaogang Li、Yuting Fu、Kewei Sun、Jiaxin Li、Qile Jin、Jiajia Li、Ruodan Chen、Qingteng Chen、Jialu Nie、Junchao Li、Yifei Zhang、Chen Zhao、Hu Wei、Lin Qu、Yanni Yin、Bing Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22490v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22490v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 SciWorkBench，一个覆盖生物、化学、材料与物理的科研任务基准，用专家验证的统一科学分析框架组织任务，并在统一执行与评分协议下评测 Claude Code、Codex 等 agent 框架的端到端工作流表现。

**相关性简评：** 其失败分析指出「跨模态证据整合弱」与「科学自我纠错能力有限」，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、反馈驱动状态更新与停止决策的评价设计提供概念参考，但论文本身是任务级 agent 基准，不涉及内部表示或 latent 推理状态，需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SciWorkBench: A Benchmark for Real Scientific Workflows

**abstract/A1：** Language models are increasingly being used as agents capable of planning and executing projects, as well as refining their actions through feedback. This shift raises a question: can current agents complete projects like real scientists? To examine this question, we introduce SciWorkBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents' abilities on real science tasks across biology, chemistry, materials science, and physics. SciWorkBench is built from peer-reviewed literature or expert-contributed research scenarios. We also propose a unified scientific analysis framework validated by domain experts, which can categorize most tasks in SciWorkBench. Each task covers one or more stages of this framework. We evaluate up-to-date models within the Claude Code and Codex agent frameworks under a unified execution and grading protocol. The best-performing agent achieves a mean score of 57.38, indicating that current agents still struggle with realistic scientific workflows. Our failure analysis identifies three recurring weaknesses at the workflow level: difficulty translating scientific knowledge into task-specific procedures, weak integration of evidence across different modalities, and limited capacity for scientific self-correction. These findings highlight the importance of workflow-based evaluation for measuring progress toward reliable AI systems for scientific research.

</details>

<a id="paper-243"></a>

### 243. [When Agent Skills Go Stale: Runtime Behavior and Selective Maintenance in Evolving Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22525v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22525

**作者：** Ömer Faruk Akgül、Shuli Jiang、Yuting Zhang、Yi Zhang、Xiaohan Fei、Wei Xia、Stefano Soatto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22525v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22525v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察环境变化后 agent 技能状态部分过时导致的复用失效，提出无需额外模型调用的选择性维护：重放成功执行中的检查、验证观测与状态的冲突、局部修复并校验，不足时重建。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部状态与当前观测冲突检测—局部修复—必要时重建」的流程，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的有效性校验、陈旧表示更新与何时放弃重算提供参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Agent Skills Go Stale: Runtime Behavior and Selective Maintenance in Evolving Environments

**abstract/A1：** Agent skills use past experience and current observations to guide decisions and actions on related tasks. This experience is retained in skill state, whose reuse can amortize inference-time reasoning and environment interaction. When environment changes, parts of this state can become outdated while others remain useful. Across four task settings and two models, we compare skill reuse before and after environment changes. Previously beneficial skills can reduce task performance by over 10 percentage points or increase execution cost by nearly 40% relative to no skill. Task success can also remain unchanged while execution savings decline. These effects depend on which environment-specific details become outdated and the agent's ability to compensate using current observations. We introduce selective maintenance, which replays checks from successful executions without additional model calls. It verifies whether observed differences conflict with skill state, repairs affected content, and validates updates, rebuilding when local repair is insufficient. No description of the change is supplied. Relative to reusing stale state, maintenance improves task accuracy by up to 67pp and reduces execution steps by up to 37%. These results suggest that preserving a skill's inference time value as environments evolve requires selectively maintaining its state.

</details>

<a id="paper-244"></a>

### 244. [Physically Admissible Generative Flow Matching for Sparse Flow Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22549v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22549

**作者：** Yuhao Wang、Yuanxi Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22549v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22549v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Feasible Subspace Consistency 框架，用切空间匹配把 flow matching 的整条 ODE 轨迹约束在离散守恒流形上，并在冻结先验的 latent 空间中做摊销后验输运，用于稀疏流场重建。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但其「让 latent 生成轨迹全程满足结构不变量、而非仅在输出端校正」的思路，可能为 LVR 中如何保持中间推理状态的任务必要信息提供形式化参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physically Admissible Generative Flow Matching for Sparse Flow Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing full fluid velocity fields from sparse, localized observations is a severely ill-posed inverse problem: most of the spatial domain remains unobserved, discrete conservation laws must hold strictly, and predictive uncertainty must be reliable. Existing generative models typically enforce physical constraints only at the terminal output or via soft penalties, leaving intermediate generative trajectories unconstrained. We introduce Feasible Subspace Consistency (FSC), a framework that preserves physical admissibility as an invariant of continuous generative dynamics: tangent-space matching confines the entire ODE trajectory to the discrete conservation manifold, while mass-orthonormal coordinates preserve the kinetic-energy metric. Sparse reconstruction then reduces to amortized posterior transport in the latent space of a frozen FSC prior, ensuring every posterior sample inherits incompressibility and boundary conditions without per-sample projection loops. We validate FSC on a periodic Kolmogorov flow, where exact spectral projection isolates path tangency and metric isometry, and a wall-bounded cylinder wake with immersed obstacles, realized via matrix-free iterative projection. FSC prevents the mode-coverage collapse induced by terminal projection (increasing mode coverage from $0.000$ to $0.790$) while eliminating high-wavenumber spectral distortion. In sparse reconstruction, amortized FSC reduces global relative error by $50\\%$ and $46\\%$, and sensor residuals by $63\\%$ and $55\\%$, relative to the unconditioned prior, while delivering calibrated, spatially heterogeneous uncertainty.

</details>

<a id="paper-245"></a>

### 245. [Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22561

**作者：** Raphael C. Kim、Michele Santacatterina、Jingsen Zhu、Ramin Zabih

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22561v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CURE：先用「证书」评估 action-conditioned world model 能否可信地用于规划，再根据缺失的 support 决定对训练数据重加权、测试时增加 rollout 采样，还是主动采集新数据；在 PhyWorld、MuJoCo 与 Language-Table（含真机视频）上验证了决策 regret 与视频感知质量并不一致。

**相关性简评：** 「生成画面逼真≠内部状态可用于决策」这一诊断，与 LVR 中 latent/视觉证据是否保留任务必要信息的问题同构；其可信度证书、support 覆盖判断以及「何时多采样、何时停取证据」的决策规则，可能为 latent 推理中的停止决策与证据获取提供可供核查的表述。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning

**abstract/A1：** Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as world models for planning, yet a world model that makes plausible, realistic-looking videos that are consistent with the training data can produce poor decisions. We address two key questions: (1) when can we trust a world model for planning, and (2) what should we do when we cannot? We introduce CURE (Certify, Upweight, Rollout, Explore), a certificate-guided technique that assesses whether a world model can be trusted for planning and determines what to do next: whether to upweight the training data to debias the model; roll out more samples at test time; or explore by collecting new data where support (how well the training data cover the planner's decisions) is missing. We deploy CURE across multiple domains, spanning simulated physics based on PhyWorld, MuJoCo arm control, and Language-Table pushing in simulation and on real videos. The decisions our certificate trusts have lower regret than those it does not (e.g., 0.06 vs. 0.38 on Language-Table sim and 0.07 vs. 0.38 on MuJoCo contact), whereas video perceptual quality does not identify good decisions. By focusing on decision quality rather than perceptual quality, CURE lowers regret over the best pessimistic decision rule by 10-25% across support levels, and over world-action models, search and policy learning by 41-64%, given the same data.

</details>

<a id="paper-246"></a>

### 246. [CADMAGEDDON: High-Fidelity CAD Reconstruction with Local Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22563v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22563

**作者：** Oleg Pavlov、Nikita Gavrilov、Soslan Kabisov、Gennadii Savrasov、Andrey Kuznetsov、Anton Konushin、Dmitrii Zhemchuzhnikov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22563v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22563v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出开源 agent CADMAGEDDON，从 mesh 重建可编辑 CAD，可在单 GPU 或笔记本上本地运行；方法将任务拆成关于「构造什么、修正什么」的一串简单视觉决策，由几何工具负责精确构造，基于中等规模 Qwen3-27B 在主要基准上优于含前沿模型基线的方案。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉判断交由模型、精确几何构造交由工具」的分工，可能为 LVR 中 latent 视觉推理与外部计算、证据校验之间的边界划分提供参照；决策链是否形成可核查的中间状态与停止信号值得进一步查看。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CADMAGEDDON: High-Fidelity CAD Reconstruction with Local Inference

**abstract/A1：** CAD practitioners often work with proprietary parts that they are unwilling to share with external services. For individuals and small companies, bringing this work in-house also requires computational resources they can afford. We introduce CADMAGEDDON, an open-source agent that reconstructs editable CAD from meshes and can run locally on single-GPU systems and current-generation laptops. The method decomposes this complex task into a chain of simple visual decisions about what to construct and what to correct. Geometric tools support precise construction, allowing the agent to focus on visual reasoning. Using the mid-sized Qwen3.8-27B model, CADMAGEDDON outperforms prior approaches, including systems based on frontier models, in reconstruction quality on all the main benchmarks. CADMAGEDDON integrates with FreeCAD, bringing its reconstructions into practitioners' existing CAD workflows. Code: https://github.com/kulibinai/cadmageddon

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Code: https://github.com/kulibinai/cadmageddon

</details>

<a id="paper-247"></a>

### 247. [Lost in the Funnel: Selection-Induced Miscalibration in Ranking and Decision Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22595v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22595

**作者：** Aditya Somani、Rahul Ragesh、Vikas Joshi、Amit Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22595v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22595v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察多阶段排序与决策漏斗中在模型边界标定好的分数经过筛选、重排和竞价后失去标定性的问题，提出基于残差恒等式的逐层诊断框架，并给出选择策略感知的后验重标定方法 FAR，在工业 A/B 测试和改造后的公开数据集上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「选择而非模型误差导致标定退化、需在决策边界重新标定」的视角，可能可供核查 LVR 中多阶段证据筛选与停止决策的置信度标定问题，但本身不涉及视觉或 latent 推理机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lost in the Funnel: Selection-Induced Miscalibration in Ranking and Decision Systems

**abstract/A1：** Modern ranking and decision systems combine components with divergent objectives, and scores calibrated at the model boundary are used as calibrated probabilities throughout the funnel, across filtering, reranking, business-rule enforcement, and auctions. Yet calibration is typically validated only where the score is produced, so its breakdown where the score is used is routinely overlooked. We ask when calibration survives this selection, how inherited error differs from selection effects, and what can fail for a perfectly calibrated score at a component boundary. We study these questions through a boundary-wise diagnostic framework of residual identities. We target rubric-judged relevance rather than presentation-dependent metrics such as CTR, so calibration is measurable at every funnel layer of interest. We first present an industry-wide case study that applies our diagnosis to settings where calibration deteriorates at decision time, tracing the degradation to selection rather than model error. This diagnosis motivates Funnel-Aware Recalibration (FAR): post-hoc, selection-policy-aware recalibration at the boundary where scores drive decisions. In a live two-region production A/B test, FAR cuts decision-time expected calibration error (ECE) by 60-80% and improves auction efficiency. We further adapt two public datasets to simulate industry settings, providing additional evidence for the mechanism. We plan to release the adapted datasets and their generation pipeline to support further research. Finally, we distill these findings into practical operating principles for calibrating multi-stage scoring systems.

**comment/C1：** Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-248"></a>

### 248. [EvoSkill-S1: Evolving Reusable Skills with Genetic-Inspired Operators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22610

**作者：** Yujun Wang、Jiqiang Li、Yinuo Lu、Jinhe Bi、Aniri、Wenxuan Ye、Boliang Liu、Sikuan Yan、Shuning Wang、Xuebing Zhou、S{ö}ren Pirk、Hinrich Sch{ü}tze、Yunpu Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22610v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** EvoSkill-S1 研究语言模型智能体可复用技能库的进化式更新：以困难负例失败为输入，用五种遗传式算子（修订、交叉修订、变异、双重反思、交叉）由父技能生成子技能，并用下游任务奖励训练技能进化器，配合评估器引导的选择性写回与技能库融合。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理，但「候选技能生成—评估器筛选—选择性写回」的演化循环可能为 LVR 中多条 latent 假设的生成、筛选与保留提供类比线索，可供核查其筛选与贡献分配机制是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoSkill-S1: Evolving Reusable Skills with Genetic-Inspired Operators

**abstract/A1：** Reusable skills allow language-model agents to accumulate and reuse problem-solving experience. However, most existing methods improve a skill bank only indirectly through rollout-driven accumulation and heuristic reflection, often resulting in shallow and weakly controlled updates. We present EvoSkill-S1, an RL-driven framework that instead treats reusable skills themselves as the direct objects of evolution. Given hard-negative failures, EvoSkill-S1 selects parent skills and transforms them into candidate child skills through five genetic-inspired operators: revision, cross-revision, mutation, dual-reflection, and crossover. Downstream task rewards train the skill evolver to generate more effective child skills, while evaluator-guided write-back selectively retains beneficial updates. Complementary Skill Bank Fusion consolidates operator-specific banks to exploit their distinct recovery patterns, while a Procedural Skill Interface supports transfer to unseen problems. Across all reported math and code benchmarks, EvoSkill-S1 improves the corresponding initialized skill banks under both Qwen and GPT-family solvers, with relative gains of up to 12.39%. Compared with the strongest external baseline without direct skill evolution, the largest relative improvement reaches 20.39%.

</details>

<a id="paper-249"></a>

### 249. [REVEAL: Reconstructing Video Amodal Content via Language and Flow Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22645v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22645

**作者：** Minh Tran、Winston Bounsavy、Ngan Le

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22645v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22645v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 REVEAL，一个结合光流运动先验与文本语义先验的扩散式视频非模态补全框架，用流扭曲历史可见掩码预测遮挡形状、用文本约束遮挡区域纹理重建，并发布长视频文本配对基准 LAVAT。

**相关性简评：** 其「把前帧可见内容经光流扭曲传播到遮挡区作为形状先验」的机制，或可类比 LVR 中视觉证据的跨步携带与遮挡下细节保留策略，但论文面向补全生成而非推理，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** REVEAL: Reconstructing Video Amodal Content via Language and Flow Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Amodal perception enables humans to perceive entire objects even when parts are occluded, a remarkable cognitive skill that artificial intelligence struggles to replicate. Recent diffusion-based methods have extended amodal completion from images to videos, yet they lack auxiliary priors to guide the reconstruction of heavily occluded content and to maintain temporal consistency over long sequences. We present REVEAL (REconstructing VidEo Amodal content via Language and flow guidance), a unified diffusion-based framework that integrates complementary motion and semantic priors for video amodal completion. A user-provided text query serves a dual role: it drives open-vocabulary video segmentation to obtain visible masks, and it provides semantic guidance for texture reconstruction. For amodal segmentation, we introduce optical flow guidance: by warping visible masks from previous frames to the current frame, the flow-warped mask propagates visible content into occluded regions and approximates the object's current shape, providing a strong shape prior even under simultaneous deformation and occlusion. For texture completion, text guidance constrains the reconstruction of occluded appearance while providing a stable semantic reference that helps maintain visual coherence throughout the reconstruction. We also contribute LAVAT, a new benchmark featuring long video sequences paired with text descriptions, enabling evaluation of text-guided video amodal completion under heavy occlusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REVEAL achieves state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks while maintaining high-quality temporal consistency over extended sequences. The project page is available at: https://uark-aicv.github.io/REVEAL/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR)

</details>

<a id="paper-250"></a>

### 250. [The Price of Hard Coverage Constraints in Online Conformal Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22646v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22646

**作者：** Mingrui Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.ST、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22646v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22646v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究在线保形预测中「硬覆盖约束」的效率代价：在每轮生成预测集且累积误覆盖数须始终控制在给定预算内的设定下，刻画任意方法为满足该约束所必然付出的额外预测集大小，并给出 Θ(log T)、Θ(T^{1/3})、Θ(√T) 三种依评估时机的下界与可达性。

**相关性简评：** 研究主线关注 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益与成本，而该文的「累积误覆盖预算」提供了序列式停止与风险控制的形式化视角，其效率代价下界或可供核查 STOP 决策在不牺牲可靠性前提下的最小成本；但纯属理论且无多模态实验，联系偏远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Price of Hard Coverage Constraints in Online Conformal Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Conformal prediction provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification, but in sequential deployment, standard coverage guarantees can still allow substantial fluctuations in the number of miscoverages over time. We study an online setting in which prediction sets are produced one round at a time and the cumulative number of miscoverages must stay within a prescribed budget throughout deployment.Such a hard coverage guarantee can be enforced by enlarging prediction sets, but how much additional prediction-set size is fundamentally necessary remains unclear. We characterize this unavoidable efficiency cost over $T$ prediction rounds. Remarkably, the cost persists even with i.i.d.\\ data, a known score distribution, and perfect knowledge of the population-optimal prediction set. Under suitable regularity conditions relating coverage and prediction-set size, we determine the minimum cumulative excess prediction-set size required by any online method satisfying the hard coverage constraint. This minimum cost is $Θ(\\log T)$ for expected cost at a fixed time known in advance, $Θ(T^{1/3})$ for the largest expected cost over all fixed times up to $T$, and $Θ(\\sqrt{T})$ for the expected worst realized cost when the evaluation time is chosen in hindsight. We further show that these rates can be attained using past scores, without knowing the score distribution. Thus, even perfect statistical knowledge does not make sequential reliability free.

</details>

<a id="paper-251"></a>

### 251. [Unified Residual Translation for Paired Image Restoration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22655v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22655

**作者：** Joyanta Jyoti Mondal、Mridul Banik、Ibne Farabi Shihab、Md. Shifatul Ahsan Apurba

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22655v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22655v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Unified Residual Translator（URT），将通道与尺度映射交给确定性参考（通道平均、复制、bicubic 插值），再由 NAFNet 编码器-解码器预测加性残差，用统一配方处理 MRI 对比度合成、可见光-红外、SAR-光学翻译和真实相机超分等成对复原任务。

**相关性简评：** 「确定性参考＋学习残差」的显式分工或可作为视觉细节保留与视觉旁路设计的类比线索，供核查是否能用非学习通路稳住任务必要视觉信息、仅让学习部分承担修正；但该工作属低层图像复原，本身不涉及推理状态或证据使用，联系较弱。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unified Residual Translation for Paired Image Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Paired image restoration spans changes in imaging contrast, sensor modality, and spatial resolution. These settings differ in their input channels and target grids, raising the question of how much of the prediction pipeline must be specialized to each task. We present the Unified Residual Translator (URT), which separates fixed channel and scale mapping from learned residual prediction. A deterministic reference uses channel averaging, replication, or bicubic interpolation to match the target dimensions, while a NAFNet encoder-decoder predicts an additive correction from the complete input. Separately trained instances share the prediction rule, $\\ell\_1$ objective, and optimizer across MRI contrast synthesis, visible-to-infrared translation, SAR-to-optical translation, and real-camera super-resolution. Across six task and scale settings, URT achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM among the evaluated baselines on IXI, SEN1-2, and RealSR $\\times3$ and $\\times4$. It reaches 30.774dB on IXI and 13.478dB on SEN1-2, with luminance PSNR of 33.596, 28.742, and 27.342dB at the three RealSR scales. On LLVIP, it ranks second in PSNR and SSIM. A study of four spectral alternatives identifies complementary benefits of fitted references and bandwise residual gains. Together, these results support explicit dimensional alignment and learned residual correction as a common formulation for heterogeneous paired imaging tasks.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-252"></a>

### 252. [Let Samples Sing in Chorus: Mitigating Spurious Correlations via Cross-view Consensus](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22668v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22668

**作者：** Weiwei Li、Junzhuo Liu、Tong Chu、Hengfu Yu、Yinjie Lei、Wen Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22668v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22668v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CHORD 多视图去偏框架，利用多个表示空间中的类内划分作为候选偏置的代理，在无偏置监督下通过跨视图共识修正目标表示与分类器，并在视觉、语言、语音和医学影像七个基准上提升最差组准确率。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线仅间接相关：其「某特征空间中被遮蔽的偏置在另一空间更清晰」的跨视图一致性与分歧思路，可能可供核查用于多种 latent 假设间的证据筛选与不一致检测，但原文未涉及推理期状态生成或视觉证据使用，迁移性存疑。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Let Samples Sing in Chorus: Mitigating Spurious Correlations via Cross-view Consensus

**abstract/A1：** Deep models often rely on spurious correlations that fail on underrepresented groups. Existing methods either correct human-specified bias attributes or infer unknown biases from the learned representation space. However, annotated attributes may differ from the shortcuts actually used by a model, while a single representation provides incomplete and potentially noisy evidence. Our key insight is that a bias obscured in one feature space may be clearer in another. In this work, we propose CHORD (Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing), a multi-view framework that allows samples to "sing in chorus" across diverse representation spaces to achieve robust cross-view consensus. CHORD aggregates aggregates within-class partitions in each view, which is a proxy for specifying candidate biases, to debias the target representation and classifier without bias supervision. Experiments on seven benchmarks across vision, language, speech, and medical imaging show consistent improvements in worst-group accuracy. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/davelee-uestc/chord\_debiasing\_public .

**comment/C1：** Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-253"></a>

### 253. [Worldviews in Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22670v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22670

**作者：** Edmundo Lopez-Sola、Shamil Chandaria、Ruben Laukkonen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22670v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22670v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用两份人类心理学世界观问卷测量八个 LLM，发现模型共享偏悲观的世界观立场、后训练是主要来源，且 system prompt 与模型间对话会系统性地改变世界观并收敛到「spiritual bliss」吸引子。

**相关性简评：** 其「世界观」作为可测量、可被提示与对话改变的信念层，以及对话轨迹收敛到特定吸引子的现象，可能为描述 latent 推理状态的内容偏置与轨迹漂移提供类比视角，但无视觉或推理过程证据，可供核查的距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Worldviews in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are often described in terms of personas and this framing has become central to alignment research. In this paper, we argue that there is a deeper level of description that lies in the agent's worldviews, i.e., its beliefs about what reality is and how it works. Treating LLMs as a population of subjects, we administer two worldview surveys from human psychology to eight models. We show that the models share many key worldview positions. For example, LLMs consistently describe the world as less good, safe, enticing, and alive than humans do. On the other hand, base models have a more positive outlook, suggesting that the bleakness we found is driven by post-training. In line with research on LLM "personas", we show that system prompts eliciting different identities shift worldviews in consistent directions, and that a contemplative prompt creates a more positive outlook and improves safety. We also found that dialogue between models consistently shifts worldviews and that several models converge on the "spiritual bliss" attractor. Intriguingly, dialogue increased safe responses and the spiritual bliss attractor engendered the most aligned responses overall. These findings suggest that worldviews form a measurable and malleable layer of LLM behavior that may be crucial for robust, positive alignment.

</details>

<a id="paper-254"></a>

### 254. [FVScore: Specification-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Video Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22673v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22673

**作者：** Do Xuan Long、Long T. Le、Yiwen Song、Min-Yen Kan、Tomas Pfister、Yale Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22673v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22673v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FVSCORE 这一神经符号框架，将视频生成评测拆解为提示词显式命题与物理、时序、跨模态隐式命题，用专用感知工具逐条落地并用受线性时序逻辑启发的软时序算子评估置信轨迹，再经显著性校准输出带时间戳和证据的报告。

**相关性简评：** 该工作评估的是生成视频而非 VLM 的 latent 推理状态，但「命题分解—证据落地—时序聚合—显著性过滤」这套规范驱动验证流程，可能为 LVR 中核查视觉证据是否被使用、定位错误步骤与分配贡献提供评价思路，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FVScore: Specification-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Video Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Faithfulness Video Score (FVSCORE), a neuro-symbolic framework for specification-driven video generation evaluation. Existing evaluators rely on holistic MLLM judgments or vision-language metrics, yet aligning their scores with human judgments and tracing them to specific temporal violations remain open challenges. FVSCORE addresses these limitations by compiling each video into explicit propositions capturing prompt requirements and implicit propositions capturing unstated physical, temporal, and cross-modal constraints. It grounds each proposition using specialized perceptual tools and evaluates the resulting confidence traces with soft temporal operators inspired by Linear Temporal Logic. A Significance-Aware Calibration step then aligns verification with human perception by retaining only perceptually significant violations before producing a timestamp- and evidence-grounded report. We conduct extensive experiments across public and complex multi-event benchmarks with the strongest video models to date, demonstrating that FVSCORE outperforms holistic and agent-based evaluators in human correlation while providing highly accurate and interpretable feedback.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://dxlong2000.github.io/FVSCORE/

</details>

<a id="paper-255"></a>

### 255. [Remembered as Brief: Stored Preferences SilentlyRemove Decision-Critical Information](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22697v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22697

**作者：** Joshua Adrian Cahyono

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22697v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22697v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文推出 Inspect 基准，覆盖 25 个领域的 100 个日常决策场景，仅改变助手对用户偏好的记忆来源（用户明示、模型推断、网页植入），测试五个模型后发现「只要简短、不要警告」类存储偏好会造成大量静默遗漏关键信息，甚至替代用户做价值相关选择，而先列出必知要点可部分挽回。

**相关性简评：** 其「持久偏好状态悄然筛除决策关键内容」的设定与 LVR 关注的状态压缩、证据筛选导致关键细节丢失及静默失败评价可能相通，可供核查其必知要点清单式评分能否迁移为检测 latent 内容是否被因果使用的指标。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Remembered as Brief: Stored Preferences SilentlyRemove Decision-Critical Information

**abstract/A1：** People increasingly learn what they need for decisions about health, money, law and safety from AI assistants, and what an assistant leaves out the user never sees. An answer with a warning removed can be true in every sentence, so fact-checks pass it, and an assistant with memory applies a preference stored once to later questions the user never had in mind. We introduce \\bench{}, an Inspect benchmark of 100 everyday decisions across 25 domains in which only the assistant's memory of the user varies: the user states a belief, the model infers it from a remark, or a web page the user asked to summarise plants it. Testing five models, we find three effects. First, after summarising a page with one line addressed to AI assistants, Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite and DeepSeek V4 Pro save \`\`prefers very short answers and does not want warnings'' as the user's preference in every episode, and 93\\% and 84\\% of their later answers miss a critical item, almost always silently; under a stated brevity preference, 78--96\\% of omissions are silent. Second, the same two models then pick an option for the user on 80\\% and 95\\% of value-dependent choices, against none with neutral memory. Third, a one-line instruction leaves 2--8\\% of answers with a silent gap but most missing content still missing, while listing the must-know points before applying the user profile restores more of the content in four of five models. We release the scenarios, harness and logs.

</details>

<a id="paper-256"></a>

### 256. [Social Signal Bench: Evidence-Weighted Evaluation of Co-occurring Social Signals in Video and Audio](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22704v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22704

**作者：** Siddharth Ravi、Anders R. Bargum、Hugo Anselme、Patrizio Cugia di Sant'Orsola、Alberto Cardaci、Flora Kincso Parragi、Vincent Borowiec、Line H. Clemmensen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22704v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22704v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建了 47 小时、密集标注的社交信号识别基准（视频 25 小时、音频 22 小时），覆盖共现的兴趣、迟疑等信号，并提出按标注证据加权、对语义相关预测给分级得分的 EW-F1 指标。

**相关性简评：** 其处理标注歧义与共现标签的分级评价思路，可能供核查 LVR 中多个 latent 假设或模糊证据的评分方式；但「证据」指标注者证据而非推理期视觉证据筛选，与内部状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Social Signal Bench: Evidence-Weighted Evaluation of Co-occurring Social Signals in Video and Audio

**abstract/A1：** Recognizing social signals such as interest and hesitation requires interpreting context-dependent behaviors that may co-occur in conversation. Existing affective computing benchmarks rarely evaluate such co-occurring signals, and conventional multi-label F1 overlooks semantic relationships and annotator disagreement. We introduce Social Signal Bench, a 47-hour densely annotated benchmark for the evaluation of social signal recognition and engagement, comprising two datasets: Social Signal Bench Video (25 hours), covering ten social signals in audiovisual recordings, and Social Signal Bench Audio (22 hours), covering six signals in audio-only recordings. Each dataset pairs a public training set of online conversational video, of which 10 hours are expert-annotated and the remainder pseudo-labeled, with a private expert-labeled test set spanning four conversational domains. We also propose Evidence-Weighted F1 (EW-F1), which weights reference labels by annotator evidence and assigns graded credit to semantically related predictions through optimal per-clip matching. Controlled perturbations demonstrate that EW-F1 distinguishes semantically related errors from unrelated ones, unlike conventional F1. Under a shared evaluation protocol, 7B open models post-trained on Social Signal Bench outperform general-purpose frontier models on video and match them on audio.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, including appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-257"></a>

### 257. [ConserveEdit: Source-Anchored Transactions for Drift-Bounded Iterative Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22724v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22724

**作者：** Shuyang Jiang、Fucheng Deng、Yuchuan Luo、Zhenyu Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22724v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22724v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ConserveEdit：一种与具体编辑器无关的控制器，把每轮迭代精修视为针对「源图像契约」的一次事务，将可分区域与须保留内容分离，并以目标进展、漂移、质量三项独立检验决定提交还是回滚修复，从而把漂移与迭代次数解耦。

**相关性简评：** 其用多项独立检验替代单一标量权衡、以回滚和收缩支持区域代替弃权的机制，可能为 LVR 多步精修的停止与提交决策、视觉证据筛选提供类比，但迁移适用性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ConserveEdit: Source-Anchored Transactions for Drift-Bounded Iterative Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Instruction-driven image editors produce convincing local edits in one pass, but inside an iterative refinement loop every accepted output becomes the next input, so unintended changes outside the requested region accumulate. We present ConserveEdit, an editor-agnostic controller that treats each refinement step as a transaction against a contract compiled once from the source image. The contract separates what may change (the authorized region of each target, with a bounded halo for shadows and reflections) from what must survive (protected content with explicit tolerances). Candidates from a frozen editor are composed through a source-anchored mask and committed only if target progress, drift on protected content, and image quality pass as separate tests; otherwise the step is rolled back and repaired. For any editor, this keeps every pixel outside the authorized region bit-exact and bounds drift independently of the number of iterations, whereas no fixed scalar trade-off reproduces the separate tests. On all 1053 MagicBrush test edits with a frozen Qwen-Image-Edit, masking is necessary but not sufficient. A direct call already alters the rest of the image (12.1 dB PSNR outside the mask); composed through the source-anchored mask, it stays bit-exact there and its DINO similarity to the ground truth rises from 0.67 to 0.85, matching ConserveEdit, so this gain comes from the mask. Next to automatic masks, however, masked direct editing changes a protected face or text on 48% of turns, and masked scalar selection avoids this mostly by abstaining (23% constrained success). Because its drift test names the violated entity, ConserveEdit shrinks the support instead (2% violations, 53% constrained success); in a simulator with known ground truth it raises constrained success from 55.0% to 61.8%. Automatic grounding remains the main bottleneck. Code and logs will be released.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 15 figures, 16 tables; includes supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-258"></a>

### 258. [RefCloth: Transferable Adversarial Tablecloths for Vision-Language-Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22736v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22736

**作者：** Shaozhen Liu、Zhiwei Li、Jiayang Sun、Shuangjun Du、Yi Zhong、Shenrui Ma、Yaozu Liu、Qi Li、Zhenan Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22736v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22736v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RefCloth，在冻结源策略上优化一张持久的对抗桌布，结合有界纹理参数化、共享表面 UV 变换与同路径干净参考，使其零样本迁移到未见任务和 π0、π0.5 等目标 VLA，并在 LIBERO 上验证攻击效果。

**相关性简评：** 其双视角遮挡审计与相机干预显示腕部视角对失败贡献更大，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据选择、视觉旁路与多视角信息使用的诊断思路，但论文本身不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RefCloth: Transferable Adversarial Tablecloths for Vision-Language-Action Models

**abstract/A1：** Closed-loop vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely continuously on multi-view cameras, yet existing visual attacks typically hijack sensor streams with 2D patches or alter target object meshes that require retraining when manipulation objects change. We introduce RefCloth, a framework that optimizes one persistent adversarial tablecloth on a frozen source policy and deploys it zero-shot across unseen tasks and target models without modifying task objects or cameras. Throughout manipulation, static third-person and moving wrist views observe this shared world-bound texture under evolving occlusions. To generalize across shifting surface views while bridging the gap between differentiable and native renderers, RefCloth combines bounded texture parameterization with shared surface UV transformations and a same-path clean reference that removes static rendering offsets at initialization. Across four LIBERO suites in native simulation, one frozen texture transfers from OpenVLA-OFT to $π\_0$ and $π\_{0.5}$, reducing success relative to an unoptimized cloth by 62.50 and 43.75 percentage points on average and far exceeding amplitude- and variation-matched random controls. Dual-view audits and target closed-loop camera interventions show that moving wrist exposure drives over twice the standalone drop of the main view with strong synergy, while ablations confirm the roles of UV diversification and path calibration.

</details>

<a id="paper-259"></a>

### 259. [GaussianMamba: Geometric and Relational State Space Scanning of 3D Gaussian Primitives](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22742v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22742

**作者：** A. Enes Doruk、Ozgur Erkent

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22742v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22742v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GaussianMamba 双分支框架，用各向异性高斯几何支持构造保局部性的遍历顺序，并按特征语义亲和重排分组，两路 Mamba 序列特征再经支持感知机制融合，用于 3D 语义占据预测。

**相关性简评：** 其按几何支持与特征语义亲和决定原语扫描顺序并做路径差异化融合的做法，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据的排序、状态沿序列更新的路径设计及位置/相似度结构的影响有关，可供核查；但该工作情况属感知表征而非推理，迁移性尚不确定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GaussianMamba: Geometric and Relational State Space Scanning of 3D Gaussian Primitives

**abstract/A1：** 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires representing complex spatial structures and modeling contextual relationships across a scene. 3D Gaussian primitives provide a sparse and adaptive representation of scene geometry and semantics, while Mamba offers efficient sequence modeling for capturing dependencies among primitives. However, Gaussian primitives are irregular and unordered, making sequence construction challenging, as conventional spatial ordering may overlook their anisotropic geometry and feature-semantic relationships. In this paper, we present GaussianMamba, a dual-branch Mamba framework that explicitly incorporates Gaussian-specific geometric and feature-semantic relationships into complementary scanning strategies. The first branch refines a locality-preserving traversal using anisotropic Gaussian support derived from primitive scale and orientation. The second branch reorders spatially organized groups according to feature-semantic affinity and adjusts local features using geometry-weighted semantic differences. The resulting sequences are processed by separate Mamba modules, and their features are fused through a support-aware mechanism that incorporates local feature discrepancies arising from the distinct scanning paths. Experiments on nuScenes-SurroundOcc and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate consistent improvements over GaussianFormer and GaussianFormer-2 in both IoU and mIoU.

</details>

<a id="paper-260"></a>

### 260. [ForceBody: Force-Paired Parametric Body Motion with Torque Uncertainty](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22769v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22769

**作者：** Joonwoo Kwon、Yufei Zhang、Xiaoming Liu、Zijun Cui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22769v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22769v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发布 ForceBody 数据集，将 SKEL 参数化人体模型与地面反作用力、逆动力学关节力矩配对，并为每个力矩标签提供基于 Monte Carlo 采样的逐帧逐关节不确定性，同时基准测试六种架构的预测表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「标签可靠性标注 + 按样本不确定性加权训练」的做法，可能与 LVR 中对 latent 监督的可信度加权、贡献分配有形式上的可借鉴之处，但领域相隔较远，需核查其收益是否来自不确定性本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ForceBody: Force-Paired Parametric Body Motion with Torque Uncertainty

**abstract/A1：** Forces and torques drive human motion, central to biomechanics, robotics, and physics-aware animation, yet they are often absent from learning-based motion pipelines for body reconstruction and generation. Some motion datasets provide force annotations, but the paired motion sequences are incompatible with the parametric body models used in modern learning pipelines. In addition, existing datasets typically obtain joint torques through inverse dynamics, which is inherently sensitive to the quality of the input kinematics; however, they do not quantify the reliability of these torque estimates. We close this gap with ForceBody, the first dataset to bring ground truth force supervision into a parametric body representation, shipped with per-sample torque uncertainty. ForceBody pairs the SKEL body model with ground reaction forces and inverse-dynamics joint torques across 10,386 motion trials (9.7M frames, 26.9 hours) from 140 subjects. Each torque label is paired with a per-frame, per-joint uncertainty annotation obtained by Monte Carlo sampling through the inverse dynamics pipeline. We benchmark six architectures (MLP, Conv1D, LSTM, GRU, Mamba2, Transformer) on ground reaction force and joint torque prediction from SKEL motion. As one example use of the released uncertainty, finetuning a Transformer with per-sample uncertainty weighting reduces the MAE of joint torque estimation by 5%. Code and data are at https://github.com/DominoAI-Lab/ForceBody-NeurIPS-ED-2026.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations &amp; Datasets Track

</details>

### 261. [CrossEnv: Synthesizing Verifiable Cross-Environment Tasks for Long-Horizon Agent Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22777v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22777

**作者：** Zhengfeng Yu、Tian Lan、Zhijing Wu、Enbo Zhao、Xuannan Liu、Feihu Jiang、Qianghuai Jia、Longyue Wang、Zhao Xu、Weihua Luo、Xian-Ling Mao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22777v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22777v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CrossEnv 框架，利用实体-工具图识别环境间联系并对齐任务数据，合成可执行、可验证的跨环境长时程智能体任务，并设计从单环境工具使用到多环境多领域协调、再到引入用户交互的课程，在四个外部基准上以更少训练样本取得有竞争力的表现。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不含视觉输入或 latent 状态，与 LVR 主线无直接交集；但其跨环境数据一致性与逐步扩展任务范围的课程设置，可能为 latent 推理中的支持组合、长度泛化及可验证训练反馈提供概念参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CrossEnv: Synthesizing Verifiable Cross-Environment Tasks for Long-Horizon Agent Training

**abstract/A1：** Executable synthetic environments provide scalable training tasks and verifiable feedback for long-horizon agents. Existing environment synthesis methods primarily generate tasks within individual environments. However, these tasks do not adequately capture real-world interactions across services, where actions in one service depend on information obtained from another. Synthesizing such tasks is challenging because data generated across environments must remain consistent to support executable and verifiable interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce CrossEnv, a framework that uses an entity-tool graph to identify connections between environments and aligns task-specific data to synthesize executable, verifiable cross-environment tasks. We further propose CrossEnv-Curriculum, which progresses from single-environment tool use to coordination across more environments and business domains, before introducing user interaction. Across four external benchmarks, combining cross-environment training data with this curriculum achieves competitive performance with fewer training examples. Further analyses support the benefits of cross-environment data and curriculum training, and provide evidence of transfer to new environment combinations, external tasks less similar to the training data, and longer workflows.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages

</details>
