### 262. [Kolibri: Color Finds the Place, Refinement Sets the Pose for RGB-D Localization in Colored Point Clouds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22829v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22829

**作者：** Leonel Aguilar、Bartu Tuncay、Christoph Hoelscher

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22829v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22829v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Kolibri：用仅 5.5 万参数的图网络描述彩色点云中 10cm 体素的颜色、局部形状与邻域，仅做「找位置」的初始位姿预测，再交由几何精配准完成 6DoF 定位，实现无需房间标签的快速 RGB-D 重定位。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把任务分解为廉价的粗候选检索与昂贵的几何精化，并考察跨房间/跨相机的外观一致性失效，可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK 的贡献分配、视觉证据筛选与计算成本权衡是否有类比价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Kolibri: Color Finds the Place, Refinement Sets the Pose for RGB-D Localization in Colored Point Clouds

**abstract/A1：** An agent or robot in a scanned building must locate its camera in rooms that no model saw during training. Point cloud registration methods can align each RGB-D frame with the building's colored point cloud, accurately but at a high cost. We observe that geometric refinement brings nearby initial poses to the same final pose, so the learned component only needs to find the place. Kolibri does this with a graph network of 55,712 parameters that describes 10 cm cubes of points, called cells, by their color, local shape and neighbors. With the same refinement, it matches GeDi on 96 unseen ScanNet rooms (81.4% against 80.3% within 5 cm and 5°) and on a held-out test of 10 further rooms, in 82 ms per frame on an RTX 2080 Ti. The saved time allows for search without a room label. In one point cloud of 100 rooms, Kolibri loses 1.3 points against knowing the room, while retrieving the room first loses 56.0. In a hospital emergency department scene from a real scan, it localizes 86.5% of the frames rendered from the scan in 384 ms each, where GeDi needs 91 minutes per frame on cubes of the scan for a similar recall. The method needs colors that agree between the frame and the point cloud. On gray frames and across cameras with different color responses, point cloud registration methods keep the lead.

</details>

<a id="paper-263"></a>

### 263. [WATERFALL: Workflow for Adaptive Training with Evolutionary Reward Formulation and Automated Learning Loops](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22854v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22854

**作者：** Eleftherios Triantafyllidis、Filippos Christianos、Zhibin Li、Bernd Bickel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22854v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22854v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WATERFALL，一个面向 POMDP 环境的种群式自动奖励发现流程：用 persona 条件生成程序化奖励候选，由 VLM 通过瑞士制锦标赛对原始视觉 rollout 进行评判以选出精英，并利用纵向评估历史迭代变异、升级或剪枝候选，在离散到连续、全可观测与部分可观测环境中与稀疏奖励 RL、内在动机和基础模型奖励合成基线比较。

**相关性简评：** 该文不研究 VLM 内部推理状态，但其「VLM 依据原始视觉 rollout 评判行为」及消融中视觉证据必要性的结论，可能为检验 latent 状态是否真正使用视觉证据、以及候选状态的搜索与剪枝式评价提供参考线索，需核查其评判协议是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WATERFALL: Workflow for Adaptive Training with Evolutionary Reward Formulation and Automated Learning Loops

**abstract/A1：** Reward design remains a central bottleneck in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in sparse-reward, long-horizon and partially observable settings requiring dependency chaining, memory, and deceptive affordance disambiguation. While recent foundation-model approaches reduce manual reward engineering, most assume goal-conditioning, task descriptions, expert demonstrations, or ground-truth metrics. These assumptions are problematic in partially observable environments, where task-relevant objectives, affordances, and dynamics must be discovered through interaction rather than disclosed a priori. We formalise this problem setting by introducing WATERFALL, an iterative population-based workflow for automated reward discovery under the strict observability constraints inherent in POMDP environments. WATERFALL achieves this by (i) synthesising diverse programmatic reward candidates via persona-conditioned generation, (ii) evaluating candidate behaviour from raw visual rollouts utilising a Swiss-system evolutionary tournament judged by vision-language models to identify elites, and (iii) leveraging longitudinal assessment histories to iteratively mutate, escalate, or prune candidates. We evaluate WATERFALL across fully observable and reveal-gated partially observable environments ranging from discrete to continuous, comparing against sparse-reward RL, intrinsic-motivation and foundation-model reward-synthesis baselines. Our results indicate that WATERFALL discovers and refines reward programs without access to privileged information, outperforming baselines that retain their native information advantages, most clearly in the POMDP regime. Extensive ablations substantiate that visual evidence, tournament-based selection, and iterative refinement are jointly important. Enforcing this contract is what separates autonomous reward discovery from benchmark-privileged reward shaping.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (poster). 78 pages, 13 figures, 19 tables. Code: https://github.com/etriantafyllidis/waterfall OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=TI1lG64bTY

</details>

<a id="paper-264"></a>

### 264. [Propose &amp; Verify: Fast Reflection Symmetry Detection via Harmonic Fields](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22856v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22856

**作者：** Yishai Lavi、Shai Avidan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22856v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22856v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FAST：一种无需训练的 3D 形状多反射对称面检测框架，将几何配对投票表示为平面朝向信号，借助球谐域中的 Funk-Hecke 定理把高斯平滑化为系数乘法，从平滑对称场中提取候选并做几何验证。

**相关性简评：** 「提议—验证」结构与在连续假设空间上聚合证据、平滑打分再筛选的思路，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的生成、证据累积与验证取舍提供概念类比，但属远领域线索，需核查其可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Propose &amp; Verify: Fast Reflection Symmetry Detection via Harmonic Fields

**abstract/A1：** Detecting multiple reflection symmetries in 3D shapes requires aggregating geometric evidence over a large space of candidate planes, making traditional voting approaches computationally expensive. We introduce the Fourier Accelerated Symmetry Transform (FAST), a training-free framework that combines geometric pair voting with efficient spectral computation. Exploiting the fact that global reflection planes pass through the shape centroid, we reduce the search to plane orientations, while geometric consistency filtering and independent witnesses suppress unreliable pair hypotheses. Our key insight is to represent these votes as a signal over plane orientations and efficiently compute a continuous, smoothed symmetry field in the spherical-harmonic domain. Specifically, we exploit the Funk-Hecke theorem, the spherical counterpart of the Fourier convolution theorem for rotationally symmetric kernels, to transform Gaussian smoothing into multiplication of harmonic coefficients. This enables efficient field evaluation, from which symmetry candidates are extracted, refined, and geometrically verified. Across two benchmarks, including a new benchmark of highly symmetric shapes, FAST achieves the highest clean multi-plane detection $F\_1$ scores among evaluated methods (0.861 and 0.947), with GPU inference below 4 ms per shape: over $100\\times$ faster than the next-fastest evaluated multi-plane GPU baseline. FAST also demonstrates strong robustness to geometric noise and competitive single-plane detection, achieving 94.9% success on clean single-plane shapes.

</details>

<a id="paper-265"></a>

### 265. [Wear and Go: Calibration Free Sparse Inertial Motion Capture via Online Sensor Setup Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22894v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22894

**作者：** Yuanshuo Tan、Licheng Zhong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22894v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22894v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MOSAIC 框架，用带时序与跨传感器注意力的网络从日常动作中累积证据，估计稀疏 IMU 的传感器归属与安装朝向，从而无需标定姿态即可把测量规范化后送入冻结的姿态估计器。

**相关性简评：** 其「单帧歧义、跨时间累积成会话级潜变量，再将输入规范化以复用冻结模型」的思路，可能为 LVR 中把视角、传感器配置等上下文潜变量与推理状态分离、以及证据累积与置信判断提供类比线索，但领域距离较远，需核查其累积与接口设计是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Wear and Go: Calibration Free Sparse Inertial Motion Capture via Online Sensor Setup Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Sparse inertial motion capture typically assumes that each sensor's anatomical identity and mounting orientation are known. We present MOSAIC (Motion-based Sensor Assignment and Inertial Calibration), a framework that recovers this setup from ordinary motion without a calibration pose. Our central insight is that body pose changes continuously while the sensor setup persists, allowing movements that are individually ambiguous to provide complementary evidence over time. A setup network processes unordered sensor streams with temporal and cross-sensor attention, then accumulates assignment and rotation evidence into a session-level estimate. The recovered setup canonicalizes measurements for frozen pretrained pose estimators, separating sensor configuration from motion reconstruction. For sensors worn at prescribed body sites, evaluation on real inertial recordings with randomized setups shows near-calibrated pose accuracy for bounded offsets and substantial benefits from accumulation under arbitrary orientations. Transfer across pose networks supports setup recovery as a reusable interface between interchangeable sensors and pretrained pose models.

</details>

<a id="paper-266"></a>

### 266. [Born for the Task: Rethinking Quantum Chemistry with Foresighted Agentic Computing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22897v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22897

**作者：** Haozhi Han、Junjie Yang、Ruge Zhang、Zihe Huang、Pengyi Xu、Bo Zhang、Yunquan Zhang、Ting Cao、Yunxin Liu、Yifeng Chen、Kun Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22897v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22897v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BORN 前瞻式 agentic 计算框架：在运行前由小规模体系外推误差、结合目标规模负载与实测硬件吞吐预测耗时，据此在精度-时间 Pareto 前沿上选取策略，并指导编码 agent 生成高性能量子化学程序。

**相关性简评：** 「先预测误差与成本、再决定执行哪种策略」与 LVR 中推理路径、计算深度选择和停止决策的收益-成本预估可能有概念联系，可供核查；但论文不涉及视觉证据或 latent 状态机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Born for the Task: Rethinking Quantum Chemistry with Foresighted Agentic Computing

**abstract/A1：** Quantum chemistry relies on general-purpose software, whose generality leaves many large-scale calculations beyond practical resource budgets. Coding agents make task-specific programs cheap to write, but not efficient ones easy to find: whether a computational strategy is accurate and fast enough becomes clear only after it actually runs at the target scale. We introduce BORN, a foresighted agentic computing framework that synthesizes high performance quantum chemistry programs on demand. BORN anticipates the behavior of each strategy before execution, extrapolating its error from representative small systems and predicting its runtime from the target-scale workload and measured hardware throughputs. Building on these predictions, BORN unfolds the preset schemes of existing packages into independently configurable algorithmic components, selects the fastest strategy within the error tolerance from the predicted accuracy-runtime Pareto frontier, and guides coding agents to compile it with component-level performance predictions. For a Hartree-Fock calculation with 50,024 basis functions, BORN finishes in 1.41h on a single CPU node, 77$\\times$ faster than the estimated runtime of a general-purpose baseline and still 9.8$\\times$ faster with development time included. On a single GPU, BORN completes the ubiquitin calculation in 2.1,h, the same order of runtime as the GPU-accelerated package Mako on 64 GPUs, and shortens the B-DNA duplex calculation from an estimated two months to 8.1,h (173$\\times$).

</details>

<a id="paper-267"></a>

### 267. [BRepA: Graph-Based B-Rep Pretraining for Unified CAD Understanding and Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22903v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22903

**作者：** Dekai Zhu、Zhaiyu Chen、Stefan Gavranovic、Slobodan Ilic

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22903v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22903v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BRepA，一个面向 CAD B-Rep 的图神经网络自监督预训练框架，通过同时重建几何与拓扑来学习通用表示，并将其用于分割生成任务与扩散模型特征对齐。

**相关性简评：** 其用预训练表示对齐生成模型内部特征以加速收敛的做法，可能为 LVR 中生成状态与预训练表示的对齐、以及结构化几何/拓扑信息在内部状态的保留提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BRepA: Graph-Based B-Rep Pretraining for Unified CAD Understanding and Generation

**abstract/A1：** Boundary representation (B-Rep) of CAD models encodes geometric primitives and their relations as a graph structure, enabling efficient shape modeling within CAD software. Learning on B-Reps for downstream tasks, such as segmentation and novel shape generation, requires specialized neural network designs. Moreover, performance improvements and accelerated training can be achieved with effective pretraining strategies, which is particularly important for tasks with limited labeled B-Rep data. In this paper, we introduce BRepA, a unified self-supervised pretraining framework that explicitly reconstructs both the geometry and topology of B-Rep objects via graph-based modeling. By learning from large-scale unlabeled CAD data only once, BRepA produces representations that generalize across tasks and significantly enhance both discriminative and generative learning. On segmentation tasks, BRepA demonstrates remarkable data efficiency. For instance, with only 1% labeled samples, BRepA pretraining improves mIoU by 32.52% compared to models trained from scratch. On generative tasks, aligning diffusion model representations with pretrained BRepA features substantially accelerates convergence, achieving nearly 2 times faster training while improving generation quality under the same computational budget, despite the diffusion model being roughly 20 times larger than BRepA. Overall, our results show that geometry- and topology-aware B-Rep pretraining provides a powerful foundation for both understanding and generating CAD structures, serving as a versatile representation backbone for diverse downstream applications. We will release the code to facilitate future research.

</details>

<a id="paper-268"></a>

### 268. [Who Pays for Byzantine Robustness? Validity, Data-Share Dictatorships, and Fairness When Only a Few Hospitals Collaborate](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22904v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22904

**作者：** Adnan Malkani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22904v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22904v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 FLamby 的三个跨机构医疗数据集上对八种 Byzantine 鲁棒聚合规则做大规模实证审计，并用以「精确孪生留出」对照把性能下降拆解为诚实数据缺失与真实攻击损伤两部分。

**相关性简评：** 其留出对照式的因果拆解思路可能为 LVR 中区分「缺失有效视觉证据」与「误导性状态干扰」的贡献分配提供核查线索，但领域相距较远，需先确认可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Pays for Byzantine Robustness? Validity, Data-Share Dictatorships, and Fairness When Only a Few Hospitals Collaborate

**abstract/A1：** Clinical federated learning (FL) collaborations typically connect only three to six hospitals, yet Byzantine-robust aggregation rules are designed and benchmarked on synthetic partitions across dozens of equal-sized clients. We conduct an empirical audit of eight standard aggregation rules: FedAvg, coordinate-wise median, trimmed mean, Krum, Multi-Krum, Bulyan, geometric median (GeoMed), and Centered Clipping (CC). We evaluate both unweighted and sample-size-weighted variants across three cross-silo medical datasets from the FLamby benchmark: Fed-Heart-Disease ($n = 4$ hospitals), Fed-TCGA-BRCA ($n = 6$ regions), and Fed-ISIC2019 ($n = 6$ centres). We execute 54,214 training runs across enumerated attacker coalitions ($f \\in \\{1, 2\\}$) paired with exact-twin leave-out controls that decompose performance drops into honest data loss versus adversarial attack damage. We report three findings. First, client-count validity checks fail to capture breakdown in skewed medical consortia: while $n = 6, f = 1$ satisfies every count condition except Bulyan's, assigning sample-size weights turns median and GeoMed into a clean-model dictatorship whenever a single hospital holds more than half the training data, and causes weighted median, GeoMed, and CC to collapse when that dominant centre attacks. Second, exact-twin leave-out controls reveal that when the largest hospital attacks, the apparent failure of count-based robust rules is predominantly caused by the loss of honest training data (7.5 to 10.4 points on ISIC) rather than adversarial perturbation (2.3 to 3.8 points on ISIC). Conversely, weight-dependent rules suffer large true attack damage (16.1 to 25.1 points on ISIC). Third, unweighted robust rules consistently discount whichever institution generates the most atypical parameter updates, penalizing the smallest hospital on Heart but penalizing larger institutions on TCGA and ISIC.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables. Code: https://github.com/adnanmalkani/byzantine-fl-bench

</details>

<a id="paper-269"></a>

### 269. [Action-outcome coupling: a pre-investment feasibility audit for offline reinforcement learning in supply chain operations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22921v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22921

**作者：** Tung Dang、The Hung Phung、Son Lam Nguyen、Tu Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、econ.EM、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22921v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22921v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向供应链等场景的离线强化学习投资决策，提出以「动作—结果耦合」方差指数刻画日志中动作对结果的可影响程度，并据此给出策略价值上界、评估误放行风险分析与四档可行性审计流程。

**相关性简评：** 其「先审计日志信息量再投入」的思路，以及在同一数据上拟合并评估贪婪策略导致选择偏差的分析，可能为 LVR 中判断 latent 收益是否来自真实证据而非状态格上的选择、以及停止与继续投入的依据提供概念借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Action-outcome coupling: a pre-investment feasibility audit for offline reinforcement learning in supply chain operations

**abstract/A1：** Prescriptive analytics initiatives in logistics, such as offline reinforcement learning and next-best-action systems, are often funded before anyone verifies that the available historical logs can support an improved decision policy. We formalise this pre-investment feasibility question via action-outcome coupling: a total-effect variance index measuring how much expected outcomes change with the logged action. Coupling provides an algorithm-independent upper bound on any policy's achievable value, expressed in monetary units per decision for both single-step and discounted sequential objectives. We also analyse the asymptotic false-licensing risk of an invalid workflow: fitting a value model and evaluating the model's greedy policy on the same data. The false-licence rate is driven mainly by the number of state cells over which selection is performed, rising from the nominal test level with one cell to near certainty with a few dozen, while overlap has a smaller effect. We then propose an audit that assigns logs to four tiers of decision support, separately for fixed and contextual policies, and returns an explicit stopping rationale. On a randomised e-commerce log, the audit endorses a fixed recommendation policy, certifying gains equal to 30 per cent of the theoretical ceiling; the remaining gap reflects bound looseness under an 80-action design. Two public logistics logs fail at different checkpoints: one because the target is an accounting identity of the action, and another because, with 27 clusters, estimated coupling stays below the design's detectability threshold even though a conservative offline learner certifies an improvement in 29 of 30 seeds.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-270"></a>

### 270. [A Transformer-Derived Iterative Preconditioner](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22941v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22941

**作者：** Patrick Lutz、Themistoklis Haris、Aditya Gangrade、Venkatesh Saligrama

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22941v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22941v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文观察训练于上下文线性方程组求解的 softmax 注意力 Transformer，从追踪 XX^⊤ 中提取出一种迭代预条件原语，并将其发展为无需学习组件的 RAPID 预条件器，附收敛性证明与条件数、共轭梯度迭代数上的验证实验。

**相关性简评：** 其“从内部计算轨迹反推可证明算法”的思路，可能为 LVR 中如何探查 latent 状态所编码的迭代更新与计算结构、以及区分内容与位置/深度贡献提供可核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Transformer-Derived Iterative Preconditioner

**abstract/A1：** Transformers trained on in-context prediction problems can learn known numerical algorithms, e.g., gradient descent. We go further, showing that their internal computations can reveal new algorithms with provable guarantees. A softmax-attention transformer trained to solve linear systems $Xw=y$ implicitly improves matrix conditioning. Tracing $XX^\\top$ reveals an iterative preconditioning primitive, which we develop into RAPID, a standalone algorithm requiring no learned components. RAPID is an anytime preconditioner for symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices, using randomized mixing and sparse local corrections to improve conditioning. Proof-of-concept experiments on synthetic and real-world problems consistently improve conditioning and reduce conjugate gradient iterations needed to reach a target accuracy. For a Haar-mixing idealization, we prove convergence to any fixed target condition number regardless of spectral distribution, with only logarithmic dependence on initial conditioning. Together, these results show how a transformer can discover a broadly applicable numerical algorithm.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-271"></a>

### 271. [DDSL: Iterative Self-Labeling for Financial Market States](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22958v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22958

**作者：** Xianbin Gu、Qingyue Zhu、Yuhong Geng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22958v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22958v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DDSL，用价格与语法约束的 Viterbi 标注器与 Transformer 分类器通过模型反馈迭代互训，构造同时编码当前与未来趋势的九状态市场表示，并考察其可学习性、标签熵与跨架构迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其“离散隐状态构造＋反馈细化＋可学习性评估”可能为 LVR 中潜在状态能否从预测时可观测信息恢复、监督信号是否自洽且不塌缩提供类比线索，但领域较远，需要核查其状态语义与视觉推理状态的对应程度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DDSL: Iterative Self-Labeling for Financial Market States

**abstract/A1：** Financial market states lack a unique ground truth and must therefore be constructed from price data. Conventional labeling methods typically define states independently of the downstream classifier, leaving unresolved whether the resulting labels can be recovered from information available at prediction time. We propose DDSL, an iterative self-labeling framework that couples a price- and grammar-guided Viterbi labeler with a Transformer classifier through model feedback. DDSL constructs a nine-state representation that jointly encodes current and future market trends while retaining structured price emissions and temporal constraints throughout refinement. Experiments on SSE50, CSI300, CSI500, and NASDAQ100 show that DDSL increases held-out joint-state macro F1 by 78%-96% over single-pass dynamic-programming labeling. An independent GRU classifier that never participates in the labeling loop reproduces the same trend, while label entropy and class coverage remain stable, indicating that the gain transfers across architectures and is not caused by label collapse. We further show that learnability is controlled by both iteration and the model-feedback weight $β\_{\\mathrm{model}}$. Iterative gains diminish as successive label systems converge, whereas stronger feedback increasingly widens the performance gap between the feedback Transformer and the independent GRU. Increased learnability is accompanied by declining segment-level price-feature separation after an initial improvement. Finally, a non-anticipative event study provides partial external validation: refinement strengthens a conditional BEAR$\\rightarrow$BULL reversal signal on the three A-share datasets, while the corresponding effect has the opposite sign on NASDAQ100. These results position DDSL as a structured framework for constructing market-state labels whose learnability can be explicitly controlled and evaluated.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Conference version published in ICAIF 2026. Code available at https://github.com/hogwild/DDSL

</details>

<a id="paper-272"></a>

### 272. [Hierarchical Diffusion Transformer for End-to-End Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22962

**作者：** Xiaoyu Wu、Yifei Wang、Weihang Guo、Chen Wei、Lydia E Kavraki、Niloofar Mireshghallah、Steven Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22962v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Hierarchical Diffusion Transformer（HiT），在像素空间扩散主干中按可学习的成员关系逐步把相关 patch token 归并为更粗粒度的组 token，用压缩预算控制平均压缩率，并证明分组能自发对应物体、部件及标题词与图像区域的对应。

**相关性简评：** 其无标注、随图像与扩散步自适应的 token 分组与压缩预算机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉状态的层级抽象、压缩率与细节保真之间的权衡提供可核查的表示构造思路，但论文本身面向图像生成，未涉及推理状态或答案使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hierarchical Diffusion Transformer for End-to-End Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Pixel-space diffusion learns visual representations and image generation jointly in a single end-to-end training stage, without a separately pretrained visual encoder. However, plain pixel-space Transformers still trail leading two-stage latent diffusion models in generation quality and training efficiency. We hypothesize that part of this gap arises from retaining one token per patch throughout the backbone, which does not explicitly encourage a visual hierarchy that summarizes related content at progressively coarser levels. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion Transformers (HiT), which progressively group related tokens and represent each group with a single token. The main Transformer bottleneck processes these compact group tokens, and ungrouping restores features to their original image positions. A compression budget regulates the average compression ratio across the batch, allowing group memberships and counts to vary across images and diffusion timesteps during both training and generation. The model exhibits emergent semantic structure, learning to group coherent objects and object parts without segmentation labels. In text-to-image generation, HiT further learns to group caption words with image patches depicting the same concepts, without region annotations. HiT achieves 1.48 FID on ImageNet $256\\times256$ and reaches comparable generation quality to JiT using approximately 40\\% of its cumulative training FLOPs. For text-to-image generation, HiT exceeds UniLiP's average score across five benchmarks with roughly $6\\times$ fewer denoiser parameters and $3\\times$ fewer forward FLOPs. These results suggest that learning a visual hierarchy within the generator breaks down the barrier to stronger image generation without external visual pretraining.

**comment/C1：** Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-273"></a>

### 273. [High-Dimensional Black-Box Optimization via Guidance Sampling of Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22965v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22965

**作者：** Xiaoyan Hu、Rohit Sonker、Jalal Butt、Jiayu Chen、Egemen Kolemen、Jeff Schneider

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22965v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22965v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BReaD：用高斯过程作为黑盒目标的可微代理，主动查询生成样本的标签，并以 UCB 作为引导信号驱动扩散采样，在无标注数据与无奖励梯度条件下寻找高奖励区域，并给出生成分布与目标分布的 TV 距离分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「以可查询代理信号引导生成轨迹、用 UCB 平衡探索与利用」的设计，可能可供核查 LVR 中何时主动获取视觉证据（LOOK）与如何分配采样步数的决策机制，但论文本身面向黑盒优化与图像生成，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** High-Dimensional Black-Box Optimization via Guidance Sampling of Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Many real-world optimization problems involve optimizing black-box objectives for which only a limited number of evaluations are available. Bayesian optimization (BO) is a powerful framework for such problems, but its effectiveness often deteriorates in high-dimensional spaces. Recent work leverages diffusion models for black-box optimization, often through pre-training a reward-conditioned score network to steer generation towards high-reward samples. Alternatively, gradient-based guidance methods assume access to a differentiable reward function from which guidance gradients are queried. However, these approaches either require large amounts of labeled data for offline training or access to exact reward gradients, limiting their applicability in black-box settings. We propose BReaD (Black-box Reward-guided Sampling of Diffusion Model), a guidance sampling framework for optimizing black-box rewards without assuming access to a labeled dataset or reward gradients. The proposed method uses a Gaussian process (GP) as a differentiable surrogate for the unknown objective and actively queries labels for the generated samples. We provide a theoretical analysis of the total-variation distance between the generated distribution and the target distribution. Using Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) as guidance signals, our method balances exploration and exploitation during generation and enables the discovery of high-reward regions in the data space. Numerical experiments on scientific discovery and image generation tasks demonstrate that our framework can effectively optimize black-box rewards in low-data scenarios.

</details>

<a id="paper-274"></a>

### 274. [Rescaling OLS-Merged Adapters To Improve Personalized Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22969v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22969

**作者：** Leander Girrbach、Niklas Schweiger、Zeynep Akata

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22969v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22969v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文分析 OLS 类适配器合并（RegMean、Mix-of-Show、Subspace Routing）在多任务激活分布重叠时系统性削弱任务信号的问题，给出量化该收缩的分解，并提出对合并更新重缩放以恢复信号强度，在多达 30 个适配器的多主体个性化及风格迁移、概念擦除等任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 这是权重/适配器合并层面的信号收缩分析，与 LVR 的推理期状态无关，但其「分布重叠导致任务信号被稀释并可定量分解」的视角，或可作为核查 latent 表示中视觉信息坍缩与多源状态融合时信号衰减的类比工具。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rescaling OLS-Merged Adapters To Improve Personalized Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Merging multiple adapters into a single image generation model reduces deployment costs while preserving diverse task capabilities or personalization modes. State-of-the-art methods such as RegMean, Mix-of-Show, and Subspace Routing fit the outputs of the merged model to those of the individual adapters using ordinary least squares (OLS). We show that this reconstruction objective systematically shrinks task-specific signals whenever the activation distributions of different tasks overlap, producing a merged adapter that is too weak for downstream use. We derive a decomposition that quantifies this shrinkage and propose rescaling the merged update to restore signal strength. On multi-subject personalization with up to 30 merged adapters, rescaling substantially improves subject fidelity over unscaled baselines. Our approach also improves results on style transfer, concept erasure, image classification, and LLM-based text classification.

</details>

<a id="paper-275"></a>

### 275. [VITAL: A Framework for Validating Agentic AI in Pharmaceutical Drug Development and Clinical Trial Operations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22973v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22973

**作者：** Sandeep Burugupalli、Pedro Matos-Filipe、Andrea Hita Ardiaca、Adam Taylor、Alex Grigorenko、Natalia Jakubiak、Xin Yu、Raghav Mani、Sherry Mangla、Alvi Lai、Rachel C. Mercado、Pratik Shah、Raja Shankar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22973v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22973v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 VITAL 框架，以预期用途、可追溯性、自主性与生命周期证据为核心，为药物研发与临床试验运营中的 agentic AI 设计风险分级的行为验证与证据要求，并在多个制药场景中落地。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据是否足以支持结论」「行为可审计重建」的验证思路可能启发 LVR 中 latent 证据充分性与推理链可追溯性的评价目标，但论文不涉及视觉状态或生成机制，联系偏概念层面，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VITAL: A Framework for Validating Agentic AI in Pharmaceutical Drug Development and Clinical Trial Operations

**abstract/A1：** Current assurance approaches do not establish whether evidence is sufficient to validate, monitor and govern agentic artificial intelligence (AI) within a pharmaceutical context of use. Plausible outputs may conceal failures in retrieval, reasoning, orchestration or human-control points, where reference standards are unavailable. We developed and operationalised VITAL (Validation by Intended use, Traceability, Autonomy and Lifecycle evidence), a validation framework for agents in clinical development, through landscape analysis, regulatory and standards mapping, expert interviews and multi-stakeholder refinement. VITAL integrates context of use, controls, behavioural validation and lifecycle evidence within a risk-adjusted framework. We applied VITAL across epidemiology and treatment-landscape analysis, site selection, enrolment rescue and trial-design trade-off support to translate autonomy, reversibility, transparency and patient impact into proportionate validation activities, evidence requirements and auditable reconstruction of system behaviour. VITAL may inform evidence requirements and lifecycle accountability for higher-risk pharmaceutical uses; evaluation should assess reproducibility across assessors, organisations and system configurations.

</details>

<a id="paper-276"></a>

### 276. [The Rhythm of Generated Video: Latent Chunking Leaves Periodic Traces in Compressed Bitstreams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22996v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22996

**作者：** Chen Ye Gan、Bryan Zhen Zhang、Gene Wen、Yuxing han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22996v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22996v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出因果视频自编码器的时间步幅使高不确定内容集中于 latent chunk 边界，导致压缩码流出现周期性比特分配脉冲（CogVideoX/Wan2.2 为四帧、LTX-Video 为八帧），并据此构建免训练的生成视频检测与生成器识别统计量。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理主线较远，但其「latent 分段边界承载不成比例的信息量且可从外部信号测出」的观察，可能为核查 latent 状态的分段粒度、边界位置对信息与计算分配的影响提供线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Rhythm of Generated Video: Latent Chunking Leaves Periodic Traces in Compressed Bitstreams

**abstract/A1：** Most AI-generated video detectors analyze decoded pixels and overlook how videos are produced. We show that causal video autoencoders leave an architecture-specific rhythm in compressed bitstreams: their temporal stride concentrates unpredictable content at latent-chunk boundaries, so rate-distortion optimized encoders allocate bits periodically. Without decoding pixels, we observe four-frame pulses in CogVideoX and Wan2.2 and eight-frame pulses in LTX-Video, which are absent from content-matched camera footage. Passing camera footage through a generator's autoencoder alone induces the pulse, supporting its proposed origin. A training-free peak-ratio statistic separates generated from camera video (AUC 0.84-0.94) and persists across tested quantization settings and H.264/H.265 encoding. Frame-size traces also identify four commercial generators, with 89.2%, 84.1%, and 83.0% accuracy under H.264, H.265, and AV1, beating the pixel-based UnivFD baseline (72.7-73.6%). Our results directly link generative architecture to measurable coding demand.

**comment/C1：** Manuscript, 9 pages, Computer vision, AI-generated video detection, compressed-domain analysis, video coding,

</details>

<a id="paper-277"></a>

### 277. [When a Larger Guidance Scale Repairs Text-to-Video Failures and When It Does Not](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23025v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23025

**作者：** Taewon Kang、Matthias Zwicker

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23025v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23025v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文是测量研究：在 Mochi 文生视频模型上考察提高 classifier-free guidance scale 能否修复三类提示遵循失败（罕见组合、否定、晚期事件），并提出从去噪前四分之一读取的「demand」分数，在视频生成前预测该修复是否有效，进而给出决策规则。

**相关性简评：** 用廉价的早期去噪内部响应预测「加大引导计算是否有收益」，并给出生成前选择干预方式的决策规则，可能可供核查其思路能否迁移到 LVR 中预测 THINK 收益与 STOP 决策；但研究对象为视频生成，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When a Larger Guidance Scale Repairs Text-to-Video Failures and When It Does Not

**abstract/A1：** When a text-to-video diffusion model ignores part of a prompt, the usual first fix is to raise the classifier-free guidance scale. This paper is a measurement study of when that fix works. On Mochi, the same increase repairs failures on rare compositions ("a snowy beach") beyond what generating again does, does not repair negations ("a highway with no vehicles"), where it fixes fewer failures than generating again does, and changes nothing for events that must happen late in a clip ("a sandcastle that a wave washes flat"). Which of these happens can be known in advance, from the prompt and one inexpensive score. The score, which we call demand, measures how strongly the denoiser reacts to the part of the prompt that the failed video leaves out, and it is read from the first quarter of denoising, before any video exists. Our main finding is a condition: raising the scale is effective only when the prompt contains no negation and no event tied to a specific time, and when demand is sufficiently high, with recovery clearly higher above a fitted threshold than below it. Published corrections for the three failure types, used as fixed probes, behave as this account predicts. A short decision rule follows: before generating again, a user can tell whether the most common fix is worth trying, and what to use when it is not.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-278"></a>

### 278. [Storing the Gemma 4 K=V Cache Once: Exact Global-KV Memory Savings in a Paged Serving Engine with Unchanged Attention Kernels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23035v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23035

**作者：** Kushagra Bainsla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23035v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23035v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文利用 Gemma 4 全局注意力层中 value 是归一化 key 投影、key 是该 value 经固定权重缩放的结构冗余，在 vLLM 中改存一行 640 值并通过两个重叠的 strided 视图复用未改动的 paged attention kernel，报告了精确等价的输出、decode 阶段 KV 内存下降 1.34–1.40 倍及吞吐提升。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，也未生成 latent，但其对注意力状态存储冗余的精确利用与 decode 内存/吞吐成本建模，可能可供核查 LVR 长 latent 序列下「实际成本」核算与状态存储开销的参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Storing the Gemma 4 K=V Cache Once: Exact Global-KV Memory Savings in a Paged Serving Engine with Unchanged Attention Kernels

**abstract/A1：** In the global attention layers of Gemma 4 31B, the value is the normalized key projection, and the key is the value scaled by a fixed weight, with rotary position encoding on one quarter of its dimensions. The Gemma 4 report states that this design reduces the global KV cache by up to 37.5%, but the open serving engines that we checked still store a full key and a full value: 1,024 values per KV head and token. We store one 640-value row instead, exact in real arithmetic, and an unchanged paged attention kernel reads it through two overlapping strided views as its key cache and its value cache. In a vLLM prototype with the 16-bit 31B model, the outputs are bit-identical to an emulation of the same arithmetic on the stock cache, 2 of 432 final answers differ from the stock engine (within its run-to-run noise), and the steady decode KV memory per request falls by a factor of 1.34 to 1.40 at 32K to 64K tokens. On a prefill/decode pair of two A100 GPUs with NIXL transfer and CPU offload, a decode engine with a full 15 GiB pool ran 1.38 times the stock decode tokens per step for 16K-token prompts (repeated to 0.06%) and 1.24 times for 8K-token prompts; a model of the vLLM block pool predicted both ratios to within 1% (the engine was 0.7% to 0.9% higher) and matched the change between them. The eager decode throughput rose by 1.30 and 1.11 times, and on this path the compact engine gave the same output tokens as the emulation for 8 prompts at batch 1. The run-time prototype makes an eager step 4% to 9% slower at equal batch, and the method does not yet stack with an 8-bit KV cache at the default scale.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-279"></a>

### 279. [Variance Restoration over Covariance Rank: Memory-Budgeted Analytic Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23040v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23040

**作者：** Elena Voss、Pixel Nomand、Marcus Hale、Sofia Reyes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23040v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23040v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究固定持久内存预算下解析持续学习的统计量压缩：观察到宽随机特征码的自协方差中非对角项主导内存，遂提出精确保留对角方差与类统计、用流式 rank-q 因子近似跨坐标交互，并以 Woodbury 恒等式求解。

**相关性简评：** 「精确保留对角、低秩近似非对角」的预算化状态压缩思路可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态或视觉证据缓存的显存分配，但原文仅面向冻结 ViT 的分类读出，与推理期状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Variance Restoration over Covariance Rank: Memory-Budgeted Analytic Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Analytic continual learning replaces iterative gradient updates with closed-form ridge regression over frozen pre-trained representations, summarizing all past sessions in two running sufficient statistics: the feature autocorrelation matrix $Σ\_t$ and the class cross-correlation matrix $Γ\_t$. Lifting backbone features into a wide random code of dimension $M \\gg D$ substantially improves linear separability across tasks, but forces $Σ\_t$ to grow quadratically as $\\mathcal{O}(M^2)$. Under a fixed persistent memory envelope $\\Bcal$, a learner faces a fundamental structural trade-off: store exact pairwise co-activations inside a narrower feature code, or expand to a wide code while compressing cross-coordinate interactions. We observe that the coordinate self-variances $v\_t = \\diag(Σ\_t)$ and class statistics $Γ\_t$ grow only linearly with $M$, whereas off-diagonal interactions dominate memory. We introduce \\method (\\textbf{Co}ordinate-\\textbf{Re}stored \\textbf{A}nalytic \\textbf{C}ontinual \\textbf{L}earning), which retains $(v\_t, Γ\_t)$ exactly, maintains off-diagonal co-activations through a streaming rank-$q$ factor $F\_t$, restores exact diagonal variances via $\\Sighat\_t = F\_t^\\top F\_t + \\Diag(v\_t - \\diag(F\_t^\\top F\_t))$, and solves the full $M\\times C$ normal equations via the Woodbury identity. Across five class-incremental benchmarks on frozen ViT-B/16, \\method matches dense full-covariance readouts within $0.09$ points while cutting persistent state by $90.7\\%$ ($35.9$ vs.\\ $385.3$ MiB). Under equal memory envelopes of $16$--$80$ MiB, prioritizing expansion width $M$ over covariance rank $q$ outperforms exact narrow readouts in $17$ of $18$ configurations by up to $+2.19$ points.

</details>

<a id="paper-280"></a>

### 280. [Pareto-Informed Smart "Predict, then Optimize" for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23076v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23076

**作者：** Aabhash Dhakal、Tim Lachner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23076v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23076v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向多目标组合优化，提出将单目标 SPO+ 扩展为 Pareto 感知的决策聚焦学习方法：依据真实 Pareto 前沿构造与采样偏好向量一致的目标决策，并以对该目标的 regret 作为损失训练预测模型，在多目标最短路与背包问题上优于 MSE 两阶段基线和标量化 SPO+ 方法。

**相关性简评：** 其「以决策 regret 而非预测精度监督」及按偏好采样目标决策的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的贡献分配、监督信号设计与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 多目标预算权衡提供可核查的表述借鉴，但与原论文的视觉推理问题无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pareto-Informed Smart "Predict, then Optimize" for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Problems

**abstract/A1：** Many real-world planning applications, from logistics, routing to resource allocation, give rise to optimization problems that need to balance multiple, conflicting objectives. While machine learning is often used to predict the unknown parameters or costs of these problems, standard models trained on prediction accuracy can lead to suboptimal plans. Decision-Focused Learning (DFL) addresses this by integrating the downstream optimization task into the training objective. However, existing DFL methods for multi-objective problems rely on simple scalarization, which fails to capture the rich trade-offs of the Pareto Frontier. To address this gap, we propose a Pareto-aware extension to single-objective Smart "Predict, then Optimize" (SPO+) for multi-objective combinatorial optimization problems. Our loss function leverages the true Pareto Frontier, forming a target decision that aligns with a sampled preference vector, and minimizes regret against this target. Experiments on multi-objective shortest path and multi-objective knapsack problems show that our approach outperforms traditional two-stage Mean Squared Error (MSE) baselines. It also surpasses scalarization-based SPO+ approaches.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 5 figures, 1 table. Accepted after peer review at the Multi-Objective Decision Making (MODeM) Workshop at ECAI 2025

</details>

<a id="paper-281"></a>

### 281. [Colombo: A Frontier-to-Frontier Autoresearch System for Oracle-Based Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23080v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23080

**作者：** Haihan Zhang、Wendao Wu、Chenheng Zhang、Chunyuan Zheng、Yanyi Li、Cong Fang、Haoxuan Li、Zhouchen Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23080v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23080v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Colombo 是一个面向 oracle-based optimization 的自动科研系统，用自组织研究程序、对抗式算法与困难实例协同上升，并借助 Lean 形式化认证维护复杂度前沿图谱，已在五十多个设置上给出最优或近最优速率。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息接口下可达保证与不可避免下界」的分析框架，可能为刻画 latent 状态的信息容量与必要计算成本提供概念参照；但论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Colombo: A Frontier-to-Frontier Autoresearch System for Oracle-Based Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Science advances from frontier to frontier as discoveries reshape what is known and create the basis for further discovery. We present Colombo, a self-organizing AI researcher that drives this renewal, first instantiated in oracle-based optimization. Here, the central question is how efficiently a problem class can be solved through a prescribed information interface and what costs no admissible algorithm can avoid. The gap between achievable guarantees and unavoidable limits defines the frontier recorded in its Complexity Atlas, a living map of frontier settings and the current state of knowledge. Self-Organization turns unresolved gaps into adaptive research programmes, coordinating conjecture formation, mathematical investigation, and replanning as evidence accumulates. Adversarial Co-Ascent develops algorithms and hard families through reciprocal challenge, exposing mechanisms of information use and concealment that guide stronger methods and proofs of unavoidable costs. Independent review and Lean-based formal certification check mathematical claims, while the Long-Term Memory carries supported findings and research experience across settings. To date, Colombo has established optimal or near-optimal rates for more than fifty settings in the Atlas. Colombo thus takes a concrete step toward Autopoietic Science: a scientific process whose validated advances regenerate the conditions of its own progress, continually reshaping how inquiry is organized and what it can discover.

</details>

<a id="paper-282"></a>

### 282. [Where Should Physics Enter? Information, Identifiability and Calibration in Probabilistic Sea Surface Temperature Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23129v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23129

**作者：** Parmitha Vangapandu、Jonnakuti Pavan Kumar、Sathwik Narkedimilli、MSVPJ Sathvik、T. M. Balakrishnan Nair、Santanu Bhattacharya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23129v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23129v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以孟加拉湾海表温度概率预报为场景，检验平流-扩散物理先验应注入异方差ConvLSTM的哪个环节，并对比损失约束、显式输运步与电流作为输入通道等设计在真实与合成数据上的增益。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息供给」与「机制注入」的分离检验、学习型隐变量（速度场）的可辨识性诊断以及逐lead校准分析，可能与LVR中latent内容是否被真正使用、贡献分配和按推理步数的校准评价形成概念类比，可供核查迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Should Physics Enter? Information, Identifiability and Calibration in Probabilistic Sea Surface Temperature Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Advection-diffusion priors are a common way to make neural sea surface temperature (SST) forecasts physics-informed. We test where such a prior must enter a heteroscedastic ConvLSTM to help, using daily NOAA OISST v2.1 for the Bay of Bengal (1982-2024) and a synthetic benchmark on the same grid with known currents of controlled strength. (i) On real data, a soft advection-diffusion residual with a learned velocity gives no reliable benefit (lead-15 CRPS -0.007 degrees C, 95% interval \[-0.022, +0.008\]); a plain temporal-smoothness penalty matches it, and equivalence within 3% is not established either. (ii) Learned velocities do not recover the currents, whether placed in the loss, in an explicit transport step, or in a transport step without neural correction: they correlate with the reference no better than a shuffled-date null, even on synthetic data where knowing the currents would improve day-15 forecasts by 16-23%. (iii) Supplied currents help on synthetic data, but mostly as information: giving them to the network as input channels, without transport, recovers 92% of the transport design's lead-5 gain, and a frozen-current numerical forecast does as well as either. (iv) With GLORYS12 reanalysis currents on real data, the transport design is 6% and 4% worse at leads 1 and 5; reanalysis advection explains essentially none of the daily OISST change (correlation 0.02), and frozen-current transport is worse than persistence. (v) The defect that matters is lead-dependent miscalibration shared by every model. One variance multiplier per lead, fitted on validation data, brings 90% coverage to 0.85-0.91 at all leads and lowers day-1 CRPS by 13-21%; fine-tuning on 15-day rollouts fixes long-lead coverage but costs short-lead skill. This version withdraws an earlier claim of an 81% CRPS reduction that came from an uncontrolled comparison.

</details>

<a id="paper-283"></a>

### 283. [TextSphere: Efficient Text-to-Image Generation via Shared Hyperspherical Alignment and Uniformity](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23155v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23155

**作者：** Yixia Zhang、Girish A. Koushik、Xilu Wang、Wenwu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23155v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23155v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TextSphere，将 Sphere Encoder 扩展到自由文本条件的图像生成：用 AdaLN 注入预训练 CLIP 文本嵌入并保留球面采样，提出 SSAU 目标在超球面上同时促进跨模态对齐与表示均匀性，少量采样步数即降低 gFID，并用 PLS-SVD 分析模态间隙。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身属图像生成，与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其超球面对齐/均匀性目标与模态间隙度量，可能可供核查 latent 状态是否保留视觉内容及跨模态表示几何的分析线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TextSphere: Efficient Text-to-Image Generation via Shared Hyperspherical Alignment and Uniformity

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in text-to-image generation have substantially improved image quality, particularly through diffusion-based methods that require multiple inference steps. In contrast, Sphere Encoder offers an efficient generative framework capable of producing images with fewer forward steps. However, extending it to free-form text is nontrivial, because its class-conditioned design lacks cross-modal alignment. To address this challenge, we propose TextSphere, an efficient text-to-image generative framework that extends Sphere Encoder with language conditioning and cross-modal alignment. We incorporate pretrained CLIP text embeddings through adaptive layer normalization (AdaLN) to guide image generation while retaining the original spherical sampling mechanism. To bridge the cross-modal representation gap, we introduce a Spherical Semantic Alignment and Uniformity (SSAU) objective that jointly promotes semantic alignment and representation uniformity on the hypersphere. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, MS-COCO-2014, and Flickr30k demonstrate that TextSphere consistently reduces generation FID (gFID) over the text-conditioned baseline while achieving reconstruction FID (rFID) below 1.0. With only 4 and 6 sampling steps, TextSphere achieves lower gFID than the compared diffusion- and GAN-based models trained from scratch on CUB-200-2011 and MS-COCO-2014, respectively. We further observe that improved reconstruction fidelity does not necessarily yield better generation quality. In addition, we use Partial Least Squares Singular Value Decomposition (PLS-SVD) to analyze cross-modal alignment and modality gap, offering insight into the effectiveness of our approach.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-284"></a>

### 284. [Déjà View: A Shared Working-Memory Model for Visual Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23177v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23177

**作者：** Mranmay Shetty、Gabriel Kreiman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23177v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23177v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个图像工作记忆模型，将动态神经活动与特征间的临时关联结合，用同一组权重在五个视觉任务上联合训练，并由显式线索指示所需反应，进而考察延迟、干扰与图像历史如何影响记忆表现。

**相关性简评：** 其共享工作记忆与线索驱动的多任务读出，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节能否在 latent 状态中稳定保持、干扰与历史造成误报、以及按任务需求读取内部状态提供可核查的建模与评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Déjà View: A Shared Working-Memory Model for Visual Tasks

**abstract/A1：** A working-memory model must remember stimuli and use its memory flexibly to perform different tasks. Here we introduce a model of working memory for images that combines dynamic activity with temporary associations between image features. We train the model on five tasks, including remembering a sample while other images are presented, categorizing distractor images while remembering the sample, and determining whether an image is new or has been presented before. The same trained model performs all tasks, guided by explicit cues about which response is required. We use this flexible memory model to ask how delay, distraction, and image history affect performance. Delays have little effect on sample-memory performance for the delays we tested, while seeing more images leads to more false alarms even when the target image appeared equally recently in all cases. On the sample-memory-plus-categorization task, the probability of correct responses on all questions decreases as more questions are asked, despite robust sample-memory performance. Finally, the model maintains stable performance across 40 brief trials of each task under the tested conditions. Together, these results demonstrate how a single working-memory system can be flexibly used to perform different tasks, and how its errors depend on both previous images and task demands.

**comment/C1：** 6 figures; includes appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-285"></a>

### 285. [Learned versus Kinematic Trajectory Prediction under Reduced Observation Frequency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23198v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23198

**作者：** Nukhbala Gadzhiev、Alexander Morochko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23198v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23198v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 Argoverse 2 上将 5 s 历史从 10 Hz 降采样至 5/2/1 Hz，比较按频率训练的 LSTM 预测器与匀速、匀加速模型，并测试用插值复用 10 Hz 模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「观测变稀疏后学习到的循环状态是否仍胜过简单运动学先验」可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据采样稀疏化与插值补偿对 latent 状态贡献的影响，但论文属轨迹预测，仅构成概念类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learned versus Kinematic Trajectory Prediction under Reduced Observation Frequency

**abstract/A1：** Trajectory prediction models for automated driving are usually trained on data with a fixed sampling frequency, while positions received via vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication arrive at 1-10 Hz. We study whether a learned predictor that uses only the observation history keeps its advantage over constant velocity (CV) and constant acceleration (CA) models when the history frequency drops. On the Argoverse 2 motion forecasting dataset, the 5 s history of the focal agent was downsampled from 10 Hz to 5, 2, and 1 Hz, and the 6 s future was predicted without maps or neighboring agents. An LSTM-based recurrent neural network (RNN) trained for each frequency was compared with the same network trained at 10 Hz and applied to sparse histories after linear interpolation. Hypotheses and thresholds were fixed before training, the main RNNs were trained with ten seeds each, and results on 24,984 test scenarios have bootstrap confidence intervals over scenarios and seeds. The average displacement error of the frequency-specific RNN grew by 3.1% at 5 Hz, 10.1% at 2 Hz, and 20.3% at 1 Hz. Contrary to our hypothesis, its advantage over the better kinematic model did not vanish on straight motion and was 24-30% on both straight motion and turns at every frequency, while for pedestrians CV was more accurate. The 10 Hz model with interpolation was at most 1.3% less accurate than the frequency-specific model down to 2 Hz but 11.5% less accurate at 1 Hz. Each hypothesis was only partly supported.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 1 figure, 2 tables. Code and per-scenario results: https://github.com/nleaxy/learned-vs-kinematic-trajectory-prediction

</details>

### 286. [Layerwise Learning-Rate Scaling in Vision Networks: Effective-Rate Identification, Power-Mean Aggregation, and Budget Dependence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21696v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21696

**作者：** Chandini Vysyaraju、Raghuvir Duvvuri、Radu Timofte、Dmitry Ignatov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21696v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21696v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究从零训练的视觉网络中层间学习率缩放，用加权幂均值刻画有效学习率并预测低预算下的精度排序，只涉及优化超参筛选，与 latent 视觉推理的状态表示、证据使用、生成或停止决策无实质联系，也无具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Layerwise Learning-Rate Scaling in Vision Networks: Effective-Rate Identification, Power-Mean Aggregation, and Budget Dependence

**abstract/A1：** Layerwise learning-rate scaling assigns different learning rates to different parts of a network. Although established in fine-tuning pretrained models, its effects in vision networks trained from scratch are commonly assessed by training each candidate recipe. We show that, in this setting, the rate-related component of a layerwise strategy is well summarized by an effective learning rate computable directly from its multiplier profile. Across matched-control comparisons, changes in test accuracy closely track changes in training loss (r = -0.996), which supports an optimization-progress explanation of the short-budget rate effect. Increasing the untuned base learning rate tenfold shifts the rate-matched contrast upward in all six tested architectures (p = 0.016) and increases the number of architectures in which a published discriminative schedule, excluded from model fitting, outperforms the uniform control from two to five of six (p = 0.031). Within the evaluated strategy space, a parameter-weighted power mean with exponent 0.35 (95% confidence interval \[0.20, 0.50\]) accounts for 42.6% of within-architecture variance, compared with 36.4% for the arithmetic mean. Ranking candidates by this scalar without training improves the selected candidate's mean percentile rank from the 26th to the 10th and reduces its mean accuracy shortfall relative to the best candidate from 3.70 to 1.12 points. The rate-related effect decays as the training budget increases; relative to tuned baselines, the overall strategy effect falls within the prespecified practical-equivalence margin in two of three architectures. A separate grouping-related contrast can persist at long budgets, although its direction and magnitude depend on the architecture.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures. Supplementary material (15 pages) included as an ancillary file. Data and analysis scripts: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24279433

</details>

<a id="paper-287"></a>

### 287. [Hybrid Quantum Selection of Learning Rates in Language-Model Training: Termination Criteria and an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21701v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21701

**作者：** Wei-Che Hung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21701v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21701v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在混合经典-量子框架下研究语言模型微调中层组学习率联合配置的选择，用 Dürr-Høyer/BBHT 最小查找与置信度停止策略，并提出基于交互效应的调度打分 oracle。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是训练期超参数（学习率调度）搜索，未涉及视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态生成或答案对证据的使用；其「停止准则」指量子搜索预算，而非推理期的继续/终止决策，与 LVR 主线无实质线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hybrid Quantum Selection of Learning Rates in Language-Model Training: Termination Criteria and an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle

**abstract/A1：** Learning-rate choices strongly affect language-model fine-tuning, but the best joint profile across layer groups is generally unknown. This paper studies quantum minimum finding for layer-group learning-rate selection within a hybrid classical-quantum training framework. It combines Durr-Hoyer minimum finding with BBHT search and compares fixed-budget and confidence-based stopping policies using a lookup oracle built from measured profile scores at fixed training checkpoints. The paper then introduces an Interaction-Guided Schedule Oracle that scores and marks joint schedules in superposition without a precomputed score table. Classical one-step loss measurements supply the main, pairwise and three-way interaction effects between layer groups. The circuit adds the effects for each schedule, compares the resulting score with the current baseline score, and marks improving schedules for amplitude amplification. Measured candidates are checked classically before acceptance. Uniform-rate training supplies the initial baseline; using schedules from classical adaptive methods as baselines for joint refinement is left for future work. In closed-loop fine-tuning of a 1.5-billion-parameter language model, each selected schedule drives the next 200 classical training steps. Benchmark accuracies lie within the range observed across five uniform-rate seeds. The results give a working example of how quantum superposition can support adaptive learning-rate selection during training; the remaining quantum limitations are discussed in the paper.

**comment/C1：** 10 figures, 11 tables (including appendices)

</details>

**排除原因：** 这是动物行为标注数据集，不涉及视觉推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策；自动行为识别仅为逐帧判别任务，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Video recordings and play behaviour annotations of group-housed dairy calves on two Dutch farms

**abstract/A1：** Play is used as an indicator of positive welfare in dairy calves, but openly available video of group-housed calves with individual behaviour annotations is scarce. We describe video from four camera deployments of about one week each in group pens of dairy calves on two farms in the Netherlands: a research and teaching dairy farm (2024 and 2025) and a commercial dairy farm (2024). The recordings comprise 1,173 video files spanning 651 h. For eight observation days, trained observers coded calf behaviour continuously in Noldus The Observer XT with an 18-item ethogram of play and other behaviours, covering 104 h of video, 90 h of which have every calf in the pen coded. The release also contains 1.1 million decoded frames from seven annotated sessions, a per-frame timestamp read by optical character recognition from the camera clock burned into each frame, per-calf segmentation tracks and per-calf frame-level play labels. The data support studies of calf behaviour and welfare and the development and evaluation of automated behaviour recognition.

</details>

### 289. [Margin Dynamics for Large Language Model Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21766v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21766

**作者：** Xingzi Xu、Mehmet Saygin Seyfioglu、Karim Bouyarmane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21766v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21766v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在概率单纯形上建立偏好对齐方法的向量场模型，将偏好/非偏好对的 margin 归约为一维 ODE，并据此分析 DPO、IPO 与 SimPO 的收敛行为与标签噪声影响。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是纯文本偏好对齐的损失动态与 margin 收敛，不涉及视觉输入、latent 推理状态或证据获取与停止决策，其 margin ODE 与 LVR 的轨迹监督和贡献分配之间也缺乏具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Margin Dynamics for Large Language Model Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Practitioners choose among LLM alignment methods largely by trial and error. We provide a theoretical foundation: each pairwise method defines a vector field on the probability simplex that governs how the model redistributes mass between preferred and dispreferred completions. Projecting onto a single preferred/dispreferred pair reduces this to a one-dimensional margin ODE with a closed-form solution. These solutions reveal that DPO margins grow without bound (logarithmically as $O(\\log t)$ in the gradient-flow limit) while IPO margins converge to a finite $1/(2τ)$; that label noise induces a qualitative phase transition in DPO (unbounded $\\to$ bounded) but affects IPO only quantitatively; and that gradient concentration under asymmetric weighting governs trainability at scale. We confirm all three predictions across five model families spanning four benchmarks. The closed-form solutions also enable principled method design: we prove that adding any label smoothing $\\varepsilon &gt; 0$ to SimPO creates a finite margin attractor at $Δ^\* = β^{-1}\\log((1-\\varepsilon)/\\varepsilon)$, while $\\varepsilon = 0$ (standard SimPO) has no attractor. We test this across three models (8B-9B) and confirm the prediction: the theory-derived configuration (TPO-S, $\\varepsilon = 0.05$) is competitive with the strongest baselines, attaining the highest mean IFEval score among the compared methods on all three, validating that the predicted finite attractor is a usable operating point rather than proposing a new state-of-the-art loss.

</details>

### 290. [Learning Calibrated and Samplable Predictive Distributions of Surface-Ocean $Δp$CO$\_2$ Using Tabular Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21769v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21769

**作者：** Gabriele Accarino、Mohammad M. Amirian、Thea H. Heimdal、Amanda R. Fay、Galen A. McKinley、Viviana Acquaviva

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.ao-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21769v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21769v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用基于梯度提升树打分函数的条件扩散模型（Treeffuser）学习海洋表层 ΔpCO₂ 的完整预测分布，并在 SOCAT 数据上评估校准性与点预测能力。

**排除原因：** 属海洋碳通量的表格数据不确定性量化应用，虽涉及条件扩散采样，但未触及视觉/多模态推理的内部表示、证据获取或停止决策，缺乏可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Calibrated and Samplable Predictive Distributions of Surface-Ocean $Δp$CO$\_2$ Using Tabular Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** The ocean absorbs almost 30% of anthropogenic CO$\_2$ emissions, but quantifying this sink requires estimating surface-ocean CO$\_2$ from observations that are sparse in space and time. Most existing machine-learning estimates of this field are deterministic, giving a single value per grid point, with uncertainty approximated post hoc via residuals, ensembles, or prescribed distributions. We instead learn a conditional predictive distribution for each observation: using Treeffuser, a conditional diffusion model with gradient-boosted-tree score functions, we estimate the full predictive distribution of $Δp$CO$\_2$ (the sea-air $p$CO$\_2$ difference) from 13 environmental, spatial, and temporal predictors. On a held-out SOCAT test set, Treeffuser attains the lowest continuous ranked probability score among the uncertainty-quantification methods evaluated (8.229 $μ$atm) and the coverage closest to the nominal 68% / 95% levels (0.686 / 0.930), while retaining point-prediction skill comparable to a deterministic XGBoost baseline (RMSE 18.4 vs. 17.7 $μ$atm; $R^2$ 0.77 vs. 0.79). These results show that conditional diffusion can provide well-calibrated, observation-level uncertainties while largely preserving point-prediction skill. Because Treeffuser is generative, its samples can support probabilistic analyses of $Δp$CO$\_2$; extending this to spatially coherent field reconstruction will require modeling dependence across grid cells and evaluation on spatially held-out data.

**comment/C1：** 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Workshop: Representations for the Physical Sciences

</details>

**title/T1：** tmol: a GPU-accelerated implementation of the Rosetta energy function for machine learning

**abstract/A1：** Physics-inspired forcefields complement generative machine learning models, showing better generalization beyond the training distribution. However, these forcefields are typically slow to evaluate, and their derivatives are difficult to integrate into ML frameworks. We introduce tmol, a GPU-native PyTorch library implementing the state-of-the-art RosettaGenFF forcefield. tmol supports efficient batched differentiable scoring and whole-structure refinement with up to 362-fold throughput increase (batched). Used as a reward in generative protein design, tmol increases hydrogen bonds and water bridges at designed interfaces.

</details>

### 292. [PromptPack: Training-Free Augmentation of Diffusion Editors for Intent-Aligned Spatial and Semantic Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21865v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21865

**作者：** Sophia Zalewski、Mustafa Munir、Enting Liu、Anjul Patney、David Dynerman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21865v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21865v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PromptPack：为扩散编辑器加两种「Edit Guides」，用 Keep/Permit/Modify 的像素角色划分和区域-提示词分配来约束编辑位置与内容，无需重训即可接入 FlowEdit FLUX、FlowEdit SD3 和 FLUX Kontext。

**排除原因：** 这是扩散图像编辑的空间可控性工作，虽用注意力掩码和更新门控注入区域提示，但目标是图像像素编辑而非 VLM 的 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PromptPack: Training-Free Augmentation of Diffusion Editors for Intent-Aligned Spatial and Semantic Control

**abstract/A1：** Many image-editing tasks require confining changes to intended spatial regions while exactly preserving user-designated source content. Instruction-driven editing pipelines often leave such user-intent underspecified, while inpainting editors condition generation on explicitly defined edit support but not on the spatial assignment of individual edit concepts. PromptPack addresses both limitations by equipping diffusion editors with two explicit 'Edit Guides': an edit-role partition that labels each pixel as 'Keep,' 'Permit,' or 'Modify' to specify where edits may occur, and a regional-prompt assignment that maps spatial regions to text prompts to specify which concepts apply where. These guides may be manually specified or, for source-object edits, automatically derived from a text-grounded SAM 3 mask. Without retraining, PromptPack injects independently embedded regional prompts into the editor's fixed-length text sequence and uses the Edit Guides to spatially mask attention, gate flow updates, and restore protected source pixels. We integrate PromptPack into FlowEdit FLUX, FlowEdit SD3, and FLUX Kontext and demonstrate improved requested-concept placement and protected-content preservation across all three integrations on PIE-Bench++. In a pairwise user study, PromptPack achieves an 87% preference rate over FlowEdit FLUX, establishing PromptPack as a practical, training-free interface for intent-aligned diffusion editing.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 21 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

### 293. [FaceRiff: Data-Efficient Unified Text-to-3D Face Asset Synthesis via Geometric Conditioning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21867v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21867

**作者：** Elly Akhoundi、Mathieu Lamarre、Thibault Goyallon、Patrick Anderson、Étienne Danvoye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21867v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21867v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FaceRiff 用预训练 SDXL 加 LoRA 与 UV 法线几何条件，从文本直接生成拓扑一致的 3D 人脸资产（网格、albedo、位移），面向数字人渲染。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 资产生成与渲染应用，未涉及多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或决策过程，缺少可与 LVR 问题对应的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FaceRiff: Data-Efficient Unified Text-to-3D Face Asset Synthesis via Geometric Conditioning

**abstract/A1：** High-resolution facial datasets with consistent UV-mapped geometry, albedo, and physically based displacement are scarce, yet such assets are essential for photorealistic digital-human rendering. Existing 3D face generation methods are often slow, single-modality, or require large specialized datasets. Meanwhile, large image diffusion models encode strong facial priors but do not natively produce lighting-independent textures, pore-scale displacement, or UV-aligned geometry. We present FaceRiff, a unified, data- and parameter-efficient framework for generating complete 3D face assets (mesh, albedo, and displacement) directly from text in a shared UV space. FaceRiff adapts a pretrained SDXL backbone with task-specific LoRA adapters and uses UV normal maps as geometric conditioning: per-instance normals guide albedo and displacement synthesis, while a dataset-mean normal provides a canonical spatial anchor for mesh generation. Using only 2,049 scans from approximately 350 identities and training on a single GPU, FaceRiff updates less than 3% of the backbone parameters per task and produces render-ready 3D face assets without per-instance optimization. Compared with FaceG2E and DreamFace, FaceRiff produces greater per-prompt diversity while preserving prompt alignment and fixed-topology UV consistency, and additionally generates lighting-independent albedo suitable for PBR relighting and pore-scale displacement absent from prior text-driven baselines. A blind study with 45 industry professionals shows that FaceRiff achieves substantially higher perceived realism than DreamFace, approaching that of real captures. Overall, FaceRiff demonstrates that pretrained diffusion priors can be adapted to unified 3D face asset synthesis under limited data.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 Conference Papers. 11 pages, 11 figures, 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-294"></a>

### 294. [Domain-Tuned Diffusion Super-Resolution for 4K Facial Asset Maps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21890v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21890

**作者：** Elly Akhoundi、Mathieu Lamarre、Thibault Goyallon、Patrick Anderson、Étienne Danvoye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21890v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21890v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向 4K 面部资产贴图（albedo 与 displacement UV 图）的超分辨率，在冻结 SDXL 上以 LoRA 做模态特定微调，并用 Tile ControlNet 保留输入结构，实现 1024 到 4096 的 4 倍放大。

**排除原因：** 属于图像生成与资产放大任务，虽然涉及视觉细节保真，但不研究多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，未见可供 LVR 核查的状态或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Domain-Tuned Diffusion Super-Resolution for 4K Facial Asset Maps

**abstract/A1：** Physically based facial assets require 4K albedo and displacement maps for cinematic close-ups, yet generative pipelines commonly produce them at only 1K. Naively applying photographic super-resolution is a poor fit: facial UV maps have domain-specific statistics, and displacement is a scalar height field whose errors directly distort geometry. We present a domain-tuned diffusion upscaler for 4x (1024 to 4096) facial asset super-resolution. Starting from a bilinearly upsampled map, we adapt a frozen SDXL backbone per modality with lightweight LoRA modules, while a Tile ControlNet preserves input structure as the diffusion prior restores pore- and wrinkle-scale detail. On held-out maps, our method produces sharper, more faithful detail than bilinear and off-the-shelf GAN and diffusion super-resolvers, reducing albedo LPIPS from 0.21 to 0.08 while avoiding misplaced or excessive displacement relief. These results show that lightweight domain adaptation can produce render-ready 4K facial asset maps without a custom super-resolution architecture.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 Technical Communications. 4 pages

</details>

### 295. [Temporal Pseudo-RGB Encoding for Label-Free Detection of VEEV Infection in Human Brain Endothelial Cells](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21892v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21892

**作者：** Zhengjie Zhu、Stephanie Valdez Trefry、Hao Lu、Abbas Alili、Yongxin Guo、Aarthi Narayanan、Metin Nafi Gurcan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21892v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21892v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文把三个时间偏移的明场显微帧堆叠为 RGB 通道以检测细胞感染，属于生物医学成像中的时序分类应用；虽含「多时间步证据融合」的编码技巧，但无内部推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 生成机制，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Temporal Pseudo-RGB Encoding for Label-Free Detection of VEEV Infection in Human Brain Endothelial Cells

**abstract/A1：** Label-free detection of viral infection is central to antiviral screening and pathogen surveillance. Most deep-learning methods analyze individual frames and do not model temporal progression. We evaluate temporal pseudo-RGB encoding, which stacks three time-offset brightfield frames ($t{-}6$ h, $t{-}3$ h, $t$) as the red, green, and blue channels of a single image so that morphological change becomes color and an ImageNet-pretrained convolutional neural network (CNN) can be trained on infection trajectories without architectural modification. In human brain microvascular endothelial cells (HBMVEC) infected with Venezuelan equine encephalitis virus (VEEV) TC-83, a multi-task network predicts infected-versus-mock status and time post-infection in one forward pass. Across three experimental runs under leave-one-row-out cross-validation, with all 36 wells scored out of fold, the encoding reaches 91.6% accuracy against 87.4% for the single-frame model (paired difference $+4.2$ percentage points, 95% CI $\[+2.5, +6.1\]$; sign-flip $p = 3\\times10^{-5}$; well-level inference, conditional on three runs). At the late-phase endpoint (40-48 h), where floating cells and debris can make conventional brightfield images difficult to interpret, the advantage widens to $+23.9$ points (95% CI $\[+19.2, +28.9\]$). Time post-infection is recovered on the fixed row split with MAE = 1.31 h ($R^2 = 0.975$). Among the temporal models tested the encoding has the highest mean accuracy, at a fraction of the cost of the recurrent and 3D convolutional alternatives, though only its margin over the single-frame model is resolved; the tested ViT-B/16 backbone showed no corresponding gain, so the finding is limited to convolutional backbones and this virus-cell system.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages. Supplementary material is included as an ancillary file

</details>
