### 296. [World-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Pollution-Aware Bird Movement Modeling Using a Multi-Instance DQN Framework](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21895v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21895

**作者：** Shivin Mangal、Divyansh Chawla、Anshu Garg、Isshaan Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21895v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21895v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将鸟类建模为环境敏感智能体，在印度二维网格上用预计算 AQI 矩阵作为环境反馈信号训练多实例 DQN 导航策略，并与 Monte Carlo、Q-Learning、价值迭代等基线比较。

**排除原因：** 属于具体生态模拟应用，仅把环境指标当作奖励来源，未涉及视觉/多模态推理的状态表示、证据获取或停止决策，缺乏与 LVR 问题相关的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** World-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Pollution-Aware Bird Movement Modeling Using a Multi-Instance DQN Framework

**abstract/A1：** Birds are modeled as environmentally sensitive agents whose movement reflects real-world air quality conditions, where AQI serves as a measurable world feedback signal representing pollution-driven consequences. Instead of relying on human preference or synthetic rewards, the system uses environmental outcomes as the primary training signal, enabling policy learning grounded in ecological impact. We propose a multi-instance reinforcement learning simulation in which agents navigate a 2D grid representation of India integrated with a precomputed AQI matrix and geographic state boundaries. The framework combines bird call metadata from the Xeno-canto dataset, spatial discretization, and real environmental data to learn adaptive navigation strategies under pollution constraints. To ensure scalability and efficiency, repeated real-time API queries are replaced by a structured AQI mapping that enables fast lookup during training and execution. Multiple baselines, including Monte Carlo, Q-Learning, and Value Iteration, are evaluated for comparison. Deep Q-Network demonstrates stronger stability, adaptability, and cumulative performance across varying environmental conditions, establishing a scalable foundation for world-feedback driven ecological decision modeling.

**comment/C1：** This work has been accepted at the GlobalSouthAI Affinity Event at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 297. [Higher-Order Interaction Reconstruction via Integral Physics-Informed Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21935

**作者：** Xin Mao、Zhen Xu、Shiyi Yang、Sukwon Yun、Yunmei Liu、Ren Wang、Tianlong Chen、Can Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 IntegralPINN 框架，用时间区间上的积分形式替代基于导数的物理残差，并结合交互张量的连续参数化，从含噪稀疏观测中重建复杂网络的高阶交互结构。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是复杂网络动力学反问题，虽涉及噪声鲁棒的状态/参数估计，但既无视觉多模态推理，也未给出可核查的 latent 表示、证据筛选或停止决策线索，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Higher-Order Interaction Reconstruction via Integral Physics-Informed Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing higher-order interactions is a fundamental challenge in complex networked systems, with applications in ecological, social, and biological networks. This inverse problem is inherently difficult due to the combinatorial complexity of higher-order structures, the strong nonlinearity of the underlying dynamics, and the prevalence of noisy and sparse observations. In this article, we propose a novel integral physics-informed neural network (IntegralPINN) framework for higher-order interaction reconstruction. Instead of { penalizing derivative-based physics residuals,} we adopt an integral formulation over temporal intervals, which naturally suppresses high-frequency noise and mitigates derivative-induced instability. We further introduce a continuous parameterization of interaction tensors, enabling tractable identification of higher-order structures via gradient-based optimization. We provide theoretical analysis establishing a noise-robust error bound that is independent of derivative amplification when the integration window satisfies an excitation condition. Extensive experiments on ecological dynamics, social contagion, and neuronal synchronization systems demonstrate that IntegralPINN achieves accurate and robust reconstruction under noisy and sparse observations, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>

### 298. [SLED: Sensor SLicing from Edge to Datacenter](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21937v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21937

**作者：** Alan Subedi、Avhishek Biswas、Apala Pramanik、Amlan Balabantaray、Mikhail Titov、Santosh Pitla、Ozgur Kilic、Mehmet C. Vuran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE、cs.DC、cs.ET、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21937v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21937v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 O-RAN 中的「传感器切片」，在单个 UE 内为各传感器流提供 MAC 层确定性隔离，并实现从边缘到 HPC 的端到端框架 SLED，以保障时延关键的遥测流量不被大流量挤占。

**排除原因：** 属无线网络资源隔离与调度系统工作，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，也无可核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SLED: Sensor SLicing from Edge to Datacenter

**abstract/A1：** Modern sensing systems generate large volumes of heterogeneous data streams from multiple sensor modalities, increasing reliance on datacenters for computation, artificial intelligence/superintelligence (AI/SI) inference, and AI/SI training. Each sensor stream may have different network and compute requirements depending on its type and data volume. However, current wireless systems typically allocate resources at the device level, leading to contention among co-located sensor streams, where bulk data transfers may starve latency-critical telemetry. To this end, this paper introduces Sensor Slicing (SL). SL provides deterministic, medium access control (MAC)-level isolation for individual sensors within a single user equipment (UE) in open radio access networks (O-RAN). Using agentic coding techniques, SL is implemented in two O-RAN-compliant packages, reducing development time by 400 times (2 h 40 min \[OCUDU\] vs. 6.5 weeks \[OAI\]), 228-fold fewer human prompts (4 vs. 913), and less than half the tokens (0.64B vs. 1.43B). An associated end-to-end framework, SLED, is implemented to establish per-sensor radio and network isolation across edge, campus, and remote high-performance computing (HPC) tiers. Over-the-air wireless experiments demonstrate that while conventional network slicing causes critical delays to increase more than 2,300-fold under high traffic load, SLED maintains stable latency. Scaling evaluations on the two RAN stacks show that throughput remains within 0.9% and 1.4% of expected allocations even as the number of sensor slices increases. The results demonstrate that SLED preserves latency-critical telemetry while effectively utilizing resources across the sensor-to-HPC pipeline. The SLED software and evaluation datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/UNL-CPN-Lab/SLED-Sensor-SLicing-from-Edge-to-Datacenter.

**comment/C1：** Accepted in IEEE Annual Congress on Artificial Intelligence of Things (IEEE AIoT) 2026. The SLED software and evaluation datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/UNL-CPN-Lab/SLED-Sensor-SLicing-from-Edge-to-Datacenter

</details>

### 299. [Honest Brokers in AI Regulation: How Public Comments Introduce Evidence to Regulators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21948v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21948

**作者：** Sarah Tran、Anna-Maria Gueorguieva、Eva Maxfield Brown、Nicholas Weber

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21948v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21948v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了美国人工智能监管相关的 40 项潜在法规与 2,664 条公众评论语料，计算抽取其中的正式引用与非正式参考，比较企业、政府、高校与公民社会等不同主体提供证据的类型与数量差异，并讨论评论者在政策过程中扮演「议题倡导者」还是「诚实中间人」。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于 AI 治理与政策制定的实证研究，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部表示、latent 状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可迁移的建模或评价线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Honest Brokers in AI Regulation: How Public Comments Introduce Evidence to Regulators

**abstract/A1：** The regulation of Artificial Intelligence is susceptible to various forms of capture by industry actors that hold undue influence over the policymaking process, due in part to their information advantage in developing these technologies. In the following paper, we investigate the roles of public commenters in providing evidence to potential regulation in the USA. We build a corpus of 40 potential regulations, and 2,664 public comments submitted by a variety of stakeholders. We computationally process these texts to extract evidence meant to inform regulators, in the form of formal citations and informal references. We find significant differences in the type and amount of evidence supplied: Holding comment length constant, business commenters introduce altogether less evidence, and government, university, and civil society commenters provide significantly more research and legal sources. The publication status of cited research is nearly evenly split, with preprints and peer-reviewed articles being introduced by commenters of all types. We further explore patterns of self-citation and find that while it is prevalent amongst all groups, a small cluster of organizations account for over half of all self-citations in our corpus. Our findings complicate a narrative that public comments alone are means to information capture. We interpret these results in terms of the role that commenters attempt to play in the policymaking process-being either an Issue Advocate or an Honest Broker. These findings on the supply of evidence in public comments shed light on an often overlooked form of policymaking that has implications for how and through what sources information is made accessible to regulators of AI.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

**内容简述：** 提出 SCAN：多中心脑 MRI 场景下，各站点以本地类原型做免训练适配，并仅共享加噪的范数受限类汇总，以隐私保护方式协作适配 3D 医学 VLM。

**排除原因：** 属联邦式医学分类适配与隐私共享工作，不涉及内部推理状态的生成与更新、视觉证据筛选或停止决策，类原型共享也难构成可核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SCAN: Site-Aware Collaborative Privacy-Preserving Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for Neurological Disease Classification

**abstract/A1：** Worldwide neuroimaging data could strengthen diagnostic foundation models, but privacy rules often prevent centralizing brain MRI scans. We present SCAN, a site-aware collaborative adaptation framework for Neurological Disease Classification that lets new sites adapt a 3D medical vision-language model (VLM) without retraining or sharing patient scans. Each site keeps exact class prototypes for local prediction and can share privacy-protected class summaries: norm-bounded sums and counts perturbed once with calibrated noise. Using 7,641 scans from 67 sites across 12 countries, covering Alzheimer's disease (AD), stroke, epilepsy and controls, we assess local adaptation, cross-site benefits, and effects on existing sites. At seven unseen sites, the frozen VLM's zero-shot AUCs were 0.833-0.945 for AD and stroke. Training-free local prototypes improved epilepsy AUC at three sites from 0.598/0.625/0.508 to 0.684/0.729/0.762 and AD AUC at one site from 0.833 to 0.863, nearly matching a classifier trained at each site (mean AUC 0.757 vs. 0.759). With differential privacy ($ε$ = 4), new sites contributed without materially reducing accuracy at existing sites (AUC change $\\leq$0.2%), although gains from sharing were limited, and membership attacks performed near chance. SCAN offers hospitals a worldwide framework to adapt and share brain MRI VLMs without exchanging scans or retraining.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.21978

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21978v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.21978v1>)

**内容简述：** 提出 WebAgents 框架，用签名的 agent.pkg 分离智能体的创建、托管与执行，并在浏览器运行时中完成验证、授权与资源绑定；主要为系统与安全层面的工程方案。

**排除原因：** 内容集中在智能体打包、权限与预算绑定及注入违规拦截，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的表示、证据筛选、停止决策等主线问题，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态或计算机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WebAgents: Unbundling Agent Creation, Hosting, and Execution for an Agent-Native Web

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are moving from isolated assistants toward autonomous agents that can plan, use tools, and coordinate with others, motivating an emerging Agentic Web of independently developed agents and services. Realizing this vision requires agent behavior to move across creators, hosts, and runtimes without coupling publication to a particular execution environment or transferring authority over user resources. We introduce WebAgents, which unbundles these roles through a signed agent.pkg that carries portable workflows, skills, schemas, and resource requirements. An OpenClaw-based agent-native browser/runtime verifies the package, obtains authorization, and binds it at run time to user-controlled models, tools, permissions, and budgets, while hosts distribute the public program and may retain private services server-side. We demonstrate this contract with three unrelated workflows executed unchanged under local and API-backed models, including literature review with live services and a website-published travel agent using semantic tools and user-selected personal resources. In a controlled suite, WebAgents explicitly blocks all 716 injected contract violations while accepting all 295 matched benign controls, with median automated pre-execution processing below 0.5 s for all three packages. We conclude by outlining a path toward NANDA-based discovery and interoperable package ecosystems while retaining execution authority in the user's runtime.

**comment/C1：** Presented at IEEE CIC 2026. 8 pages

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Should the Lamp Go? Ambient-Aware Lamp Placement for Portraits

**abstract/A1：** Portraits shape first impressions, and lighting is an important factor. Prior work, such as lighting estimation and portrait relighting, edits lighting after capture but does not guide where to place a light before the shot. This leaves an open question: where should a light go to best light a face? Therefore, we propose LightMatch, a preference-grounded inverse lighting framework that disentangles the controllable light from ambient illumination and optimizes its placement. We first learn a Bradley-Terry quality model from pairwise human judgments. A perception head then regresses spherical-harmonic lighting from frozen self-supervised features via cross-attention. Finally, a placement solver isolates the light via spherical-harmonic linearity and optimizes its reachable position. On held-out environments, LightMatch attains 87.8% of the oracle improvement, outperforming the conventional solver that ignores ambient light by a relative 28.4% and the same pipeline with the widely used DPR estimator by a relative 70.5%. It reaches 73.2% on real OLAT faces of unseen identities and, on our real captures, cuts lamp-direction error by 37.7%.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 12 figures

</details>

### 303. [A Hybrid Exact-Heuristic Solver for the Integrated Passenger and Parcel Transportation Problem](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22043v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22043

**作者：** The Dung Bui、Ha Thu Nguyen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22043v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22043v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文描述 SOICT 2026 竞赛中「乘客与包裹联合运输」问题的求解器，将常数时间插入检验、贪心加 ALNS 局部搜索与基于分支定界、拉格朗日界的精确路径选择引擎结合，并按实例规模自动切换。

**排除原因：** 内容属车辆路径与集合划分的组合优化工程，与 LVR 的内部表示、latent 状态生成及监督方式无实质联系；按规模切换求解器与看门狗保底虽涉及计算预算，但缺乏可供核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Hybrid Exact-Heuristic Solver for the Integrated Passenger and Parcel Transportation Problem

**abstract/A1：** We describe the solver we submitted to the second round of the SOICT 2026 contest on the Integrated Passenger and Parcel Transportation problem, a selective pickup-and-delivery routing problem with time windows, open routes and heterogeneous taxis. The solver is a single-file, standard-library Python program that combines (i) a constant-time insertion test based on latest-arrival slack, (ii) greedy insertion, local search and an Adaptive Large Neighborhood Search (ALNS) with simulated-annealing acceptance, and (iii) an exact route-selection engine that enumerates, for every taxi, the best feasible route for each request set and then solves the resulting set-packing problem by branch-and-bound strengthened with route-based large neighborhood search, Lagrangian bounds and reduced-cost fixing. The engine is chosen automatically from the instance size, and an independent verifier and a watchdog guarantee that a valid answer is always printed. On the official evaluation (38 scored instances, 5-minute limit) the solver obtained a total benefit of 487,237, finished every run within 277 s and used at most 279 MB; 28 of the 38 instances terminated well before the limit.

**comment/C1：** 4 pages, 2 figures, 3 tables. Technical Report for the SOICT 2026 Student Contest

</details>

**排除原因：** 属于遥感/环境分割的迁移学习与标注扩充工作，没有视觉推理状态、证据获取、停止决策或 latent 表示的使用与监督，SCSE 解码器注意力与位置编码消融也只是常规分割工程细节，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-Efficient Algal Bloom Segmentation in Freshwater Bodies Using a Three-Stage Transfer Learning Pipeline with Attention-Enhanced Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Harmful algal blooms threaten water quality and public health, yet automated segmentation of bloom regions in freshwater imagery is limited by scarce pixel-level annotations. We present a transfer-learning pipeline that adapts a Swin Transformer U-Net with SCSE decoder attention from ImageNet-1K to water-body segmentation (RIWA, LaRS) and then to algal blooms, together with a model-assisted annotation protocol that expands the labelled algae corpus from 145 to 655 images. In a single-seed ablation on a 41-image validation split (checkpoint S2-A4 and its variants), corpus expansion is the dominant factor (+11 mIoU points); water-body pretraining helps only on the expanded corpus (+1.1 points over ImageNet initialization); SCSE attention adds 0.3 points; and a learnable positional encoding lowers mIoU by 0.6 points and is omitted. Adding 476 images, 188 of them algae-free, reduces false-positive area on non-water scenes from 22.6% to 4.9%. Against nine baselines on a 256-image test split, a separately trained checkpoint of the same architecture (S2-A13) reaches mIoU 0.919 and the highest algae IoU (0.902); a near-duplicate audit indicates train/test overlap in this split, so these scores are likely optimistic.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables

We give two counterexamples to its published recipe with higher final loss than standard k-means (Lloyd, 1982) from identical initial centers. Mass-based revival can destroy a global optimum and make the ratio of expected losses unbounded even with k-means++ seeding.

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22076

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究如何从航拍影像估计屋顶光伏装机容量，提出 Direct Head：保留 U-Net 分割与软掩码池化，同时由光伏区域特征和面积描述符直接回归非负容量，避免把容量绑定为面积与功率密度之积。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感分割加回归的常规任务应用，没有涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，软掩码池化仅为任务特定特征池化，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Segmentation-Guided Direct Capacity Estimation of Rooftop Photovoltaics from Aerial Imagery

**abstract/A1：** Distribution planners need installation-level inventories of rooftop photovoltaic (PV) capacity, but interconnection records may be incomplete or spatially aggregated. Conventional image-based estimators segment PV arrays, convert the mask to physical area, and multiply by an estimated power density, coupling capacity prediction to mask-derived area. We propose Direct Head, a segmentation-guided architecture that retains U-Net segmentation and soft-mask pooling while directly regressing nonnegative capacity from PV-region features and area descriptors. We evaluate three independent runs on 7,590 French rooftop installations under the original split and a department-based holdout. Relative to a parameter-matched area-density neural model, Direct Head reduces mean absolute error by 8.7% and 12.5%, and symmetric mean absolute percentage error by 14.0% and 20.7%, respectively. Comparisons with conventional estimators, controlled architecture variants, auxiliary-loss ablations, and representative cases show that segmentation provides useful spatial evidence without requiring capacity to be determined by an area-density product.

</details>

### 307. [BernBRS: Certified Backward Reachability for Deep Bernstein Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22083v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22083

**作者：** Ibrahim Albool、Yasser Shoukry

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22083v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22083v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对采用 Bernstein 多项式激活的 DeepBern-Net，提出 BernBRS 框架，同时计算反向可达集的内、外认证近似，并通过按激活模式与松弛间隙精确分解来细分输入域，从而提升可扩展性。

**排除原因：** 主题是神经网络形式化验证与可达集界定，未涉及视觉/多模态推理、latent 状态生成或证据使用，也与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等决策问题无具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BernBRS: Certified Backward Reachability for Deep Bernstein Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Ensuring the safety of complex neural network systems requires mathematically rigorous verification. While backward reachability analysis is a critical tool for identifying safe initial states, computing tight, formal bounds remains notoriously difficult to scale. Recent advancements have demonstrated that DeepBern-Nets neural networks utilizing Bernstein polynomial activation functions possess advantageous mathematical properties that enable exceptionally fast and tight forward reachability analysis. Building upon this efficiency, this paper introduces BernBRS, a highly accurate and scalable framework for the backward reachability analysis of DeepBern-Nets. BernBRS simultaneously computes certified inner and outer approximations of the Backward Reachable Set (BRS). Rather than relying on single convex bounding, our method intelligently refines a cover of the input space by analyzing activation patterns, splitting the input domain precisely where an exact decomposition of the relaxation gap predicts the most significant reduction in error. This targeted refinement enables high levels of scalability to highly complex and massive architectures that remain intractable for existing certified methods. We demonstrate the efficacy of our framework on high-dimensional models, including a linearized closed-loop approximation of the Half-Cheetah controller and a distilled Bernstein student of a Transformer block and we empirically study how these model-level guarantees relate to the original Half-Cheetah and TinyBERT classifier, respectively.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VTT: Reading Vehicle Size and Powertrain Type from Motion Trajectory Across Unseen Car Models

**abstract/A1：** Vehicle size and powertrain information can enrich trajectory data for traffic analysis and simulation. We present VTT, a dataset and benchmark for identifying these attributes from motion and controller area network (CAN) signals across unseen car models. The dataset contains 9,062 drives, about 3,950 h, from 1,431 vehicles and 174 car models. It provides three footprint-based size classes, powertrain labels, and evaluation on unseen car models and unseen drivers. Using 60-s windows, we compare four sequence architectures, statistical features and physical features proposed by large language models. Training with auxiliary physical targets, data augmentation and task-specific weighting raises drive-level macro-F1 to 92.6-93.0% for battery-electric versus combustion vehicles and from 62.9% to 67.1% for size classification. On public logs from 4,061 additional drivers, the full-input model identifies powertrain correctly for 86 of 88 car models; size accuracy is 63.2%, close to the internal vehicle-level result of 63.5%. Physical-feature analysis links powertrain recognition to deceleration behaviour and size recognition to steering response. Errors near size-class boundaries and limited gains from longer observations highlight the importance of label resolution and training-fleet coverage for cross-model generalisation.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22133

**作者：** Yilin Lyu、Kang Liu、Wei Wang、Qifan Zhang、Ling Yin、Yushi Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22133v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22133v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReMoE，用特征引导的混合专家路由组合可复用的灾后移动性恢复模式，并用未来灾害条件预测做残差修正，以提升跨灾种恢复预测的可迁移性与可解释性。

**排除原因：** 属灾害移动性预测的领域应用，虽用 MoE 路由与残差修正，但无视觉/多模态推理状态、证据获取或 latent 生成环节，与 LVR 只存在泛泛的模块化组合类比，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReMoE: An Interpretable Mixture-of-Experts Framework for Human Mobility Recovery Forecasting Across Disasters

**abstract/A1：** Human mobility often declines sharply following disasters and subsequently exhibits complex recovery dynamics. Effective forecasting of this recovery is critical for timely assessment and resource allocation. However, existing approaches are often developed for individual events and operate as black boxes, limiting their transferability and interpretability beyond the events they were trained on. Recent studies have identified recurring post-disaster mobility recovery types across disasters, suggesting that recovery knowledge may be transferable. Motivated by this, we propose ReMoE, an interpretable mixture-of-experts framework for cross-disaster mobility recovery forecasting. ReMoE uses a feature-guided router to adaptively combine specialized experts that learn these heterogeneous yet reusable recovery types, producing a region-specific recovery pattern. It further refines this pattern through residual correction informed by forecasts of future hazard conditions. We evaluate ReMoE on a large-scale mobility dataset covering 18 disaster events spanning hurricanes, floods, winter storms, and wildfires. ReMoE is competitive in-distribution and achieves the strongest performance on unseen events and hazard types, with larger gains under greater distribution shifts. Further analyses show that ReMoE yields interpretable learning outcomes and maintains robust performance under uncertainty. Broadly, ReMoE offers a transferable and traceable forecaster that can support anticipatory post-disaster assessment.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by KDD 2027

</details>

**排除原因：** 该工作属于 DIY 实验仪器与 agent 运维的工程案例，不涉及内部视觉表示、latent 状态生成、轨迹训练或推理证据与停止决策的机制分析，「从晚期参考帧反向追溯」仅为人工标注流程，未提供可供迁移的 LVR 线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Big Brains, Tiny Board: Pupavision, an Agentic Physical AI Instrument for Monitoring Drosophila Pupariation and Lifecycle Transitions

**abstract/A1：** Many biology experiments are limited by the availability and ease of use of instruments that monitor and record them. For example, the timing of pupariation in the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster, a widely used model organism, can provide biologically relevant information about growth regulation and is often recorded manually. Here, we present Pupavision, a small physical AI instrument built from a single-board Linux computer and a day/night camera for roughly 45 to 75 US dollars in parts. The key idea is that a frontier coding agent runs its agentic harness directly on the tiny board. The model thinks in the cloud, but the agent works on the device. The agent assists image capture, writes code to repair errors, and checks the experiment every day to keep raw data capture reliable. If recording stops, the researcher gets a ping on their mobile phone. A simple deterministic capture service records one image every ten minutes and never depends on the agent. We report a 26-day pilot in a fly incubator (about 3,580 frames), including eleven days in which the agent was logged out and capture continued, and a 51-hour outage whose cause led to the layered design. A researcher-in-the-loop workflow then produces per-vial pupariation curves by tracing selected pupae backward in time from a late reference frame. Future work will validate the assay for lifecycle tracking accuracy against expert-labeled data and expand real-time computer vision analysis. Pupavision offers a blueprint for do-it-yourself agentic instruments that put frontier intelligence on a tiny board, with deterministic code underneath. We present this as an early case study from a pilot lab deployment and contribute lessons learned toward a future where scientific domain experts are empowered by DIY agentic hardware.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 311. [Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22140v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22140

**作者：** Aamod Varma、Shuyi Jia、Victor Fung

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22140v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22140v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究晶体结构合成数据与扩散预训练以提升材料属性预测，属材料科学图表示学习，与视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态生成、视觉证据使用和停止决策无实质联系，也未提供具体可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synthetic Crystal Data Generation for Diffusion Pretraining in Material Property Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion-based pretraining has emerged as a powerful strategy for learning transferable representations in graph neural networks for materials property prediction without requiring quantum-mechanical labels such as energies, forces, and stresses for every pretraining structure. However, the method retains a critical dependency: it requires structures residing near local minima of a potential energy surface, which have traditionally been obtained through costly DFT relaxations of known or physically synthesized compounds. This reliance constrains both the scale and the structural diversity of available pretraining data, leaving large regions of crystal structure space unexplored. In this work, we connect diffusion-based pretraining with synthetic structure generation to reduce this bottleneck. We introduce a synthetic data generation pipeline combining three complementary strategies: perturb-and-relax, generative sampling via MatterGen, and protostructure enumeration. Where structural relaxation is required, we use fast machine-learned interatomic potentials instead of performing a new DFT relaxation for each generated structure. We further compare a coordinate-denoising control with continuous-time variance-preserving (VP) and sub-VP diffusion objectives using a randomly initialized Orb-v2 backbone. The source comparison covers seven MatBench property-prediction tasks, and an eighth task, \\texttt{mp\\\_gap}, is included in the protostructure scaling study. Every synthetic-pretraining condition numerically outperforms random initialization on all seven source-comparison tasks. Scaling the protostructure corpus from 20k to 165k under a fixed 400-epoch schedule numerically matches or exceeds MP-20k pretraining on most tasks; because this protocol increases both data diversity and optimization updates, the result reflects joint data-and-compute scaling.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Accepted at the AI4Mat Workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 312. [When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22144v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22144

**作者：** Bingnan Zhao、Harshala Gammulle、Tharindu Fernando

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22144v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22144v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在四个小型可复现的 Modelica 办公建筑案例中，将 SAC 智能体直接训练在导出的 FMU 上，并与调优的规则控制器和近乎最优参考（穷举搜索或完美预见动态规划）比较，以厘清 DRL 在 HVAC 监督控制中的真实收益。

**排除原因：** 研究的是建筑能耗控制策略与基线强度对比，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的状态表示、证据获取与 latent 生成，也未提供可迁移的推理机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When does deep reinforcement learning pay off in HVAC supervisory control? Four small Modelica case studies trained directly on FMUs

**abstract/A1：** Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) is often proposed for the supervisory control of heating, ventilation and air-conditioning (HVAC) systems. The reported gains are, however, difficult to interpret, because the baselines are often weak and the attainable optimum is seldom known. This paper examines four small, reproducible case studies based on an office building in Brisbane, Australia: pre-cooling under a time-of-use tariff; chiller staging with condenser water reset; chilled water supply temperature reset with a wet cooling coil; and chilled water storage under time-of-use prices and a demand charge. The plants were modelled with the Modelica Buildings library, and in three of the cases a soft actor-critic agent was trained directly on the exported functional mock-up unit (FMU). Each agent was compared with tuned rule-based controllers and, where practicable, with a near-optimal reference from hourly exhaustive search or dynamic programming with perfect foresight. In the two cases without storage the agent reached the near-optimal reference, but the margin above a well-chosen rule was small: 1.3% for condenser water reset and 0.18% for chilled water reset. In the latter case two common reset rules consumed more energy than a fixed set point. With a chilled water storage tank the margin was considerably larger. The agent outperformed both rules (-7.8%, compared with -3.9% and -6.3%) and adopted midday charging during the solar off-peak period, yet it obtained only about 45% of the saving achieved by the perfect-foresight schedule and did little to limit the demand peak. In the pre-cooling case a hand-tuned rule remained superior (-12.0% compared with -5.6%). These results suggest that studies of DRL for HVAC control should state the margin between the best rule and a near-optimal reference. Some practical difficulties encountered in training on co-simulation FMUs are also reported.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. Code, models and trained agents: https://github.com/Bingnan-Zhao/hvac-drl-lab

</details>

<a id="paper-313"></a>

### 313. [Readie: Low-Latency Code Sandboxes for AI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22171v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22171

**作者：** Arjun Sivaraman、Zhaoheng Li、Alan Shi、Sanjay Malisetty、Eugene Wu、Yongjoo Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DB、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22171v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22171v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Readie，通过学习一组小型启动快照并求解覆盖式优化问题，降低 AI agent 代码沙箱的冷启动延迟。

**排除原因：** 属于 agent 代码执行的系统基础设施与快照调度优化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据使用或停止决策，也没有可迁移的 latent 表示线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Readie: Low-Latency Code Sandboxes for AI Agents

**abstract/A1：** From quantitative research to personal assistance, AI agents act by iteratively generating and executing code. To decouple the agent harness from code execution, many agents offload execution to dedicated systems called code sandboxes. However, existing code sandboxes suffer from slow startup. Whether they rely on custom snapshots or a single fat image, the root cause is the same: it is hard to build a compact, pre-warmed snapshot that can be shared across diverse agent-generated code while covering nearly all the artifacts (e.g., packages, datasets) it requires. In this work, we propose learned startup snapshots for low-latency code sandboxes. Our insight is that while it is hard for a single snapshot to be both compact and cover most workloads, a set of snapshots can achieve both. Each snapshot is small, initializes quickly, and covers a fraction of agent-generated code; together, the set covers most of it. We learn this set from a large training workload by formulating snapshot construction as an optimization problem and solving it with an approximation algorithm. Planned on 15K public and LLM-synthesized scripts, our system, Readie, reduces startup latency by 3-22$\\times$ and end-to-end runtime by 1.3-3.3$\\times$ compared to existing approaches at the same storage budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-314"></a>

### 314. [CorrectCast: Selective Multimodal Context for Scale-Aware Residual Correction in Precipitation Nowcasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22178v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22178

**作者：** Henan Wang、Shengwu Xiong、Yifang Zhang、Pengfei Duan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22178v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22178v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CorrectCast，在冻结降水临近预报主干的前提下，用轻量修正器结合 QPE、PWV 等多模态观测和尺度感知调制来估计残差修正。

**排除原因：** 属于气象临近预报的输出场后处理工作，修正作用在预测降水场而非模型内部推理状态，未涉及视觉信息保留、latent 状态生成或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CorrectCast: Selective Multimodal Context for Scale-Aware Residual Correction in Precipitation Nowcasting

**abstract/A1：** Precipitation nowcasting models suffer from errors in intensity, location, and spatial structure. Addressing these errors by retraining nowcaster backbones can be computationally expensive and requires architecture-specific modifications. To overcome these limitations, we propose CorrectCast, a decoupled residual correction framework that refines completed nowcasts while keeping the base nowcaster frozen. By operating on predicted precipitation fields, CorrectCast requires training only a lightweight corrector, reducing computational overhead while providing a unified correction interface across different nowcasting models. Conditioned on the base nowcast and historical quantitative precipitation estimation (QPE) and precipitable water vapor (PWV) observations, the corrector selects multimodal context and employs scale-aware modulation to estimate residual corrections. On the Mississippi River Basin (MRB) and contiguous United States (CONUS) datasets, CorrectCast improves six base nowcasters spanning extrapolation-based, deterministic, and generative approaches, with absolute gains in mean CSI averaging 0.0233 and 0.0290 across the six models, respectively.

</details>

### 315. [ARG-Avatar: Animation-Rendering Guidance for Efficient Feedforward Avatar Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22207v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22207

**作者：** Jianjin Xu、Soham Dasgupta、Fernando De la Torre

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22207v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22207v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个轻量前馈框架 ARG-Avatar，利用动画与渲染提供的几何对应关系（FACRoPE 注意力与中间 token 渲染流程）从少量图像重建可动画 3D 人体化身，以更小参数与算力达到 SOTA 重建质量。

**排除原因：** 属于 3D 人体化身重建与渲染效率问题，虽涉及几何对应引导的 token 注意力，但对象是化身外观与几何，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、潜在推理或证据与停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ARG-Avatar: Animation-Rendering Guidance for Efficient Feedforward Avatar Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing animatable 3D human avatars from images is important for applications such as gaming and augmented reality. Feedforward methods make avatar reconstruction accessible by reducing inference time to a few seconds, but recent approaches rely heavily on scaling model capacity, training data, and computation. We ask whether such scaling is necessary. To this end, we introduce ARG-Avatar, an efficient feedforward framework with only 68M trainable parameters that achieves SOTA reconstruction quality. Our key insight is that 3D animation and rendering already provide strong geometric correspondences that existing methods largely attempt to learn from data. In light of this, we design the Foreground Avatar Coordinate RoPE attention (FACRoPE) and Intermediate Token Rendering (ITR) pipeline to guide avatar tokens to attend to their corresponding patches. Experiments across multiple datasets show that ARG-Avatar consistently outperforms prior methods when using two or more input images. Compared with LHM++, ARG-Avatar uses approximately 9$\\times$ fewer trainable parameters, 4.8$\\times$ fewer training sequences, and 14$\\times$ less training compute, while running 6$\\times$ faster and achieving a 2.7 dB PSNR improvement on the out-of-distribution NeuMan benchmark.

</details>

### 316. [Joint-ID: Transformer-based Joint Image Enhancement and Depth Estimation for Underwater Environments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22212v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22212

**作者：** Geonmo Yang、Gilhwan Kang、Juhui Lee、Younggun Cho

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22212v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22212v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Joint-ID，用 Transformer 联合进行水下图像增强与深度估计，并构建带多种退化与场景深度的合成数据集。

**排除原因：** 属于水下成像多任务恢复与深度估计的应用工作，虽共享分层特征空间，但未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Joint-ID: Transformer-based Joint Image Enhancement and Depth Estimation for Underwater Environments

**abstract/A1：** Underwater imaging is a challenging task due to factors such as scattering, absorption, and turbulence, which degrade image quality and limit visibility. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for enhancing underwater images that leverages the benefits of joint learning for simultaneous image enhancement and depth estimation. We introduce Joint-ID, a transformer-based neural network that can obtain high-perceptual image quality and depth information from raw underwater images. Our approach formulates a multi-modal objective function that addresses invalid depth, lack of sharpness, and image degradation based on color and local texture. We design an end-to-end training pipeline that enables joint restoration and depth estimation in a shared hierarchical feature space. Additionally, we propose a synthetic dataset with various distortions and scene depths for multi-task learning. We evaluate Joint-ID on synthetic and standard datasets, as well as real underwater images with diverse spectra and harsh turbidity, demonstrating its effectiveness for underwater image enhancement and depth estimation. Furthermore, we demonstrate the ability of Joint-ID to perform feature matching and saliency detection for visually guided underwater robots. Our proposed method has the potential to improve the visual perception of underwater environments and benefit applications such as oceanography and underwater robotics. Supplements are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/joint-id/home

### 317. [Fine-Grained Japanese Clinical Named Entity Recognition: A Controlled Comparison of BERT and Open-Weight Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22219v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22219

**作者：** Feng Han、Ayako Yagahara、Kenji Hirata、Atsushi Yoshida、Kohsuke Kudo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22219v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22219v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在日语临床细粒度命名实体识别基准上，比较 BERT-CRF 与九个开放权重 LLM 的抽取性能，并考察 JSON Schema 约束、QLoRA 微调及输出 token 上限的影响。

**排除原因：** 属一般信息抽取的模型对比与生成设置调参，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据使用或推理决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fine-Grained Japanese Clinical Named Entity Recognition: A Controlled Comparison of BERT and Open-Weight Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Background: Generative large language models (LLMs) offer an alternative to encoder-based clinical named entity recognition (NER), but their performance on fine-grained Japanese extraction tasks and sensitivity to generation settings remain uncertain. Methods: We compared two fine-tuned BERT-CRF baselines with nine open-weight LLMs on iCorpus, which defines 113 entity categories. The evaluation used 433 sentences from 36 test documents; 1,672 sentences were used for training. LLMs were evaluated using zero-shot prompting with and without JSON Schema constraints and three-seed QLoRA fine-tuning. Fine-tuned models were assessed under different output-token limits, including 900 and larger model-specific limits of 2,600 or 4,096 tokens. Performance was measured using sentence-level label-surface matching with relaxed and exact-surface F1. Results: The highest zero-shot relaxed F1 was 0.337, compared with 0.836-0.841 for the BERT baselines. At the larger output limits, all eight fine-tuned LLMs with at least 13B total parameters exceeded both BERT baselines (relaxed F1, 0.882-0.914; exact-surface F1, 0.840-0.880). Across three-seed means, increasing the output limit improved relaxed F1 by 0.052-0.085, alongside higher recall and fewer length-limited outputs. JSON Schema constraints changed relaxed F1 by -0.014 to +0.008 among seven models outside the two reasoning-oriented configurations, while medical-general model pairs showed no consistent advantage for medical specialization. Conclusions: On this historical Japanese case-report benchmark, task-specific fine-tuning combined with adequate generation capacity was associated with stronger LLM extraction performance. Output-token limits materially affected comparisons with BERT, whereas enforcing output structure alone did not overcome low zero-shot accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 33 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables; includes appendices with supplementary tables and one supplementary figure

</details>

### 318. [TRIDENT: Efficient Triple-Task Learning of Dehazing, Depth, and Uncertainty Estimation for Underwater 3-D Robot Visual Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22224v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22224

**作者：** Geonmo Yang、Younggun Cho

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22224v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22224v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TRIDENT，一个共享单骨干、三分支并行解码器的轻量多任务模型，同时做水下图像增强、深度估计与不确定性估计，并在标准数据集上报告精度与内存/推理效率优势。

**排除原因：** 属于水下机器人感知的三任务联合学习与效率工程，不确定性头仅为辅助输出，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策机制，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线的具体联系不足。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRIDENT: Efficient Triple-Task Learning of Dehazing, Depth, and Uncertainty Estimation for Underwater 3-D Robot Visual Perception

**abstract/A1：** Underwater visual systems often suffer from blurry textures and low color contrast due to the inevitable light propagation. These issues can significantly degrade the perception of stable robotic operations. In this paper, we introduce a novel learning-based sensing system that tackles the multidimensional vision tasks in underwater; concretely, we deal with image enhancement, depth estimation, and uncertainty for 3-D visual systems. Also, we propose a TRIDENT model in a fast and lightweight manner; TRIDENT consists of three parallelized decoders and one backbone structure for efficient feature sharing. In addition, it is designed to be trained to express complex parameterization. In experimental evaluation on several standard datasets, we demonstrate that TRIDENT significantly outperforms other existing methods on image enhancement and depth estimation. Despite performing three tasks, our model has better efficiency than the others for both memory size and inference time. Finally, our joint learning approach demonstrates robustness in feature matching and seamlessly extends from 2-D to 3-D vision tasks. Supplements are available at https://sites.google.com/view/underwater-trident/home

### 319. [CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22235v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22235

**作者：** Qi Shao、Zhihao Cao、Shuhao Zhai、Taoyu Wu、Zhuoxiao Li、Anh Nguyen、Long Hu、Xianzhi Li、Hong Liang、Baoru Huang、Liang Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22235v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22235v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向全景相机的多智能体 Gaussian splatting SLAM 框架，用 SO(3) 上的球面特征相关做回环检测、以全局 Sim(3) 一致性剔除对称假回环，并通过带对数尺度正则的姿态图优化与全向 Gaussian 融合同时修正轨迹与地图。

**排除原因：** 该文属三维建图与 SLAM 定位问题，其回环验证依赖几何一致性优化，不涉及视觉推理中的内部表示、推理状态生成或证据使用，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure

**abstract/A1：** Dense collaborative mapping is essential for search and rescue, building inspection, and warehouse automation, where panoramic cameras capture the entire surroundings in a single frame. Unlike pinhole agents that share views only at similar headings, panoramic agents observe overlapping scenes at any 3D orientation, yet searching full rotations also produces mutually consistent false loop closures across distinct symmetric rooms. Moreover, continuous monocular scale drift splits rigidly merged maps at revisited surfaces, while depth noise at short baselines distorts loop translation directions. To address these challenges, we propose CoSphere-SLAM, the first multi-agent Gaussian splatting SLAM framework for panoramic cameras, whose spherical loop closure aligns spherical features over $\\mathrm{SO}(3)$ via fast Fourier correlation across arbitrary orientations and rejects symmetric false closures through global $\\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ consensus. Building on these closures, directional global optimization solves a per-keyframe $\\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ pose graph with log-scale regularization to correct scale drift while separating optical-flow translation directions from depth-based magnitudes. Finally, omnidirectional Gaussian fusion transforms each Gaussian with its keyframe similarity and renders Gaussians at their maximum ray response to jointly refine poses and maps. Experiments on indoor and cross-session multi-agent panoramic benchmarks demonstrate that CoSphere-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art trajectory accuracy and rendering quality among single-agent and multi-agent methods. Project website: https://cosphere-slam.github.io.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Project website: https://cosphere-slam.github.io

</details>

### 320. [Cross3DGS: Unified Vulkan-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting Training from Workstations to Mobile Devices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22240v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22240

**作者：** Hiroyuki Sato、Yusuke Takimoto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22240v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22240v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Cross3DGS 提出一个基于 Vulkan 的跨厂商、跨平台 3D Gaussian Splatting 训练器，通过按设备资源限制和驱动失败情况筛选可行的执行调度，使训练可在工作站、手机与 Apple 设备上完成。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦 3DGS 训练的可移植性与图形后端工程，不涉及内部视觉推理状态、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 生成，其按硬件约束选择调度的机制也仅与「动态计算」字面相似，缺少可核查的实质借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross3DGS: Unified Vulkan-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting Training from Workstations to Mobile Devices

**abstract/A1：** Scenes captured with handheld and wearable cameras are still shipped to a CUDA workstation for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) training. Training on the device that captured them, or on its companion phone or laptop, requires more than translating kernels: GPUs and drivers differ in parallel features, memory limits, and command submission, and these differences decide whether a training schedule executes and returns the correct update. We present Cross3DGS, a cross-vendor, cross-platform Vulkan trainer that keeps one optimizer and chooses its execution schedule per device in two steps: rules on device limits, workload size, and observed driver failures exclude the schedules known not to run correctly, and a fixed preference order selects one of the remaining feasible schedules. The same core completes standard-quality training (30k updates, one million Gaussians) on every configuration we measured, spanning NVIDIA, Intel, Arm, Qualcomm, AMD, and Apple GPUs and including Android devices and an iPhone, whereas the existing portable trainers VkSplat and Brush do not run on some of the mobile GPUs. Averaged over 11 scenes on a workstation, Cross3DGS has higher quality than VkSplat with no significant difference in training time, and Brush reports about three times the training time at comparable quality. The final quality of Cross3DGS is nearly identical across devices. A mobile application turns a handheld capture into a viewable model entirely on the device.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the WACV 2027 Workshop on Eyes of the Future (EOF). 19 pages including appendix

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multispectral-Polarization-Infrared Multi-Aperture Camouflaged Vehicle Detection

**abstract/A1：** Camouflaged vehicle detection is challenging because camouflage suppresses discriminative cues across sensing modalities. Existing multimodal methods often require precise pixel-level registration and feature-level fusion, creating a trade-off between accuracy and latency. We propose a spectral-polarization-infrared tri-modal detection framework co-designed with a multi-aperture imaging setup to exploit complementary imaging physics. A consensus-guided decision-level fusion strategy combines cross-modal detections within bounded registration tolerances, reducing dependence on precise alignment while maintaining accuracy and real-time performance. We construct a tri-modal camouflaged vehicle dataset containing infrared, polarization, and spectral data. Experiments show that the proposed method achieves 82.7% mean Average Precision (mAP) with an end-to-end latency of 16 ms, outperforming representative feature-level fusion approaches. On the public MID-1K dataset, the framework generalizes to RGB-Depth-Infrared modalities without architectural modification and improves mAP@0.5 by 5.7% over state-of-the-art methods. These results demonstrate the potential of decision-level consensus fusion and multi-aperture sensing for robust and deployable camouflaged vehicle detection.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 10 figures

</details>

### 322. [A Visual Information Extraction System Using Multimodal Large Language Models for Scanned Cognitive and Behavioral Assessments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22252v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22252

**作者：** Enshuo Hsu、Linh Nguyen、Sunyang Fu、Heath Goodrum、Sandra Stansberry、Kirk Roberts、Hongfang Liu、Yen-Chi L. Le

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22252v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作构建了一个模块化多模态 LLM 系统，用于扫描版认知与行为评估表（MMSE、MoCA、Mini-Cog、NICHQ Vanderbilt）的表单分类与字段/分数抽取，并以双模型分歧触发人工复核。

**排除原因：** 这是面向临床表单结构化的 OCR/信息抽取应用，双模型分歧仅作为任务级人工复核触发器，不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 生成与使用，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Visual Information Extraction System Using Multimodal Large Language Models for Scanned Cognitive and Behavioral Assessments

**abstract/A1：** Objective. Paper-based assessments are common in patient care, but handwriting and scanning artifacts make these forms difficult to process at scale. This study proposes a modular, multimodal LLM-based system with human oversight for extracting fields and scores. We conducted a qualitative analysis of the use of LLMs for hand-drawing analysis. Materials and Methods. The system processes cognitive assessments (i.e., MMSE, MoCA, Mini-Cog) and the NICHQ Vanderbilt Assessment using two modules: 1) a form classifier that assigns a scanned page to a form type, followed by a corresponding 2) score extractor that extracts fields and scores into a schema. A human-in-the-loop design utilizing dual-model validation engages human annotators when two LLMs disagree, limiting manual review to challenging pages only. Results. End-to-end accuracy for cognitive assessments was 88%, with most form types exceeding 90%. For the NICHQ Vanderbilt, overall accuracy reached 97.5%, with all form types above 96%. The human-in-the-loop design further improved cognitive assessment accuracy to 97.5% while reducing manual work by 63.8%. In the Mini-Cog Clock Drawing task, qualitative analysis showed that 62.3% of all extraction errors stemmed from ambiguous drawing, in which cases the LLM provided reasonable readings. Discussion. Compared to current manual data extraction, our LLM-based system reduces processing time by 10 to 20 times and costs by over 12 times. The modular design allows seamless expansion to additional form types. Conclusion. Our system provides an accurate, scalable, and cost-efficient solution for structuring data trapped in handwritten paper forms for clinical decision support.

</details>

### 323. [SARVIA: Fast-Adapting the Strong SAR Foundation Models That Are Already in Your MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22273v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22273

**作者：** Yuxuan Li、Zichen Tian、Qianru Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22273v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22273v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SARVIA，用可计算答案的自监督视觉问题对 MLLM 做短程后预训练，再导出适配后的视觉编码器作 SAR 下游骨干。

**排除原因：** 工作核心是 SAR 领域适配与预训练成本，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；问答式训练只是适配目标，与 latent 视觉信息的保留和使用无具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SARVIA: Fast-Adapting the Strong SAR Foundation Models That Are Already in Your MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) foundation models provide transferable representations for diverse perception tasks in all-weather Earth observation. However, their development often relies on costly large-scale SAR pretraining, raising the question of whether such extensive domain-specific pretraining is necessary. We find that modern multimodal large language models (MLLMs) already provide visual encoders that can outperform dedicated SAR foundation models as downstream initializations. This motivates a new paradigm: reuse general visual capabilities and devote domain training to brief specialization. We therefore introduce SARVIA, an adaptive post-pretraining method that specializes modern MLLMs using self-supervised visual questions with computable answers. These questions are constructed from unlabeled observations through general image tasks that capture spatial relations and SAR-specific tasks that exploit native measurements and SAR--optical correspondence. A shared answer-prediction objective jointly trains low-rank adapters across visual and language components, with loss-driven task sampling. We then export the adapted visual encoder as a standalone backbone. With 600 adaptation steps and 2 GPU-hours, SARVIA outperforms the compared foundation models on all six SAR detection and segmentation benchmarks. Compared with dedicated SAR pretraining, SARVIA reduces domain-training costs by orders of magnitude while improving visual transfer across architectures and scales. This new paradigm shifts the emphasis from repeated, costly pretraining to a reusable specialization process that can benefit from advances in open MLLMs. Dataset and code: https://github.com/zcablii/SARVIA.

</details>

### 324. [SeasonRainBench: Global Seasonal Precipitation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22281v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22281

**作者：** Qiuyan Sun、Mengxuan Chen、Haoyuan Liang、Guowen Li、Zhen Huang、Jianhao Lin、Juepeng Zheng、Haohuan Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22281v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22281v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建全球季节降水预报基准 SeasonRainBench（1–5 个月提前量、含多个业务中心与气候模式模拟数据），并提出配套指标与一个从模拟数据向真实地球迁移的预报模型。

**排除原因：** 属于地球系统预报的基准与业务模型工作，其「模拟预训练 + 迁移」线索是通用域迁移，未涉及视觉或多模态推理内部状态、latent 生成与证据使用，缺乏可核查的 LVR 联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SeasonRainBench: Global Seasonal Precipitation

**abstract/A1：** Accurate seasonal precipitation forecasts help societies prepare for drought, flooding, and crop failure months in advance. While data-driven methods have made rapid progress in weather and subseasonal forecasting, operational seasonal precipitation prediction still relies on numerical ensemble systems. These systems are computationally expensive and exhibit biases and drift that increase with lead time, motivating more efficient data-driven alternatives. Yet this transition faces three linked challenges: comparing data-driven forecasts with operational ones requires (1) a global benchmark that does not exist, scoring them requires (2) metrics that capture operational priorities beyond anomaly correlation (ACC) and RMSE, and training them must (3) rely on a single, short observational record. To address the first two challenges, we introduce \\BenchName, the first such benchmark, covering monthly precipitation forecasts 1--5 months ahead. It pairs global $1^\\circ$ satellite precipitation with oceanic and atmospheric predictors, includes seven operational centers, and provides 310,944 simulated months from 52 climate models for training. Beyond standard scores, we introduce two operationally motivated metrics for anomaly strength and monthly rain-belt position. To address the third challenge, we develop \\MODEL, which learns from simulations what the record lacks and transfers it to Earth through three modules: SimForced aligns the climate background, SimMemory transfers a history encoder pretrained on simulations, and SimAnalog supplements Earth labels with simulated analog outcomes. Extensive experiments show that \\MODEL{} improves ACC, CRPS, anomaly strength, and rain-belt position by 12.0\\%, 3.6\\%, 2.9\\%, and 0.3\\% over the strongest operational or data-driven model, opening a path from simulated worlds to Earth system prediction.

</details>

### 325. [Breaking Visual Conventions: Spectrally Coupled Weight Perturbation for Creative Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22318v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22318

**作者：** Fu Feng、Ruoheng Zhou、Yucheng Xie、Xu Yang、Jing Wang、Xin Geng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22318v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22318v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CosPert，一种免训练方法：对注意力投影的奇异向量因子施加耦合随机扰动，并按去噪阶段调度扰动强度，以提升文生图的新颖性与多样性。

**排除原因：** 论文关注图像生成的视觉创意与质量，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选或推理期计算分配，权重扰动线索与 LVR 机制缺乏具体可核查的联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Breaking Visual Conventions: Spectrally Coupled Weight Perturbation for Creative Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image (T2I) models often produce high-quality images that follow familiar visual conventions, even in response to open-ended prompts such as \`\`a creative \[CLS\]''. Existing methods encourage novelty by exploring semantic conditions or modifying features and guidance during denoising. Pretrained weights offer a complementary source of creative variation, but unstructured perturbations can have little visual effect or degrade image quality. We introduce Coupled Spectral Perturbation (CosPert), a training-free method that targets attention projections in layers identified by a one-time sensitivity analysis. For each projection, CosPert uses a shared random transformation and its transpose to couple updates to the singular-vector factors while perturbing the spectral gains. A common relative budget calibrates the magnitude of weight changes across projections, and a sigmoid schedule concentrates their influence in early denoising and gradually attenuates them during refinement. CosPert provides plug-and-play creativity enhancement at inference time. Once the perturbations are prepared, sampling requires no auxiliary networks or additional model evaluations. On both FLUX.1 and Stable Diffusion 3.5, CosPert enhances visual creativity, producing more varied object structures while preserving category identity. Across 50 object categories, CosPert achieves the highest mean CREward among the evaluated methods while maintaining strong prompt alignment.

</details>

### 326. [Fair Bubble Sort: Provably Optimal Fair Ranking with Continuous Sensitive Attributes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22352v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22352

**作者：** Antonio Ferrara、Fabio Vitale、Atsushi Miyauchi、Francesco Bonchi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY、cs.DS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22352v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22352v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究连续敏感属性下的公平排序：提出基于 Kendall 相关的 CPP 公平性约束，以及通过定向相邻交换、以 Kemeny 距离最小为目标的后处理算法 Fair Bubble Sort，并给出最优性与近似保证。

**排除原因：** 该工作属公平排序的组合优化与理论分析，不涉及视觉或多模态表示、生成式推理状态、训练监督或证据获取与停止决策，「相邻交换」等线索也仅是排序算法本身，与 LVR 问题缺乏实质可借鉴联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fair Bubble Sort: Provably Optimal Fair Ranking with Continuous Sensitive Attributes

**abstract/A1：** Most fair-ranking methods assume categorical protected attributes, requiring continuous attributes such as age or income to be discretized into groups. This introduces arbitrary thresholds, loses fine-grained information, and ignores the natural ordering of the attribute. In this paper, we study the problem of fair ranking with continuous protected attributes without relying on thresholding. We introduce Continuous Pairwise Parity (CPP), a fairness notion based on Kendall correlation between ranking positions and the protected attribute, and seek rankings that satisfy a prescribed CPP constraint while minimizing their Kemeny distance from an initial score-based ranking. To solve this problem, we propose Fair Bubble Sort (FBS), an efficient post-processing algorithm based on targeted adjacent swaps. We prove that FBS is exactly optimal for the unweighted Kemeny distance and provide approximation guarantees for the score-weighted setting. FBS is output-sensitive, with running time determined by the number of swaps required to meet the fairness constraint. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that FBS is scalable and consistently outperforms discretization-based baselines, achieving a superior fairness-accuracy Pareto front.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 9 figures, 4 tables. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. Code: https://github.com/Ambress92/FairBubbleSort

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22368

**作者：** Kangjie Huang、Yucheng Xin、Xinchun Wang、Dong Liu、Yan Fan、Shuming Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22368v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22368v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文考察机械臂在等待与保持参考系变化下视觉伺服恢复（CAD 反馈 vs 地标）的完成率差异，属机器人抓取与位姿跟踪评测；「等待」指物理保持时长而非推理停止决策，也未涉及 VLM 内部状态表示、证据使用或 latent 生成，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Seeing What Still Holds: How Waiting Control Changes Cooperative Recovery

**abstract/A1：** Changing the holding reference during a fixed-duration wait can reverse the ranking of visual recovery methods. We introduce AuditEmbodied, a paired execution and trajectory evaluation framework that independently varies holding references and waiting duration while keeping recovery executors frozen. A 960-execution factorial on 20 initialization seeds compares CAD-feedback and landmark-based recovery from identical post-wait physical states within each holding and waiting condition. Protected completion requires successful recovery under the declared execution constraints. At a 1 s wait with registered wrist references, their protected completion counts change from 6/40 versus 19/40 under registered holding to 34/40 versus 22/40 under position-only holding. Trajectory analysis localizes the main separation to grasp formation: small observation-time target residuals precede large tracking errors. Joint diagnostics and controlled interventions characterize persistent tracking constraints. On fresh seeds, gradual position reanchoring improves CAD recovery's long-wait completion by 42.5 percentage points and reduces mean complete recovery time by 6.06 s relative to registered holding. Additional evaluations with pretrained policies, handover, and a mass-matched geometry characterize holding-reference sensitivity across executors and physical regimes. These findings establish holding reference as an explicit evaluation condition for comparing visual recovery performance.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3figures

</details>

### 328. [CutAgent: Creative-Gene-Driven Planning and Experience-Guided Self-Refinement for High-CTR E-commerce Advertising Video Creation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22418v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22418

**作者：** Xuyue Huang、Guanbo Huang、Jiasong Feng、Run Ling、Ao Ma、Xi Zhao、Yaoyu Li、Jie Gao、Ke Cao、Wei Feng、Zhanjie Zhang、Hao Wang、Jinghe Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22418v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22418v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CutAgent 双智能体框架，将电商广告视频创作建模为「规划—执行—自演化」流程，通过「创意基因」连接产品理解与视频制作，并用高分 CTR 数据训练与记忆反思迭代。

**排除原因：** 工作重心是广告视频的脚本规划、素材检索与渲染流程，其「创意基因」与失败经验记忆属制作侧结构化表示，未涉及视觉或多模态推理内部状态、证据筛选与 latent 生成，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CutAgent: Creative-Gene-Driven Planning and Experience-Guided Self-Refinement for High-CTR E-commerce Advertising Video Creation

**abstract/A1：** E-commerce advertising video creation requires both high-level creative planning and reliable multi-modal production. However, existing methods often map product attributes directly to scripts or editing decisions without explicitly modeling advertising strategies, narrative structures, and production styles. They also have limited ability to reuse past production failures. To address these limitations, we propose $\\textbf{CutAgent}$, a dual-agent framework that formulates advertising video creation as a Plan--Execute--Self-Evolve process. CutAgent comprises two collaborative agents, $\\textbf{GeneAgent}$ and $\\textbf{VideoAgent}$, connected by a structured creative gene that bridges product understanding and video production. GeneAgent maps multi-modal product inputs---textual attributes together with product images---to creative genes that specify narrative units and production rules. To train GeneAgent, we construct $\\textbf{CutAgent-70K}$ with approximately 70K samples of product attributes/images, reverse-extracted creative genes, cleaned clip pools, and real-world CTR, and apply high-CTR supervised fine-tuning followed by CTR-guided GRPO. Guided by the generated gene, VideoAgent retrieves category-then-gene failure--correction experiences from a long-term memory as constraints for shot-script planning, then coordinates material retrieval, narration, subtitles, music, and timeline rendering. Independently, a reflection loop diagnoses quality defects, revises deficient production decisions, and writes verified corrections back to memory for later retrieval. Experiments on $\\textbf{CutAgent-Eval}$ show that CutAgent outperforms representative baselines in e-commerce advertising video creation, achieving the highest online CTR of $\\textbf{2.21\\%}$.

</details>

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 MedImgAnnotator，一个半自动批量医学图像标注与预处理工具，集编辑、掩膜匹配、目标检测与插件管理于一体，并以标注耗时和易用性评估其效率提升。

**排除原因：** 论文属标注工具工程与效率评估，未涉及 VLM 内部状态、推理生成或证据筛选决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MedImgAnnotator: batch wise medical image annotation and preprocessing tool

**abstract/A1：** The curation of large cohort datasets for developing deep learning models demands annotating or editing over the AI-predicted labels. However, openly available annotation tools are time-consuming, as they involve complex navigational steps and do not support batch processing. This study aimed to develop a simple, fast, semiautomated batch-annotation tool named MedImgAnnotator. The tool was created by integrating editing tools, image mask pair matching logic, preprocessing functions, object detection tool, and a plugin management system. Its performance was evaluated in terms of active annotation time and ease of use by the developer and 12 users using open-source MR and CT scans. Additionally, the tool had been used by the MICCAI 2026 CARE Challenge liver segmentation best performance winner to annotate liver masks from 6412 MRI volumes. The evaluation results showed that MedImgAnnotator reduced active annotation time from 206.5+/-32.87 (average active annotation time of openly available tools) seconds to 59 seconds. The Wilcoxon signed-rank test of 12 users evaluation results recorded P values of 0.032 and 0.008 for the active annotation time and higher ease of use rating metrics, respectively, indicating that the current tool significantly reduces annotation time and enhances ease of use. Moreover, the tool utilization for annotating different regions of interest from different modality scans and body regions shows its usability across modalities and areas of interest. MedImgAnnotator, which significantly reduces active annotation time for large imaging cohorts, is available as an executable file and as a Python script. Access link: https://ewunateassaye.github.io/developed-tools/medimgannotator.html

</details>

**排除原因：** 虽然讨论 latent 分布与解码，但对象是图像生成中的 tokenizer 解码器而非 VLM 推理状态，未涉及视觉细节保留、推理轨迹、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sample-Aligned Decoders for Latent Generative Models

**abstract/A1：** Latent generative models synthesize images by decoding generated latents, yet their decoder is trained only to reconstruct encoder latents and never sees the latents it decodes at test time, so the residual errors of the generator are rendered directly into the final image. Existing remedies retrain the generator, add cost at every sampling step, or make the decoder robust to perturbed real latents, assuming that generator errors resemble such perturbations. We find that they do not. Generated latents lie on the encoder latent manifold as closely as real ones, yet follow a different distribution. The decoder should therefore be adapted to the distribution of generated latents, which raises two challenges: (1) unpaired supervision, since a generated latent has no ground-truth image, and (2) latent-space drift, since a decoder freed from paired targets forgets the latent space it must decode. We propose Sample-Aligned Decoders (SAD), a post-training framework that fine-tunes only the decoder of a frozen generator at no inference cost. Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment matches decoded generated latents to unpaired real images in a frozen foundation-model feature space, and Encoder Anchor Preservation keeps the decoder close to the original one on encoder latents. Our analysis shows that the alignment is effective only on generated latents, where it corrects each sample in proportion to how much more often the generator produces such latents than the encoder; on encoder latents it can only work against the anchor. SAD improves four state-of-the-art ImageNet generators across three tokenizers, e.g., FID-50K from 1.15 to 0.98 for REPA-E, complements discriminator guidance, and extends to text-to-image generation. Our code and models are available at https://torpedo2003.github.io/SAD/.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://torpedo2003.github.io/SAD/

</details>

**排除原因：** 主题为图像发布前的防护性扰动生成，属图像生成与版权防护方向，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、视觉证据使用或 latent 推理机制，也无可迁移至 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fast and Strong Image Protection against Diffusion Personalization

**abstract/A1：** Personalized text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce a person from a handful of photographs. Image protection perturbs each image before publication so that fine-tuning on it fails, but it optimizes every perturbation from scratch, so its cost grows with the number of images. We present the protective diffusion model (PDM), which is trained once and samples the perturbation of a new image over many denoising steps. PDM distills an iterative teacher and is then post-trained with the target residual projection (TRP) loss as a reward, a stronger objective than existing ones. PDM outperforms nine baselines on faces and artworks and nearly matches its teacher at a tenth of the cost.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EfficientVPR: Toward Efficient Visual Place Recognition via Scene-Aware Prompt Tuning and Adaptive Feature Enhancement

**abstract/A1：** Visual place recognition (VPR) faces critical challenges in handling extreme environmental variations while meeting the computational constraints of practical applications. Current methods predominantly address these challenges by either scaling up model capacity or employing computationally intensive reranking stages, creating a significant efficiency bottleneck. To overcome this limitation, we propose EfficientVPR, a lightweight one-stage framework that achieves speed-accuracy trade-offs through two key innovations: i) a scene-aware visual prompt tuning method which adapts pretrained features with less parameters while dynamically adjusting to sample-specific characteristics, and ii) an instance-dependent key local feature enhancement module that further reinforces discriminative regions. Comprehensive evaluations on Pitts250k, MSLS, Eynsham, AmsterTime and SVOX demonstrate that our method establishes a new SOTA for DINOv2-small models by outperforming all same-scale competitors, and delivers a $73\\times$ speedup with 60\\% lower-dimensional features while maintaining competitive (within 2.5% average R@1 gap) against the SOTA DINOv2-large-based two-stage method. Our code will be available at https://github.com/WiniTang/EfficientVPR.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 11 figures, accepted at CVPR 2026

### 334. [ATS: Anti-Aliased Triangle Splatting with Exact Per-Primitive Integrals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22512

**作者：** Brent Zoomers、Joni Vanherck、Leander Winters、Jeroen Put、Lode Jorissen、Nick Michiels

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究半透明三角形的场景表示渲染，给出窗口在像素上的精确闭式积分及梯度计算，用于抗锯齿、保守剔除和多尺度训练。

**排除原因：** 属图形学抗锯齿与图元剔除技术，不涉及多模态内部表示、推理状态生成或证据与停止决策，未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ATS: Anti-Aliased Triangle Splatting with Exact Per-Primitive Integrals

**abstract/A1：** Triangle Splatting represents scenes using semi-transparent triangles, but it evaluates each triangle at the pixel center and culls any triangle with an inradius smaller than one pixel. The former causes severe aliasing, while the latter culls most of the scene at lower resolutions. We show that the integral of the Triangle Splatting window over a pixel admits an exact closed-form solution. The angle bisectors split the triangle into three sub-triangles, in each of which the window is a power of the distance to one edge. Each piece therefore has a closed-form antiderivative, and Green's theorem reduces the integral and its gradients to one term per side of the sub-triangle clipped against the pixel. The integral over the whole triangle, which we show has a closed form, then allows conservative culling, removing only triangles that cannot meet the renderer's alpha cutoff at any pixel. This exact formulation anti-aliases existing models without retraining, exhibits less temporal aliasing than 2x2 supersampling, and enables multi-scale training, matching or outperforming supersampled training at reduced resolutions.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 7 figures, 9 tables

</details>
