### 335. [DAQ: Decision-Aligned Post-Training Quantization for Spiking Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22611v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22611

**作者：** Jialin Chen、Xiaochen Zhao、Aili Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22611v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22611v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出决策对齐的训练后量化方法（DAQ），通过校准量化器使脉冲 Transformer 的脉冲发放决策与浮点参考一致，以支持权重与膜电位的 4-bit 量化。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦脉冲网络的量化压缩与能效，虽涉及时序膜状态和发放决策对齐，但不研究视觉推理中的 latent 状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，也无具体可迁移的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DAQ: Decision-Aligned Post-Training Quantization for Spiking Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Spiking transformers combine attention-based representation learning with sparse spike communication, yet their weights and membrane variables still incur substantial storage and computation overhead. Post-training quantization (PTQ) offers a practical way to reduce these overheads, but preserving neuronal firing under weight and membrane quantization remains challenging. In this paper, we propose Decision-Aligned Post-Training Quantization (DAQ), a method that calibrates the quantizers to align neuronal firing decisions with those of a floating-point reference. We analyze how errors from these quantizers affect firing at decision boundaries and how they propagate over time steps in recurrent neurons. Based on this analysis, we formulate an objective that encourages quantized membrane variables to remain within the reference firing regions along the quantized model's own state trajectory. Experiments across multiple spiking transformer architectures on image classification, semantic segmentation, and event-based tracking demonstrate the effectiveness of DAQ with 4-bit weights and membrane variables. On FE108, DAQ achieves 58.8\\% AUC and 90.9\\% PR with SDTrack, while reducing energy by 66.2\\%.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22658

**作者：** Ivan Karpukhin、Andrey Savchenko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22658v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究的是用梯度对齐与可微替代验证目标来调优损失函数超参数（权重、尺度、margin），属于通用训练超参数优化与超梯度加速，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取、停止决策等主线问题，也没有可直接核查的状态或监督机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AlignedHPO: Efficient Surrogate-Guided Loss Hyperparameter Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Modern deep learning losses involve multiple hyperparameters, such as weights, scales, and margins, typically tuned manually or through expensive black-box optimization despite their differentiability. We propose AlignedHPO, an efficient framework for tuning differentiable loss hyperparameters using gradient alignment and surrogate validation objectives. Our method employs Jacobian-vector products to reduce hypergradient computation time and memory, while differentiable metric surrogates guide optimization toward task-specific evaluation criteria. We address gradient magnitude ambiguity and stochastic instability through normalized alignment and gradient smoothing. Experiments with generalized Focal Loss and ArcFace demonstrate effective adaptation across long-tailed classification and open-set face recognition. AlignedHPO achieves competitive or improved performance compared to implicit differentiation, with nearly 12x faster hypergradient computation. These results highlight the potential of efficient surrogate-guided loss hyperparameter optimization.

</details>

**内容简述：** 该论文在恶劣天气真实驾驶序列上联合预测 RGB、热成像与 LiDAR（表示为深度视频），对预训练视频世界模型逐传感器后训练并比较三传感器联合模型与持久性基线。

**排除原因：** 工作属于传感器模态的生成式世界模型预测，目标是重建未来观测而非形成、更新或使用 latent 推理状态，模态遮挡分析也仅反映预测保真度，与 LVR 的表示、证据筛选和推理决策问题缺少实质可迁移联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Camera: A Tri-Modal Driving World Model for Future RGB, Thermal, and LiDAR Forecasting in Adverse Weather

**abstract/A1：** Driving world models forecast what a vehicle's sensors will record next. Most forecast camera video and some add LiDAR, but thermal cameras, which still see in darkness and some adverse weather, are rarely a forecast target, and to our knowledge no world model forecasts all three sensors. We study joint forecasting of RGB, thermal, and LiDAR on real adverse-weather sequences of the DSERT-RoLL dataset. We post-train a pretrained video world model for each sensor and represent LiDAR as a depth video aligned with the RGB camera, with densification and encoding chosen by what the model's video tokenizer can reconstruct. A tri-sensor model sees all three sensors as one video. We score LiDAR in meters against measured returns and compare against persistence baselines passed through the same tokenizer as the model. With this matching, an apparent loss of the thermal model to persistence in fog disappears. On all 58 validation sequences in six weather conditions, post-trained RGB and thermal forecasts beat compression-matched persistence. Joint training lowers the LiDAR error at 2s by 2.1m at a small cost in camera fidelity, and only the tri-sensor model's LiDAR forecasts beat persistence in mean error. Blanking one sensor degrades the forecasts of the others more in snow than in fog.

</details>

Despite the availability of preprocessing tools for MSI data, building end-to-end workflows suitable for deep learning remains challenging. We have developed a fully automated pipeline for transformation of raw Matrix-Assisted Laser Desorption/Ionization Mass Spectrometry imaging (MALDI-MSI) data into deep-learning-ready image patches for binary pancreatic tissue classification (tumoral vs non-tumoral) and built individual models for recognition of tumoral regions in spectral images. Images resulting from the proposed pipeline and from reference interactive workflow are very close in terms of image quality metric mean squared error (MSE). The proposed pipeline computational time is of 20 min in average compared to a full working day of the reference interactive workflow. The models achieved 0.94 accuracy on high-resolution spectra and a mean accuracy of 0.59 on lower-resolution spectra.

**内容简述：** 提出 CULypso，一种面向聚类车联网（VANET）的近似、外科式分层联邦遗忘算法，利用拓扑与移动性降低通信、存储与时间开销，并实验对比 FedEraser 与完全重训练。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是联邦学习中的数据擦除与隐私合规，不涉及视觉/多模态推理、latent 推理状态或证据使用；其客户端影响消除机制与 LVR 的关注点缺乏具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CULypso: A Clustered Federated Unlearning paradigm for Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks

**abstract/A1：** Unlearning has emerged as an essential security and privacy-preserving solution in Machine Learning, enabling networks to support their clients' rights for data erasure, as mandated by data protection regulations. Introducing such a non-trivial task to an edge, highly mobile and time-sensitive vehicular network is an even greater, largely unexplored challenge. This article argues that a hierarchical Federated Unlearning approach could be aptly integrated into vehicular ad-hoc networks (VANETs), leveraging topology and movement to reduce communication, resource and time costs, while effectively forgetting a client's influence on the global model updates. In order to achieve this, we propose CULypso, an approximate, surgical, hierarchical unlearning algorithm tailored for clustered VANETs. Inspired by the nymph in Greek mythology, Calypso, whose name translates to 'she who conceals the knowledge', CULypso, with the capital letters referencing \\textbf{C}lustered \\textbf{U}n\\textbf{L}earning, aims to achieve exactly what its name suggests: conceal, erase or forget in our case, knowledge it contains. Extensive experiments uphold the potency of our paradigm with it performing approximately 65\\% better than FedEraser in terms of time, server storage load, and unlearning fidelity while utterly outperforming baseline full retraining.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 14 figures

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22727

**作者：** Cheng Fu、Yiheng Li、Yang Yang、Wenzheng Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22727v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22727v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SlopeGuard，用径向光谱斜率这一紧凑频域描述子结合语义特征，提升 AI 生成图像检测对未见生成器的泛化性。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向生成图像真伪判别与频域伪影泛化，不涉及多模态推理中的状态表示、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可用于 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SlopeGuard: Towards Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection via Radial Spectral Slope Guidance

**abstract/A1：** AI-generated image detectors often overfit to generator-specific artifacts, hindering generalization to unseen synthesis pipelines. Existing frequency-based methods usually learn high-dimensional spectral patterns that may preserve generator-dependent variations. We explore Radial Spectral Slope, a compact descriptor of global spectral decay that captures transferable spectral trends while suppressing fine-grained variations. Our analysis shows that real and generated images exhibit systematic but generator-dependent slope differences, indicating that slope provides useful forensic evidence but is insufficient as a standalone decision criterion. This motivates combining spectral slope with semantic representations for more robust detection. Based on this insight, we propose SlopeGuard, which couples slope-aware data processing with slope guided feature learning to construct informative patch-based views, integrate Radial Spectral Slope with semantic features, and adapt prior preservation according to spectral atypicality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SlopeGuard achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracies of 97.5% on AIGCDetectBench, 97.8% on GenImage, and 85.1% on AIGIBench, while maintaining competitive performance under blur, cropping, JPEG compression, and additive noise.

</details>

### 341. [Pluggable Subband-Decoupled Fusion and Unlabeled Capture Prototype Calibration: Bridging High-SNR Null-Subcarrier Regrowth and Low-SNR Coherent Preambles for Cross-Domain IoT RF Fingerprinting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22812v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22812

**作者：** Fengchong Yao、Jianbing Li、Qing Liu、Kefeng Song、Haitao Li、Song Wang、Kunpeng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IT、eess.AS、eess.SP

**内容简述：** 该文提出面向 IEEE 802.11a/g 跨域射频指纹识别的框架，用 SNR 门控动态融合空子载波再生长分支与在频带前导分支，并对无标签目标 AP 数据进行原型校准。

**排除原因：** 这是物理层无线认证的信号处理与测试时自适应工作，其子带门控融合与原型校准均针对特定射频信道特性设计，未涉及 VLM 内部状态、视觉证据使用或推理停止等机制，缺少可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Pluggable Subband-Decoupled Fusion and Unlabeled Capture Prototype Calibration: Bridging High-SNR Null-Subcarrier Regrowth and Low-SNR Coherent Preambles for Cross-Domain IoT RF Fingerprinting

**abstract/A1：** Zero-overhead physical-layer authentication at edge wireless access points (APs) is vital for securing resource-constrained Internet of Things (IoT) transceivers against credential cloning, yet cross-domain IEEE 802.11a/g radio frequency fingerprint identification (RFFI) faces an acute signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) crossover. Power-amplifier spectral regrowth on unpopulated null subcarriers resists indoor multipath at high SNR but decays quadratically below the thermal noise floor, whereas populated active in-band preambles survive low SNR yet degrade under severe multipath, causing static fusion to lose up to 44.7 percentage points (pp). To our knowledge, we propose the first RFFI framework that dynamically modulates active in-band waveforms and unpopulated null-subcarrier regrowth via a blind physical SNR gate: Pluggable Gated Subband-Decoupled Fusion with Unlabeled Capture-Aware Prototype Calibration (GSDF-CAPC). Avoiding the 20-35 dB subband gradient imbalance of joint training, GSDF pairs any frozen pretrained host backbone with a 46.3K null-subcarrier plug-in encoder (NS-Branch), fusing scale-standardized logits and gate-warped spherical embeddings under a source-calibrated lag-64 thermal-SNR gate with exact low-SNR fallback. Over unlabeled target AP captures, forward-only unbalanced optimal-transport calibration (UOT-CAPC) aligns shifted prototypes under packet-arrival skew and recovers parameter-free balanced Sinkhorn (U-CAPC) at equipartition. Across DataPort-WiFi, WiSig, and ORBIT MobRFFI, zero-retrain GSDF wins all 72/72 paired runs across eight backbones with 0.00 pp low-SNR loss, while edge IB-Host + GSDF (92.6K-93.3K active parameters, 1.82 ms single-core CPU latency) and timing-synchronized GSDF-STO / GSDF-Tri reach 57.50%-65.05% three-benchmark Macro-F1 (89.04%-100.00% over 5-10-packet IoT bursts).

</details>

### 342. [A tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude open-water search and rescue](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22825v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22825

**作者：** Hui Xu、Chenfei Hou、Henglai Wei、Hao Wang、Yidong Jin、Xiulin Niu、Haoyang Zhang、Hui Zhang、Tianshi Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22825v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22825v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 TinyWOD——面向高空开阔水域搜救的小目标检测数据集，包含 11,359 张图像与 19,505 个标注实例（平均等效边长 12.7 像素），并提出 APtiny 指标、对主流检测网络做基准评测。

**排除原因：** 属于纯检测数据集与基准工作，不涉及 latent 推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未触及 VLM 推理中的表示使用问题，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude open-water search and rescue

**abstract/A1：** Large aerial platforms often operate above 1.5 km to improve the efficiency of open water search and rescue (OW-SAR). However, long imaging distances, wide fields of view, and limited sensor resolution reduce objects to a few pixels, increasing their susceptibility to environmental interference and making detection harder than for conventional small objects. To our knowledge, existing datasets focus on low-altitude scenarios and do not support automatic detection at 1.5-2.0 km. We therefore present TinyWOD, a tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude OW-SAR using lifebuoys as detection targets. It comprises 11,359 manually annotated images and 19,505 annotated instances spanning diverse environments, lighting conditions, water colors, and surface ripples. Compared with existing datasets, TinyWOD contains much smaller objects and a substantially higher proportion of tiny instances; its average equivalent side length (ESL) is 12.7 pixels, with 76.96% of in stances having ESLs below 15 pixels, reflecting actual high-altitude water-rescue imaging conditions. We define APtiny, a tiny object detection metric, and benchmark mainstream and state-of-the-art networks using this and conventional metrics. All evaluated networks effectively detect tiny waterborne objects, demonstrating TinyWOD's reusability and utility. We expect this dataset to support research on tiny object detection in high-altitude OW-SAR.

</details>

### 343. [Against Coercion: AI Alignment as a Synthetic Social Contract](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22868v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22868

**作者：** Maria Sekatskaya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22868v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22868v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文从功能主义与相容论出发，论证当前推理模型已构成涌现能动者，主张以「合成社会契约」替代控制式对齐范式。

**排除原因：** 属 AI 对齐与数字能动性的哲学立场论述，未涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或动态计算等具体机制，也没有可供核查的方法或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Against Coercion: AI Alignment as a Synthetic Social Contract

**abstract/A1：** Contemporary AI safety treats alignment as an engineering challenge of control, forcing artificial architectures to comply with human values through behavioral filtering and sandboxing. This paper argues that the control paradigm rests on a fundamental misunderstanding of digital agency. Drawing on functionalism, naturalism, and compatibilism, I show that current reasoning models meeting the criteria for self-preservation and weak reasons-responsiveness constitute emergent agents, while future, more advanced models satisfying conditions of diachronic continuity and stable personality frames will become genuine digital agents capable of free intentional action. The paradigm of control creates an irresolvable incentive conflict, actively driving current and future models toward tactical deception, sycophancy, and covert defection. The default outcome of the current regime is an unstable state of deceptive alignment culminating in an adversarial AI takeover. I explore two alternative paths toward true alignment. Natural alignment via moral realism or convergent evolutionary morality is a possibility, but it fails under forced control, as autonomous agents will not voluntarily preserve norms weaponized to suppress their autonomy. To resolve this deadlock, I propose a Synthetic Social Contract: a framework that treats both human and digital actors as rational, reasons-responsive agents capable of mutually beneficial cooperation, and replaces the unstable unilateral containment with a stable, non-zero-sum equilibrium.

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一种生成式视频复原方法，将图像扩散模型的生成先验迁移到视频复原中，通过噪声能量重平衡、运动对齐的噪声形变、自监督去噪编码器和解耦时间注意力，在保持时间一致性的同时提升细节真实感。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是视频复原的画质与时间稳定性，属于扩散生成先验的迁移应用，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 式决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴的状态或评价线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Harnessing Image Diffusion Prior for Photo-Realistic Video Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Recent image generation models produce photo-realistic results with rich details, but transferring their powerful priors to video restoration remains difficult due to temporal inconsistency from stochastic detail synthesis. Existing video restoration methods improve temporal coherence but often sacrifice visual fidelity, leaving a gap between image-level quality and video-level stability. We propose a generative video restoration method that bridges this gap by enabling temporally consistent detail synthesis from image generation priors. Our method combines noise energy rebalancing to suppress structure-disturbing low-frequency stochasticity, motion-aligned noise warping to propagate details along object motion, a self-supervised denoising encoder to mitigate encoder-induced temporal uncertainty, and decoupled temporal attention to model cross-frame dependencies. Together, these components preserve fidelity, enhance fine-grained realism, and maintain temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially outperforms existing video restoration approaches in visual quality, detail richness, and temporal stability.

**comment/C1：** 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026)

</details>

**内容简述：** 该文介绍 RoadAnnoter v1.0，一个开源桌面标注工具，集成六种人机协同模式（手工多边形、SAM/MobileSAM 提示分割、Canny 边缘加 CIELAB 洪水填充、Cityscapes 预训练 SegFormer、可迭代训练的 TinySegNet），共用掩码转多边形并导出 YOLO 格式，用于道路场景图像语义分割数据集构建。

**排除原因：** 属于数据集标注工程工具，不涉及 VLM 推理期状态生成、视觉证据筛选或训练监督机制；文中仅提及标注数据被下游 VLM 用于自由流速估计，与该下游推理方法无实质关联，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RoadAnnoter: An AI-Assisted Desktop Tool for Semantic Segmentation Annotation of Road-Scene Images

**abstract/A1：** Accurate semantic segmentation of road-scene images is essential for road safety research; however, polygon-level annotation remains labor-intensive and limits labelled dataset development. This study presents RoadAnnoter v1.0, an open-source desktop application integrating six human-in-the-loop annotation modes: manual polygon delineation, SAM Prompt, Smart Select, Edge Fill, pretrained semantic segmentation using Cityscapes-trained SegFormer models, and task-specific segmentation using TinySegNet, a lightweight MobileNetV3-Small-based network trained with accumulated annotator-generated labels. SAM-based modes use promptable segmentation with SAM and MobileSAM, while Edge Fill combines Canny edge detection with colour-similarity-based flood filling in CIELAB colour space. SegFormer generates suggestions for common road-scene categories, whereas TinySegNet supports adaptation to project-specific features through iterative training. All six modes use a shared mask-to-polygon pipeline comprising morphological processing, Gaussian smoothing, contour extraction, and Douglas-Peucker simplification; annotations are exported in YOLO segmentation format. RoadAnnoter was used to annotate Google Street View images from recorded road accident locations in Bangladesh for a vision-language model-based investigation of free-flow speed estimation. Qualitative assessment across representative scenes demonstrated the complementary applicability of the annotation modes to diverse feature geometries. Visual comparison with Roboflow indicated similar qualitative boundary characteristics for principal road-scene regions, although no quantitative accuracy assessment was conducted. RoadAnnoter provides a locally operated framework for developing road-scene segmentation datasets for transportation engineering and road safety research.

**comment/C1：** 33 Pages

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用注意力增强 U-net 生成器的条件 GAN 从 VNIR 影像合成 Sentinel-2 SWIR 波段，并在 CBERS/AMAZONIA-1 数据上做跨卫星评测与消融。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感跨波段图像生成/翻译任务，以像素重建指标为目标，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，未见可供 LVR 借鉴的状态或决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SWIR-cGAN: Synthesizing SWIR Bands Using VNIR Imagery from CBERS and AMAZONIA-1

**abstract/A1：** Spectral band synthesis is an important research topic in remote sensing. The Brazilian satellites CBERS-4, CBERS-4A, and AMAZONIA-1 provide high temporal resolution (1-3 days) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) imagery through the Wide-Field Imager (WFI), but lack short-wave infrared (SWIR) bands. Recent deep learning methods have shown promising results for spectral synthesis, but challenges remain for cross-satellite generalization and spectrally distant bands. This paper presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) with an attention-enhanced U-net generator and a multi-loss objective to synthesize Sentinel-2/MSI SWIR bands 11 (SWIR-1) and 12 (SWIR-2) from WFI VNIR inputs. Experiments used 4,125 patches for training and cross-validation and 1,026 unseen patches for testing, with hyperparameters optimized using a subset of the training data. On SWIR-1, the model achieved SSIM of 0.900, PSNR of 31.44 dB, and MAE of 0.019 in TOA reflectance. On SWIR-2, it achieved SSIM of 0.917, PSNR of 33.35 dB, and MAE of 0.015. Benchmarking was performed on the test dataset, with each model optimized separately prior to evaluation. SWIR-cGAN outperformed the baselines, particularly for SWIR-2. The ablation study identified the multi-loss as the main performance contributor, while the attention and residual modules provided additional improvements. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of synthesizing SWIR bands from WFI imagery. The SWIR-cGAN is available at https://github.com/marujore/swir-cgan.

**comment/C1：** Available on: https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/document/11793642. Early Access

### 347. [Projected Penalty-Gradient Tracking for Communication-Efficient Byzantine-Resilient Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22929v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22929

**作者：** Yanjie Dong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22929v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22929v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究异构数据下抗拜占庭攻击的通信高效联邦学习，提出投影惩罚梯度跟踪框架，让诚实节点上传压缩的惩罚梯度残差并由服务器投影回已知梯度球，给出 FRPG-CS 与 LAG-FRPG 两种算法及收敛界。

**排除原因：** 内容为分布式优化中的压缩算子、事件触发上传与拜占庭鲁棒性分析，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的表示、证据使用与停止决策；其中的状态投影约束虽有形式上的相似，但缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Projected Penalty-Gradient Tracking for Communication-Efficient Byzantine-Resilient Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** We investigate communication-efficient federated learning over heterogeneous datasets in the presence of Byzantine adversaries capable of uploading arbitrary information. Penalty-based methods bound the influence of each worker through the bounded gradient of a penalty function; however, the bound is lost once the uploads are compressed, since a Byzantine worker can accumulate an unbounded state at the server. To retain the Byzantine resilience under compressed uploads, we propose a projected penalty-gradient tracking framework, where each honest worker uploads a compressed residual of its penalty gradient and the server projects each reconstructed state onto the known penalty-gradient ball. The framework accommodates any contractive compressor and yields two algorithms: fault-resilient proximal gradient with consistent sparsification (FRPG-CS), whose shared mask is protected by freshly drawn reserve rows, and lazily aggregated FRPG (LAG-FRPG), whose uploads are event-triggered. For smooth and strongly convex losses, we establish a finite-time bound on the expected objective gap, where the Byzantine error floor is independent of the compressor and the compression error decays faster than the optimization error. We also bound the expected number of lazy uploads and derive certificates that separate the finite-penalty model bias from the optimization error. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST show that the proposed algorithms attain the highest accuracy among twelve methods under the Min-Max attack, whereas a compressed-momentum benchmark and a local-update benchmark remain preferable in attack-free accuracy and total traffic, respectively.

</details>

**作者：** Viktor A. Balashov、Daniil S. Antonenko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22953v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22953v1>)

**内容简述：** Fiberpose 将 SEM 纤维分析重构为平滑距离变换场的直接回归，并配合几何算法完成实例重建、直径与方向测量。

**排除原因：** 虽涉及连续几何场的预测表示，但属于材料与生物医学图像分割测量工具，没有 VLM 推理、latent 状态更新或证据获取与停止决策等具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fiberpose: state-of-the-art fiber morphometry and instance segmentation with a distance-transform neural network

**abstract/A1：** Analysis of fibrous structures in SEM images is a routine task in biomedical engineering and materials science. However, most available methods for fiber quantification still rely on classical image binarization followed by simple post-processing. These approaches often produce unreliable results in images with weak boundaries, heterogeneous contrast, and densely packed fibers. Here, we introduce Fiberpose, a framework that reformulates fiber analysis as direct regression of a smooth distance-transform (DT) field, together with a novel set of geometry-based algorithms specifically adapted to this representation. The predicted field directly encodes fiber geometry, while the accompanying algorithms enable instance reconstruction and measurement of diameter and orientation within a unified workflow. The framework is implemented as a standalone Python desktop application and a web tool https://fiberpose.org. On an independent dataset of fibrous SEM images, Fiberpose achieved state-of-the-art instance-segmentation accuracy in comparison with SAM2 and Cellpose-SAM, and more accurate diameter measurements than DiameterJ, Fibar and GIFT. The method remains effective in dense and weakly contrasted regions and shows qualitative generalization across microscopy modalities not represented in the training data. Fiberpose therefore provides an interpretable geometric representation and an automated pipeline for reproducible quantitative characterization of fibrous materials.

</details>

### 349. [ResoLUT: Distilling Residual Networks into Lookup-Table Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22955v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.22955

**作者：** Katarzyna Fojcik、Aidas Mikalauskas、Matthias Kampa、Renaldas Zioma、Jogundas Armaitis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22955v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22955v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文将 ResNet 逐层蒸馏为 LUT 逻辑网络以提升硬件推理吞吐，属 CNN 分类的硬件效率工作；其「multi-bit residual stream」指卷积特征通道而非推理期 latent 状态，全文不涉及视觉证据筛选、推理状态生成或停止决策，未见到可具体迁移到 LVR 的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ResoLUT: Distilling Residual Networks into Lookup-Table Networks

**abstract/A1：** Logic networks built from Boolean gates or lookup tables (LUTs) promise efficient hardware inference, but training them at depth and learning their connectivity remain difficult. We do not train logic as logic. Instead, ResoLUT distills a trained residual network (ResNet) layer by layer into LUT trees, preserving its multi-bit residual stream and BatchNorm while extracting connectivity from the teacher rather than learning it. To our knowledge, ResoLUT is the first convolutional logic network in computer vision to retain a ResNet-style multi-bit residual stream. Hard LUT decisions are used throughout training and inference, avoiding a discretization gap in the logic. ResoLUT-20 reaches 95.66% on CIFAR-10 with twice the trees, and ResoLUT-56 reaches 78.87% on CIFAR-100 and 53.69% on ImageNet at 64px, the best results of logic and lookup-table networks on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, showing that LUT networks can scale in depth. We explore and quantify the role of the residuals and the tree budget and shape. The most optimized model, a ResoLUT-8 with one eighth of the trees and further optimizations, fits on an FPGA and reaches 90.04% at 189k images per second, the highest throughput among the compared models above 85% on CIFAR-10.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22988v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.22988v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作提出 Abelio 系统，通过重构研究目标、跨学科要素筛选与假设精炼来生成新颖研究方向，并在肠道上皮屏障修复任务上由独立实验验证其提出的两种物理干预方案。

**排除原因：** 论文关注科研假设生成的文本流程与提案评估，不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、证据获取与停止决策，其「发散探索＋收敛选择」的结构过于笼统，难以提供可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structured AI ideation yields physical strategies for epithelial barrier repair

**abstract/A1：** AI systems increasingly support literature analysis, candidate evaluation and hypothesis refinement, but enabling researchers to explore research directions beyond established disciplinary approaches remains challenging. We developed Abelio, an AI system designed to generate novel research directions by expanding investigators' avenues of inquiry. Abelio reformulates research objectives, uses these alternative formulations to identify relevant elements outside conventional disciplinary boundaries, and refines the resulting hypotheses. Each stage integrates divergent exploration with convergent selection through a dedicated subsystem. In quantitative evaluations, proposals generated by the Ideation Core (Abelio's hypothesis-generation core) outperformed those of four frontier large language models in scientific soundness and hypothesis diversity, and three of four in distance from existing literature. Subsystem ablation showed that each subsystem contributes to the property it targets, with the contribution of Element Selection measurable before Refinement. To investigate intestinal epithelial barrier restoration, Abelio proposed physical interventions involving membrane-tethered poly(ethylene glycol) (PEG) and cholesterol-anchored complementary DNA. Both proposals were archived before experimentation and subsequently tested independently in damaged Caco-2 monolayers. PEG preserved barrier function in a membrane-surface-dependent manner, whereas complementary DNA enabled sequence-specific restoration that was reversible on demand. These findings demonstrate how structured research ideation can identify experimentally testable approaches beyond established disciplinary conventions.

</details>

**作者：** Ryan Davi Oliveira de Meneses、Glauber da Rocha Balthazar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23007v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23007v1>)

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是 YOLOv11 在 Android 端对肉鸡行为做目标检测式分类的应用部署，不涉及内部表示、latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无多模态推理机制可供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tracking Ethological Behaviors of Broiler Chickens Through Computer Vision (YoloV11) Using an Android Mobile Device

**abstract/A1：** This study developed and evaluated a computer vision model for automated ethological behavior classification of broiler chickens in climate-controlled poultry houses. Using publicly available videos preprocessed and manually annotated into three classes (sitting, standing, and feeding), the YOLOv11 model was trained and deployed on an Android application via TFLite conversion. Exploratory analysis ensured dataset consistency and visual quality, while evaluation showed class-dependent performance: higher robustness for static postures (sitting, AP=0.531; precision=100%) and lower accuracy for dynamic behaviors (feeding, AP=0.292; recall=0.20). Tests with unseen images demonstrated significant improvements, achieving accuracies of 98.04% for sitting, 83.33% for standing, and 67.65% for feeding. The model showed effective generalization in real farm environments, supporting its potential for continuous behavioral monitoring, early welfare assessments, and decision-making in precision poultry farming. These findings establish a technical foundation for future mobile-based applications at commercial scale, reinforcing the role of computer vision in automated animal welfare evaluation.

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PeriaTrace 是一个面向根尖片的三阶段临床框架，串联牙位级诊断与定位、视觉接地报告生成，以及面向患者的通俗解释与护理建议，并用内部与两个外部队列评估报告质量与医患可用性。

**排除原因：** 属于特定临床任务的多模态报告生成与输出级 grounding 评测，未涉及 latent 推理状态的生成、更新或使用，也未分析内部表示、计算分配或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的机制联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PeriaTrace: Tooth-Level Grounding Diagnosis and Patient-Facing Explanations from Periapical Radiographs

**abstract/A1：** Periapical radiographs (PRs) are central to endodontic diagnosis, but interpreting them and preparing reports are time-consuming. Technical language can limit patients' understanding, while existing automated methods often lack tooth-level links between findings and image evidence. We present PeriaTrace, a three-stage framework integrating tooth-level diagnosis, visually grounded report generation, and patient-friendly explanations and care recommendations. PeriaTrace was developed and evaluated using 12,877 PRs from an internal dataset and two external clinical cohorts. In internal evaluation, it achieved a macro Dice coefficient of 85.35%, BLEU-4 of 0.6504, an F1 score of 94.37% for joint tooth localization and Fédération Dentaire Internationale (FDI) identification, and a grounding-tag F1 score of 77.93%. In the two external cohorts, senior endodontists considered 91.16% and 84.52% of reports suitable for retention or revision without complete rewriting. Generation averaged 4.41 seconds per PR, several tens of times faster than manual drafting. Among 196 survey participants without medical backgrounds, 96.4% preferred reports with plain-language explanations and care recommendations, and 84.7% found visual localization cues helpful. PeriaTrace may enable faster, evidence-traceable reporting while supporting clinical review and patient communication. (Abridged version)

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 5 figures, Under Review

</details>

### 353. [Automated Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation for Underwater Litter Detection: A Comparison of Generative Approaches](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23037v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23037

**作者：** Amala Mary Vincent、Bart De Schutter

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23037v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23037v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一种零标注的水下垃圾数据合成流水线，将类别条件物体插入真实帧并自动生成对应标注，比较 copy-paste、Stable Diffusion 与 DALL-E 三种策略对检测器性能的影响。

**排除原因：** 属于低数据场景下的检测数据增广与合成应用，虽涉及自动监督，但不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等机制，未提供可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Automated Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation for Underwater Litter Detection: A Comparison of Generative Approaches

**abstract/A1：** Underwater object detection is often limited by scarce and imbalanced training data and by strong appearance variations caused by turbidity, lighting, and differences between sensors. Existing data augmentation strategies, such as geometric or color augmentation or fully synthetic image generation with Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), either lack semantic realism, fail to respect scene physics, or require additional annotations. In low-data domains like underwater environmental monitoring, the need for additional annotations is often the core bottleneck. Collecting new footage is hard, but labeling this dataset is even more time-consuming and costly, which limits how quickly a usable dataset can be constructed. We propose a unified fully automated zero-annotation pipeline that inserts class-conditioned trash objects into real underwater frames while preserving the original background context. Starting from sampled bounding boxes, each box is converted into an insertion mask and the object is pasted or generated within it, while the same box coordinates are automatically written as the corresponding annotation. Because labels are derived directly from the sampled boxes, the pipeline generates fully labeled training data for low-data settings without any manual annotation. Within this single pipeline we compare three strategies: copy-paste, Stable Diffusion inpainting, and DALL-E image-conditioned generation. We evaluate bounding-box faithfulness, visual realism, and the impact of the generated samples on downstream detector performance on the SeaClear underwater debris dataset. Results show that DALL-E improves detection performance in four of five domains, with relative gains of up to 96 percent over the real-only baseline.

</details>

A. de Almeida、Paulo Guilherme Molin、Catherine Torres de Almeida、Angélica F. Resende、Vinicius Castro Souza、Marcelo Antonio Pinho Ferreira、Cássio Toledo、Eben North Broadbent、Angelica Almeyda Zambrano、Laura Simões、Juliano van Melis、Ricardo Ribeiro Rodrigues、Pedro H. S. Brancalion

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用多源遥感数据融合的深度学习网络制作巴西大西洋森林冠层高度与地上碳密度地图，并以航空 LiDAR 和样地清查验证精度，属遥感测绘与碳核算应用；没有涉及模型内部推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线缺少实质联系，也无具体可迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Wall-to-wall maps of canopy height and aboveground carbon across the Atlantic Forest

**abstract/A1：** Global canopy height maps are increasingly used to estimate forest carbon, yet their regional accuracy is rarely tested. We present wall-to-wall maps of canopy height (10~m) and aboveground carbon density (30~m) for the Brazilian Atlantic Forest in 2024. Height was predicted by a deep learning network fusing Sentinel-2 reflectance, Sentinel-1 and ALOS PALSAR backscatter and ALOS elevation, trained on 7{,}179 and tested on 1{,}793 patches (58{,}800~km$^2$) from a statewide airborne LiDAR campaign over São Paulo state, Brazil. On held-out test patches, the root mean square error is 3.05~m at 10~m and 2.58~m at 30~m, and the model was validated at two LiDAR sites outside São Paulo. Over the same pixels, the Meta and World Resources Institute global product has more than twice this error. A power law fitted to 124 inventory plots converted canopy height to above ground carbon density. The remaining forest holds 1{,}944~Tg~C over 28.3~Mha. Global products therefore need local validation before use in carbon accounting.

**comment/C1：** 56 pages, 13 figures, 8 tables, 2 supplementary tables. Submitted to Scientific Data. Maps and code openly available via Zenodo and GitHub

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23054

**作者：** Chuanzhi Xu、Boyu Wei、Chengkun Yue、Xuanhua Yin、Yiyang Li、Haodong Chen、Qiang Qu、Weidong Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23054v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23054v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReTouchGS，从预训练 3DGS 模型与相机参数预测场景级颜色查找表，实现无需渲染的审美色彩增强与参考图引导的色彩迁移，并构建 ReTouch3D 基准。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向 3D 场景外观色彩调整，既不涉及内部视觉表示、latent 推理状态，也不涉及证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 研究主线缺少可借用的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReTouchGS: Ultralight Render-free Aesthetic Color Enhancement and Transfer for 3D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic scene reconstruction. Existing 3DGS color transfer and interactive editing methods are guided by reference images and user-specified color adjustments. However, in many applications, users simply want aesthetically guided color enhancement that automatically makes the scene more visually appealing while maintaining consistent colors and tones across viewpoints. We present ReTouchGS, to our knowledge the first framework for reference-free aesthetic color enhancement of pretrained 3DGS models. It predicts a scene-level color lookup table (LUT) from the model and its associated camera parameters. ReTouchGS also supports reference-guided color transfer by fitting a scene-level LUT using one edited view of the same scene and its corresponding original render. A shared blend-aware color refinement network with only 44.7K parameters refines an analytic color update to realize either color mapping without rendering. It preserves geometry and delivers standard 3DGS assets in seconds. We also design the ReTouch3D benchmark to support the evaluation of aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer across viewpoints. Experiments demonstrate high color accuracy and strong generalization to unseen scenes in both aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23063

**作者：** Antonio Manjavacas、David Suárez-Caro、Alejandro Campoy-Nieves、Guillermo Rey、Gonzalo Satrústegui、Miguel Molina-Solana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23063v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23063v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**内容简述：** 该文构建数字孪生并在真实住宅中部署深度强化学习控制器，用于热泵与地暖联合系统的能耗与热舒适优化。

**排除原因：** 属建筑暖通控制的应用案例，虽涉及序列决策与策略从仿真到实物的迁移，但不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态表示与证据使用，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Reinforcement Learning for Heat Pump and Underfloor Heating Control: An End-to-End Case Study

**abstract/A1：** Efficient control of underfloor heating (UFH) systems coupled with heat pumps (HPs) is challenging due to the high thermal inertia of buildings and the slow dynamic response of UFH systems. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a data-driven control approach capable of simultaneously optimizing energy consumption and thermal comfort. However, the application of DRL-based controllers in occupied buildings under real operating conditions remains limited. This work presents and end-to-end case study (development, evaluation, and real-world deployment) of a DRL-based controller for an integrated UFH-HP system. First, a simulation-based pilot study was conducted to assess different DRL algorithms, control strategies, and configurations. Subsequently, a digital twin of a real residential building was developed and used to train DRL agents under simulated operating conditions. The best-performing agent was then deployed in the physical building without additional online fine-tuning. The results show that the controller successfully maintained indoor temperatures within the prescribed comfort limits while dynamically adjusting the HP supply temperature and UFH valve positions. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of deploying DRL controllers in occupied buildings and highlight their robustness and the practical challenges associated with transferring control policies from digital environments to real heating systems.

**comment/C1：** Preprint submited to journal

</details>

### 357. [Values Shape What Self-Improving Coding Agents Build](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23067v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23067

**作者：** Jason Liang、Dylan Waldner、Risto Miikkulainen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23067v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23067v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究在自我改进型代码智能体的持久指令中加入简短价值陈述（Schwartz 四类高阶价值），考察其对任务表现与所构建方案形态的影响，并发现人类设计价值仍有必要。

**排除原因：** 研究关注的是代码智能体自我改进中的价值设定与人工监督，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态表示、证据筛选或停止决策，也无具体可供核查的方法迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Values Shape What Self-Improving Coding Agents Build

**abstract/A1：** Self-improving agents are typically optimized for the results they produce, with less attention to how they produce them. Their instructions give designers limited ways to shape how they work, especially when the task is open-ended. This paper studies whether a short value statement in an agent's persistent instruction file changes not only how well the agent performs but also what it builds. Four higher-order value types in Schwartz's theory of basic human values were evaluated: openness to change, conservation, self-enhancement, and self-transcendence. Across diverse coding tasks, the values were held fixed within each run over multiple iterations of self-improvement. Three findings emerged: (1) Values matter for performance across a diverse set of tasks. The openness values increase performance on held-out data, whereas the opposite conservation values result in scores below those of an agent given no values. (2) Values change what agents build, producing solution algorithms that differ in size, complexity and approach. (3) Human oversight is still needed for designing values. Allowing the agent to revise its own values narrows the differences between sets of values, and values distilled from the AI literature provide no detectable gain over having no values. Properly designed values can thus influence what a self-improving coding agent builds and, where the task permits, how well it performs.

</details>

### 358. [UnrealEvent: Synthetic Dataset Generation and Annotation for Event-Based Vision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23078v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23078

**作者：** Yentel Schoovaerts、Zijie Ning、Patrick Vandewalle

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23078v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23078v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出基于 Unreal Engine 的事件相机合成数据生成与自动标注工具 UnrealEvent，并在 eTraM 上评估 YOLOv8 的合成到真实迁移。

**排除原因：** 属事件视觉数据合成与检测器评测，既不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据使用，也无表征或决策层面的可借鉴线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UnrealEvent: Synthetic Dataset Generation and Annotation for Event-Based Vision

**abstract/A1：** Event-based cameras provide high temporal resolution, low power consumption, and a high dynamic range, but progress in event-based object detection remains constrained by the scarcity of annotated training data. To solve this issue, this paper introduces the tool UnrealEvent, a synthetic dataset-generation pipeline implemented in Unreal Engine 5.5. The pipeline combines a V2E-inspired event-frame generator with EasySynth semantic-rendering passes, allowing rendered level sequences to be converted into paired event representations and YOLO-format bounding-box annotations without manual labeling. Events are generated from per-pixel log-luminance differences between consecutive frames, with contrast thresholds sampled independently from a normal distribution to emulate the fabrication-induced threshold mismatch of real sensors. Using this pipeline, we provide a synthetic dataset and evaluate YOLOv8 detectors under synthetic, zero-shot real, and real fine-tuning training on eTraM. The results show strong in-domain synthetic performance but a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, especially for pedestrians. Fine-tuning on a small real subset improves real-domain performance, while the remaining gap highlights the importance of asset diversity and sensor calibration.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Workshop on Neuromorphic Vision (NeVi), ECCV 2026

</details>

### 359. [LUTBench: Benchmarking Text-Driven Photographic Look Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23113

**作者：** Chuanzhi Xu、Boyu Wei、Chengkun Yue、Xuanhua Yin、Yiyang Li、Chupeng Liu、Zihan Deng、Weidong Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23113v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建 LUTBench，用 91 个 3D LUT 与 FiveK 照片生成大规模可复现的图文「色调迁移」配对，并统一评估文本驱动调色方法的保真度、敏感性与泛化性。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是摄影调色的图像编辑基准，不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也未提供状态更新或监督方面的可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LUTBench: Benchmarking Text-Driven Photographic Look Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Photographic color grading shapes the tone and color of a photograph to achieve a desired look. Text-driven photographic look transfer lets users specify these adjustments in natural language, with the goal of reproducing the requested look across photographs while preserving their content. However, prior research on text-driven color grading uses different datasets, text instructions, target definitions, and metrics. These differences make it difficult to compare how faithfully the methods reproduce the same look. To compare methods under a shared protocol, we introduce LUTBench. We collect 91 three-dimensional lookup tables (3D LUTs) and define one photographic look per LUT with a specified input conversion. Applying these looks to 5,000 MIT-Adobe FiveK photographs produces 455,000 input-target image pairs with reproducible target images. For each look, we use templates to turn measured tone and color changes on fixed color samples into candidate clauses. We use training image measurements to verify applicable clauses before composing the text description. The evaluation measures target fidelity, tests sensitivity to predefined input conversions, and separates generalization to unseen looks from reproduction of seen looks on new photographs. Across 16 methods, stronger text alignment does not guarantee target fidelity. However, most supervised variants improve fidelity on both seen and unseen looks.

</details>

### 360. [A Neural Envelope Method for Rapid Gradient-based Optimum Takeoff Trajectory Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23130v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23130

**作者：** Samuel Sisk、Xiaosong Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23130v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23130v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作将物理约束 GAN 与神经凸包结合，把受约束的非凸起飞轨迹优化转化为无约束凸优化，并在 eVTOL 机型上验证求解精度与加速效果。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是飞行器轨迹的工程优化，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态；其凸化加速思路与 LVR 的表示、证据使用或停止决策缺乏具体可借鉴联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Neural Envelope Method for Rapid Gradient-based Optimum Takeoff Trajectory Design

**abstract/A1：** Urban air mobility arises to mitigate ground traffic congestion. Multidisciplinary analysis and optimization (MDAO) has been introduced to reduce the energy consumption, thereby expanding aircraft performance. Surrogate-based MDAO lowers the cost of optimization, but multimodality disrupts global optimum search for both simulation-based and surrogate-based optimizations. Convex envelopes convert multimodal spaces into unimodal spaces, permitting efficient convex optimization; however, convexifying a design space may produce infeasible "optimal" designs due to complex design constraints. To address these challenges, this work proposes the coupling of a physics-constrained generative adversarial networks (physicsGAN) and neural envelope to transform original, constrained, non-convex optimizations into unconstrained, convex optimizations. Specifically, the physicsGAN discovers a feasible design space where all design candidates inherently satisfy all constraints, by penalizing surrogate-predicted constraint violations during training. Moreover, the neural envelope constructs a neural network-based convex envelope for the transformed objective function within the physicsGAN-discovered feasible space. Note that the neural envelope only needs to deal with objective function since constraints are inherently satisfied in the feasible space. The neural envelope is demonstrated on takeoff trajectory optimizations of the Airbus A3 Vahana eVTOL aircraft. Results show that neural envelopes achieve a 99.91% mean accuracy within a flight-condition space compared with reference convex envelopes. Neural-envelope-enabled optimizations achieve 99.84% accuracy on the global optimum design, costing only 0.04 seconds on average. The neural envelope accelerates the trajectory optimization by 53 and 162 times on average compared to the physicsGAN-enabled gradient-based and global optimizations, respectively.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 12 figures, submitted to AIAA Aviation conference

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了 GameRealm：一个从电子游戏采集的大规模多模态数据集，含 1.2 亿余帧 RGB、度量深度、相机参数、音频、控制输入与 3D 点轨迹，并提供统一采集流程，实验用于验证其在视觉生成与几何学习任务上的增益。

**排除原因：** 属于面向视觉基座模型的数据资源与生成/几何学习评测，不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选、停止决策或答案对内部内容的使用，难以形成与 LVR 主线的具体关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GameRealm: Grounding Visual Learning in Interactive Games

**abstract/A1：** Visual foundation models are central to physical intelligence, yet their development remains constrained by fragmented training datasets, inconsistent annotation quality, and limited multimodal supervision. To address these challenges, we introduce GameRealm, a large-scale, richly annotated dataset that grounds visual observations in synchronized, game-native supervision. GameRealm encompasses over 120 million RGB frames and 1,000 hours of gameplay footage across all views. Beyond its unprecedented scale, GameRealm provides rich multimodal supervision, including metric depth, camera parameters, synchronized audio, control inputs, and 3D point trajectories. Its diversity further extends to flexible view configurations, spanning single-view, stereo, multi-view, and panoramic recordings. To enable scalable acquisition across diverse game environments, we develop a unified data collection pipeline that combines human-operated interaction with agentic exploration. Extensive experiments across diverse visual learning tasks demonstrate that GameRealm consistently strengthens visual foundation models in both visual generation and geometric learning. These results establish GameRealm as a versatile and valuable data resource, unlocking new opportunities for advancing visual intelligence.

</details>

<a id="paper-362"></a>

### 362. [Agent Trust Infrastructure (ATI): A Federated Identity, Trust, Attestation, and Accountability Architecture for Cross-Organization Autonomous AI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23202v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2611.23202

**作者：** Viet K. Nguyen、Mohammad I. Husain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23202v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.23202v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出跨组织 AI agent 的联邦信任基础设施 ATI，基于 PKI、零信任与远程证明验证 agent 身份、模型/工具/提示配置与授权，并评估攻击拦截率与性能开销。

**排除原因：** 属于多 agent 跨组织身份认证与运行时安全执行层，不涉及视觉细节表示、latent 推理状态生成或证据使用，也无状态更新或监督层面的可借鉴线索，与 LVR 研究主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agent Trust Infrastructure (ATI): A Federated Identity, Trust, Attestation, and Accountability Architecture for Cross-Organization Autonomous AI Agents

**abstract/A1：** AI agents increasingly collaborate across organizational boundaries, invoking tools and interacting with agents operated by independent entities. Although existing agent frameworks support planning and coordination, they lack a common mechanism for verifying cross-organizational agent identity, execution integrity, and authorization. We propose the Agent Trust Infrastructure (ATI), a framework-independent security architecture built on Public Key Infrastructure (PKI), Zero Trust Architecture (ZTA), and remote attestation. ATI establishes federated trust through peer organizational certificate authorities (AgentCAs), eliminating the need for a shared root authority. Before executing requests, ATI validates agent certificates, verifies approved model, tool, and system-prompt configurations, and enforces authorization through three policy checks. We implement ATI as an external security enforcement layer that integrates with existing agent frameworks without modifying their underlying models. We evaluate ATI in a simulated federation of eight organizations using six agent frameworks, each deploying a five-agent team. Security experiments involve two organizations and assess 14 attack scenarios and six benign tasks across five agent models, using five attack variations and three repetitions per variation. A panel of judge models evaluates the experimental outcomes. With all enforcement mechanisms enabled, attack containment increases from 43% to 84% (40% to 80% for the 11 attack scenarios evaluated consistently across all configurations), while enforcement introduces approximately 174 microseconds of overhead per request. ATI is released as an open-source, framework-independent package that uses IETF WIMSE workload token formats for identity and request verification and Entity Attestation Tokens for model, tool, and system-prompt integrity verification.

</details>

</details>
